// === V211 CHARACTER XP CURVE ===============================================
// XP needed to go from the CURRENT level to the next one.
// Early levels are deliberately slower than before; from level 10 onward
// the requirement grows by 20% per level so progression stays meaningful
// without the old runaway x1.4 curve.
const XP_REQ_EARLY={1:200,2:300,3:450,4:650,5:900,6:1200,7:1600,8:2100,9:2700,10:3400};
function xpRequiredForLevel(level){
  const L=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(level)||1));
  if(L<=10)return XP_REQ_EARLY[L];
  return Math.max(3400,Math.round((3400*Math.pow(1.20,L-10))/100)*100);
}
function syncXpRequirement(){P.xn=xpRequiredForLevel(P.lvl);return P.xn;}

let transitioning=false;

const EN=[],PR=[],PT=[],LOOT=[];
const PLAYER_CANNONBALLS=[]; // V126: dwarf shots; separate from hostile boss projectiles

function gnomeFireCannonball(){
  // Select ONCE at the moment of the shot. No target search is done in the
  // projectile update loop.
  let target=null;
  if(P.tid!=null){
    target=EN.find(function(e){return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0&&targetIsValid(e)})||null;
    if(target){
      const edge=(target.sz&&target.sz>30)?Math.max(0,(target.sz-30)*.4):0;
      if(Math.hypot(target.x-P.x,target.y-P.y)>playerBasicRange()+edge)target=null;
    }
  }
  if(!target)target=findNearBasic();
  P.tid=target?target.id:null;
  if(!target)return false;

  const dx=target.x-P.x,dy=target.y-P.y;
  const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const speed=7;

  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.shootT=1;
  P.recoil=0;

  const muzzleX=P.x+dx/dist*24;
  const muzzleY=P.y-7+dy/dist*10;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.push({
    x:muzzleX,
    y:muzzleY,
    vx:dx/dist*speed,
    vy:dy/dist*speed,
    remaining:dist,
    target:target
  });

  // Very small game-layer muzzle kick so firing while running still reads
  // clearly without adding effects into the sprite sheet.
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    PT.push({
      x:muzzleX,y:muzzleY,
      vx:dx/dist*(1.2+i*.45)+(Math.random()-.5)*.6,
      vy:dy/dist*(1.2+i*.45)+(Math.random()-.5)*.6,
      life:6,ml:6,sz:1.5+i*.45,
      col:i===0?'#ffd36a':'#c47a32'
    });
  }
  return true;
}
function updatePlayerCannonballs(){
  // Extremely small loop: move each ball toward the coordinates captured at
  // fire time. At arrival, use the game's already proven damage routine.
  for(let i=PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const b=PLAYER_CANNONBALLS[i];
    b.x+=b.vx;
    b.y+=b.vy;
    b.remaining-=Math.hypot(b.vx,b.vy);

    if(b.remaining<=0){
      const t=b.target;
      if(t&&t.hp>0){
        try{
          applyBasicAttackHit(t);
        }catch(_){
          const raw=Math.max(1,Math.floor(P.atk||12)-(t.def||0));
          t.hp=Math.max(0,t.hp-raw);
          t.flash=6;t.aggro=true;
        }
      }
      PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.splice(i,1);
    }
  }
}
function drawPlayerCannonballs(){
  const _vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),_vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  for(const b of PLAYER_CANNONBALLS){
    const x=b.x-cam.x,y=b.y-cam.y;
    if(x<-24||y<-24||x>_vw+24||y>_vh+24)continue;
    cx.save();

    // Small but clearly readable cannonball on a phone screen.
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(214,125,42,.30)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x,y,10,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.fillStyle='#24272b';
    cx.strokeStyle='#d68a3d';
    cx.lineWidth=2;
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x,y,7,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.stroke();

    cx.fillStyle='rgba(255,221,155,.75)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x-2,y-2,2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.restore();
  }
}

// === V141 ARCHER BASIC ARROW ==============================================
const PLAYER_ARROWS=[];

function archerFireArrow(){
  let target=null;

  // Prefer the target already locked by Smart Attack.
  if(P.tid!=null){
    target=EN.find(function(e){
      return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0&&targetIsValid(e);
    })||null;

    if(target){
      const range=playerBasicRange();
      if(P.scene==='clanboss1'&&target.isClanBoss){
        if(clanBossDistanceFromBody(target,P.x,P.y)>range)target=null;
      }else{
        const edge=(target.sz&&target.sz>30)?Math.max(0,(target.sz-30)*.4):0;
        if(Math.hypot(target.x-P.x,target.y-P.y)>range+edge)target=null;
      }
    }
  }

  if(!target)target=findNearBasic();
  P.tid=target?target.id:null;
  if(!target)return false;

  const dx=target.x-P.x,dy=target.y-P.y;
  const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const speed=12;

  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.shootT=1;
  P.recoil=0;

  // Start near the bow hand / chest rather than from the character's feet.
  const startX=P.x+Math.cos(ang)*20;
  const startY=P.y-18+Math.sin(ang)*8;

  PLAYER_ARROWS.push({
    x:startX,y:startY,
    vx:Math.cos(ang)*speed,
    vy:Math.sin(ang)*speed,
    remaining:dist,
    target:target,
    ang:ang
  });

  // Tiny bow release read; deliberately light, no glow spam.
  PT.push({
    x:startX,y:startY,
    vx:-Math.cos(ang)*.7,vy:-Math.sin(ang)*.7,
    life:5,ml:5,sz:1.5,col:'#d8c18b'
  });
  return true;
}

function updatePlayerArrows(){
  for(let i=PLAYER_ARROWS.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const a=PLAYER_ARROWS[i];
    a.x+=a.vx;
    a.y+=a.vy;
    a.remaining-=Math.hypot(a.vx,a.vy);

    if(a.remaining<=0){
      const t=a.target;
      if(t&&t.hp>0){
        try{
          applyBasicAttackHit(t);
        }catch(_){
          const raw=Math.max(1,Math.floor(P.atk||12)-(t.def||0));
          t.hp=Math.max(0,t.hp-raw);
          t.flash=6;t.aggro=true;
        }

        // Small pixel impact only.
        for(let j=0;j<3;j++){
          PT.push({
            x:t.x+(Math.random()-.5)*5,
            y:t.y-8+(Math.random()-.5)*5,
            vx:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,
            vy:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,
            life:7,ml:7,sz:1.3+Math.random(),
            col:j===0?'#e9e1c6':'#8a6b42'
          });
        }
      }
      PLAYER_ARROWS.splice(i,1);
    }
  }
}

function drawPlayerArrows(){
  const _vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),_vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  for(const a of PLAYER_ARROWS){
    const x=a.x-cam.x,y=a.y-cam.y;
    if(x<-28||y<-28||x>_vw+28||y>_vh+28)continue;

    cx.save();
    cx.translate(x,y);
    cx.rotate(a.ang);

    // Pixel-like wooden shaft.
    cx.fillStyle='#b98b55';
    cx.fillRect(-10,-1,16,2);

    // Pale metal arrow head.
    cx.fillStyle='#d9dde0';
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(10,0);
    cx.lineTo(5,-3);
    cx.lineTo(5,3);
    cx.closePath();
    cx.fill();

    // Small dark-green fletching matching the ranger palette.
    cx.fillStyle='#556b3b';
    cx.fillRect(-11,-3,4,2);
    cx.fillRect(-11,1,4,2);

    cx.restore();
  }
}

const EN=[],PR=[],PT=[],LOOT=[];
const PLAYER_CANNONBALLS=[]; // V126: dwarf shots; separate from hostile boss projectiles

function gnomeFireCannonball(){
  // Select ONCE at the moment of the shot. No target search is done in the
  // projectile update loop.
  let target=null;
  if(P.tid!=null){
    target=EN.find(function(e){return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0&&targetIsValid(e)})||null;
    if(target){
      const edge=(target.sz&&target.sz>30)?Math.max(0,(target.sz-30)*.4):0;
      if(Math.hypot(target.x-P.x,target.y-P.y)>playerBasicRange()+edge)target=null;
    }
  }
  if(!target)target=findNearBasic();
  P.tid=target?target.id:null;
  if(!target)return false;

  const dx=target.x-P.x,dy=target.y-P.y;
  const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const speed=7;

  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.shootT=1;
  P.recoil=0;

  const muzzleX=P.x+dx/dist*24;
  const muzzleY=P.y-7+dy/dist*10;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.push({
    x:muzzleX,
    y:muzzleY,
    vx:dx/dist*speed,
    vy:dy/dist*speed,
    remaining:dist,
    target:target
  });

  // Very small game-layer muzzle kick so firing while running still reads
  // clearly without adding effects into the sprite sheet.
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    PT.push({
      x:muzzleX,y:muzzleY,
      vx:dx/dist*(1.2+i*.45)+(Math.random()-.5)*.6,
      vy:dy/dist*(1.2+i*.45)+(Math.random()-.5)*.6,
      life:6,ml:6,sz:1.5+i*.45,
      col:i===0?'#ffd36a':'#c47a32'
    });
  }
  return true;
}
function updatePlayerCannonballs(){
  // Extremely small loop: move each ball toward the coordinates captured at
  // fire time. At arrival, use the game's already proven damage routine.
  for(let i=PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const b=PLAYER_CANNONBALLS[i];
    b.x+=b.vx;
    b.y+=b.vy;
    b.remaining-=Math.hypot(b.vx,b.vy);

    if(b.remaining<=0){
      const t=b.target;
      if(t&&t.hp>0){
        try{
          applyBasicAttackHit(t);
        }catch(_){
          const raw=Math.max(1,Math.floor(P.atk||12)-(t.def||0));
          t.hp=Math.max(0,t.hp-raw);
          t.flash=6;t.aggro=true;
        }
      }
      PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.splice(i,1);
    }
  }
}
function drawPlayerCannonballs(){
  const _vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),_vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  for(const b of PLAYER_CANNONBALLS){
    const x=b.x-cam.x,y=b.y-cam.y;
    if(x<-24||y<-24||x>_vw+24||y>_vh+24)continue;
    cx.save();

    // Small but clearly readable cannonball on a phone screen.
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(214,125,42,.30)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x,y,10,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.fillStyle='#24272b';
    cx.strokeStyle='#d68a3d';
    cx.lineWidth=2;
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x,y,7,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.stroke();

    cx.fillStyle='rgba(255,221,155,.75)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x-2,y-2,2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.restore();
  }
}

// === V141 ARCHER BASIC ARROW ==============================================
const PLAYER_ARROWS=[];

function archerFireArrow(){
  let target=null;

  // Prefer the target already locked by Smart Attack.
  if(P.tid!=null){
    target=EN.find(function(e){
      return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0&&targetIsValid(e);
    })||null;

    if(target){
      const range=playerBasicRange();
      if(P.scene==='clanboss1'&&target.isClanBoss){
        if(clanBossDistanceFromBody(target,P.x,P.y)>range)target=null;
      }else{
        const edge=(target.sz&&target.sz>30)?Math.max(0,(target.sz-30)*.4):0;
        if(Math.hypot(target.x-P.x,target.y-P.y)>range+edge)target=null;
      }
    }
  }

  if(!target)target=findNearBasic();
  P.tid=target?target.id:null;
  if(!target)return false;

  const dx=target.x-P.x,dy=target.y-P.y;
  const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const speed=12;

  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.shootT=1;
  P.recoil=0;

  // Start near the bow hand / chest rather than from the character's feet.
  const startX=P.x+Math.cos(ang)*20;
  const startY=P.y-18+Math.sin(ang)*8;

  PLAYER_ARROWS.push({
    x:startX,y:startY,
    vx:Math.cos(ang)*speed,
    vy:Math.sin(ang)*speed,
    remaining:dist,
    target:target,
    ang:ang
  });

  // Tiny bow release read; deliberately light, no glow spam.
  PT.push({
    x:startX,y:startY,
    vx:-Math.cos(ang)*.7,vy:-Math.sin(ang)*.7,
    life:5,ml:5,sz:1.5,col:'#d8c18b'
  });
  return true;
}

function updatePlayerArrows(){
  for(let i=PLAYER_ARROWS.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const a=PLAYER_ARROWS[i];
    a.x+=a.vx;
    a.y+=a.vy;
    a.remaining-=Math.hypot(a.vx,a.vy);

    if(a.remaining<=0){
      const t=a.target;
      if(t&&t.hp>0){
        try{
          applyBasicAttackHit(t);
        }catch(_){
          const raw=Math.max(1,Math.floor(P.atk||12)-(t.def||0));
          t.hp=Math.max(0,t.hp-raw);
          t.flash=6;t.aggro=true;
        }

        // Small pixel impact only.
        for(let j=0;j<3;j++){
          PT.push({
            x:t.x+(Math.random()-.5)*5,
            y:t.y-8+(Math.random()-.5)*5,
            vx:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,
            vy:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,
            life:7,ml:7,sz:1.3+Math.random(),
            col:j===0?'#e9e1c6':'#8a6b42'
          });
        }
      }
      PLAYER_ARROWS.splice(i,1);
    }
  }
}

function drawPlayerArrows(){
  const _vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),_vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  for(const a of PLAYER_ARROWS){
    const x=a.x-cam.x,y=a.y-cam.y;
    if(x<-28||y<-28||x>_vw+28||y>_vh+28)continue;

    cx.save();
    cx.translate(x,y);
    cx.rotate(a.ang);

    // Pixel-like wooden shaft.
    cx.fillStyle='#b98b55';
    cx.fillRect(-10,-1,16,2);

    // Pale metal arrow head.
    cx.fillStyle='#d9dde0';
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(10,0);
    cx.lineTo(5,-3);
    cx.lineTo(5,3);
    cx.closePath();
    cx.fill();

    // Small dark-green fletching matching the ranger palette.
    cx.fillStyle='#556b3b';
    cx.fillRect(-11,-3,4,2);
    cx.fillRect(-11,1,4,2);

    cx.restore();
  }
}

const MAX_PARTICLES=140; // V56 mobile combat cap; keeps effects visible without GC spikes

  if(now<(e.aiHitFxUntil||0)){

let __ppaHudNextAt=0;
function update(){
  PPAOnlineTick();
  ppaCleanupDeathRemnants();
  updateConsumableHud(false);
  updatePremiumRegen();
  if(P.dead)return;
  v174AiTickPlayerDebuffs();

  const _smartMove=updateSmartAttackInput();
  // V139: Smart Attack only supplies movement when joystick is neutral.
  // Any live joystick input keeps full manual movement while target lock stays active.
  const _moveX=_smartMove?_smartMove.x:jX;
  const _moveY=_smartMove?_smartMove.y:jY;

  // V174: class-AI crowd control is numeric only (no particle FX).
  const _aiMoveMul=Date.now()<(P.aiRootUntil||0)?0:(Date.now()<(P.aiSlowUntil||0)?Math.max(.30,Number(P.aiSlowMul)||.55):1);
  P.vx=_moveX*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  P.vy=_moveY*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  let nx=P.x+P.vx, ny=P.y+P.vy;

  // Keep in bounds (per scene)
  const sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  nx=Math.max(sc.bounds.minX,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxX,nx));
  ny=Math.max(sc.bounds.minY,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxY,ny));

  if(P.scene==='arena'){
    // Stay on the circular arena floor — no wandering into the black void
    const c=sc.center,rad=460;
    const dx=nx-c.x,dy=ny-c.y,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
    if(dist>rad){nx=c.x+dx/dist*rad;ny=c.y+dy/dist*rad}
  }else if(P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam'){
    const mv=pvpSlide(P.scene,P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
    // Dungeon walls — slide along corridors, never clip through
    const mv=dgSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clansiege'){
    const mv=clanSiegeSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clanboss1'){
    const mv=clanBossMoveWithBodyCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='worldboss'){
    const mv=worldBossMoveWithCrystalCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='fartzone'){
    // Exact user-drawn meadow boundary: outside = solid collision.
    // Do not alter town/building hitboxes or other scenes.
    if(!pointInPolygonFartZone(nx,ny)){
      nx=P.x;ny=P.y;
    }
  }else{
    // Solid buildings — can't walk through walls or across rooftops
    const resolved=resolveCollision(nx,ny);
    nx=resolved.x;ny=resolved.y;
  }
  // V104: keep the player's visible foot shadow outside Phoenix/mob shadows.
  // The map/wall collision above remains authoritative; this only adds body blocking.
  if(P.scene==='dungeon'||P.scene==='arena'){
    const _bodyMv=ppaMoveWithWorldBodyCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*.24);
    nx=_bodyMv.x;ny=_bodyMv.y;
  }
  P.x=nx;P.y=ny;
  if(P.scene==='clansiege')clanSiegeUpdateCapture();

  // Portal collision
  if(P.portalCD>0)P.portalCD--;
  if(P.portalCD<=0&&!transitioning&&sc.portal){
    const p=sc.portal;
    if(Math.hypot(P.x-p.x,P.y-p.y)<p.r+20){
      changeScene(p.target);
    }
  }
  // Town teleport -> dungeon (walk into the rune circle)
  if(P.portalCD<=0&&!transitioning&&P.scene==='safe'&&sc.teleport&&sc.teleport.target){
    const tp=sc.teleport;
    if(Math.hypot(P.x-tp.x,P.y-tp.y)<tp.r){
      changeScene(tp.target);
    }
  }
  // 1x1 duel preparation: both players may move, but PvP damage stays locked for 10 seconds.
  updatePvpDuelWarmup();

  // Arena ladder: only a fully cleared stage advances.
  if(P.scene==='arena'){
    if(waveActive&&arenaWaveKilled>=arenaWaveTotal&&arenaActiveCount()===0){
      arenaRecordWave(arenaStageProgress());
      waveActive=false;
      waveTimer=180; // 3 sec break: enough to read the HUD, short enough to keep pace
    }
    if(!waveActive&&waveTimer>0){
      waveTimer--;
      if(waveTimer<=0)startWave();
    }
  }

  if(Math.abs(P.vx)>0.1||Math.abs(P.vy)>0.1)P.bob+=0.28;
  else P.bob+=0.04;

  if(P.shootT<=0){
    // Update facing based on horizontal movement for side sprite flip
    if(P.vx > 0.1) P.face = 1;
    else if(P.vx < -0.1) P.face = -1;
    // When moving vertically, keep last horizontal facing
  }

  if(P.shootCD>0)P.shootCD--;
  if(P.shootT>0)P.shootT-=0.12;
  if(P.recoil>0)P.recoil-=0.1;

  if(attackQueued&&P.shootCD<=0){
    attackQueued=false;

    // Preserve the joystick state. Combat must never cancel movement.
    const movingNow=playerIsMoving();

    if(playerUsesGnomeSprites()){
      try{gnomeFireCannonball()}catch(_){PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0}
    }else if(playerUsesArcherSprites()){
      try{archerFireArrow()}catch(_){PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0}
    }else{
      melee();
    }

    P.shootCD=Math.max(1,Math.round(60/((P.atkSpd||1)*shopAtkSpeedMul())));

    if(movingNow && P.attackMode!=='melee' && !playerUsesArcherSprites() && !playerUsesPriestSprites() && !playerUsesMageSprites()){
      // Gnome/ranged legacy behavior stays unchanged:
      // projectile can fire while the running animation continues.
      P.attacking=false;
      P.anim='run';
      P.animTimer=Math.min(P.animTimer||0,6);
      P.runAttackT=14;
    }else{
      // Assassin AND Archer now use their real ATTACK sprites while moving.
      // Movement itself is NOT cancelled; joystick/Smart Attack still moves P.x/P.y.
      P.attacking=true;
      P.anim='attack';
      P.animFrame=0;
      P.animTimer=0;
      P.runAttackT=movingNow?14:0;
    }
  }

  if(P.runAttackT>0)P.runAttackT--;

  // Sprite animation state machine:
  // attack > run > idle.
  // Assassin and Archer keep their real ATTACK animation while moving.
  // Gnome keeps its approved run+separate-projectile behavior.
  const movingForAnim=playerIsMoving();
  if(P.attacking&&movingForAnim&&P.attackMode!=='melee'&&!playerUsesArcherSprites()&&!playerUsesPriestSprites()&&!playerUsesMageSprites()){
    P.attacking=false;
    P.anim='run';
    P.animTimer=0;
    P.animFrame=P.animFrame%Math.max(1,playerAnimDef('run').frames);
  }

  if(P.attacking){
    P.animTimer++;
    const a=playerAnimDef('attack');
    const frameDur = Math.max(1, Math.round(60/a.fps));
    if(P.animTimer >= frameDur){
      P.animTimer = 0;
      P.animFrame++;
      if(P.animFrame >= a.frames){
        P.attacking = false;
        P.animFrame = 0;
        P.animTimer = 0;
      }
    }
  } else {
    const moving = movingForAnim;
    const nextAnim = moving ? 'run' : 'idle';
    if(P.anim !== nextAnim){
      // Smooth transition: don't reset to frame 0 if coming from similar pose
      P.anim = nextAnim;
      P.animFrame = 0;
      P.animTimer = 0;
    }
    const a = playerAnimDef(nextAnim);
    P.animTimer++;
    const frameDur = Math.max(1, Math.round(60 / a.fps));
    if(P.animTimer >= frameDur){
      P.animTimer = 0;
      P.animFrame = (P.animFrame + 1) % a.frames;
    }
  }

  // Сброс зависшей цели:
  // если моб умер, ушёл слишком далеко, скрылся за стеной
  // или игрок покинул его комнату — красная метка исчезает.
  if(P.tid!=null){
    const locked=EN.find(x=>x.id===P.tid);
    if(!targetIsValid(locked))P.tid=null;
  }

  // Fart Zone mine production + 30-minute guard respawns.
  updateFartZoneSystem();

  // Enemies
  for(let i=EN.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const e=EN[i];
    const dx=P.x-e.x,dy=P.y-e.y,d2=dx*dx+dy*dy;
    const reach=42+(e.sz-30)*0.35;
    // В подземелье одновременно размещено более 500 мобов.
    // Далёкие нетронутые мобы "спят" уже после 500px. Проверяем квадрат расстояния,
    // чтобы не делать сотни Math.hypot() на мобильном каждый кадр.
    if(P.scene==='dungeon'&&!e.isBoss&&!e.aggro&&e.hp===e.mhp&&d2>250000){
      if(e.atkCD>0)e.atkCD--;
      e.bob+=0.025;
      continue;
    }
    // V276 FPS: Fart Zone can hold 39–65 guards. Untouched guards far away
    // do not need movement/path/attack AI until the player approaches.
    if(P.scene==='fartzone'&&e.isFartGuard&&!e.aggro&&e.hp===e.mhp&&d2>360000){
      if(e.atkCD>0)e.atkCD--;
      e.bob+=0.025;
      continue;
    }
    const d=Math.sqrt(d2);
    if(e.isAiFighter){
      v174UpdateAiTrainingFighter(e,d);
      continue;
    }
    if(e.isBoss){
      // Boss/static siege objects never leave their anchor.
      if(e.hx!==undefined){e.x=e.hx;e.y=e.hy}

      if(e.isClanSiegeCrystal){
        // Destructible objective: does not move and never attacks.
      }else if(e.isWorldCrystalBoss){
        worldCrystalBossUpdate(e,dx,dy,d);
      }else if(e.isDungeon21Boss){
        dungeon21BossUpdate(e,dx,dy,d);
      }else if(e.isClanBoss){
        if(clanBossIsCerberus(e)){
          cerberusUpdateVisual(e,dx,dy,d);
        }else{
          clanBossUpdateAoe(e,d);
          if(e.aoeState==='idle')clanBossUpdateWhip(e,dx,dy,d);
        }
      }else if(d<=reach&&e.atkCD<=0){
        if(Math.random()*100<effectivePlayerDodge()){
          showPickup('Уворот!','#88ffcc');
        }else{
          P.hp-=playerDmg(e.dmg,e.dmgType||'physical');
        }
        e.atkCD=mobAttackCooldown(e);
        for(let j=0;j<6;j++)PT.push({x:P.x,y:P.y-15,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*5,vy:(Math.random()-.5)*5,life:12,ml:12,sz:2+Math.random()*2,col:'#ff0000'});
      }
    }else{
      // === STANDARD MOB AGGRO / ROOM LEASH ===
      // A mob wakes only when the player enters this mob's personal aggro radius.
      // Dungeon mobs never leave their own room.
      // If the player runs out, the mob reaches the room edge, drops aggro,
      // and returns to its exact spawn point.
      if(e.hx===undefined){e.hx=e.x;e.hy=e.y;e.hunter=true}

      const homeD=Math.hypot(e.x-e.hx,e.y-e.hy);
      let chaseX=P.x,chaseY=P.y;
      let playerInOwnRoom=true;

      if(P.scene==='arena'){
        e.aggro=true;
      }else if(P.scene==='fartzone'&&e.isFartGuard){
        const _fm=fartMineById(e.fartMineId);
        if(_fm){
          const _minePlayerD=Math.hypot(P.x-_fm.x,P.y-_fm.y);
          // Every guard protects its mine, not just its own personal position.
          if(_minePlayerD<=FART_GUARD_AGGRO_RADIUS||d<=FART_GUARD_AGGRO_RADIUS*.82)e.aggro=true;
          if(e.aggro&&_minePlayerD>FART_GUARD_AGGRO_RADIUS*2.25)e.aggro=false;
        }
      }else if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
        playerInOwnRoom=(e.room===undefined)||dgPointInRoom(e.room,P.x,P.y,0);

        // Proximity aggro is individual, not a whole-room group aggro.
        if(!e.aggro && playerInOwnRoom && d<=dungeonAggroRadiusFor(e)){
          e.aggro=true;
          e.edgeChase=false;
          e.edgeWait=0;
        }

        // Direct damage already sets e.aggro=true in the hit code.
        // Once player is outside this room, chase only to the nearest room edge.
        if(e.aggro && e.room!==undefined && !playerInOwnRoom){
          e.edgeChase=true;
          const edgePad=Math.max(12,e.sz*0.22);
          const edge=dgClampToRoom(e.room,P.x,P.y,edgePad);
          chaseX=edge.x;
          chaseY=edge.y;

          const edgeD=Math.hypot(edge.x-e.x,edge.y-e.y);
          if(edgeD<=Math.max(14,e.sp*3.2)){
            e.aggro=false;
            e.edgeChase=false;
            e.edgeWait=0;
          }
        }else if(e.aggro){
          e.edgeChase=false;
          e.edgeWait=0;
        }
      }else{
        if(d<=AGGRO_R)e.aggro=true;
        if(e.aggro&&(d>LEASH_R||homeD>LEASH_R))e.aggro=false;
      }

      if(playerSmokeActive()){
        e.aggro=false;
        e.edgeChase=false;
        e.edgeWait=0;
      }

      let tx,ty,move=false;

      if(e.aggro){
        const cdx=chaseX-e.x,cdy=chaseY-e.y;
        const cd=Math.hypot(cdx,cdy);
        const stopDist=(P.scene==='dungeon'&&e.edgeChase)?10:(e.isFartGuard?FART_GUARD_ATTACK_REACH:reach);
        if(cd>stopDist){
          tx=cdx/cd*e.sp*P.slowMul*(typeof v189EnemyMoveMul==='function'?v189EnemyMoveMul(e):1);
          ty=cdy/cd*e.sp*P.slowMul*(typeof v189EnemyMoveMul==='function'?v189EnemyMoveMul(e):1);
          move=true;
        }
      }else if(homeD>4){
        const s2=Math.min(e.sp*P.slowMul*(typeof v189EnemyMoveMul==='function'?v189EnemyMoveMul(e):1),homeD);
        tx=(e.hx-e.x)/homeD*s2;
        ty=(e.hy-e.y)/homeD*s2;
        move=true;
      }

      if(e.isFartGuard){
        e.fartMoving=!!move;
        if(move&&Math.abs(tx)>0.02)e.face=tx<0?-1:1;
      }

      if(e.type&&e.type.caveSpider){
        if(move){
          if(Math.abs(tx)>Math.abs(ty))e.spiderDir=tx<0?2:3;
          else e.spiderDir=ty<0?0:1;
          e.spiderMoving=true;
        }else{
          e.spiderMoving=false;
        }
      }

      if(e.animPack){
        if(move){
          if(Math.abs(tx)>Math.abs(ty))e.animDir=tx<0?2:3;
          else e.animDir=ty<0?0:1;
          e.animMoving=true;
        }else{
          e.animMoving=false;
        }
      }

      if(move){
        if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
          const bx=e.x,by=e.y;
          const mv=dgSlide(e.x,e.y,tx,ty,e.sz*0.3);

          if(e.room!==undefined){
            const roomPad=Math.max(10,e.sz*0.18);
            if(dgPointInRoom(e.room,mv.x,mv.y,roomPad)){
              e.x=mv.x;e.y=mv.y;
            }else{
              const cl=dgClampToRoom(e.room,mv.x,mv.y,roomPad);
              if(dgCircleWalk(cl.x,cl.y,e.sz*0.3)){
                e.x=cl.x;e.y=cl.y;
              }
            }
          }else{
            e.x=mv.x;e.y=mv.y;
          }

          // Collision can make the exact mathematical edge unreachable.
          // A short stall therefore counts as "reached room boundary".
          if(e.edgeChase){
            const moved=Math.hypot(e.x-bx,e.y-by);
        

===== cannonball #1 @78984605 =====
rtPos:{x:CLAN_SIEGE_RESPAWNS[3].x,y:CLAN_SIEGE_RESPAWNS[3].y},
  hasLeaveBtn:true
};

// === V211 CHARACTER XP CURVE ===============================================
// XP needed to go from the CURRENT level to the next one.
// Early levels are deliberately slower than before; from level 10 onward
// the requirement grows by 20% per level so progression stays meaningful
// without the old runaway x1.4 curve.
const XP_REQ_EARLY={1:200,2:300,3:450,4:650,5:900,6:1200,7:1600,8:2100,9:2700,10:3400};
function xpRequiredForLevel(level){
  const L=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(level)||1));
  if(L<=10)return XP_REQ_EARLY[L];
  return Math.max(3400,Math.round((3400*Math.pow(1.20,L-10))/100)*100);
}
function syncXpRequirement(){P.xn=xpRequiredForLevel(P.lvl);return P.xn;}

const EN=[],PR=[],PT=[],LOOT=[];
const PLAYER_CANNONBALLS=[]; // V126: dwarf shots; separate from hostile boss projectiles

function gnomeFireCannonball(){
  // Select ONCE at the moment of the shot. No target search is done in the
  // projectile update loop.
  let target=null;
  if(P.tid!=null){
    target=EN.find(function(e){return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0&&targetIsValid(e)})||null;
    if(target){
      const edge=(target.sz&&target.sz>30)?Math.max(0,(target.sz-30)*.4):0;
      if(Math.hypot(target.x-P.x,target.y-P.y)>playerBasicRange()+edge)target=null;
    }
  }
  if(!target)target=findNearBasic();
  P.tid=target?target.id:null;
  if(!target)return false;

  const dx=target.x-P.x,dy=target.y-P.y;
  const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const speed=7;

  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.shootT=1;
  P.recoil=0;

  const muzzleX=P.x+dx/dist*24;
  const muzzleY=P.y-7+dy/dist*10;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.push({
    x:muzzleX,
    y:muzzleY,
    vx:dx/dist*speed,
    vy:dy/dist*speed,
    remaining:dist,
    target:target
  });

  // Very small game-layer muzzle kick so firing while running still reads
  // clearly without adding effects into the sprite sheet.
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    PT.push({
      x:muzzleX,y:muzzleY,
      vx:dx/dist*(1.2+i*.45)+(Math.random()-.5)*.6,
      vy:dy/dist*(1.2+i*.45)+(Math.random()-.5)*.6,
      life:6,ml:6,sz:1.5+i*.45,
      col:i===0?'#ffd36a':'#c47a32'
    });
  }
  return true;
}
function updatePlayerCannonballs(){
  // Extremely small loop: move each ball toward the coordinates captured at
  // fire time. At arrival, use the game's already proven damage routine.
  for(let i=PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const b=PLAYER_CANNONBALLS[i];
    b.x+=b.vx;
    b.y+=b.vy;
    b.remaining-=Math.hypot(b.vx,b.vy);

    if(b.remaining<=0){
      const t=b.target;
      if(t&&t.hp>0){
        try{
          applyBasicAttackHit(t);
        }catch(_){
          const raw=Math.max(1,Math.floor(P.atk||12)-(t.def||0));
          t.hp=Math.max(0,t.hp-raw);
          t.flash=6;t.aggro=true;
        }
      }
      PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.splice(i,1);
    }
  }
}
function drawPlayerCannonballs(){
  const _vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),_vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  for(const b of PLAYER_CANNONBALLS){
    const x=b.x-cam.x,y=b.y-cam.y;
    if(x<-24||y<-24||x>_vw+24||y>_vh+24)continue;
    cx.save();

    // Small but clearly readable cannonball on a phone screen.
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(214,125,42,.30)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x,y,10,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.fillStyle='#24272b';
    cx.strokeStyle='#d68a3d';
    cx.lineWidth=2;
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x,y,7,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.stroke();

    cx.fillStyle='rgba(255,221,155,.75)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x-2,y-2,2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.restore();
  }
}

// === V141 ARCHER BASIC ARROW ==============================================
const PLAYER_ARROWS=[];

function archerFireArrow(){
  let target=null;

  // Prefer the target already locked by Smart Attack.
  if(P.tid!=null){
    target=EN.find(function(e){
      return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0&&targetIsValid(e);
    })||null;

    if(target){
      const range=playerBasicRange();
      if(P.scene==='clanboss1'&&target.isClanBoss){
        if(clanBossDistanceFromBody(target,P.x,P.y)>range)target=null;
      }else{
        const edge=(target.sz&&target.sz>30)?Math.max(0,(target.sz-30)*.4):0;
        if(Math.hypot(target.x-P.x,target.y-P.y)>range+edge)target=null;
      }
    }
  }

  if(!target)target=findNearBasic();
  P.tid=target?target.id:null;
  if(!target)return false;

  const dx=target.x-P.x,dy=target.y-P.y;
  const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const speed=12;

  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.shootT=1;
  P.recoil=0;

  // Start near the bow hand / chest rather than from the character's feet.
  const startX=P.x+Math.cos(ang)*20;
  const startY=P.y-18+Math.sin(ang)*8;

  PLAYER_ARROWS.push({
    x:startX,y:startY,
    vx:Math.cos(ang)*speed,
    vy:Math.sin(ang)*speed,
    remaining:dist,
    target:target,
    ang:ang
  });

  // Tiny bow release read; deliberately light, no glow spam.
  PT.push({
    x:startX,y:startY,
    vx:-Math.cos(ang)*.7,vy:-Math.sin(ang)*.7,
    life:5,ml:5,sz:1.5,col:'#d8c18b'
  });
  return true;
}

function updatePlayerArrows(){
  for(let i=PLAYER_ARROWS.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const a=PLAYER_ARROWS[i];
    a.x+=a.vx;
    a.y+=a.vy;
    a.remaining-=Math.hypot(a.vx,a.vy);

    if(a.remaining<=0){
      const t=a.target;
      if(t&&t.hp>0){
        try{
          applyBasicAttackHit(t);
        }catch(_){
          const raw=Math.max(1,Math.floor(P.atk||12)-(t.def||0));
          t.hp=Math.max(0,t.hp-raw);
          t.flash=6;t.aggro=true;
        }

        // Small pixel impact only.
        for(let j=0;j<3;j++){
          PT.push({
            x:t.x+(Math.random()-.5)*5,
            y:t.y-8+(Math.random()-.5)*5,
            vx:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,
            vy:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,
            life:7,ml:7,sz:1.3+Math.random(),
            col:j===0?'#e9e1c6':'#8a6b42'
          });
        }
      }
      PLAYER_ARROWS.splice(i,1);
    }
  }
}

function drawPlayerArrows(){
  const _vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),_vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  for(const a of PLAYER_ARROWS){
    const x=a.x-cam.x,y=a.y-cam.y;
    if(x<-28||y<-28||x>_vw+28||y>_vh+28)continue;

    cx.save();
    cx.translate(x,y);
    cx.rotate(a.ang);

    // Pixel-like wooden shaft.
    cx.fillStyle='#b98b55';
    cx.fillRect(-10,-1,16,2);

    // Pale metal arrow head.
    cx.fillStyle='#d9dde0';
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(10,0);
    cx.lineTo(5,-3);
    cx.lineTo(5,3);
    cx.closePath();
    cx.fill();

    // Small dark-green fletching matching the ranger palette.
    cx.fillStyle='#556b3b';
    cx.fillRect(-11,-3,4,2);
    cx.fillRect(-11,1,4,2);

    cx.restore();
  }
}

<!-- V138 SMART ATTACK APPROACH
ONE TAP:
1. Select nearest valid mob in the current visible/legal area.
2. If outside this class's normal attack range, hero runs toward that ONE mob.
3. Existing collision handles walls/map/boss bodies.
4. On entering attack range, hero stops and performs ONE normal attack.
5. No automatic selection of the next mob. Tap attack again for another command.

- Joystick can be used during Smart Attack; it does not cancel target lock.
- PvP does NOT use auto-chase; positioning remains manual.

- Phoenix is NOT reused in 21+; separate boss slot intentionally left open.

<!-- V120
CLEANUP:
- Dungeon 21+ real level requirement enabled (21+).

- chest appearance: common 95.8%, rare 4.0%, ancient 0.2% per chest;
- low-chance useful rewards only: PPA, normal/premium sharpening, premium HP regen,
  teleport scroll, rare rune, rare material, extra Titan shard;

<!-- V122 ACCOUNT / PREMIUM / AUCTION SYSTEM
FREE:
- all gameplay remains available;
- may buy on auction;
- cannot sell/direct-trade until activation.

ACTIVATED:
- verified lifetime paid Gram >= 1 OR any purchased Premium bundle;
- direct trade permanently unlocked;
- auction selling unlocked.

AUCTION:
- 1 lifetime paid Gram = 1 simultaneous lot;
- maximum 10 lots;
- spending Gram in auction/shops does NOT increase slots.

PREMIUM STORAGE:
- deposit requires active Premium;
- a purchased Premium bundle also grants Premium-storage access;
- if access later expires, existing items can always be withdrawn.

SERVER HOOKS:
- PPA_RECORD_PAID_GRAM(amount)
- PPA_SET_PAID_GRAM_TOTAL(total)
- PPA_SET_PREMIUM_STATUS(data)
-->

<!-- V125 GNOME APPROVED ALL STATES
- Based on V122 (the last clean gameplay/economy baseline).
- Gnome only: approved IDLE + approved RUN + approved ATTACK.
- 8 directions × 4 frames for every state.
- All source frames isolated from labels/grid/background.
- All three states normalized to identical 256×256 cells.
- Same feet-center anchor in every frame to prevent visual jumping.
- No sprite muzzle-flash/smoke effects; effects remain a separate game layer.
- No horizontal mirroring for gnome; all 8 directions are real frames.
- Other classes are untouched.
-->

<!-- V126 GNOME FIX
- Fixed horizontal directions for approved gnome art:
  E/W swapped correctly, NE/NW and SE/SW corrected.
- Gnome visual size is exactly 50% of the old V125 gnome draw scale.
- Gnome shadow/bob/dust scale down with the sprite.
- Gnome basic attack is now ranged:
  a physical cannonball leaves the cannon and flies toward the selected target.
- Cannonball damage is applied on impact, not instantly.
- Slight homing follows a moving selected target.
- Cannonballs stop on dungeon/clan-arena walls.
- Lightweight game-layer smoke/impact particles only; sprite art remains effect-free.
- Other classes still use the original melee system.
-->

<!-- V127 GNOME HOTFIX
- Right/left is no longer selected from conflicting generated rows.
  E/W, NE/NW and SE/SW now use one approved side + horizontal mirror.
- Compact 128px-cell atlases reduce Android decode/memory load.
- Gnome made visible again (~70–75% of assassin visually); no more tiny dot.
- Cannonball rewritten as a minimal safe projectile:
  no wall ray checks, no homing loop, no continuous smoke spam.
- Damage is applied only when the cannonball reaches the selected mob.
-->

<!-- V128 GNOME SHOT STABILITY HOTFIX
- Approved run/direction mirroring from V127 is unchanged.
- Gnome cannot disappear if the attack atlas is late to decode:
  approved idle is used as a temporary visual fallback.
- Cannonball no longer scans enemies every frame.
- Cannonball no longer raycasts walls and has no per-frame smoke.
- Target is selected once; projectile flies on a fixed path.
- On arrival damage uses the already-existing skillEnemyHit() routine.
- Fire/update/draw are guarded so a projectile error cannot stop the game loop.
-->

<!-- V129 GNOME + FRESH CLASS TEST
GNOME SHOT:
- visible 7px cannonball with a small bronze outline/glow;
- speed reduced from 13 to 7 so flight is visible on Android;
- starts farther from the character, near the cannon muzzle;
- drawn above the player/world sprites.

HISTORICAL NOTE:
- the temporary fresh-character comparison mode used during class balancing has been removed from this release build.
-->

<!-- V130 PAID CLASS CHANGE
- Premium Shop -> Premium-status section now contains "Смена класса" for 10 Gram.
- Allowed only in Мирном городе.
- Level, XP, rebirths, currencies, inventory and account progress are kept.
- Earned stat points are refunded for redistribution on the new class.
- Equipped + bag + personal/Premium-storage gear of the OLD class is converted
  to equivalent NEW-class gear while preserving rarity, enhancement, rolled
  stats, item level, BM and UID.
- Clan storage and auction lots are never converted.
- Converted gear becomes account-bound and is excluded from auction/direct trade
  to prevent market arbitrage through repeated class changes.
- This purchase spends Gram but does NOT count as a verified lifetime paid top-up.
- The old temporary startup reset is removed; paid class change uses persistent progression.
-->

<!-- V131 FULL CLASS BALANCE L1-L40
- One authoritative CLASS_BALANCE table for all 8 classes.
- Every class has clean level-1 and clean level-40:
  HP, MP, ATK, DEF, movement speed, attack speed, basic range,
  crit chance, dodge and crit damage.
- Stats interpolate every level from 1 to 40; lvl40+ uses lvl40 clean baseline.
- Gear and allocated stat points are applied ON TOP of clean class stats.
- Normal attack no longer has the old hidden +10 damage.
- V135 supersedes the old fixed-hit-count idea. Player classes are role-balanced,
  not tuned to kill a particular mob in a particular number of hits.
- Gnome cannonball now uses NORMAL attack damage, not skill damage.
- Basic target acquisition obeys the current class's attack range.
- Attack cooldown obeys the current class's attack speed.
- Movement now actually follows the recomputed class movement speed.
- Old universal +20 HP / +10 MP per level removed.
- Paid class change and fresh sprite/class test use the same central balance table.
-->

<!-- V132 RUN AND ATTACK
- Joystick movement and basic attack now work at the same time.
- While moving, RUN animation stays active and combat is executed independently.
- Standing still still uses the normal ATTACK sprite sheet.
- Starting to run during a standing attack immediately returns to RUN animation.
- Melee recoil no longer fights the joystick while moving.
- Gnome can fire cannonballs while running; projectile/muzzle effect is separate
  from the run sprite, so legs keep animating continuously.
- Attack speed/cooldown still follows each class's V131 balance.
-->

<!-- V133 SOFT EARLY-GAME BALANCE
- V135 does not use hit-count targets. The existing mob table remains as-is.
- Mob damage and move speed reduced in the first 10 levels.
- Dungeon aggro radius ramps from 90 (lvl1-2) to 170 (lvl7-10);
  lvl11+ keeps the old 200 radius.
- Low-level mobs attack more slowly; attack cadence gradually reaches the old rate.
- V132 run-and-attack remains unchanged.
- V131 full class stat curves remain unchanged.
-->

<!-- V134 SIMPLE 7-LEVEL ANTIFARM
Ordinary mob reward multiplier by playerLevel - mobLevel:
0..7   = 100%
8..10  = 70%
11..14 = 40%
15..19 = 20%
20+    = 5%

Applied to:
- XP
- Gold
- ordinary-mob PPA chance
- ordinary gear chance
- materials
- normal sharpening stones
- grimoires
- ordinary Phoenix-feather chance

NOT applied to bosses/world bosses/clan bosses.
No BM, Premium or donor-power calculations are involved.
-->

<!-- V135 ROLE-BASED PLAYER BALANCE ONLY
MOBS ARE UNCHANGED.

Class identity:
- Гном: very high damage per shot, 0.70 attack/sec at lvl1; slow heavy cannon.
- Ассасин: lower damage per hit, fastest melee attack speed + high crit.
- Лучник: medium hit damage, high attack speed, longest standard range.
- Маг: strong hit, slower attacks, lowest HP.
- Страж: lowest DPS group, very high HP/DEF.
- Берсерк: high sustained melee DPS.
- Паладин: middle ground between damage and defence.
- Жрец: lowest pure DPS group; future value is healing/support.

Only CLASS_BALANCE and matching class-selection lvl1 display were changed.
V134 anti-farm remains.
MOB_TABLE and DUNGEON21_ARCHETYPES are byte-for-byte unchanged.
-->

<!-- V136 RUN FACE SHOT DIRECTION
- Run-and-attack movement is unchanged: joystick still controls where the hero moves.
- During the actual shot/attack moment the sprite turns toward the selected target.
- After a short aim window the running sprite returns to the movement direction.
- Works for gnome 8-direction sprites and legacy directional classes.
- No mob stats, class balance, drops or anti-farm values changed.
-->

<!-- V137 COMBAT FEEL RECOVERY
PLAYER COMBAT ONLY:
- Removed the 1→40 interpolated class-stat experiment.
- Restored simple class bases close to the older game feel.
- Restored +20 HP / +10 MP per level and generic +1.5 ATK per level.
- Restored +10 basic-hit base damage for punchier normal attacks.
- Gnome: ATK 30, 1.00 shot/sec, 360 basic range.
- Assassin: ATK 15, 1.55 attack/sec, 20% base crit.
- Archer remains fast ranged, Mage heavy/slower, Tank durable, etc.
- Paid class change and fresh class testing use the same simple class table.

ASSASSIN / MELEE ANIMATION:
- Movement is NOT cancelled by attacking.
- Melee classes play their real ATTACK sprite while moving.
- Assassin no longer attacks with the running/belly pose.
- Ranged gnome still runs while its separate cannonball projectile fires.

UNCHANGED:
- MOB_TABLE
- Dungeon 21+ mob archetypes
- V134 7-level anti-farm
- Gnome approved run directions / mirroring
- Cannonball visuals
- Run-and-attack controls
- Face-target-while-attacking behavior
-->

<!-- V138 SMART ATTACK APPROACH
ONE TAP:
1. Select nearest valid mob in the current visible/legal area.
2. If outside this class's normal attack range, hero runs toward that ONE mob.
3. Existing collision handles walls/map/boss bodies.
4. On entering attack range, hero stops and performs ONE normal attack.
5. No automatic selection of the next mob. Tap attack again for another command.

CONTROL:
- Joystick can be used during Smart Attack; it does not cancel target lock.
- PvP does NOT use auto-chase; positioning remains manual.
- Ranged classes stop at their own range; melee classes run close.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm and class combat values are unchanged from V137.
-->

<!-- V139 SMART ATTACK + JOYSTICK
- Smart Attack and joystick now work together.
- Press attack once -> nearest valid target stays locked.
- If joystick is neutral, character auto-runs into attack range.
- If joystick is being used, the player controls movement manually while the
  target remains locked and the character keeps aiming at that target.
- When the target enters attack range, one attack is executed when cooldown is ready.
- Releasing joystick resumes auto-approach toward the same target.
- No automatic next-target chaining.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm and class stats are unchanged from V138/V137.
-->

<!-- V140 SMART ATTACK JOYSTICK HOTFIX
- Fixed joystick snapping back to center when Smart Attack starts.
- startSmartAttack() no longer calls resetJoystick().
- Holding the joystick while pressing Attack preserves jX/jY and pointer ownership.
- Smart Attack only auto-moves when joystick is actually neutral.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm, class stats and combat balance are unchanged.
-->

 from 63x142 source aspect to a 96x96 inventory icon.
- Resource images are hard-clipped to one bag slot.
-->

<!-- V122 ACCOUNT / PREMIUM / AUCTION SYSTEM
FREE:
- all gameplay remains available;
- may buy on auction;
- cannot sell/direct-trade until activation.

ACTIVATED:
- verified lifetime paid Gram >= 1 OR any purchased Premium bundle;
- direct trade permanently unlocked;
- auction selling unlocked.

AUCTION:
- 1 lifetime paid Gram = 1 simultaneous lot;
- maximum 10 lots;
- spending Gram in auction/shops does NOT increase slots.

PREMIUM STORAGE:
- deposit requires active Premium;
- a purchased Premium bundle also grants Premium-storage access;
- if access later expires, existing items can always be withdrawn.

SERVER HOOKS:
- PPA_RECORD_PAID_GRAM(amount)
- PPA_SET_PAID_GRAM_TOTAL(total)
- PPA_SET_PREMIUM_STATUS(data)
-->

<!-- V125 GNOME APPROVED ALL STATES
- Based on V122 (the last clean gameplay/economy baseline).
- Gnome only: approved IDLE + approved RUN + approved ATTACK.
- 8 directions × 4 frames for every state.
- All source frames isolated from labels/grid/background.
- All three states normalized to identical 256×256 cells.
- Same feet-center anchor in every frame to prevent visual jumping.
- No sprite muzzle-flash/smoke effects; effects remain a separate game layer.
- No horizontal mirroring for gnome; all 8 directions are real frames.
- Other classes are untouched.
-->

<!-- V126 GNOME FIX
- Fixed horizontal directions for approved gnome art:
  E/W swapped correctly, NE/NW and SE/SW corrected.
- Gnome visual size is exactly 50% of the old V125 gnome draw scale.
- Gnome shadow/bob/dust scale down with the sprite.
- Gnome basic attack is now ranged:
  a physical cannonball leaves the cannon and flies toward the selected target.
- Cannonball damage is applied on impact, not instantly.
- Slight homing follows a moving selected target.
- Cannonballs stop on dungeon/clan-arena walls.
- Lightweight game-layer smoke/impact particles only; sprite art remains effect-free.
- Other classes still use the original melee system.
-->

<!-- V127 GNOME HOTFIX
- Right/left is no longer selected from conflicting generated rows.
  E/W, NE/NW and SE/SW now use one approved side + horizontal mirror.
- Compact 128px-cell atlases reduce Android decode/memory load.
- Gnome made visible again (~70–75% of assassin visually); no more tiny dot.
- Cannonball rewritten as a minimal safe projectile:
  no wall ray checks, no homing loop, no continuous smoke spam.
- Damage is applied only when the cannonball reaches the selected mob.
-->

<!-- V128 GNOME SHOT STABILITY HOTFIX
- Approved run/direction mirroring from V127 is unchanged.
- Gnome cannot disappear if the attack atlas is late to decode:
  approved idle is used as a temporary visual fallback.
- Cannonball no longer scans enemies every frame.
- Cannonball no longer raycasts walls and has no per-frame smoke.
- Target is selected once; projectile flies on a fixed path.
- On arrival damage uses the already-existing skillEnemyHit() routine.
- Fire/update/draw are guarded so a projectile error cannot stop the game loop.
-->

<!-- V129 GNOME + FRESH CLASS TEST
GNOME SHOT:
- visible 7px cannonball with a small bronze outline/glow;
- speed reduced from 13 to 7 so flight is visible on Android;
- starts farther from the character, near the cannon muzzle;
- drawn above the player/world sprites.

HISTORICAL NOTE:
- the temporary fresh-character comparison mode used during class balancing has been removed from this release build.
-->

<!-- V130 PAID CLASS CHANGE
- Premium Shop -> Premium-status section now contains "Смена класса" for 10 Gram.
- Allowed only in Мирном городе.
- Level, XP, rebirths, currencies, inventory and account progress are kept.
- Earned stat points are refunded for redistribution on the new class.
- Equipped + bag + personal/Premium-storage gear of the OLD class is converted
  to equivalent NEW-class gear while preserving rarity, enhancement, rolled
  stats, item level, BM and UID.
- Clan storage and auction lots are never converted.
- Converted gear becomes account-bound and is excluded from auction/direct trade
  to prevent market arbitrage through repeated class changes.
- This purchase spends Gram but does NOT count as a verified lifetime paid top-up.
- The old temporary startup reset is removed; paid class change uses persistent progression.
-->

<!-- V131 FULL CLASS BALANCE L1-L40
- One authoritative CLASS_BALANCE table for all 8 classes.
- Every class has clean level-1 and clean level-40:
  HP, MP, ATK, DEF, movement speed, attack speed, basic range,
  crit chance, dodge and crit damage.
- Stats interpolate every level from 1 to 40; lvl40+ uses lvl40 clean baseline.
- Gear and allocated stat points are applied ON TOP of clean class stats.
- Normal attack no longer has the old hidden +10 damage.
- V135 supersedes the old fixed-hit-count idea. Player classes are role-balanced,
  not tuned to kill a particular mob in a particular number of hits.
- Gnome cannonball now uses NORMAL attack damage, not skill damage.
- Basic target acquisition obeys the current class's attack range.
- Attack cooldown obeys the current class's attack speed.
- Movement now actually follows the recomputed class movement speed.
- Old universal +20 HP / +10 MP per level removed.
- Paid class change and fresh sprite/class test use the same central balance table.
-->

<!-- V132 RUN AND ATTACK
- Joystick movement and basic attack now work at the same time.
- While moving, RUN animation stays active and combat is executed independently.
- Standing still still uses the normal ATTACK sprite sheet.
- Starting to run during a standing attack immediately returns to RUN animation.
- Melee recoil no longer fights the joystick while moving.
- Gnome can fire cannonballs while running; projectile/muzzle effect is separate
  from the run sprite, so legs keep animating continuously.
- Attack speed/cooldown still follows each class's V131 balance.
-->

<!-- V133 SOFT EARLY-GAME BALANCE
- V135 does not use hit-count targets. The existing mob table remains as-is.
- Mob damage and move speed reduced in the first 10 levels.
- Dungeon aggro radius ramps from 90 (lvl1-2) to 170 (lvl7-10);
  lvl11+ keeps the old 200 radius.
- Low-level mobs attack more slowly; attack cadence gradually reaches the old rate.
- V132 run-and-attack remains unchanged.
- V131 full class stat curves remain unchanged.
-->

<!-- V134 SIMPLE 7-LEVEL ANTIFARM
Ordinary mob reward multiplier by playerLevel - mobLevel:
0..7   = 100%
8..10  = 70%
11..14 = 40%
15..19 = 20%
20+    = 5%

Applied to:
- XP
- Gold
- ordinary-mob PPA chance
- ordinary gear chance
- materials
- normal sharpening stones
- grimoires
- ordinary Phoenix-feather chance

NOT applied to bosses/world bosses/clan bosses.
No BM, Premium or donor-power calculations are involved.
-->

<!-- V135 ROLE-BASED PLAYER BALANCE ONLY
MOBS ARE UNCHANGED.

Class identity:
- Гном: very high damage per shot, 0.70 attack/sec at lvl1; slow heavy cannon.
- Ассасин: lower damage per hit, fastest melee attack speed + high crit.
- Лучник: medium hit damage, high attack speed, longest standard range.
- Маг: strong hit, slower attacks, lowest HP.
- Страж: lowest DPS group, very high HP/DEF.
- Берсерк: high sustained melee DPS.
- Паладин: middle ground between damage and defence.
- Жрец: lowest pure DPS group; future value is healing/support.

Only CLASS_BALANCE and matching class-selection lvl1 display were changed.
V134 anti-farm remains.
MOB_TABLE and DUNGEON21_ARCHETYPES are byte-for-byte unchanged.
-->

<!-- V136 RUN FACE SHOT DIRECTION
- Run-and-attack movement is unchanged: joystick still controls where the hero moves.
- During the actual shot/attack moment the sprite turns toward the selected target.
- After a short aim window the running sprite returns to the movement direction.
- Works for gnome 8-direction sprites and legacy directional classes.
- No mob stats, class balance, drops or anti-farm values changed.
-->

<!-- V137 COMBAT FEEL RECOVERY
PLAYER COMBAT ONLY:
- Removed the 1→40 interpolated class-stat experiment.
- Restored simple class bases close to the older game feel.
- Restored +20 HP / +10 MP per level and generic +1.5 ATK per level.
- Restored +10 basic-hit base damage for punchier normal attacks.
- Gnome: ATK 30, 1.00 shot/sec, 360 basic range.
- Assassin: ATK 15, 1.55 attack/sec, 20% base crit.
- Archer remains fast ranged, Mage heavy/slower, Tank durable, etc.
- Paid class change and fresh class testing use the same simple class table.

ASSASSIN / MELEE ANIMATION:
- Movement is NOT cancelled by attacking.
- Melee classes play their real ATTACK sprite while moving.
- Assassin no longer attacks with the running/belly pose.
- Ranged gnome still runs while its separate cannonball projectile fires.

UNCHANGED:
- MOB_TABLE
- Dungeon 21+ mob archetypes
- V134 7-level anti-farm
- Gnome approved run directions / mirroring
- Cannonball visuals
- Run-and-attack controls
- Face-target-while-attacking behavior
-->

<!-- V138 SMART ATTACK APPROACH
ONE TAP:
1. Select nearest valid mob in the current visible/legal area.
2. If outside this class's normal attack range, hero runs toward that ONE mob.
3. Existing collision handles walls/map/boss bodies.
4. On entering attack range, hero stops and performs ONE normal attack.
5. No automatic selection of the next mob. Tap attack again for another command.

CONTROL:
- Joystick can be used during Smart Attack; it does not cancel target lock.
- PvP does NOT use auto-chase; positioning remains manual.
- Ranged classes stop at their own range; melee classes run close.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm and class combat values are unchanged from V137.
-->

<!-- V139 SMART ATTACK + JOYSTICK
- Smart Attack and joystick now work together.
- Press attack once -> nearest valid target stays locked.
- If joystick is neutral, character auto-runs into attack range.
- If joystick is being used, the player controls movement manually while the
  target remains locked and the character keeps aiming at that target.
- When the target enters attack range, one attack is executed when cooldown is ready.
- Releasing joystick resumes auto-approach toward the same target.
- No automatic next-target chaining.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm and class stats are unchanged from V138/V137.
-->

<!-- V140 SMART ATTACK JOYSTICK HOTFIX
- Fixed joystick snapping back to center when Smart Attack starts.
- startSmartAttack() no longer calls resetJoystick().
- Holding the joystick while pressing Attack preserves jX/jY and pointer ownership.
- Smart Attack only auto-moves when joystick is actually neutral.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm, class stats and combat balance are unchanged.
-->

<!-- V141 ARCHER INTEGRATION
- Added the approved green-hood Archer as a real player sprite.
- Idle / Run / Attack: 8 directions × 4 frames.
- W direction is an exact mirror baked into the sprite atlas; no AI redraw.
- Archer basic attack now fires a lightweight visible arrow projectile.
- Arrow uses the SAME existing basic-attack damage/crit/DEF calculation.
- Archer keeps Smart Attack + joystick behavior from V140.
- Moving Archer keeps run animation while aiming/shooting toward the locked target.
- Standing Archer uses the real attack animation.
- Melee slash visual is now limited to melee classes only.
UNCHANGED:
- Smart Attack V140 joystick behavior
- MOB_TABLE
- Dungeon 21+ mob archetypes
- 7-level anti-farm
- CLASS_BASE stats / Archer balance values
- Gnome approved sprites, mirroring and cannonball

<!-- V131 FULL CLASS BALANCE L1-L40
- One authoritative CLASS_BALANCE table for all 8 classes.
- Every class has clean level-1 and clean level-40:
  HP, MP, ATK, DEF, movement speed, attack speed, basic range,
  crit chance, dodge and crit damage.
- Stats interpolate every level from 1 to 40; lvl40+ uses lvl40 clean baseline.
- Gear and allocated stat points are applied ON TOP of clean class stats.
- Normal attack no longer has the old hidden +10 damage.
- V135 supersedes the old fixed-hit-count idea. Player classes are role-balanced,
  not tuned to kill a particular mob in a particular number of hits.
- Gnome cannonball now uses NORMAL attack damage, not skill damage.
- Basic target acquisition obeys the current class's attack range.
- Attack cooldown obeys the current class's attack speed.
- Movement now actually follows the recomputed class movement speed.
- Old universal +20 HP / +10 MP per level removed.
- Paid class change and fresh sprite/class test use the same central balance table.
-->

<!-- V132 RUN AND ATTACK
- Joystick movement and basic attack now work at the same time.
- While moving, RUN animation stays active and combat is executed independently.
- Standing still still uses the normal ATTACK sprite sheet.
- Starting to run during a standing attack immediately returns to RUN animation.
- Melee recoil no longer fights the joystick while moving.
- Gnome can fire cannonballs while running; projectile/muzzle effect is separate
  from the run sprite, so legs keep animating continuously.

<!-- V133 SOFT EARLY-GAME BALANCE

<!-- V134 SIMPLE 7-LEVEL ANTIFARM

<!-- V138 SMART ATTACK APPROACH
ONE TAP:
1. Select nearest valid mob in the current visible/legal area.
2. If outside this class's normal attack range, hero runs toward that ONE mob.
3. Existing collision handles walls/map/boss bodies.
4. On entering attack range, hero stops and performs ONE normal attack.
5. No automatic selection of the next mob. Tap attack again for another command.

CONTROL:
- Joystick can be used during Smart Attack; it does not cancel target lock.
- PvP does NOT use auto-chase; positioning remains manual.
- Ranged classes stop at their own range; melee classes run close.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm and class combat values are unchanged from V137.
-->

<!-- V139 SMART ATTACK + JOYSTICK
- Smart Attack and joystick now work together.
- Press attack once -> nearest valid target stays locked.
- If joystick is neutral, character auto-runs into attack range.
- If joystick is being used, the player controls movement manually while the
  target remains locked and the character keeps aiming at that target.
- When the target enters attack range, one attack is executed when cooldown is ready.
- Releasing joystick resumes auto-approach toward the same target.
- No automatic next-target chaining.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm and class stats are unchanged from V138/V137.
-->

- This changes DRAWING SIZE ONLY. Stats, hit logic, speed, attack range,
  Smart Attack, mobs, drops and balance are unchanged.
-->

// === V211 CHARACTER XP CURVE ===============================================
// XP needed to go from the CURRENT level to the next one.
// Early levels are deliberately slower than before; from level 10 onward
// the requirement grows by 20% per level so progression stays meaningful
// without the old runaway x1.4 curve.
const XP_REQ_EARLY={1:200,2:300,3:450,4:650,5:900,6:1200,7:1600,8:2100,9:2700,10:3400};
function xpRequiredForLevel(level){
  const L=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(level)||1));
  if(L<=10)return XP_REQ_EARLY[L];
  return Math.max(3400,Math.round((3400*Math.pow(1.20,L-10))/100)*100);
}
function syncXpRequirement(){P.xn=xpRequiredForLevel(P.lvl);return P.xn;}

let transitioning=false;

const EN=[],PR=[],PT=[],LOOT=[];
const PLAYER_CANNONBALLS=[]; // V126: dwarf shots; separate from hostile boss projectiles

function gnomeFireCannonball(){
  // Select ONCE at the moment of the shot. No target search is done in the
  // projectile update loop.
  let target=null;
  if(P.tid!=null){
    target=EN.find(function(e){return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0&&targetIsValid(e)})||null;
    if(target){
      const edge=(target.sz&&target.sz>30)?Math.max(0,(target.sz-30)*.4):0;
      if(Math.hypot(target.x-P.x,target.y-P.y)>playerBasicRange()+edge)target=null;
    }
  }
  if(!target)target=findNearBasic();
  P.tid=target?target.id:null;
  if(!target)return false;

  const dx=target.x-P.x,dy=target.y-P.y;
  const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const speed=7;

  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.shootT=1;
  P.recoil=0;

  const muzzleX=P.x+dx/dist*24;
  const muzzleY=P.y-7+dy/dist*10;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.push({
    x:muzzleX,
    y:muzzleY,
    vx:dx/dist*speed,
    vy:dy/dist*speed,
    remaining:dist,
    target:target
  });

  // Very small game-layer muzzle kick so firing while running still reads
  // clearly without adding effects into the sprite sheet.
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    PT.push({
      x:muzzleX,y:muzzleY,
      vx:dx/dist*(1.2+i*.45)+(Math.random()-.5)*.6,
      vy:dy/dist*(1.2+i*.45)+(Math.random()-.5)*.6,
      life:6,ml:6,sz:1.5+i*.45,
      col:i===0?'#ffd36a':'#c47a32'
    });
  }
  return true;
}
function updatePlayerCannonballs(){
  // Extremely small loop: move each ball toward the coordinates captured at
  // fire time. At arrival, use the game's already proven damage routine.
  for(let i=PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const b=PLAYER_CANNONBALLS[i];
    b.x+=b.vx;
    b.y+=b.vy;
    b.remaining-=Math.hypot(b.vx,b.vy);

    if(b.remaining<=0){
      const t=b.target;
      if(t&&t.hp>0){
        try{
          applyBasicAttackHit(t);
        }catch(_){
          const raw=Math.max(1,Math.floor(P.atk||12)-(t.def||0));
          t.hp=Math.max(0,t.hp-raw);
          t.flash=6;t.aggro=true;
        }
      }
      PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.splice(i,1);
    }
  }
}
function drawPlayerCannonballs(){
  const _vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),_vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  for(const b of PLAYER_CANNONBALLS){
    const x=b.x-cam.x,y=b.y-cam.y;
    if(x<-24||y<-24||x>_vw+24||y>_vh+24)continue;
    cx.save();

    // Small but clearly readable cannonball on a phone screen.
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(214,125,42,.30)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x,y,10,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.fillStyle='#24272b';
    cx.strokeStyle='#d68a3d';
    cx.lineWidth=2;
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x,y,7,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.stroke();

    cx.fillStyle='rgba(255,221,155,.75)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x-2,y-2,2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.restore();
  }
}

// === V141 ARCHER BASIC ARROW ==============================================
const PLAYER_ARROWS=[];

function archerFireArrow(){
  let target=null;

  // Prefer the target already locked by Smart Attack.
  if(P.tid!=null){
    target=EN.find(function(e){
      return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0&&targetIsValid(e);
    })||null;

    if(target){
      const range=playerBasicRange();
      if(P.scene==='clanboss1'&&target.isClanBoss){
        if(clanBossDistanceFromBody(target,P.x,P.y)>range)target=null;
      }else{
        const edge=(target.sz&&target.sz>30)?Math.max(0,(target.sz-30)*.4):0;
        if(Math.hypot(target.x-P.x,target.y-P.y)>range+edge)target=null;
      }
    }
  }

  if(!target)target=findNearBasic();
  P.tid=target?target.id:null;
  if(!target)return false;

  const dx=target.x-P.x,dy=target.y-P.y;
  const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const speed=12;

  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.shootT=1;
  P.recoil=0;

  // Start near the bow hand / chest rather than from the character's feet.
  const startX=P.x+Math.cos(ang)*20;
  const startY=P.y-18+Math.sin(ang)*8;

  PLAYER_ARROWS.push({
    x:startX,y:startY,
    vx:Math.cos(ang)*speed,
    vy:Math.sin(ang)*speed,
    remaining:dist,
    target:target,
    ang:ang
  });

  // Tiny bow release read; deliberately light, no glow spam.
  PT.push({
    x:startX,y:startY,
    vx:-Math.cos(ang)*.7,vy:-Math.sin(ang)*.7,
    life:5,ml:5,sz:1.5,col:'#d8c18b'
  });
  return true;
}

function updatePlayerArrows(){
  for(let i=PLAYER_ARROWS.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const a=PLAYER_ARROWS[i];
    a.x+=a.vx;
    a.y+=a.vy;
    a.remaining-=Math.hypot(a.vx,a.vy);

    if(a.remaining<=0){
      const t=a.target;
      if(t&&t.hp>0){
        try{
          applyBasicAttackHit(t);
        }catch(_){
          const raw=Math.max(1,Math.floor(P.atk||12)-(t.def||0));
          t.hp=Math.max(0,t.hp-raw);
          t.flash=6;t.aggro=true;
        }

        // Small pixel impact only.
        for(let j=0;j<3;j++){
          PT.push({
            x:t.x+(Math.random()-.5)*5,
            y:t.y-8+(Math.random()-.5)*5,
            vx:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,
            vy:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,
            life:7,ml:7,sz:1.3+Math.random(),
            col:j===0?'#e9e1c6':'#8a6b42'
          });
        }
      }
      PLAYER_ARROWS.splice(i,1);
    }
  }
}

function drawPlayerArrows(){
  const _vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),_vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  for(const a of PLAYER_ARROWS){
    const x=a.x-cam.x,y=a.y-cam.y;
    if(x<-28||y<-28||x>_vw+28||y>_vh+28)continue;

    cx.save();
    cx.translate(x,y);
    cx.rotate(a.ang);

    // Pixel-like wooden shaft.
    cx.fillStyle='#b98b55';
    cx.fillRect(-10,-1,16,2);

    // Pale metal arrow head.
    cx.fillStyle='#d9dde0';
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(10,0);
    cx.lineTo(5,-3);
    cx.lineTo(5,3);
    cx.closePath();
    cx.fill();

    // Small dark-green fletching matching the ranger palette.
    cx.fillStyle='#556b3b';
    cx.fillRect(-11,-3,4,2);
    cx.fillRect(-11,1,4,2);

    cx.restore();
  }
}

function gnomeFireCannonball(){
  // Select ONCE at the moment of the shot. No target search is done in the
  // projectile update loop.
  let target=null;
  if(P.tid!=null){
    target=EN.find(function(e){return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0&&targetIsValid(e)})||null;
    if(target){
      const edge=(target.sz&&target.sz>30)?Math.max(0,(target.sz-30)*.4):0;
      if(Math.hypot(target.x-P.x,target.y-P.y)>playerBasicRange()+edge)target=null;
    }
  }
  if(!target)target=findNearBasic();
  P.tid=target?target.id:null;
  if(!target)return false;

  const dx=target.x-P.x,dy=target.y-P.y;
  const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const speed=7;

  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.shootT=1;
  P.recoil=0;

  const muzzleX=P.x+dx/dist*24;
  const muzzleY=P.y-7+dy/dist*10;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.push({
    x:muzzleX,
    y:muzzleY,
    vx:dx/dist*speed,
    vy:dy/dist*speed,
    remaining:dist,
    target:target
  });

  // Very small game-layer muzzle kick so firing while running still reads
  // clearly without adding effects into the sprite sheet.
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    PT.push({
      x:muzzleX,y:muzzleY,
      vx:dx/dist*(1.2+i*.45)+(Math.random()-.5)*.6,
      vy:dy/dist*(1.2+i*.45)+(Math.random()-.5)*.6,
      life:6,ml:6,sz:1.5+i*.45,
      col:i===0?'#ffd36a':'#c47a32'
    });
  }
  return true;
}
function updatePlayerCannonballs(){
  // Extremely small loop: move each ball toward the coordinates captured at
  // fire time. At arrival, use the game's already proven damage routine.
  for(let i=PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const b=PLAYER_CANNONBALLS[i];
    b.x+=b.vx;
    b.y+=b.vy;
    b.remaining-=Math.hypot(b.vx,b.vy);

    if(b.remaining<=0){
      const t=b.target;
      if(t&&t.hp>0){
        try{
          applyBasicAttackHit(t);
        }catch(_){
          const raw=Math.max(1,Math.floor(P.atk||12)-(t.def||0));
          t.hp=Math.max(0,t.hp-raw);
          t.flash=6;t.aggro=true;
        }
      }
      PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.splice(i,1);
    }
  }
}
function drawPlayerCannonballs(){
  const _vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),_vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  for(const b of PLAYER_CANNONBALLS){
    const x=b.x-cam.x,y=b.y-cam.y;
    if(x<-24||y<-24||x>_vw+24||y>_vh+24)continue;
    cx.save();

    // Small but clearly readable cannonball on a phone screen.
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(214,125,42,.30)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x,y,10,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.fillStyle='#24272b';
    cx.strokeStyle='#d68a3d';
    cx.lineWidth=2;
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x,y,7,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.stroke();

    cx.fillStyle='rgba(255,221,155,.75)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x-2,y-2,2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.restore();
  }
}

// === V141 ARCHER BASIC ARROW ==============================================
const PLAYER_ARROWS=[];

function archerFireArrow(){
  let target=null;

  // Prefer the target already locked by Smart Attack.
  if(P.tid!=null){
    target=EN.find(function(e){
      return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0&&targetIsValid(e);
    })||null;

    if(target){
      const range=playerBasicRange();
      if(P.scene==='clanboss1'&&target.isClanBoss){
        if(clanBossDistanceFromBody(target,P.x,P.y)>range)target=null;
      }else{
        const edge=(target.sz&&target.sz>30)?Math.max(0,(target.sz-30)*.4):0;
        if(Math.hypot(target.x-P.x,target.y-P.y)>range+edge)target=null;
      }
    }
  }

  if(!target)target=findNearBasic();
  P.tid=target?target.id:null;
  if(!target)return false;

  const dx=target.x-P.x,dy=target.y-P.y;
  const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const speed=12;

  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.shootT=1;
  P.recoil=0;

  // Start near the bow hand / chest rather than from the character's feet.
  const startX=P.x+Math.cos(ang)*20;
  const startY=P.y-18+Math.sin(ang)*8;

  PLAYER_ARROWS.push({
    x:startX,y:startY,
    vx:Math.cos(ang)*speed,
    vy:Math.sin(ang)*speed,
    remaining:dist,
    target:target,
    ang:ang
  });

  // Tiny bow release read; deliberately light, no glow spam.
  PT.push({
    x:startX,y:startY,
    vx:-Math.cos(ang)*.7,vy:-Math.sin(ang)*.7,
    life:5,ml:5,sz:1.5,col:'#d8c18b'
  });
  return true;
}

function updatePlayerArrows(){
  for(let i=PLAYER_ARROWS.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const a=PLAYER_ARROWS[i];
    a.x+=a.vx;
    a.y+=a.vy;
    a.remaining-=Math.hypot(a.vx,a.vy);

    if(a.remaining<=0){
      const t=a.target;
      if(t&&t.hp>0){
        try{
          applyBasicAttackHit(t);
        }catch(_){
          const raw=Math.max(1,Math.floor(P.atk||12)-(t.def||0));
          t.hp=Math.max(0,t.hp-raw);
          t.flash=6;t.aggro=true;
        }

        // Small pixel impact only.
        for(let j=0;j<3;j++){
          PT.push({
            x:t.x+(Math.random()-.5)*5,
            y:t.y-8+(Math.random()-.5)*5,
            vx:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,
            vy:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,
            life:7,ml:7,sz:1.3+Math.random(),
            col:j===0?'#e9e1c6':'#8a6b42'
          });
        }
      }
      PLAYER_ARROWS.splice(i,1);
    }
  }
}

function drawPlayerArrows(){
  const _vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),_vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  for(const a of PLAYER_ARROWS){
    const x=a.x-cam.x,y=a.y-cam.y;
    if(x<-28||y<-28||x>_vw+28||y>_vh+28)continue;

    cx.save();
    cx.translate(x,y);
    cx.rotate(a.ang);

    // Pixel-like wooden shaft.
    cx.fillStyle='#b98b55';
    cx.fillRect(-10,-1,16,2);

    // Pale metal arrow head.
    cx.fillStyle='#d9dde0';
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(10,0);
    cx.lineTo(5,-3);
    cx.lineTo(5,3);
    cx.closePath();
    cx.fill();

    // Small dark-green fletching matching the ranger palette.
    cx.fillStyle='#556b3b';
    cx.fillRect(-11,-3,4,2);
    cx.fillRect(-11,1,4,2);

    cx.restore();
  }
}

// === V211 CHARACTER XP CURVE ===============================================
// XP needed to go from the CURRENT level to the next one.
// Early levels are deliberately slower than before; from level 10 onward
// the requirement grows by 20% per level so progression stays meaningful
// without the old runaway x1.4 curve.
const XP_REQ_EARLY={1:200,2:300,3:450,4:650,5:900,6:1200,7:1600,8:2100,9:2700,10:3400};
function xpRequiredForLevel(level){
  const L=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(level)||1));
  if(L<=10)return XP_REQ_EARLY[L];
  return Math.max(3400,Math.round((3400*Math.pow(1.20,L-10))/100)*100);
}
function syncXpRequirement(){P.xn=xpRequiredForLevel(P.lvl);return P.xn;}

function gnomeFireCannonball(){

  // projectile update loop.

    }

  P.shootT=1;

    x:muzzleX,

let __ppaHudNextAt=0;
function update(){
  PPAOnlineTick();
  ppaCleanupDeathRemnants();
  updateConsumableHud(false);
  updatePremiumRegen();
  if(P.dead)return;
  v174AiTickPlayerDebuffs();

  const _smartMove=updateSmartAttackInput();
  // V139: Smart Attack only supplies movement when joystick is neutral.
  // Any live joystick input keeps full manual movement while target lock stays active.
  const _moveX=_smartMove?_smartMove.x:jX;
  const _moveY=_smartMove?_smartMove.y:jY;

  // V174: class-AI crowd control is numeric only (no particle FX).
  const _aiMoveMul=Date.now()<(P.aiRootUntil||0)?0:(Date.now()<(P.aiSlowUntil||0)?Math.max(.30,Number(P.aiSlowMul)||.55):1);
  P.vx=_moveX*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  P.vy=_moveY*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  let nx=P.x+P.vx, ny=P.y+P.vy;

  // Keep in bounds (per scene)
  const sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  nx=Math.max(sc.bounds.minX,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxX,nx));
  ny=Math.max(sc.bounds.minY,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxY,ny));

  if(P.scene==='arena'){
    // Stay on the circular arena floor — no wandering into the black void
    const c=sc.center,rad=460;
    const dx=nx-c.x,dy=ny-c.y,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
    if(dist>rad){nx=c.x+dx/dist*rad;ny=c.y+dy/dist*rad}
  }else if(P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam'){
    const mv=pvpSlide(P.scene,P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
    // Dungeon walls — slide along corridors, never clip through
    const mv=dgSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clansiege'){
    const mv=clanSiegeSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clanboss1'){
    const mv=clanBossMoveWithBodyCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='worldboss'){
    const mv=worldBossMoveWithCrystalCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='fartzone'){
    // Exact user-drawn meadow boundary: outside = solid collision.
    // Do not alter town/building hitboxes or other scenes.
    if(!pointInPolygonFartZone(nx,ny)){
      nx=P.x;ny=P.y;
    }
  }else{
    // Solid buildings — can't walk through walls or across rooftops
    const resolved=resolveCollision(nx,ny);
    nx=resolved.x;ny=resolved.y;
  }
  // V104: keep the player's visible foot shadow outside Phoenix/mob shadows.
  // The map/wall collision above remains authoritative; this only adds body blocking.
  if(P.scene==='dungeon'||P.scene==='arena'){
    const _bodyMv=ppaMoveWithWorldBodyCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*.24);
    nx=_bodyMv.x;ny=_bodyMv.y;
  }
  P.x=nx;P.y=ny;
  if(P.scene==='clansiege')clanSiegeUpdateCapture();

  // Portal collision
  if(P.portalCD>0)P.portalCD--;
  if(P.portalCD<=0&&!transitioning&&sc.portal){
    const p=sc.portal;
    if(Math.hypot(P.x-p.x,P.y-p.y)<p.r+20){
      changeScene(p.target);
    }
  }
  // Town teleport -> dungeon (walk into the rune circle)
  if(P.portalCD<=0&&!transitioning&&P.scene==='safe'&&sc.teleport&&sc.teleport.target){
    const tp=sc.teleport;
    if(Math.hypot(P.x-tp.x,P.y-tp.y)<tp.r){
      changeScene(tp.target);
    }
  }
  // 1x1 duel preparation: both players may move, but PvP damage stays locked for 10 seconds.
  updatePvpDuelWarmup();

  // Arena ladder: only a fully cleared stage advances.
  if(P.scene==='arena'){
    if(waveActive&&arenaWaveKilled>=arenaWaveTotal&&arenaActiveCount()===0){
      arenaRecordWave(arenaStageProgress());
      waveActive=false;
      waveTimer=180; // 3 sec break: enough to read the HUD, short enough to keep pace
    }
    if(!waveActive&&waveTimer>0){
      waveTimer--;
      if(waveTimer<=0)startWave();
    }
  }

  if(Math.abs(P.vx)>0.1||Math.abs(P.vy)>0.1)P.bob+=0.28;
  else P.bob+=0.04;

  if(P.shootT<=0){
    // Update facing based on horizontal movement for side sprite flip
    if(P.vx > 0.1) P.face = 1;
    else if(P.vx < -0.1) P.face = -1;
    // When moving vertically, keep last horizontal facing
  }

  if(P.shootCD>0)P.shootCD--;
  if(P.shootT>0)P.shootT-=0.12;
  if(P.recoil>0)P.recoil-=0.1;

  if(attackQueued&&P.shootCD<=0){
    attackQueued=false;

    // Preserve the joystick state. Combat must never cancel movement.
    const movingNow=playerIsMoving();

    if(playerUsesGnomeSprites()){
      try{gnomeFireCannonball()}catch(_){PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0}
    }else if(playerUsesArcherSprites()){
      try{archerFireArrow()}catch(_){PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0}

    }

    P.shootCD=Math.max(1,Math.round(60/((P.atkSpd||1)*shopAtkSpeedMul())));

    if(movingNow && P.attackMode!=='melee' && !playerUsesArcherSprites() && !playerUsesPriestSprites() && !playerUsesMageSprites()){
      // Gnome/ranged legacy behavior stays unchanged:
      // projectile can fire while the running animation continues.
      P.attacking=false;
      P.anim='run';
      P.animTimer=Math.min(P.animTimer||0,6);
      P.runAttackT=14;
    }else{
      // Assassin AND Archer now use their real ATTACK sprites while moving.
      // Movement itself is NOT cancelled; joystick/Smart Attack still moves P.x/P.y.
      P.attacking=true;
      P.anim='attack';
      P.animFrame=0;
      P.animTimer=0;
      P.runAttackT=movingNow?14:0;
    }
  }

  if(P.runAttackT>0)P.runAttackT--;

  // Sprite animation state machine:
  // attack > run > idle.
  // Assassin and Archer keep their real ATTACK animation while moving.
  // Gnome keeps its approved run+separate-projectile behavior.
  const movingForAnim=playerIsMoving();
  if(P.attacking&&movingForAnim&&P.attackMode!=='melee'&&!playerUsesArcherSprites()&&!playerUsesPriestSprites()&&!playerUsesMageSprites()){
    P.attacking=false;
    P.anim='run';
    P.animTimer=0;
    P.animFrame=P.animFrame%Math.max(1,playerAnimDef('run').frames);
  }

  if(P.attacking){
    P.animTimer++;
    const a=playerAnimDef('attack');
    const frameDur = Math.max(1, Math.round(60/a.fps));
    if(P.animTimer >= frameDur){
      P.animTimer = 0;
      P.animFrame++;
      if(P.animFrame >= a.frames){
        P.attacking = false;
        P.animFrame = 0;
        P.animTimer = 0;
      }
    }
  } else {
    const moving = movingForAnim;
    const nextAnim = moving ? 'run' : 'idle';
    if(P.anim !== nextAnim){
      // Smooth transition: don't reset to frame 0 if coming from similar pose
      P.anim = nextAnim;
      P.animFrame = 0;
      P.animTimer = 0;
    }
    const a = playerAnimDef(nextAnim);
    P.animTimer++;
    const frameDur = Math.max(1, Math.round(60 / a.fps));
    if(P.animTimer >= frameDur){
      P.animTimer = 0;
      P.animFrame = (P.animFrame + 1) % a.frames;
    }
  }

  // Сброс зависшей цели:
  // если моб умер, ушёл слишком далеко, скрылся за стеной
  // или игрок покинул его комнату — красная метка исчезает.
  if(P.tid!=null){
    const locked=EN.find(x=>x.id===P.tid);
    if(!targetIsValid(locked))P.tid=null;
  }

  // Fart Zone mine production + 30-minute guard respawns.
  updateFartZoneSystem();

  // Enemies
  for(let i=EN.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const e=EN[i];
    const dx=P.x-e.x,dy=P.y-e.y,d2=dx*dx+dy*dy;
    const reach=42+(e.sz-30)*0.35;
    // В подземелье одновременно размещено более 500 мобов.
    // Далёкие нетронутые мобы "спят" уже после 500px. Проверяем квадрат расстояния,
    // чтобы не делать сотни Math.hypot() на мобильном каждый кадр.
    if(P.scene==='dungeon'&&!e.isBoss&&!e.aggro&&e.hp===e.mhp&&d2>250000){
      if(e.atkCD>0)e.atkCD--;
      e.bob+=0.025;
      continue;
    }
    // V276 FPS: Fart Zone can hold 39–65 guards. Untouched guards far away

      if(e.atkCD>0)e.atkCD--;
      e.bob+=0.025;
      continue;
