    if(e.isAiFighter){
      v174UpdateAiTrainingFighter(e,d);
      continue;
    }
    if(e.isBoss){
      // Boss/static siege objects never leave their anchor.
      if(e.hx!==undefined){e.x=e.hx;e.y=e.hy}

      if(e.isClanSiegeCrystal){
        // Destructible objective: does not move and never attacks.
      }else if(e.isWorldCrystalBoss){
        worldCrystalBossUpdate(e,dx,dy,d);
      }else if(e.isDungeon21Boss){
        dungeon21BossUpdate(e,dx,dy,d);

          cerberusUpdateVisual(e,dx,dy,d);
        }else{

          if(e.aoeState==='idle')clanBossUpdateWhip(e,dx,dy,d);
        }
      }else if(d<=reach&&e.atkCD<=0){
        if(Math.random()*100<effectivePlayerDodge()){
          showPickup('Уворот!','#88ffcc');
        }else{
          P.hp-=playerDmg(e.dmg,e.dmgType||'physical');
        }
        e.atkCD=mobAttackCooldown(e);
        for(let j=0;j<6;j++)PT.push({x:P.x,y:P.y-15,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*5,vy:(Math.random()-.5)*5,life:12,ml:12,sz:2+Math.random()*2,col:'#ff0000'});
      }
    }else{
      // === STANDARD MOB AGGRO / ROOM LEASH ===
      // A mob wakes only when the player enters this mob's personal aggro radius.
      // Dungeon mobs never leave their own room.
      // If the player runs out, the mob reaches the room edge, drops aggro,
      // and returns to its exact spawn point.
      if(e.hx===undefined){e.hx=e.x;e.hy=e.y;e.hunter=true}

      const homeD=Math.hypot(e.x-e.hx,e.y-e.hy);
      let chaseX=P.x,chaseY=P.y;
      let playerInOwnRoom=true;

      if(P.scene==='arena'){
        e.aggro=true;
      }else if(P.scene==='fartzone'&&e.isFartGuard){
        const _fm=fartMineById(e.fartMineId);
        if(_fm){
          const _minePlayerD=Math.hypot(P.x-_fm.x,P.y-_fm.y);
          // Every guard protects its mine, not just its own personal position.
          if(_minePlayerD<=FART_GUARD_AGGRO_RADIUS||d<=FART_GUARD_AGGRO_RADIUS*.82)e.aggro=true;
          if(e.aggro&&_minePlayerD>FART_GUARD_AGGRO_RADIUS*2.25)e.aggro=false;
        }
      }else if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
        playerInOwnRoom=(e.room===undefined)||dgPointInRoom(e.room,P.x,P.y,0);

        // Proximity aggro is individual, not a whole-room group aggro.
        if(!e.aggro && playerInOwnRoom && d<=dungeonAggroRadiusFor(e)){
          e.aggro=true;
          e.edgeChase=false;
          e.edgeWait=0;
        }

        // Direct damage already sets e.aggro=true in the hit code.
        // Once player is outside this room, chase only to the nearest room edge.
        if(e.aggro && e.room!==undefined && !playerInOwnRoom){
          e.edgeChase=true;
          const edgePad=Math.max(12,e.sz*0.22);
          const edge=dgClampToRoom(e.room,P.x,P.y,edgePad);
          chaseX=edge.x;
          chaseY=edge.y;

          const edgeD=Math.hypot(edge.x-e.x,edge.y-e.y);
          if(edgeD<=Math.max(14,e.sp*3.2)){
            e.aggro=false;
            e.edgeChase=false;
            e.edgeWait=0;
          }
        }else if(e.aggro){
          e.edgeChase=false;
          e.edgeWait=0;
        }
      }else{
        if(d<=AGGRO_R)e.aggro=true;
        if(e.aggro&&(d>LEASH_R||homeD>LEASH_R))e.aggro=false;
      }

      if(playerSmokeActive()){
        e.aggro=false;
        e.edgeChase=false;
        e.edgeWait=0;
      }

      let tx,ty,move=false;

      if(e.aggro){
        const cdx=chaseX-e.x,cdy=chaseY-e.y;
        const cd=Math.hypot(cdx,cdy);
        const stopDist=(P.scene==='dungeon'&&e.edgeChase)?10:(e.isFartGuard?FART_GUARD_ATTACK_REACH:reach);
        if(cd>stopDist){
          tx=cdx/cd*e.sp*P.slowMul*(typeof v189EnemyMoveMul==='function'?v189EnemyMoveMul(e):1);
          ty=cdy/cd*e.sp*P.slowMul*(typeof v189EnemyMoveMul==='function'?v189EnemyMoveMul(e):1);
          move=true;
        }
      }else if(homeD>4){
        const s2=Math.min(e.sp*P.slowMul*(typeof v189EnemyMoveMul==='function'?v189EnemyMoveMul(e):1),homeD);
        tx=(e.hx-e.x)/homeD*s2;
        ty=(e.hy-e.y)/homeD*s2;
        move=true;
      }

      if(e.isFartGuard){
        e.fartMoving=!!move;
        if(move&&Math.abs(tx)>0.02)e.face=tx<0?-1:1;
      }

      if(e.type&&e.type.caveSpider){
        if(move){
          if(Math.abs(tx)>Math.abs(ty))e.spiderDir=tx<0?2:3;
          else e.spiderDir=ty<0?0:1;
          e.spiderMoving=true;
        }else{
          e.spiderMoving=false;
        }
      }

      if(e.animPack){
        if(move){
          if(Math.abs(tx)>Math.abs(ty))e.animDir=tx<0?2:3;
          else e.animDir=ty<0?0:1;
          e.animMoving=true;
        }else{
          e.animMoving=false;
        }
      }

      if(move){
        if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
          const bx=e.x,by=e.y;
          const mv=dgSlide(e.x,e.y,tx,ty,e.sz*0.3);

          if(e.room!==undefined){
            const roomPad=Math.max(10,e.sz*0.18);
            if(dgPointInRoom(e.room,mv.x,mv.y,roomPad)){
              e.x=mv.x;e.y=mv.y;
            }else{
              const cl=dgClampToRoom(e.room,mv.x,mv.y,roomPad);
              if(dgCircleWalk(cl.x,cl.y,e.sz*0.3)){
                e.x=cl.x;e.y=cl.y;
              }
            }
          }else{
            e.x=mv.x;e.y=mv.y;
          }

          // Collision can make the exact mathematical edge unreachable.
          // A short stall therefore counts as "reached room boundary".
          if(e.edgeChase){
            const moved=Math.hypot(e.x-bx,e.y-by);
            if(mo

===== Cannonball #5 @97853567 =====
else ln.innerHTML='ФЕНИКС ПОВЕРЖЕН<div class="sub">Открыты уровни 21–40</div>';
        ln.classList.add('show');
        setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2500);
      }
    }
  }

  // Respawns + loot pickup (dungeon)
  if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
    const now=Date.now();
    for(let i=RESPAWN_Q.length-1;i>=0;i--){
      if(now>=RESPAWN_Q[i].at){const si=RESPAWN_Q[i].si;RESPAWN_Q.splice(i,1);spawnMobAtPoint(si,true);}
    }
    if(DUNGEON_MODE==='1-20'&&now>=BOSS_READY_AT&&!EN.some(e=>e.isBoss))spawnBoss();
  }
  // Loot pickup (all scenes) — items go to the character bag
  {
    for(let i=LOOT.length-1;i>=0;i--){
      const l=LOOT[i];l.bob+=0.1;
      const _petEquipped=!!(INV.equipped&&INV.equipped.pet);
      let _petDist=_petEquipped?Math.hypot(l.x-PET_FOLLOW.x,l.y-PET_FOLLOW.y):99999;
      if(_petEquipped&&_petDist<180&&_petDist>24){

        l.x+=_mx*_ms;l.y+=_my*_ms;_petDist=Math.hypot(l.x-PET_FOLLOW.x,l.y-PET_FOLLOW.y);
      }
      const _pickedByPet=_petEquipped&&_petDist<30;
      if(Math.hypot(l.x-P.x,l.y-P.y)<48||_pickedByPet){

          showPickup((_pickedByPet?'🐾 ':'')+'PPA +'+(l.amount||1),'#f3b34f');

          showPickup((_pickedByPet?'🐾 ':'')+'Перо Феникса +1','#ff925f');
          scheduleCombatSave();sendInvState();sendBlacksmithState();
        }else if(l.kind==='material'){
          INV.materials[l.name]=(INV.materials[l.name]||0)+(l.amount||1);

          showPickup((_pickedByPet?'🐾 ':'')+l.name+' ×'+(l.amount||1),RCOL_P[l.rarity]||'#a7adb5');
          scheduleCombatSave();sendBlacksmithState();

        }else if(l.kind==='consumable'){
          INV.consumables=INV.consumables||{};
          var _ck=String(l.consumable||'');
          INV.consumables[_ck]=(Number(INV.consumables[_ck])||0)+(l.amount||1);
          showPickup((_pickedByPet?'🐾 ':'')+(l.icon||'◆')+' '+l.name+' ×'+(l.amount||1),l.color||'#d69cff');

        try{if(window.keeperQuestOnPickup)window.keeperQuestOnPickup(l)}catch(_){}
        LOOT.splice(i,1);

    }
  }

  // Crystal world-boss projectiles + personal post-kill chests.

  // Boss fireballs (dungeon): fly, burn on walls, hurt the player
  if(P.scene==='dungeon'||P.scene==='clanboss1'){
    for(let i=PR.length-1;i>=0;i--){

      PT.push({x:f.x,y:f.y,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*1.6,vy:(Math.random()-.5)*1.6,life:13,ml:13,sz:2.5+Math.random()*3,col:Math.random()<0.5?'#ff6622':'#ffbb33'});
      let kill=f.life<=0;
      if(P.scene==='dungeon'&&!dgWalk(f.x,f.y))kill=true;
      if(P.scene==='clanboss1'&&!clanBossWalk(f.x,f.y,0))kill=true;
      if(!kill&&Math.hypot(f.x-P.x,f.y-P.y)<26){
        if(Math.random()*100<effectivePlayerDodge())showPickup('Уворот!','#88ffcc');
        else P.hp-=playerDmg(f.dmg,f.dmgType||'magic');
        kill=true;
        for(let j=0;j<8;j++)PT.push({x:P.x,y:P.y-10,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*6,vy:(Math.random()-.5)*6,life:14,ml:14,sz:2+Math.random()*3,col:'#ff5522'});
      }
      if(kill){
        for(let j=0;j<8;j++)PT.push({x:f.x,y:f.y,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*5,vy:(Math.random()-.5)*5,life:12,ml:12,sz:2+Math.random()*3,col:'#ff8833'});
        PR.splice(i,1);
      }
    }
  }

  // Smoke screen: keep a visible cloud around the assassin while the buff is active.
  if(playerSmokeActive()){
    P.smokeFxTick=(P.smokeFxTick||0)+1;
    if(P.smokeFxTick%3===0){
      for(let k=0;k<3;k++){
        let a=Math.random()*Math.PI*2,r=Math.random()*62;
        PT.push({x:P.x+Math.cos(a)*r,y:P.y+Math.sin(a)*r,
          vx:(Math.random()-.5)*.8,vy:-.25-Math.random()*.7,
          life:24,ml:24,sz:4+Math.random()*6,col:Math.random()<.5?'#57405f':'#2e2933'});
      }
    }
  }else{
    P.smokeFxTick=0;P.smokeDodgeBonus=0;
  }

  // Particles
  for(let i=PT.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const p=PT[i];p.x+=p.vx;p.y+=p.vy;p.vx*=.94;p.vy*=.94;p.life--;
    if(p.life<=0)PT.splice(i,1);
  }
  {
    const particleCap=(P.scene==='dungeon')?96:MAX_PARTICLES;
    if(PT.length>particleCap)PT.splice(0,PT.length-particleCap);
  }

  // Regen
  if(P.hp<P.mhp)P.hp=Math.min(P.mhp,P.hp+0.06);
  if(P.mp<P.mmp){if(typeof v189NaturalManaRegen==='function')v189NaturalManaRegen();else P.mp=Math.min(P.mmp,P.mp+0.12);}

  refreshMobInfoPanel(false);

  // V189: Priest Divine Rebirth can cancel death before the normal respawn flow.
  if(P.hp<=0&&typeof v189TryDivineRebirth==='function')v189TryDivineRebirth();

  // Death
  if(P.hp<=0){
    if(P.scene==='clanboss1')clanBossPlayerDeath(false);
    P.dead=true;
    document.getElementById('fKil').textContent=P.kil;
    document.getElementById('fLvl').textContent=P.lvl;
    const rb=document.getElementById('respawnBtn');
    if(rb)rb.textContent=(P.scene==='dungeon'||P.scene==='clanboss1')?'ВОЗРОДИТЬСЯ В РЕСП-ЗОНЕ':'ВОЗРОДИТЬСЯ В ГОРОДЕ';
    document.getElementById('over').style.display='flex';
    if(P.scene==='pvp1'&&window.PPA_AI_TRAINING_ACTIVE===true&&typeof v225AiPlayerDefeated==='function'){
      v225AiPlayerDefeated();
    }
  }

  // V255 FPS AUDIT: DOM HUD refresh is visual only; 10 Hz is enough and avoids
// dozens of style/text/layout writes on every animation frame.
const __ppaHudNow=Date.now();
if(__ppaHudNow>=__ppaHudNextAt){
  __ppaHudNextAt=__ppaHudNow+100;
  // Potion auto-refill
    if(typeof INV!=='undefined'){
      updatePotions();
      document.getElementById('potCount').textContent=String(INV.potions.hp||0);
      document.getElementById('leaveBtn').style.display=(P.scene==='clanboss1'||P.scene==='worldboss'||P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam'||(P.scene==='clansiege'&&PPA_SIEGE.phase==='won'))?'block':'none';

    document.getElementById('mpF').style.width=(P.mp/P.mmp*100)+'%';

    document.getElementById('mpTxt').textContent=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(P.mp))+' / '+Math.ceil(P.mmp);

      _ppaTopCoin.src=PPA_DROP_IMG.src;
    }

    if(gEl)gEl.textContent='🪙 '+INV.gold;

          else waveEl.textContent='Круг '+arenaCycle+' | Ур. '+arenaLevelForStage(waveNum)+' | Осталось: '+Math.max(0,arenaWaveTotal-arenaWaveKilled)+' | На арене: '+arenaActiveCount();
        }else if(waveTimer>0){
          let next=waveNum===42?1:waveNum+1,nextCycle=waveNum===42?arenaCycle+1:arenaCycle;
          let nextText=next===21?'БОСС: ФЕНИКС':(next===42?'БОСС: ВЛАДЫКА СКВЕРНЫ':'ур. '+arenaLevelForStage(next));
          waveEl.textContent='Следующий: круг '+nextCycle+' · '+nextText+' через '+Math.ceil(waveTimer/60)+'с';
        }else waveEl.textContent='Круг '+arenaCycle+' | этап '+waveNum;
      }else if(P.scene==='dungeon'&&DUNGEON_MODE==='41-60'){

          :'';

          pvpCd.innerHTML=scoreHead+'<div style="font-size:10px;color:#c8bca8;margin-bottom:3px">МОЖНО ЗАНИМАТЬ ПОЗИЦИЮ · УРОН

===== Cannonball #6 @97883549 =====
af=e.animMoving
        ? ((Math.floor((Date.now()+(e.animPhase||0)*43)/e.animPack.ms))%4)
        : 0;
      const ar=(e.animDir===undefined?1:e.animDir);
      const dh=e.animPack.h*eliteScale;
      const dw=dh*(MOB_ANIM_FW/MOB_ANIM_FH);
      cx.save();
      cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
      if(e.flash>0){
        cx.globalAlpha=0.72;
        cx.filter='brightness(1.8)';
      }
      cx.drawImage(
        e.animPack.img,
        af*MOB_ANIM_FW,ar*MOB_ANIM_FH,MOB_ANIM_FW,MOB_ANIM_FH,
        sx-dw/2,sy-dh*0.78+bob,dw,dh
      );
      cx.filter='none';
      cx.globalAlpha=1;
      cx.restore();
    }
    // Fart Zone guards: always draw the COMPLETE 128×128 source image.
    // A small foot-pivot step animation prevents static sprites from "floating".
    else if(e.isFartGuard && e.sprite && e.sprite.complete && e.sprite.naturalWidth>0){
      const baseH=Math.max(88,Math.min(112,e.sz*2.55))*eliteScale;
      const baseW=baseH*(e.sprite.naturalWidth/e.sprite.naturalHeight);
      const moving=!!e.fartMoving;
      const phase=(Date.now()/105)+(e.fartWalkPhase||0);
      const step=moving?Math.sin(phase):0;
      const lift=moving?Math.abs(Math.sin(phase))*2.2:0;
      const tilt=moving?step*0.035:0;
      const sxScale=moving?(1+Math.abs(step)*0.018):1;
      const syScale=moving?(1-Math.abs(step)*0.025):1;

      cx.save();
      cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
      if(e.flash>0){cx.globalAlpha=.74;cx.filter='brightness(1.8)';}
      cx.translate(sx,sy+bob-lift);
      cx.rotate(tilt*(e.face||1));
      cx.scale((e.face||1)*sxScale,syScale);
      // Pivot from feet. No source cropping = no chopped heads/arms/weapons.
      cx.drawImage(e.sprite,-baseW/2,-baseH*.80,baseW,baseH);
      cx.filter='none';cx.globalAlpha=1;
      cx.restore();
    }
    // Other dungeon monster sprites.
    else if(e.sprite && e.sprite.complete && e.sprite.naturalWidth>0){
      // Scavenger slime is exactly about 1/4 of the current player on screen:
      // 22 world px × 1.18 camera = ~26 px. Other monsters keep their old sizes.
      const dh=(isScavengerSlime?25:Math.max(54,Math.min(92,e.sz*2.0)))*eliteScale;
      const widthMul=isScavengerSlime?(e.slimeWidthMul||1):1;
      const dw=dh*(e.sprite.naturalWidth/e.sprite.naturalHeight)*widthMul;
      cx.save();
      cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
      // Tiny hit flash without replacing the sprite silhouette.
      if(e.flash>0){
        cx.globalAlpha=0.72;
        cx.filter='brightness(1.8)';
      }
      if(isScavengerSlime&&e.slimeFlip){
        cx.translate(sx,0);
        cx.scale(-1,1);
        cx.drawImage(e.sprite,-dw/2,sy-dh*0.78+bob,dw,dh);
      }else{
        cx.drawImage(e.sprite,sx-dw/2,sy-dh*0.78+bob,dw,dh);
      }
      cx.filter='none';
      cx.globalAlpha=1;
      cx.restore();
    }else{
      // Fallback only if a sprite failed to load.
      cx.fillStyle=e.flash>0?'#ffffff':e.col;
      cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy+bob,e.sz*0.42*eliteScale,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
    }
    // HP bar
    if(e.hp<e.mhp||e.isDungeonElite){
      const bw=e.sz*(e.isDungeonElite?2.3:1),bh=e.isDungeonElite?7:4;
      const barY=sy-e.sz*0.65*eliteScale;
      cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.78)';
      cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,barY,bw,bh);
      cx.fillStyle=e.isDungeonElite?'#b25cff':'#ff3333';
      cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,barY,bw*Math.max(0,e.hp/e.mhp),bh);
      if(e.isDungeonElite){cx.strokeStyle='#f2c45e';cx.lineWidth=1;cx.strokeRect(sx-bw/2,barY,bw,bh)}
    }
    // Уровень и имя моба
    if(e.lvl&&e.type&&e.type.n){
      cx.textAlign='center';
      cx.font=e.isDungeonElite?'bold 11px monospace':'bold 9px monospace';
      const _eliteName=(e.isDungeonElite?'⚠ ЭЛИТА · ':'')+'['+e.lvl+'] '+e.type.n;
      const _nameY=sy-e.sz*0.75*eliteScale;
      cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.82)';
      cx.fillText(_eliteName,sx+1,_nameY+1);
      cx.fillStyle=e.isDungeonElite?'#ffd76a':'#ffdd88';
      cx.fillText(_eliteName,sx,_nameY);
      cx.textAlign='left';
    }
  }

  // Crystal projectiles from the world boss.
  if(P.scene==='worldboss')drawWorldCrystalProjectiles();

  // Boss fireballs
  for(const f of PR){
    const fx=f.x-cam.x,fy=f.y-cam.y;
    const fg=cx.createRadialGradient(fx,fy,0,fx,fy,15);
    fg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,240,180,0.95)');
    fg.addColorStop(0.4,'rgba(255,140,40,0.75)');
    fg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,60,0,0)');
    cx.fillStyle=fg;cx.beginPath();cx.arc(fx,fy,15,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  }

  // Online players from Realtime Presence (same scene only).
  drawOnlinePlayers();

  // Equipped visuals.
  updatePetFollower();
  drawPetFollower();

  // Wings and cloak stay as equipment/craft items,
  // but are NOT rendered in the world.
  // Player is always rendered in the normal world pass.
  // The old reward-only late-render branch could make the hero invisible
  // between Titan death and personal-chest pickup on some mobile browsers.
  drawPlayer();

  // Player projectiles are rendered above world sprites so shots stay readable.
  try{drawPlayerCannonballs()}catch(_){}
  try{drawPlayerArrows()}catch(_){}

  // Artifact floats above/beside the character.
  drawEquippedArtifact();

  // V104 depth for the original Phoenix and all ordinary dungeon/arena mobs.
  // When the player walks behind their ground shadow, their sprite body is redrawn on top.
  ppaDrawWorldBodyOccluders();

  // V89 depth sorting for the large clan boss.
  // If the player's feet are behind the boss, the boss body covers the player.
  if(P.scene==='clanboss1'){
    const _depthBoss=clanBossCombatTarget();
    if(_depthBoss)drawClanBossBodyOccluder(_depthBoss);
  }

  // World boss depth: player disappears behind the Titan when walking behind its body.
  if(P.scene==='worldboss'){
    const _worldDepthBoss=worldCrystalBossEntity();
    if(_worldDepthBoss)drawWorldCrystalBossOccluder(_worldDepthBoss);
  }

  // V204: small frameless character name follows the hero in world space.
  try{drawPlayerNickname()}catch(_){}

  drawMinimap();

  // Return to physical screen coordinates for screen-space effects.
  cx.restore();

  // Vignette — cached until the canvas size changes.
  cx.fillStyle=ppaGetVignetteGradient();cx.fillRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
}

// === DUNGEON MINIMAP ===
// Pre-rendered walkable mask (1px per mask cell), drawn once
const mmBase=document.createElement('canvas');
mmBase.width=DG.gw;mmBase.height=DG.gh;
(function(){
  const m=mmBase.getContext('2d');
  m.fillStyle='#0a0a0a';m.fillRect(0,0,DG.gw,DG.gh);
  for(let y=0;y<DG.gh;y++)for(let x=0;x<DG.gw;x++){
    if(DG_WALK[y*DG.gw+x]){m.fillStyle='#6e6e6e';m.fillRect(x,y,1,1)}
  }
  // Boss hall marker
  m.fillStyle='#7a1a0a';

let __v218MinimapAt=0;
function drawMinimap(){
  const mm=document.getElementById('mmap');
  if(P.scene!=='dungeon'){
    __v218MinimapAt=0;
    if(mm.style.display!=='none')mm.style.display='none';
    return;
  }
  mm.style.display='block';
  const now=(typeof performance!=='undefined'&&performance.now)?performance.now():Date.now();
  if(now-__v218MinimapAt<320)return;
  __v218MinimapAt=now;
  const m=mm.getContext('2d');
  m.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
  m.clearRect(0,0,200,133);
  m.drawImage(mmBase,0,0,200,133);
  const sxr=200/SCENES.dungeon.w,syr=133/SCENES.dungeon.h;
  // Viewport rectangle respects the dungeon camera zoom.
  const z=cameraZoom(),vw=cv.width/z,vh=cv.height/z;
  m.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.4)';m.lineWidth=1;
  m.strokeRect(cam.x*sxr,cam.y*syr,vw*sxr,vh*syr);
  // Mobs + boss
  for(const e of EN){
    if(e.isBoss){m.fillStyle='#ff3311';m.fillRect(e.x*sxr-2.5,e.y*syr-2.5,5,5)}
    else{m.fillStyle='#ffaa33';m.fillRect(e.x*sxr-1.5,e.y*syr-1.5,3,3)}
  }
  // Player (blinking)
  const blink=Math.sin(Date.now()*0.008)>-0.3;
  if(blink){m.fillStyle='#ffff44';m.fillRect(P.x*sxr-2,P.y*syr-2,4,4)}
}

function drawNPC(){
  const t=Date.now()*0.003;
  const groundDrop=12; // visually seat every NPC on the town floor
  for(const npc of ((SCENES[P.scene]&&SCENES[P.scene].npcs)||[])){
    if(npc.hidden)continue;
    const nx=npc.x-cam.x,ny=npc.y-cam.y;
    if(nx<-220||ny<-260||nx>cv.width+220||ny>cv.height+220)continue;
    const gy=ny+groundDrop;
    const glow=Math.sin(t*3+npc.x*0.001)*0.12+0.88;

    // Dark contact shadow so the NPC base visibly touches the ground.
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.30)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(nx,gy+15,47,13,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    // Small interaction glow directly on the ground.
    const g=cx.createRadialGradient(nx,gy,0,nx,gy,70);
    g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,190,70,'+(0.18*glow)+')');
    g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,190,70,0)');
    cx.fillStyle=g;cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(nx,gy+8,70,28,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    // Actual supplied NPC art, lowered to a common ground baseline.
    if(npc.img&&npc.img.complete&&npc.img.naturalWidth>0){
      const dh=npc.id==='dungeon'?126:(npc.id==='fartzone_guide'?90:112);
      const dw=dh*(npc.img.naturalWidth/npc.img.naturalHeight);
      cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
      cx.drawImage(npc.img,nx-dw/2,gy-dh+18,dw,dh);
    }else if(npc.id==='clan'){
      // Temporary in-game pixel silhouette until the dedicated Clan Master sprite is approved.
      cx.save();
      cx.translate(Math.round(nx),Math.round(gy));
      cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
      cx.fillStyle='#151116';cx.fillRect(-22,-84,44,70);
      cx.fillStyle='#2b1718';cx.fillRect(-17,-72,34,50);
      cx.fillStyle='#9a6b2d';cx.fillRect(-3,-63,6,34);
      cx.fillStyle='#c89642';cx.fillRect(-8,-50,16,4);
      cx.fillStyle='#241b18';cx.fillRect(-14,-101,28,23);
      cx.fillStyle='#0a090b';cx.fillRect(-10,-96,20,18);
      cx.fillStyle='#d0a452';cx.fillRect(-4,-91,3,3);cx.fillRect(2,-91,3,3);
      cx.fillStyle='#7c5327';cx.fillRect(-26,-72,8,48);cx.fillRect(18,-72,8,48);
      cx.fillStyle='#c8953c';cx.font='bold 19px serif';cx.textAlign='center';cx.fillText('⚜',0,-28);
      cx.restore();
    }

    cx.fillStyle='rgba(255,220,120,'+glow+')';
    cx.font='bold 12px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
    cx.fillText('▶ '+npc.label,nx,gy-158);
    cx.textAlign='left';
  }
}
function drawTeleport(){
  const tp=SCENES.safe.teleport;
  const tx=tp.x-cam.x,ty=tp.y-cam.y;
  const t=Date.now()*0.002;
  const spin=Date.now()*0.004;

  // Outer glow
  const g=cx.createRadialGradient(tx,ty,0,tx,ty,tp.r*2.2);
  g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(80,180,255,0.35)');
  g.addColorStop(0.5,'rgba(40,100,200,0.15)');
  g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(20,60,150,0)');
  cx.fillStyle=g;
  cx.beginPath();cx.arc(tx,ty,tp.r*2.2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Rotating rune rings
  cx.save();cx.translate(tx,ty);
  for(let i=0;i<2;i++){
    cx.save();
    cx.rotate(spin*(i===0?1:-1.3));
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba('+(100+i*40)+','+(180+i*30)+',255,'+(0.7-i*0.2)+')';
    cx.lineWidth=3-i;
    cx.setLineDash([12,8]);
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(0,0,tp.r*(0.7+i*0.3),0,Math.PI*2);cx.stroke();
    cx.setLineDash([]);
    // Rune dots on the ring
    for(let a=0;a<6;a++){
      const ang=a*Math.PI/3;
      cx.fillStyle='rgba(120,200,255,'+(0.6-i*0.2)+')';
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.arc(Math.cos(ang)*tp.r*(0.7+i*0.3),Math.sin(ang)*tp.r*(0.7+i*0.3),3-i,0,Math.PI*2);
      cx.fill();
    }
    cx.restore();
  }
  // Swirling center
  for(let i=0;i<8;i++){
    const a=i*Math.PI/4+spin*2;
    const r=tp.r*0.5*(0.5+Math.sin(t*2+i)*0.3);
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(150,210,255,'+(0.3+Math.sin(t*3+i)*0.2)+')';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(Math.cos(a)*r,Math.sin(a)*r,4,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  }
  cx.restore();

  // Label
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(120,200,255,0.8)';
  cx.font='bold 13px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.fillText(tp.label,tx,ty-tp.r-12);
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(150,150,150,0.5)';cx.font='8px monospace';
  cx.fillText('Вход в подземелье',tx,ty-tp.r-26);
  cx.textAlign='left';
}

function drawPortal(){
  const p=SCENES[P.scene].portal;
  const px=p.x-cam.x,py=p.y-cam.y;
  const t=Date.now()*0.003;
  const pulse=Math.sin(t*2)*0.1+0.9;

  // Outer glow
  const g=cx.createRadialGradient(px,py,0,px,py,p.r*2);
  g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(100,60,255,0.4)');
  g.addColorStop(0.5,'rgba(80,40,200,0.2)');
  g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(60,20,150,0)');
  cx.fillStyle=g;
  cx.beginPath();cx.arc(px,py,p.r*2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Portal swirl rings
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    const r=p.r*(0.5+i*0.25)*pulse;
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba('+ (140+i*30) +','+ (80+i*40) +',255,'+(0.6-i*0.15)+')';
    cx.lineWidth=3-i;
    cx.beginPath();
    for(let a=0;a<Math.PI*2;a+=0.1){
      const wobble=Math.sin(a*5+t+i)*4;
      const rx=r+wobble;
      const x=px+Math.cos(a+t*(1+i*0.3))*rx;
      const y=py+Math.sin(a+t*(1+i*0.3))*rx;
      if(a===0)cx.moveTo(x,y);else cx.lineTo(x,y);
    }
    cx.closePath();cx.stroke();
  }

  // Center glow
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(180,120,255,0.3)';
  cx.beginPath();cx.arc(px,py,p.r*0.4,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Label
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(200,160,255,0.9)';
  cx.font='bold 14px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.fillText('► '+p.label,px,py-p.r-18);
  if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(230,210,255,0.72)';cx.font='bold 10px monospace';
    cx.fillText('ТЕЛЕПОРТ В МИРНЫЙ ГОРОД',px,py-p.r-34);
  }
  cx.textAlign='left';
}


var PET_FOLLOW={x:0,y:0,scene:'',phase:0,dir:'S'};

function resetPetFollower(){
  PET_FOLLOW.x=P.x-(P.face||1)*50;PET_FOLLOW.y=P.y+22;PET_FOLLOW.scene=P.scene;PET_FOLLOW.phase=0;PET_FOLLOW.dir='S';
}
function updatePetFollower(){
  var it=INV&&INV.equipped?INV.equipped.pet:null;if(!it)return;
  if(PET_FOLLOW.scene!==P.scene||Math.hypot(PET_FOLLOW.x-P.x,PET_FOLLOW.y-P.y)>330)resetPetFollower();
  var mv=Math.hypot(P.vx||0,P.vy||0),dxn,dyn;
  if(mv>.15){dxn=(P.vx||0)/mv;dyn=(P.vy||0)/mv}
  else{var pf=playerFacing4();dxn=pf==='E'?1:pf==='W'?-1:0;dyn=pf==='S'?1:pf==='N'?-1:0}
  var tx=P.x-dxn*52-dyn*16,ty=P.y-dyn*52+dxn*16+10;
  var dx=tx-PET_FOLLOW.x,dy=ty-PET_FOLLOW.y,dd=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
  var step=Math.min(dd,Math.max(1.7,Math.min(6.3,dd*.11)));
  if(dd>1){PET_FOLLOW.

===== Cannonball #7 @110378387 =====
){}
      try{syncAllItemBM()}catch(_){}

<!-- V130 PAID CLASS CHANGE
- Premium Shop -> Premium-status section now contains "Смена класса" for 10 Gram.
- Allowed only in Мирном городе.
- Level, XP, rebirths, currencies, inventory and account progress are kept.
- Earned stat points are refunded for redistribution on the new class.
- Equipped + bag + personal/Premium-storage gear of the OLD class is converted
  to equivalent NEW-class gear while preserving rarity, enhancement, rolled
  stats, item level, BM and UID.
- Clan storage and auction lots are never converted.
- Converted gear becomes account-bound and is excluded from auction/direct trade
  to prevent market arbitrage through repeated class changes.
- This purchase spends Gram but does NOT count as a verified lifetime paid top-up.
- The old temporary startup reset is removed; paid class change uses persistent progression.
-->

<!-- V131 FULL CLASS BALANCE L1-L40
- One authoritative CLASS_BALANCE table for all 8 classes.
- Every class has clean level-1 and clean level-40:
  HP, MP, ATK, DEF, movement speed, attack speed, basic range,
  crit chance, dodge and crit damage.
- Stats interpolate every level from 1 to 40; lvl40+ uses lvl40 clean baseline.
- Gear and allocated stat points are applied ON TOP of clean class stats.
- Normal attack no longer has the old hidden +10 damage.
- V135 supersedes the old fixed-hit-count idea. Player classes are role-balanced,
  not tuned to kill a particular mob in a particular number of hits.
- Gnome cannonball now uses NORMAL attack damage, not skill damage.
- Basic target acquisition obeys the current class's attack range.
- Attack cooldown obeys the current class's attack speed.
- Movement now actually follows the recomputed class movement speed.
- Old universal +20 HP / +10 MP per level removed.
- Paid class change and fresh sprite/class test use the same central balance table.
-->

<!-- V134 SIMPLE 7-LEVEL ANTIFARM

Applied to:
- XP

- ordinary gear chance
- materials

- grimoires
- ordinary Phoenix-feather chance

NOT applied to bosses/world bosses/clan bosses.

<!-- V138 SMART ATTACK APPROACH
ONE TAP:
1. Select nearest valid mob in the current visible/legal area.
2. If outside this class's normal attack range, hero runs toward that ONE mob.
3. Existing collision handles walls/map/boss bodies.
4. On entering attack range, hero stops and performs ONE normal attack.
5. No automatic selection of the next mob. Tap attack again for another command.

CONTROL:
- Joystick can be used during Smart Attack; it does not cancel target lock.
- PvP does NOT use auto-chase; positioning remains manual.
- Ranged classes stop at their own range; melee classes run close.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm and class combat values are unchanged from V137.
-->

<!-- V139 SMART ATTACK + JOYSTICK
- Smart Attack and joystick now work together.
- Press attack once -> nearest valid target stays locked.
- If joystick is neutral, character auto-runs into attack range.
- If joystick is being used, the player controls movement manually while the
  target remains locked and the character keeps aiming at that target.
- When the target enters attack range, one attack is executed when cooldown is ready.
- Releasing joystick resumes auto-approach toward the same target.
- No automatic next-target chaining.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm and class stats are unchanged from V138/V137.
-->

- Smart Attack only auto-moves when joystick is actually neutral.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm, class stats and combat balance are unchanged.

<!-- V129 GNOME + FRESH CLASS TEST

<!-- V130 PAID CLASS CHANGE
- Premium Shop -> Premium-status section now contains "Смена класса" for 10 Gram.
- Allowed only in Мирном городе.
- Level, XP, rebirths, currencies, inventory and account progress are kept.
- Earned stat points are refunded for redistribution on the new class.
- Equipped + bag + personal/Premium-storage gear of the OLD class is converted
  to equivalent NEW-class gear while preserving rarity, enhancement, rolled
  stats, item level, BM and UID.
- Clan storage and auction lots are never converted.
- Converted gear becomes account-bound and is excluded from auction/direct trade
  to prevent market arbitrage through repeated class changes.
- This purchase spends Gram but does NOT count as a verified lifetime paid top-up.
- The old temporary startup reset is removed; paid class change uses persistent progression.
-->

<!-- V131 FULL CLASS BALANCE L1-L40
- One authoritative CLASS_BALANCE table for all 8 classes.
- Every class has clean level-1 and clean level-40:
  HP, MP, ATK, DEF, movement speed, attack speed, basic range,
  crit chance, dodge and crit damage.
- Stats interpolate every level from 1 to 40; lvl40+ uses lvl40 clean baseline.
- Gear and allocated stat points are applied ON TOP of clean class stats.
- Normal attack no longer has the old hidden +10 damage.
- V135 supersedes the old fixed-hit-count idea. Player classes are role-balanced,
  not tuned to kill a particular mob in a particular number of hits.
- Gnome cannonball now uses NORMAL attack damage, not skill damage.
- Basic target acquisition obeys the current class's attack range.
- Attack cooldown obeys the current class's attack speed.
- Movement now actually follows the recomputed class movement speed.
- Old universal +20 HP / +10 MP per level removed.
- Paid class change and fresh sprite/class test use the same central balance table.
-->

<!-- V132 RUN AND ATTACK
- Joystick movement and basic attack now work at the same time.
- While moving, RUN animation stays active and combat is executed independently.
- Standing still still uses the normal ATTACK sprite sheet.
- Starting to run during a standing attack immediately returns to RUN animation.
- Melee recoil no longer fights the joystick while moving.
- Gnome can fire cannonballs while running; projectile/muzzle effect is separate
  from the run sprite, so legs keep animating continuously.
- Attack speed/cooldown still follows each class's V131 balance.
-->

<!-- V133 SOFT EARLY-GAME BALANCE
- V135 does not use hit-count targets. The existing mob table remains as-is.
- Mob damage and move speed reduced in the first 10 levels.
- Dungeon aggro radius ramps from 90 (lvl1-2) to 170 (lvl7-10);
  lvl11+ keeps the old 200 radius.
- Low-level mobs attack more slowly; attack cadence gradually reaches the old rate.
- V132 run-and-attack remains unchanged.
- V131 full class stat curves remain unchanged.
-->

<!-- V134 SIMPLE 7-LEVEL ANTIFARM
Ordinary mob reward multiplier by playerLevel - mobLevel:
0..7   = 100%
8..10  = 70%
11..14 = 40%
15..19 = 20%
20+    = 5%

Applied to:
- XP
- Gold
- ordinary-mob PPA chance
- ordinary gear chance
- materials
- normal sharpening stones
- grimoires
- ordinary Phoenix-feather chance

NOT applied to bosses/world bosses/clan bosses.
No BM, Premium or donor-power calculations are involved.
-->

<!-- V135 ROLE-BASED PLAYER BALANCE ONLY
MOBS ARE UNCHANGED.

Class identity:
- Гном: very high damage per shot, 0.70 attack/sec at lvl1; slow heavy cannon.
- Ассасин: lower damage per hit, fastest melee attack speed + high crit.
- Лучник: medium hit damage, high attack speed, longest standard range.
- Маг: strong hit, slower attacks, lowest HP.
- Страж: lowest DPS group, very high HP/DEF.
- Берсерк: high sustained melee DPS.
- Паладин: middle ground between damage and defence.
- Жрец: lowest pure DPS group; future value is healing/support.

Only CLASS_BALANCE and matching class-selection lvl1 display were changed.
V134 anti-farm remains.
MOB_TABLE and DUNGEON21_ARCHETYPES are byte-for-byte unchanged.
-->

<!-- V136 RUN FACE SHOT DIRECTION

- During the actual shot/attack moment the sprite turns toward the selected target.
- After a short aim window the running sprite returns to the movement direction.
- Works for gnome 8-direction sprites and legacy directional classes.
- No mob stats, class balance, drops or anti-farm values changed.
-->

<!-- V137 COMBAT FEEL RECOVERY
PLAYER COMBAT ONLY:
- Removed the 1→40 interpolated class-stat experiment.
- Restored simple class bases close to the older game feel.
- Restored +20 HP / +10 MP per level and generic +1.5 ATK per level.
- Restored +10 basic-hit base damage for punchier normal attacks.
- Gnome: ATK 30, 1.00 shot/sec, 360 basic range.
- Assassin: ATK 15, 1.55 attack/sec, 20% base crit.
- Archer remains fast ranged, Mage heavy/slower, Tank durable, etc.
- Paid class change and fresh class testing use the same simple class table.

ASSASSIN / MELEE ANIMATION:
- Movement is NOT cancelled by attacking.
- Melee classes play their real ATTACK sprite while moving.
- Assassin no longer attacks with the running/belly pose.
- Ranged gnome still runs while its separate cannonball projectile fires.

UNCHANGED:
- MOB_TABLE
- Dungeon 21+ mob archetypes
- V134 7-level anti-farm
- Gnome approved run directions / mirroring
- Cannonball visuals
- Run-and-attack controls
- Face-target-while-attacking behavior
-->

<!-- V138 SMART ATTACK APPROACH
ONE TAP:
1. Select nearest valid mob in the current visible/legal area.
2. If outside this class's normal attack range, hero runs toward that ONE mob.
3. Existing collision handles walls/map/boss bodies.
4. On entering attack range, hero stops and performs ONE normal attack.
5. No automatic selection of the next mob. Tap attack again for another command.

CONTROL:
- Joystick can be used during Smart Attack; it does not cancel target lock.
- PvP does NOT use auto-chase; positioning remains manual.
- Ranged classes stop at their own range; melee classes run close.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm and class combat values are unchanged from V137.
-->

<!-- V139 SMART ATTACK + JOYSTICK
- Smart Attack and joystick now work together.
- Press attack once -> nearest valid target stays locked.
- If joystick is neutral, character auto-runs into attack range.
- If joystick is being used, the player controls movement manually while the
  target remains locked and the character keeps aiming at that target.
- When the target enters attack range, one attack is executed when cooldown is ready.
- Releasing joystick resumes auto-approach toward the same target.
- No automatic next-target chaining.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm and class stats are unchanged from V138/V137.
-->

<!-- V140 SMART ATTACK JOYSTICK HOTFIX
- Fixed joystick snapping back to center when Smart Attack starts.
- startSmartAttack() no longer calls resetJoystick().
- Holding the joystick while pressing Attack preserves jX/jY and pointer ownership.
- Smart Attack only auto-moves when joystick is actually neutral.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm, class stats and combat balance are unchanged.
-->

<!-- V122 ACCOUNT / PREMIUM / AUCTION SYSTEM
FREE:
- all gameplay remains available;
- may buy on auction;
- cannot sell/direct-trade until activation.

ACTIVATED:
- verified lifetime paid Gram >= 1 OR any purchased Premium bundle;
- direct trade permanently unlocked;
- auction selling unlocked.

AUCTION:
- 1 lifetime paid Gram = 1 simultaneous lot;
- maximum 10 lots;
- spending Gram in auction/shops does NOT increase slots.

PREMIUM STORAGE:
- deposit requires active Premium;
- a purchased Premium bundle also grants Premium-storage access;
- if access later expires, existing items can always be withdrawn.

SERVER HOOKS:
- PPA_RECORD_PAID_GRAM(amount)
- PPA_SET_PAID_GRAM_TOTAL(total)
- PPA_SET_PREMIUM_STATUS(data)
-->

<!-- V125 GNOME APPROVED ALL STATES
- Based on V122 (the last clean gameplay/economy baseline).
- Gnome only: approved IDLE + approved RUN + approved ATTACK.
- 8 directions × 4 frames for every state.
- All source frames isolated from labels/grid/background.
- All three states normalized to identical 256×256 cells.

- No horizontal mirroring for gnome; all 8 directions are real frames.
- Other classes are untouched.

<!-- V126 GNOME FIX
- Fixed horizontal directions for approved gnome art:
  E/W swapped correctly, NE/NW and SE/SW corrected.
- Gnome visual size is exactly 50% of the old V125 gnome draw scale.
- Gnome shadow/bob/dust scale down with the sprite.
- Gnome basic attack is now ranged:
  a physical cannonball leaves the cannon and flies toward the selected target.
- Cannonball damage is applied on impact, not instantly.
- Slight homing follows a moving selected target.
- Cannonballs stop on dungeon/clan-arena walls.
- Lightweight game-layer smoke/impact particles only; sprite art remains effect-free.
- Other classes still use the original melee system.
-->

<!-- V127 GNOME HOTFIX
- Right/left is no longer selected from conflicting generated rows.
  E/W, NE/NW and SE/SW now use one approved side + horizontal mirror.
- Compact 128px-cell atlases reduce Android decode/memory load.
- Gnome made visible again (~70–75% of assassin visually); no more tiny dot.
- Cannonball rewritten as a minimal safe projectile:
  no wall ray checks, no homing loop, no continuous smoke spam.
- Damage is applied only when the cannonball reaches the selected mob.
-->

<!-- V128 GNOME SHOT STABILITY HOTFIX
- Approved run/direction mirroring from V127 is unchanged.
- Gnome cannot disappear if the attack atlas is late to decode:
  approved idle is used as a temporary visual fallback.
- Cannonball no longer scans enemies every frame.
- Cannonball no longer raycasts walls and has no per-frame smoke.
- Target is selected once; projectile flies on a fixed path.
- On arrival damage uses the already-existing skillEnemyHit() routine.

<!-- V130 PAID CLASS CHANGE
- Premium Shop -> Premium-status section now contains "Смена класса" for 10 Gram.
- Allowed only in Мирном городе.
- Level, XP, rebirths, currencies, inventory and account progress are kept.
- Earned stat points are refunded for redistribution on the new class.
- Equipped + bag + personal/Premium-storage gear of the OLD class is converted
  to equivalent NEW-class gear while preserving rarity, enhancement, rolled
  stats, item level, BM and UID.
- Clan storage and auction lots are never converted.
- Converted gear becomes account-bound and is excluded from auction/direct trade
  to prevent market arbitrage through repeated class changes.
- This purchase spends Gram but does NOT count as a verified lifetime paid top-up.
- The old temporary startup reset is removed; paid class change uses persistent progression.
-->

<!-- V131 FULL CLASS BALANCE L1-L40
- One authoritative CLASS_BALANCE table for all 8 classes.
- Every class has clean level-1 and clean level-40:
  HP, MP, ATK, DEF, movement speed, attack speed, basic range,
  crit chance, dodge and crit damage.
- Stats interpolate every level from 1 to 40; lvl40+ uses lvl40 clean baseline.
- Gear and allocated stat points are applied ON TOP of clean class stats.
- Normal attack no longer has the old hidden +10 damage.
- V135 supersedes the old fixed-hit-count idea. Player classes are role-balanced,
  not tuned to kill a particular mob in a particular number of hits.
- Gnome cannonball now uses NORMAL attack damage, not skill damage.
- Basic target acquisition obeys the current class's attack range.
- Attack cooldown obeys the current class's attack speed.
- Movement now actually follows the recomputed class movement speed.
- Old universal +20 HP / +10 MP per level removed.
- Paid class change and fresh sprite/class test use the same central balance table.
-->

<!-- V132 RUN AND ATTACK
- Joystick movement and basic attack now work at the same time.
- While moving, RUN animation stays active and combat is executed independently.
- Standing still still uses the normal ATTACK sprite sheet.
- Starting to run during a standing attack immediately returns to RUN animation.
- Melee recoil no longer fights the joystick while moving.
- Gnome can fire cannonballs while running; projectile/muzzle effect is separate
  from the run sprite, so legs keep animating continuously.
- Attack speed/cooldown still follows each class's V131 balance.
-->

<!-- V133 SOFT EARLY-GAME BALANCE
- V135 does not use hit-count targets. The existing mob table remains as-is.
- Mob damage and move speed reduced in the first 10 levels.
- Dungeon aggro radius ramps from 90 (lvl1-2) to 170 (lvl7-10);
  lvl11+ keeps the old 200 radius.
- Low-level mobs attack more slowly; attack cadence gradually reaches the old rate.
- V132 run-and-attack remains unchanged.
- V131 full class stat curves remain unchanged.
-->

<!-- V134 SIMPLE 7-LEVEL ANTIFARM
Ordinary mob reward multiplier by playerLevel - mobLevel:
0..7   = 100%
8..10  = 70%
11..14 = 40%
15..19 = 20%
20+    = 5%

Applied to:
- XP
- Gold
- ordinary-mob PPA chance
- ordinary gear chance
- materials
- normal sharpening stones
- grimoires
- ordinary Phoenix-feather chance

NOT applied to bosses/world bosses/clan bosses.
No BM, Premium or donor-power calculations are involved.
-->

<!-- V135 ROLE-BASED PLAYER BALANCE ONLY
MOBS ARE UNCHANGED.

Class identity:
- Гном: very high damage per shot, 0.70 attack/sec at lvl1; slow heavy cannon.
- Ассасин: lower damage per hit, fastest melee attack speed + high crit.
- Лучник: medium hit damage, high attack speed, longest standard range.
- Маг: strong hit, slower attacks, lowest HP.
- Страж: lowest DPS group, very high HP/DEF.
- Берсерк: high sustained melee DPS.
- Паладин: middle ground between damage and defence.
- Жрец: lowest pure DPS group; future value is healing/support.

Only CLASS_BALANCE and matching class-selection lvl1 display were changed.
V134 anti-farm remains.
MOB_TABLE and DUNGEON21_ARCHETYPES are byte-for-byte unchanged.
-->

<!-- V136 RUN FACE SHOT DIRECTION
- Run-and-attack movement is unchanged: joystick still controls where the hero moves.
- During the actual shot/attack moment the sprite turns toward the selected target.
- After a short aim window the running sprite returns to the movement direction.
- Works for gnome 8-direction sprites and legacy directional classes.
- No mob stats, class balance, drops or anti-farm values changed.
-->

<!-- V137 COMBAT FEEL RECOVERY
PLAYER COMBAT ONLY:
- Removed the 1→40 interpolated class-stat experiment.
- Restored simple class bases close to the older game feel.
- Restored +20 HP / +10 MP per level and generic +1.5 ATK per level.
- Restored +10 basic-hit base damage for punchier normal attacks.
- Gnome: ATK 30, 1.00 shot/sec, 360 basic range.
- Assassin: ATK 15, 1.55 attack/sec, 20% base crit.
- Archer remains fast ranged, Mage heavy/slower, Tank durable, etc.
- Paid class change and fresh class testing use the same simple class table.

ASSASSIN / MELEE ANIMATION:
- Movement is NOT cancelled by attacking.
- Melee classes play their real ATTACK sprite while moving.
- Assassin no longer attacks with the running/belly pose.
- Ranged gnome still runs while its separate cannonball projectile fires.

UNCHANGED:
- MOB_TABLE
- Dungeon 21+ mob archetypes
- V134 7-level anti-farm
- Gnome approved run directions / mirroring
- Cannonball visuals
- Run-and-attack controls
- Face-target-while-attacking behavior
-->

<!-- V138 SMART ATTACK APPROACH
ONE TAP:
1. Select nearest valid mob in the current visible/legal area.
2. If outside this class's normal attack range, hero runs toward that ONE mob.
3. Existing collision handles walls/map/boss bodies.
4. On entering attack range, hero stops and performs ONE normal attack.
5. No automatic selection of the next mob. Tap attack again for another command.

CONTROL:
- Joystick can be used during Smart Attack; it does not cancel target lock.
- PvP does NOT use auto-chase; positioning remains manual.
- Ranged classes stop at their own range; melee classes run close.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm and class combat values are unchanged from V137.
-->

<!-- V139 SMART ATTACK + JOYSTICK
- Smart Attack and joystick now work together.
- Press attack once -> nearest valid target stays locked.
- If joystick is neutral, character auto-runs into attack range.
- If joystick is being used, the player controls movement manually while the
  target remains locked and the character keeps aiming at that target.
- When the target enters attack range, one attack is executed when cooldown is ready.
- Releasing joystick resumes auto-approach toward the same target.
- No automatic next-target chaining.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm and class stats are unchanged from V138/V137.
-->

<!-- V140 SMART ATTACK JOYSTICK HOTFIX
- Fixed joystick snapping back to center when Smart Attack starts.
- startSmartAttack() no longer calls resetJoystick().
- Holding the joystick while pressing Attack preserves jX/jY and pointer ownership.
- Smart Attack only auto-moves when joystick is actually neutral.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm, class stats and combat balance are unchanged.
-->

- Archer basic attack now fires a lightweight visible arrow projectile.
- Arrow uses the SAME existing basic-attack damage/crit/DEF calculation.
- Archer keeps Smart Attack + joystick behavior from V140.
- Moving Archer keeps run animation while aiming/shooting toward the locked target.
- Standing Archer uses the real attack animation.
- Melee slash visual is now limited to melee classes only.
UNCHANGED:
- Smart Attack V140 joystick behavior

<!-- V122 ACCOUNT / PREMIUM / AUCTION SYSTEM
FREE:
- all gameplay remains available;
- may buy on auction;
- cannot sell/direct-trade until activation.

ACTIVATED:
- verified lifetime paid Gram >= 1 OR any purchased Premium bundle;
- direct trade permanently unlocked;
- auction selling unlocked.

AUCTION:
- 1 lifetime paid Gram = 1 simultaneous lot;
- maximum 10 lots;
- spending Gram in auction/shops does NOT increase slots.

PREMIUM STORAGE:
- deposit requires active Premium;
- a purchased Premium bundle also grants Premium-storage access;
- if access later expires, existing items can always be withdrawn.

SERVER HOOKS:
- PPA_RECORD_PAID_GRAM(amount)
- PPA_SET_PAID_GRAM_TOTAL(total)
- PPA_SET_PREMIUM_STATUS(data)
-->

<!-- V125 GNOME APPROVED ALL STATES
- Based on V122 (the last clean gameplay/economy baseline).
- Gnome only: approved IDLE + approved RUN + approved ATTACK.
- 8 directions × 4 frames for every state.
- All source frames isolated from labels/grid/background.
- All three states normalized to identical 256×256 cells.
- Same feet-center anchor in every frame to prevent visual jumping.
- No sprite muzzle-flash/smoke effects; effects remain a separate game layer.
- No horizontal mirroring for gnome; all 8 directions are real frames.
- Other classes are untouched.
-->

<!-- V126 GNOME FIX
- Fixed horizontal directions for approved gnome art:
  E/W swapped correctly, NE/NW and SE/SW corrected.
- Gnome visual size is exactly 50% of the old V125 gnome draw scale.
- Gnome shadow/bob/dust scale down with the sprite.
- Gnome basic attack is now ranged:
  a physical cannonball leaves the cannon and flies toward the selected target.
- Cannonball damage is applied on impact, not instantly.
- Slight homing follows a moving selected target.
- Cannonballs stop on dungeon/clan-arena walls.
- Lightweight game-layer smoke/impact particles only; sprite art remains effect-free.
- Other classes still use the original melee system.
-->

<!-- V127 GNOME HOTFIX
- Right/left is no longer selected from conflicting generated rows.
  E/W, NE/NW and SE/SW now use one approved side + horizontal mirror.
- Compact 128px-cell atlases reduce Android decode/memory load.
- Gnome made visible again (~70–75% of assassin visually); no more tiny dot.
- Cannonball rewritten as a minimal safe projectile:
  no wall ray checks, no homing loop, no continuous smoke spam.
- Damage is applied only when the cannonball reaches the selected mob.
-->

<!-- V128 GNOME SHOT STABILITY HOTFIX
- Approved run/direction mirroring from V127 is unchanged.
- Gnome cannot disappear if the attack atlas is late to decode:
  approved idle is used as a temporary visual fallback.
- Cannonball no longer scans enemies every frame.
- Cannonball no longer raycasts walls and has no per-frame smoke.
- Target is selected once; projectile flies on a fixed path.
- On arrival damage uses the already-existing skillEnemyHit() routine.
- Fire/update/draw are guarded so a projectile error cannot stop the game loop.
-->

3. Existing collision handles walls/map/boss bodies.
4. On entering attack range, hero stops and performs ONE normal attack.
5. No automatic selection of the next mob. Tap attack again for another command.

CONTROL:
- Joystick can be used during Smart Attack; it does not cancel target lock.
- PvP does NOT use auto-chase; positioning remains manual.
- Ranged classes stop at their own range; melee classes run close.
- Mobs, drops, anti-farm and class combat values are unchanged from V137.
-->

function playerUsesGnomeSprites(){
  return typeof classKeyFromName==='function' && classKeyFromName(P.cls)==='gnome';
}
function playerUsesArcherSprites(){
  return typeof classKeyFromName==='function' && classKeyFromName(P.cls)==='archer';
}
function playerUsesAssassinSprites(){
  return typeof classKeyFromName==='function' && classKeyFromName(P.cls)==='assassin';
}
function playerUsesTankSprites(){
  return typeof classKeyFromName==='function' && classKeyFromName(P.cls)==='tank';
}
function playerUsesBerserkerSprites(){
  return typeof classKeyFromName==='function' && classKeyFromName(P.cls)==='barbarian';
}
function playerUsesPriestSprites(){
  return typeof classKeyFromName==='function' && classKeyFromName(P.cls)==='priest';
}
function playerUsesMageSprites(){
  return typeof classKeyFromName==='function' && classKeyFromName(P.cls)==='mage';
}
function playerUsesPaladinSprites(){
  return typeof classKeyFromName==='function' && classKeyFromName(P.cls)==='paladin';
}
function playerUsesEightDirSprites(){
  return playerUsesGnomeSprites()||playerUsesArcherSprites()||
    (playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&P.anim==='run');
}
function playerAnimDef(name){
  if(playerUsesGnomeSprites() && GNOME_ANIM[name]){
    const a=GNOME_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return GNOME_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesArcherSprites() && ARCHER_ANIM[name]){
    const a=ARCHER_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return ARCHER_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesAssassinSprites() && ASSASSIN_ANIM[name]){
    const a=ASSASSIN_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return ASSASSIN_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesTankSprites() && TANK_ANIM[name]){
    const a=TANK_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return TANK_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesBerserkerSprites() && BERSERKER_ANIM[name]){
    const a=BERSERKER_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return BERSERKER_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesPriestSprites() && PRIEST_ANIM[name]){
    const a=PRIEST_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return PRIEST_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesMageSprites() && MAGE_ANIM[name]){
    const a=MAGE_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return MAGE_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesPaladinSprites() && PALADIN_ANIM[name]){
    const a=PALADIN_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return PALADIN_ANIM.idle;
  }
  return ANIM[name]||ANIM.idle;
}
// Canonical direction index: 0=N,1=NE,2=E,3=SE,4=S,5=SW,6=W,7=NW.
function dir8Canonical(dx,dy){
  if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)<0.001)return Number.isFinite(P.dir8)?P.dir8:4;
  let a=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  let n=(a+Math.PI/2)%(Math.PI*2);
  if(n<0)n+=Math.PI*2;
  return Math.round(n/(Math.PI/4))%8;
}
function playerDir8(){
  // Run-and-attack: movement continues in joystick direction, but the
  // character visually turns toward the shot/target for the attack moment.
  const aimingNow=(P.attacking||P.runAttackT>0||P.shootT>0);
  if(aimingNow && Number.isFinite(P.meleeAng)){
    P.dir8=dir8Canonical(Math.cos(P.meleeAng),Math.sin(P.meleeAng));
    return P.dir8;
  }
  if(Math.hypot(P.vx,P.vy)>0.15){
    P.dir8=dir8Canonical(P.vx,P.vy);
  }
  if(!Number.isFinite(P.dir8))P.dir8=4;
  return P.dir8;
}

function classKeyFromName(name){
  var n=String(name||'').trim().toUpperCase();
  var aliases={
    'СТРАЖ':'tank','ТАНК':'tank',
    'ВАРВАР':'barbarian','БЕРСЕРК':'barbarian',
    'ПАЛАДИН':'paladin',
    'ГНОМ':'gnome','ГНОМ-КАНОНИР':'gnome',
    'ЛУЧНИК':'archer',
    'МАГ':'mage',
    'УБИЙЦА':'assassin','АССАСИН':'assassin',
    'ЖРЕЦ':'priest'
  };
  if(aliases[n])return aliases[n];
  for(var k in CLASS_SKILLS){
    if(String(CLASS_SKILLS[k].name||'').trim().toUpperCase()===n)return k;
  }
  return null;
}

var ALL_GRIMOIRES=(function(){
  var a=[];
  Object.keys(GRIMOIRE_CATALOG).forEach(function(ck){
    ['active','passive'].forEach(function(t){
      (GRIMOIRE_CATALOG[ck][t]||[]).forEach(function(s){
        a.push({id:s.id,n:s.n,ic:s.ic,type:t,classKey:ck,className:GRIMOIRE_CATALOG[ck].name,d:s.d});
      });
    });
  });
  return a;
})();
function findGrimoireSkill(id){
  for(var i=0;i<ALL_GRIMOIRES.length;i++){
    if(ALL_GRIMOIRES[i].id===id){
      var g=ALL_GRIMOIRES[i],a=GRIMOIRE_ART[id]||{};
      if(!g.cardArt)g.cardArt=a.card||'';
      if(!g.iconArt)g.iconArt=a.icon||'';
      return g;
    }
  }
  return null;
}
function skillRank(id){return Math.max(0,Math.min(3,(INV.skillRanks&&INV.skillRanks[id])||0));}
function skillRule(rank){
  if(rank<=0)return {cost:1,chance:1,next:1};
  if(rank===1)return {cost:2,chance:.45,next:2};
  if(rank===2)return {cost:4,chance:.40,next:3};
  return null;
}
function grimChanceForLevel(lv){
  lv=Math.max(1,Math.min(20,(lv|0)||1));
  if(lv<=5)return .0030;
  if(lv<=10)return .0036;
  if(lv<=15)return .0045;
  return .0060;
}
function pushGrimoireDrop(e){
  if(!ALL_GRIMOIRES.length)return;
  var g=ALL_GRIMOIRES[Math.floor(Math.random()*ALL_GRIMOIRES.length)];
  LOOT.push({
    x:e.x+(Math.random()-.5)*40,y:e.y+(Math.random()-.5)*40,
    kind:'grimoire',skillId:g.id,name:'Гримуар · '+g.n,skillName:g.n,
    classKey:g.classKey,className:g.className,skillType:g.type,rarity:'rare',icon:'📖',bob:Math.random()*6
  });
}
function tryGrimoireUpgrade(skillId){
  var sk=findGrimoireSkill(skillId);if(!sk)return;
  var ck=classKeyFromName(P.cls);
  if(sk.classKey!==ck){showPickup('Гримуар другого класса: '+sk.className,'#ff8888');return;}
  var rank=skillRank(skillId),rule=skillRule(rank);
  if(!rule){showPickup(sk.n+' уже III ранга','#ffd168');return;}
  var have=(INV.grimoires&&INV.grimoires[skillId])||0;
  if(have<rule.cost){showPickup('Нужно '+rule.cost+' гримуар(а) · есть '+have,'#ffb36a');return;}
  INV.grimoires[skillId]=have-rule.cost;
  var ok=Math.random()<rule.chance;
  if(ok){
    INV.skillRanks[skillId]=rule.next;
    var roman=['','I','II','III'][rule.next];
    showPickup(sk.n+' · ранг '+roman+' открыт!','#8dff9a');
  }else{
    showPickup('Неудача · '+rule.cost+' гримуар(а) сгорели','#ff6868');
  }
  recomputeStats();
}
// Determine which sprite row to use based on facing direction
// 0=back/up, 1=front/down, 2=side
function getDirRow(){
  // During standing attack OR run-and-attack, face the target/shot while
  // movement itself remains controlled by the joystick.
  if((P.attacking||P.runAttackT>0||P.shootT>0) && P.meleeAng !== undefined){
    const ax = Math.cos(P.meleeAng);
    const ay = Math.sin(P.meleeAng);
    if(Math.abs(ay) > Math.abs(ax) * 0.8){
      return ay > 0 ? 1 : 0;
    }
    return 2;
  }
  // During movement, use velocity with smooth direction switching
  const sp = Math.sqrt(P.vx*P.vx + P.vy*P.vy);
  if(sp > 0.5){
    // Use a threshold that favors vertical for more natural 4-dir movement
    if(Math.abs(P.vy) > Math.abs(P.vx) * 0.8){
      P.lastDir = P.vy > 0 ? 1 : 0;
    } else if(Math.abs(P.vx) > Math.abs(P.vy) * 0.8) {
      P.lastDir = 2;
    } else {
      // Diagonal: pick based on dominant axis
      if(Math.abs(P.vy) >= Math.abs(P.vx)) {
        P.lastDir = P.vy > 0 ? 1 : 0;
      } else {
        P.lastDir = 2;
      }
    }
  }
  return P.lastDir;
}

}
function pvpBuyShopItem(id){
  pvpShopEnsure();
  const it=PVP_SHOP_ITEMS.find(x=>x.id===id);
  if(!it){sendArenaMenuNotice('Предмет не найден');return false}
  if(PVP_RATING.rating<it.minRating){sendArenaMenuNotice('Нужен рейтинг '+it.minRating+' · '+pvpRankInfo(it.minRating).name);return false}
  const bought=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(PVP_SHOP_DAILY.bought[id])||0));
  if(bought>=it.limit){sendArenaMenuNotice('Дневной лимит этого товара исчерпан');return false}
  const have=(Number(INV.arenaTokens)||0);
  if(have<it.price){sendArenaMenuNotice('Не хватает жетонов арены · нужно '+it.price);return false}
  INV.arenaTokens=have-it.price;
  it.grant();
  PVP_SHOP_DAILY.bought[id]=bought+1;
  pvpShopSave();saveGame();sendInvState();sendArenaMenuState();
  sendArenaMenuNotice(it.name+' куплено · −'+it.price+' жетонов');
  return true;
}
window.PPA_ARENA_SHOP_BUY=pvpBuyShopItem;

// Match rewards. Kept modest so purchased attempts cannot become an infinite token loop.
function pvpMatchTokenReward(mode,won){
  if(mode==='5x5')return won?35:8;
  if(mode==='3x3')return won?25:8;
  return won?15:5;
}
function pvpSettleCompletedMatch(won,serverData){
  if(PVP_MATCH.settled)return PVP_MATCH.lastSettlement||null;
  PVP_MATCH.settled=true;
  const rating=pvpApplyRatingResult(!!won,serverData);
  let tokens=pvpMatchTokenReward(PVP_MATCH.mode,!!won);
  if(serverData&&Number.isFinite(Number(serverData.tokens)))tokens=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(serverData.tokens)));
  INV.arenaTokens=(Number(INV.arenaTokens)||0)+tokens;
  saveGame();sendInvState();sendArenaMenuState();
  const result={rating:rating,tokens:tokens};
  PVP_MATCH.lastSettlement=result;
  return result;
}

const PVP_HISTORY_KEY='pxPvpHistoryV59';
function pvpLoadHistory(){
  try{
    var a=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(PVP_HISTORY_KEY)||'[]');
    return Array.isArray(a)?a.slice(0,10):[];
  }catch(_){return []}
}
let PVP_HISTORY=pvpLoadHistory();
function pvpSaveHistory(){
  try{localStorage.setItem(PVP_HISTORY_KEY,JSON.stringify(PVP_HISTORY.slice(0,10)))}catch(_){}
}
function pvpFreshStats(){
  return {kills:0,deaths:0,damageDealt:0,damageTaken:0,startedAt:Date.now(),endedAt:0};
}
function pvpStatsSnapshot(){
  var st=PVP_MATCH.stats||pvpFreshStats();
  var end=st.endedAt||Date.now();
  return {
    kills:Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(st.kills)||0)),
    deaths:Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(st.deaths)||0)),
    damageDealt:Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(st.damageDealt)||0)),
    damageTaken:Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(st.damageTaken)||0)),
    durationSec:Math.max(0,Math.floor((end-(Number(st.startedAt)||end))/1000))
  };
}
function pvpRecordMatchHistory(localWon,settlement){
  var st=pvpStatsSnapshot();
  var rec={
    at:Date.now(),
    mode:PVP_MATCH.mode||'1x1',
    won:!!localWon,
    score:pvpMatchScoreText(),
    kills:st.kills,deaths:st.deaths,
    damageDealt:st.damageDealt,damageTaken:st.damageTaken,
    durationSec:st.durationSec,
    ratingAfter:settlement&&settlement.rating?settlement.rating.after:PVP_RATING.rating,
    ratingDelta:settlement&&settlement.rating?settlement.rating.delta:0,
    tokens:settlement?settlement.tokens:0
  };
  PVP_HISTORY.unshift(rec);
  PVP_HISTORY=PVP_HISTORY.slice(0,10);
  pvpSaveHistory();
  return rec;
}
function pvpUpdateStats(data){
  if(!PVP_MATCH.stats)PVP_MATCH.stats=pvpFreshStats();
  data=data||{};
  ['kills','deaths','damageDealt','damageTaken'].forEach(function(k){
    if(Number.isFinite(Number(data[k])))PVP_MATCH.stats[k]=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(data[k])));
  });
  if(Number.isFinite(Number(data.addKills)))PVP_MATCH.stats.kills+=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(data.addKills)));
  if(Number.isFinite(Number(data.addDeaths)))PVP_MATCH.stats.deaths+=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(data.addDeaths)));
  if(Number.isFinite(Number(data.addDamageDealt)))PVP_MATCH.stats.damageDealt+=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(data.addDamageDealt)));
  if(Number.isFinite(Number(data.addDamageTaken)))PVP_MATCH.stats.damageTaken+=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(data.addDamageTaken)));
  return pvpStatsSnapshot();
}
window.PPA_PVP_MATCH_STATS=pvpUpdateStats;
window.PPA_PVP_GET_HISTORY=function(){return PVP_HISTORY.slice()};

let PVP_MATCH={active:false,mode:null,side:'blue',matchId:null,round:1,blue:0,red:0,firstTo:0,maxRounds:0,attemptSpent:false,settled:false,lastSettlement:null,stats:null,lastRecord:null};
function pvpMatchSpec(mode){
  return mode==='1x1'?{firstTo:2,maxRounds:3,scene:'pvp1'}:{firstTo:3,maxRounds:5,scene:'pvpteam'};
}
function pvpMatchScoreText(){return PVP_MATCH.blue+' : '+PVP_MATCH.red}
function pvpRoundSpawn(){
  if(P.scene==='pvp1'){
    const sp=PVP_DUEL_SPAWNS[PVP_LOCAL_SIDE][0];P.x=sp.x;P.y=sp.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='pvpteam'){
    const ids=PVP_PREVIEW_MODE==='3x3'?[1,2,3]:[0,1,2,3,4];
    const arr=PVP_TEAM_SPAWNS[PVP_LOCAL_SIDE]||PVP_TEAM_SPAWNS.blue;
    const sp=arr[ids[Math.floor(ids.length/2)]];P.x=sp.x;P.y=sp.y;
  }
  P.vx=0;P.vy=0;P.dead=false;P.hp=P.mhp;P.mp=P.mmp;P.tid=null;P.attacking=false;P.shootT=0;P.recoil=0;
}
function pvpStartNextRound(){
  if(!PVP_MATCH.active)return;
  pvpRoundSpawn();
  startPvpDuelWarmup();
  updateUI();
}
function pvpFmtTime(sec){
  sec=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(sec)||0));
  var m=Math.floor(sec/60),ss=sec%60;
  return String(m).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(ss).padStart(2,'0');
}
function pvpShowResult(localWon,settlement){
  const ov=document.getElementById('pvpResultOverlay');
  const title=document.getElementById('pvpResultTitle');
  const score=document.getElementById('pvpResultScore');
  const rd=document.getElementById('pvpResultRating');
  const tk=document.getElementById('pvpResultTokens');
  const st=pvpStatsSnapshot();
  if(title){title.textContent=localWon?'ПОБЕДА':'ПОРАЖЕНИЕ';title.style.color=localWon?'#ffd27a':'#ff7777'}
  if(score)score.textContent=pvpMatchScoreText();
  if(rd&&settlement){
    const d=settlement.rating.delta;
    rd.textContent=settlement.rating.rank.name+' · '+settlement.rating.after+' ('+(d>=0?'+':'')+d+')';
  }
  if(tk&&settlement)tk.textContent='+'+settlement.tokens+' жетонов арены';
  const map={pvpStatKills:st.kills,pvpStatDeaths:st.deaths,pvpStatDealt:st.damageDealt,pvpStatTaken:st.damageTaken,pvpStatTime:pvpFmtTime(st.durationSec)};
  Object.keys(map).forEach(function(id){var el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=map[id]});
  if(ov)ov.style.display='flex';
}
function pvpFinishMatch(localWon,serverData){
  pvpCombatEnabled=false;pvpWarmupUntil=0;pvpFightBannerUntil=0;
  if(!PVP_MATCH.stats)PVP_MATCH.stats=pvpFreshStats();
  PVP_MATCH.stats.endedAt=Date.now();
  // Server may deliver authoritative final stats together with the result.
  if(serverData&&serverData.stats)pvpUpdateStats(serverData.stats);
  const settlement=pvpSettleCompletedMatch(!!localWon,serverData||null);
  PVP_MATCH.lastRecord=pvpRecordMatchHistory(!!localWon,settlement);
  PVP_MATCH.active=false;
  pvpShowResult(!!localWon,settlement);
  sendArenaMenuState();updateUI();
}
function pvpRoundResult(result){
  if(!PVP_MATCH.active)return {ok:false,error:'no_match'};
  pvpCombatEnabled=false;pvpWarmupUntil=0;pvpFightBannerUntil=0;
  let winner=null;
  if(result==='blue'||result==='red')winner=result;
  else if(result==='win')winner=PVP_LOCAL_SIDE;
  else if(result==='loss')winner=PVP_LOCAL_SIDE==='blue'?'red':'blue';
  if(!winner)return {ok:false,error:'bad_result'};
  PVP_MATCH[winner]++;
  const localWonRound=winner===PVP_LOCAL_SIDE;
  pvpRoundBannerText=localWonRound?'РАУНД ВЫИГРАН':'РАУНД ПРОИГРАН';
  pvpRoundBannerUntil=Date.now()+1800;
  if(PVP_MATCH.blue>=PVP_MATCH.firstTo||PVP_MATCH.red>=PVP_MATCH.firstTo){
    const localWon=PVP_MATCH[PVP_LOCAL_SIDE]>=PVP_MATCH.firstTo;
    setTimeout(function(){pvpFinishMatch(localWon)},850);
    return {ok:true,finished:true,score:pvpMatchScoreText()}
  }
  PVP_MATCH.round++;
  setTimeout(function(){pvpStartNextRound()},1800);
  return {ok:true,finished:false,round:PVP_MATCH.round,score:pvpMatchScoreText()}
}
function pvpStartConfirmedMatch(info){
  info=info||{};
  let mode=String(info.mode||'1x1').toLowerCase().replace('×','x');
  if(mode==='pvp11'||mode==='11')mode='1x1';
  if(mode==='pvp33'||mode==='33')mode='3x3';
  if(mode==='pvp55'||mode==='55')mode='5x5';
  if(!['1x1','3x3','5x5'].includes(mode))return {ok:false,error:'bad_mode'};
  if(PVP_MATCH.active)return {ok:false,error:'match_active'};
  if(!pvpConsumeAttempt(mode))return {ok:false,error:'no_attempts'};
  const spec=pvpMatchSpec(mode);
  PVP_PREVIEW_MODE=mode;
  PVP_LOCAL_SIDE=info.side==='red'?'red':'blue';
  PVP_MATCH={active:true,mode:mode,side:PVP_LOCAL_SIDE,matchId:info.matchId||null,round:1,blue:0,red:0,firstTo:spec.firstTo,maxRounds:spec.maxRounds,attemptSpent:true,settled:false,lastSettlement:null,stats:pvpFreshStats(),lastRecord:null};
  const ov=document.getElementById('pvpResultOverlay');if(ov)ov.style.display='none';
  closeArenaMenu();
  changeScene(spec.scene);
  return {ok:true,mode:mode,attemptsLeft:pvpAttemptLeft(pvpAttemptKind(mode)),firstTo:spec.firstTo,maxRounds:spec.maxRounds}
}
function pvpCancelMatch(opts){
  opts=opts||{};
  const oldMode=PVP_MATCH.mode,spent=PVP_MATCH.attemptSpent;
  pvpCombatEnabled=false;pvpWarmupUntil=0;pvpFightBannerUntil=0;
  PVP_MATCH.active=false;
  if(opts.refund===true&&spent&&oldMode)pvpRefundAttempt(oldMode);
  sendArenaMenuState();updateUI();return {ok:true,refunded:!!(opts.refund&&spent)}
}
function pvpExitAfterMatch(){
  const ov=document.getElementById('pvpResultOverlay');if(ov)ov.style.display='none';
  changeScene('safe');
}

function startPvpDuelWarmup(){
  pvpCombatEnabled=false;
  pvpWarmupUntil=Date.now()+PVP_WARMUP_MS;
  pvpFightBannerUntil=0;
  attackQueued=false;P.tid=null;
  updateUI();
}
function stopPvpDuelWarmup(){
  pvpWarmupUntil=0;pvpFightBannerUntil=0;pvpCombatEnabled=false;
}
function updatePvpDuelWarmup(){
  const isDuel=P.scene==='pvp1';
  const isTeamMatch=P.scene==='pvpteam'&&PVP_MATCH.active;
  if(!isDuel&&!isTeamMatch)return;
  if(!pvpCombatEnabled&&pvpWarmupUntil>0&&Date.now()>=pvpWarmupUntil){
    pvpWarmupUntil=0;
    pvpCombatEnabled=true;
    pvpFightBannerUntil=Date.now()+1400;
    updateUI();
  }
}

// Server bridge. Queueing itself never spends an attempt.
// Attempt is spent only when PPA_PVP_MATCH_STARTED is called after a real match is confirmed.
window.PPA_PVP_MATCH_STARTED=pvpStartConfirmedMatch;
window.PPA_PVP_ROUND_RESULT=pvpRoundResult;
window.PPA_PVP_MATCH_CANCELLED=pvpCancelMatch;

    duelLeft:pvpAttemptLeft('duel'),teamLeft:pvpAttemptLeft('team'),
    arenaTokens:(typeof INV!=='undefined'&&Number(INV.arenaTokens))||0,
    rating:Object.assign({},PVP_RATING),rank:pvpRankInfo(PVP_RATING.rating),shop:pvpShopState(),
    stats:pvpStatsSnapshot(),history:PVP_HISTORY.slice()};
};

  n=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(n)||0));INV.arenaTokens=(Number(INV.arenaTokens)||0)+n;
  saveGame();sendArenaMenuState();sendInvState();return INV.arenaTokens;
};
window.PPA_PVP_CAN_DAMAGE=function(){return (P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam')&&pvpCombatEnabled};
window.PPA_PVP_DUEL_SPAWNS=PVP_DUEL_SPAWNS;
window.PPA_PVP_SET_LOCAL_SIDE=function(side){
  PVP_LOCAL_SIDE=side==='red'?'red':'blue';
  if(P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam')pvpRoundSpawn();
};
function pvpMaskForScene(scene){return scene==='pvp1'?PVP_MASK_DUEL:PVP_MASK_TEAM}
function pvpPointWalk(scene,x,y){
  const m=pvpMaskForScene(scene),gx=Math.floor(x/m.cellX),gy=Math.floor(y/m.cellY);
  if(gx<0||gy<0||gx>=m.gw||gy>=m.gh)return false;
  return m.walk[gy*m.gw+gx]===1
}
function pvpCircleWalk(scene,x,y,r){
  const d=r*.78;
  return pvpPointWalk(scene,x,y)&&pvpPointWalk(scene,x+d,y)&&pvpPointWalk(scene,x-d,y)&&pvpPointWalk(scene,x,y+d)&&pvpPointWalk(scene,x,y-d)&&pvpPointWalk(scene,x+d*.7,y+d*.7)&&pvpPointWalk(scene,x-d*.7,y+d*.7)&&pvpPointWalk(scene,x+d*.7,y-d*.7)&&pvpPointWalk(scene,x-d*.7,y-d*.7)
}
function pvpSlide(scene,ox,oy,dx,dy,r){
  let nx=ox+dx,ny=oy+dy;
  if(pvpCircleWalk(scene,nx,ny,r))return {x:nx,y:ny};
  if(pvpCircleWalk(scene,nx,oy,r))return {x:nx,y:oy};
  if(pvpCircleWalk(scene,ox,ny,r))return {x:ox,y:ny};
  return {x:ox,y:oy}
}

function worldBossLootUpdate(){
  if(!WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active)return;
  const now=Date.now();

  if(P.scene!=='worldboss'){
    worldBossFallbackReward();
    return;
  }
  if(now>=WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.expiresAt){
    worldBossFallbackReward();
    return;
  }

  for(const c of WORLD_BOSS_CHESTS){
    const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(now-c.born)/720));
    const ease=1-Math.pow(1-t,3);
    c.x=c.sx+(c.tx-c.sx)*ease;
    c.y=c.sy+(c.ty-c.sy)*ease;
    c.bob+=0.08;

    // Сундуки персональные: пет их не подбирает.
    if(t>=0.92&&Math.hypot(P.x-c.x,P.y-c.y)<55){
      worldBossGrantChestReward(c);
      break;
    }
  }
}
function drawWorldBossPersonalChests(){
  if(!WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active||P.scene!=='worldboss')return;
  const now=Date.now();
  const _wbViewW=cv.width/cameraZoom();
  for(const c of WORLD_BOSS_CHESTS){
    const sx=c.x-cam.x;
    const sy=c.y-cam.y+Math.sin(c.bob)*3;
    const ancient=c.rarity==='ancient',rare=c.rarity==='rare';
    const edge=ancient?'#d999ff':(rare?'#6bbaff':'#e0b56c');
    const body=ancient?'#613875':(rare?'#234d72':'#6b4528');
    const lid=ancient?'#9b62bd':(rare?'#3d7aa6':'#95683a');

    cx.save();
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.38)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+13,19,6,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.globalAlpha=.18;
    cx.fillStyle=edge;
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy,28,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
    cx.globalAlpha=1;

    cx.fillStyle=body;cx.strokeStyle=edge;cx.lineWidth=2;
    cx.fillRect(sx-16,sy-5,32,19);cx.strokeRect(sx-16,sy-5,32,19);
    cx.fillStyle=lid;cx.fillRect(sx-17,sy-12,34,10);cx.strokeRect(sx-17,sy-12,34,10);
    cx.fillStyle=edge;cx.fillRect(sx-3,sy-3,6,9);
    cx.restore();
  }

  // Небольшой таймер выбора.
  const sec=Math.max(0,Math.ceil((WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.expiresAt-now)/1000));
  cx.save();
  cx.font='bold 12px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.fillStyle='#d9efff';
  cx.fillText('ВЫБЕРИ 1 СУНДУК · '+sec+' сек',_wbViewW/2,36);
  cx.textAlign='left';cx.restore();
}

// V178: independent post-kill heartbeat. It is intentionally separate from
// the main update() chain so one unrelated combat/UI exception cannot strand
// the player or the personal reward phase after the Titan dies.
let WORLD_BOSS_POSTKILL_HEARTBEAT=null;
function worldBossEnsurePostKillHeartbeat(){
  if(WORLD_BOSS_POSTKILL_HEARTBEAT)return;
  WORLD_BOSS_POSTKILL_HEARTBEAT=setInterval(function(){
    try{
      if(!WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active){
        clearInterval(WORLD_BOSS_POSTKILL_HEARTBEAT);
        WORLD_BOSS_POSTKILL_HEARTBEAT=null;
        return;
      }
      if(P.scene!=='worldboss'){worldBossFallbackReward();return}
      const now=Date.now();
      if(now>=WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.expiresAt){worldBossFallbackReward();return}
      transitioning=false;
      if(P.hp>0)P.dead=false;
      for(const c of WORLD_BOSS_CHESTS){
        c.x=c.tx;c.y=c.ty;c.bob+=0.05;
        if(Math.hypot(P.x-c.x,P.y-c.y)<58){
          worldBossGrantChestReward(c);
          break;
        }
      }
    }catch(err){
      console.error('V178 world boss heartbeat',err);
    }
  },150);
}

function worldCrystalBossResetFx(){
  WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES.length=0;
  WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX=null;
  WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX=null;
}
// V177: hard unlock after Crystal Titan death. The killing hit can happen while
// Smart Attack, an attack animation or a captured joystick pointer is active.
// Reset every transient combat/input state before the post-kill chest phase.
function worldBossPostKillUnlock(bossX,bossY){
  try{cancelSmartAttack()}catch(_){}
  P.tid=null;
  attackQueued=false;
  P.attacking=false;P.shootT=0;P.recoil=0;P.runAttackT=0;
  P.anim='idle';P.animFrame=0;P.animTimer=0;
  P.vx=0;P.vy=0;
  // Stop every input pointer that can survive the killing frame on Android.
  try{if(typeof activePointer!=='undefined')activePointer=null}catch(_){}
  try{if(typeof joyPointer!=='undefined')joyPointer=null}catch(_){}
  try{resetJoystick()}catch(_){}
  worldCrystalBossResetFx();
  WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES.length=0;
  PR.length=0;
  // Never keep the hero buried under the Titan's former body/loot origin.
  const bx=Number(bossX)||WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_POS.x,by=Number(bossY)||WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_POS.y;
  if(Math.hypot(P.x-bx,P.y-by)<125){
    const dirs=[[0,1],[1,0],[-1,0],[0,-1],[.707,.707],[-.707,.707],[.707,-.707],[-.707,-.707]];
    for(let rr=150;rr<=270;rr+=30){
      let placed=false;
      for(const q of dirs){
        const tx=bx+q[0]*rr,ty=by+q[1]*rr;
        if(worldBossCircleWalk(tx,ty,(P.sz||60)*.24)){P.x=tx;P.y=ty;placed=true;break}
      }
      if(placed)break;
    }
  }
  // This scene is not transitioning after a kill; make sure no stale lock survives.
  transitioning=false;
  try{updateUI()}catch(_){}
}
function spawnWorldCrystalBoss(){
  if(typeof worldBossDailyAvailable==='function'&&!worldBossDailyAvailable()){
    worldBossLootReset();worldCrystalBossResetFx();return null;
  }
  worldBossLootReset();
  worldCrystalBossResetFx();
  const now=Date.now();
  const e={
    id:++eid,isBoss:true,isWorldCrystalBoss:true,
    type:{n:'КРИСТАЛЬНЫЙ ТИТАН'},
    x:WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_POS.x,y:WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_POS.y,
    hx:WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_POS.x,hy:WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_POS.y,
    hp:WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_HP,mhp:WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_HP,
    xp:1,def:18,dmg:WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILE_DAMAGE,dmgType:'magic',
    sp:0,sz:142,col:'#6aaeff',face:1,bob:0,flash:0,atkCD:0,
    wbDir:'down',wbState:'idle',wbActionUntil:0,wbAoeImpactAt:0,wbAoePending:false,
    wbNextCrystal:now+WORLD_CRYSTAL_CRYSTAL_MS,
    wbNextAoe:now+worldCrystalBossNextAoeDelay()
  };
  EN.push(e);
  return e;
}
