      if(ln){ln.innerHTML='КРИСТАЛЬНЫЙ ТИТАН ВОЗРОДИЛСЯ<div class="sub">Новый ежедневный цикл · 18:00</div>';ln.classList.add('show');setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2500)}
    }
  }catch(_){}
},1000);

function worldCrystalBossBodyGeometry(e){
  if(!e)return null;
  return{
    cx:e.x,cy:e.y+WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_SHADOW.cy,
    rx:WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_SHADOW.rx,ry:WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_SHADOW.ry
  };
}
function worldCrystalBossBodyMetric(e,x,y){
  const g=worldCrystalBossBodyGeometry(e);
  if(!g)return Infinity;
  const px=x,py=y+(P.sz||60)*.45;
  const rx=g.rx+(P.sz||60)*.38;
  const ry=g.ry+(P.sz||60)*.14;
  const dx=(px-g.cx)/Math.max(1,rx),dy=(py-g.cy)/Math.max(1,ry);
  return dx*dx+dy*dy;
}
function worldCrystalBossBodyFreeAt(x,y,fromX,fromY){
  const e=worldCrystalBossEntity();
  if(!e)return true;
  const q=worldCrystalBossBodyMetric(e,x,y);
  if(q>=1)return true;
  if(fromX!==undefined&&fromY!==undefined){
    const prev=worldCrystalBossBodyMetric(e,fromX,fromY);
    if(prev<1&&q>prev)return true;
  }
  return false;
}
function worldBossMoveWithCrystalCollision(x,y,dx,dy,r){
  let m=worldBossSlide(x,y,dx,dy,r);
  if(worldCrystalBossBodyFreeAt(m.x,m.y,x,y))return m;
  m=worldBossSlide(x,y,dx,0,r);
  if(worldCrystalBossBodyFreeAt(m.x,m.y,x,y))return m;
  m=worldBossSlide(x,y,0,dy,r);
  if(worldCrystalBossBodyFreeAt(m.x,m.y,x,y))return m;
  return{x:x,y:y};
}
function worldCrystalBossPlayerIsBehind(e){
  if(!e)return false;
  const playerFoot=P.y+(P.sz||60)*.40;
  const bossFoot=e.y+WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_SHADOW.cy+8;
  const overlapX=Math.abs(P.x-e.x)<WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_DRAW*.46+(P.sz||60)*.30;
  const overlapY=playerFoot>bossFoot-WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_DRAW*.73&&playerFoot<bossFoot+5;
  return overlapX&&overlapY;
}
function worldCrystalBossLaunch(e){
  const dx=P.x-e.x,dy=(P.y-18)-e.y;
  const d=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const speed=8.5;
  WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES.push({
    x:e.x,y:e.y-105,
    vx:dx/d*speed,vy:dy/d*speed,
    life:220,born:Date.now(),angle:Math.atan2(dy,dx),
    dmg:WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILE_DAMAGE
  });
}
function worldCrystalBossDoAoe(e,now){
  WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX={x:e.x,y:e.y+8,born:now,duration:950,r:WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_RADIUS};
  e.wbState='aoe';
  e.wbActionUntil=now+900;
  const d=Math.hypot(P.x-e.x,(P.y+(P.sz||60)*.45)-(e.y+8));
  if(d<=WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_RADIUS){
    if(Math.random()*100<effectivePlayerDodge()){
      showPickup('Уворот от AOE!','#88ffcc');
    }else{
      P.hp-=playerDmg(WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_DAMAGE,'magic');
      showPickup('Кристальный удар','#66bbff');
    }
  }
}
function worldCrystalBossUpdate(e,dx,dy,d){
  if(!e||e.hp<=0)return;
  e.x=e.hx;e.y=e.hy;
  e.wbDir=worldCrystalBossDir(dx,dy);
  const now=Date.now();

  // Exact 5-second crystal cadence.
  if(now>=e.wbNextCrystal){
    while(now>=e.wbNextCrystal)e.wbNextCrystal+=WORLD_CRYSTAL_CRYSTAL_MS;
    worldCrystalBossLaunch(e);
    if(e.wbState==='idle'){
      e.wbState='throw';
      e.wbActionUntil=now+520;
    }
  }

  // Random AoE cadence: next interval is one of 7 / 12 / 16 seconds.
  if(now>=e.wbNextAoe&&!e.wbAoePending){
    e.wbNextAoe=now+worldCrystalBossNextAoeDelay();
    e.wbAoePending=true;
    e.wbAoeImpactAt=now+620;
    e.wbState='stomp';
    e.wbActionUntil=now+700;
    WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX={x:e.x,y:e.y+10,born:now,duration:700};
  }
  if(e.wbAoePending&&now>=e.wbAoeImpactAt){
    e.wbAoePending=false;
    worldCrystalBossDoAoe(e,now);
  }
  if(!e.wbAoePending&&now>=e.wbActionUntil&&e.wbState!=='idle'){
    e.wbState='idle';
  }
}
function worldCrystalBossUpdateProjectiles(){
  if(P.scene!=='worldboss'){WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES.length=0;return}
  for(let i=WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const f=WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES[i];
    f.x+=f.vx;f.y+=f.vy;f.life--;
    let kill=f.life<=0||!worldBossPointWalk(f.x,f.y);
    if(!kill&&Math.hypot(f.x-P.x,f.y-(P.y-8))<30){
      if(Math.random()*100<effectivePlayerDodge())showPickup('Уворот!','#88ffcc');
      else P.hp-=playerDmg(f.dmg,'magic');
      kill=true;
      // Tiny impact only: avoids heavy particle spam on mobile.
      for(let k=0;k<3;k++)PT.push({
        x:f.x,y:f.y,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*2.5,vy:(Math.random()-.5)*2.5,
        life:9,ml:9,sz:2,col:'#66bbff'
      });
    }
    if(kill)WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES.splice(i,1);
  }
}
function drawWorldCrystalFxAtlas(img,fx,size){
  if(!fx||!img.complete||!img.naturalWidth)return;
  const age=Date.now()-fx.born;
  if(age<0||age>=fx.duration)return;
  const fr=Math.min(3,Math.floor(age/fx.duration*4));
  const sx=fx.x-cam.x,sy=fx.y-cam.y;
  cx.save();cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
  cx.drawImage(img,fr*192,0,192,192,sx-size/2,sy-size/2,size,size);
  cx.restore();
}
function drawWorldCrystalBossGroundFx(e){
  if(WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX&&Date.now()-WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX.born>=WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX.duration)WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX=null;
  if(WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX&&Date.now()-WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX.born>=WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX.duration)WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX=null;
  if(WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX)drawWorldCrystalFxAtlas(imgWorldCrystalStomp,WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX,210);
  if(WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX)drawWorldCrystalFxAtlas(imgWorldCrystalAoe,WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX,WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_RADIUS*2.08);
}
function drawWorldCrystalBossBody(e,depthOnly){
  if(!e||!imgWorldCrystalBoss.complete||!imgWorldCrystalBoss.naturalWidth)return;
  const sx=e.x-cam.x,sy=e.y-cam.y;
  const ds=WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_DRAW;
  const col=worldCrystalBossDirCol(e.wbDir||'down');
  const row=worldCrystalBossActionRow(e.wbState||'idle');
  cx.save();cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
  cx.drawImage(
    imgWorldCrystalBoss,col*256,row*256,256,256,
    sx-ds/2,sy-ds+28,ds,ds
  );
  cx.restore();
}
function drawWorldCrystalBoss(e){
  const sx=e.x-cam.x,sy=e.y-cam.y;
  drawWorldCrystalBossGroundFx(e);

  // Shadow is also the physical body collision footprint.
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.42)';
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.ellipse(
    sx,sy+WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_SHADOW.cy,
    WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_SHADOW.rx,WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_SHADOW.ry,
    0,0,Math.PI*2
  );
  cx.fill();

  drawWorldCrystalBossBody(e,false);

  // Lightweight hit flash: simple outline, no canvas filter.
  if(e.flash>0){
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(150,220,255,.72)';
    cx.lineWidth=3;
    cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+8,105,38,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.stroke();
  }

  const bw=210,bh=10,barY=sy-WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_DRAW+38;
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.76)';cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,barY,bw,bh);
  cx.fillStyle='#3aa8ff';cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,barY,bw*Math.max(0,e.hp/e.mhp),bh);

// === V211 CHARACTER XP CURVE ===============================================
// XP needed to go from the CURRENT level to the next one.
// Early levels are deliberately slower than before; from level 10 onward
// the requirement grows by 20% per level so progression stays meaningful
// without the old runaway x1.4 curve.
const XP_REQ_EARLY={1:200,2:300,3:450,4:650,5:900,6:1200,7:1600,8:2100,9:2700,10:3400};
function xpRequiredForLevel(level){
  const L=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(level)||1));
  if(L<=10)return XP_REQ_EARLY[L];
  return Math.max(3400,Math.round((3400*Math.pow(1.20,L-10))/100)*100);
}
function syncXpRequirement(){P.xn=xpRequiredForLevel(P.lvl);return P.xn;}

let transitioning=false;

const EN=[],PR=[],PT=[],LOOT=[];
const PLAYER_CANNONBALLS=[]; // V126: dwarf shots; separate from hostile boss projectiles

function gnomeFireCannonball(){
  // Select ONCE at the moment of the shot. No target search is done in the
  // projectile update loop.
  let target=null;
  if(P.tid!=null){
    target=EN.find(function(e){return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0&&targetIsValid(e)})||null;
    if(target){
      const edge=(target.sz&&target.sz>30)?Math.max(0,(target.sz-30)*.4):0;
      if(Math.hypot(target.x-P.x,target.y-P.y)>playerBasicRange()+edge)target=null;
    }
  }
  if(!target)target=findNearBasic();
  P.tid=target?target.id:null;
  if(!target)return false;

  const dx=target.x-P.x,dy=target.y-P.y;
  const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const speed=7;

  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.shootT=1;
  P.recoil=0;

  const muzzleX=P.x+dx/dist*24;
  const muzzleY=P.y-7+dy/dist*10;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.push({
    x:muzzleX,
    y:muzzleY,
    vx:dx/dist*speed,
    vy:dy/dist*speed,
    remaining:dist,
    target:target
  });

  // Very small game-layer muzzle kick so firing while running still reads
  // clearly without adding effects into the sprite sheet.
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    PT.push({
      x:muzzleX,y:muzzleY,
      vx:dx/dist*(1.2+i*.45)+(Math.random()-.5)*.6,
      vy:dy/dist*(1.2+i*.45)+(Math.random()-.5)*.6,
      life:6,ml:6,sz:1.5+i*.45,
      col:i===0?'#ffd36a':'#c47a32'
    });
  }
  return true;
}
function updatePlayerCannonballs(){
  // Extremely small loop: move each ball toward the coordinates captured at
  // fire time. At arrival, use the game's already proven damage routine.
  for(let i=PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const b=PLAYER_CANNONBALLS[i];
    b.x+=b.vx;
    b.y+=b.vy;
    b.remaining-=Math.hypot(b.vx,b.vy);

    if(b.remaining<=0){
      const t=b.target;
      if(t&&t.hp>0){
        try{
          applyBasicAttackHit(t);
        }catch(_){
          const raw=Math.max(1,Math.floor(P.atk||12)-(t.def||0));
          t.hp=Math.max(0,t.hp-raw);
          t.flash=6;t.aggro=true;
        }
      }
      PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.splice(i,1);
    }
  }
}
function drawPlayerCannonballs(){
  const _vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),_vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  for(const b of PLAYER_CANNONBALLS){
    const x=b.x-cam.x,y=b.y-cam.y;
    if(x<-24||y<-24||x>_vw+24||y>_vh+24)continue;
    cx.save();

    // Small but clearly readable cannonball on a phone screen.
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(214,125,42,.30)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x,y,10,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.fillStyle='#24272b';
    cx.strokeStyle='#d68a3d';
    cx.lineWidth=2;
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x,y,7,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.stroke();

    cx.fillStyle='rgba(255,221,155,.75)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x-2,y-2,2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.restore();
  }
}

// === V141 ARCHER BASIC ARROW ==============================================
const PLAYER_ARROWS=[];

function archerFireArrow(){
  let target=null;

  // Prefer the target already locked by Smart Attack.
  if(P.tid!=null){
    target=EN.find(function(e){
      return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0&&targetIsValid(e);
    })||null;

    if(target){
      const range=playerBasicRange();
      if(P.scene==='clanboss1'&&target.isClanBoss){
        if(clanBossDistanceFromBody(target,P.x,P.y)>range)target=null;
      }else{
        const edge=(target.sz&&target.sz>30)?Math.max(0,(target.sz-30)*.4):0;
        if(Math.hypot(target.x-P.x,target.y-P.y)>range+edge)target=null;
      }
    }
  }

  if(!target)target=findNearBasic();
  P.tid=target?target.id:null;
  if(!target)return false;

  const dx=target.x-P.x,dy=target.y-P.y;
  const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const speed=12;

  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.shootT=1;
  P.recoil=0;

  // Start near the bow hand / chest rather than from the character's feet.
  const startX=P.x+Math.cos(ang)*20;
  const startY=P.y-18+Math.sin(ang)*8;

  PLAYER_ARROWS.push({
    x:startX,y:startY,
    vx:Math.cos(ang)*speed,
    vy:Math.sin(ang)*speed,
    remaining:dist,
    target:target,
    ang:ang
  });

  // Tiny bow release read; deliberately light, no glow spam.
  PT.push({
    x:startX,y:startY,
    vx:-Math.cos(ang)*.7,vy:-Math.sin(ang)*.7,
    life:5,ml:5,sz:1.5,col:'#d8c18b'
  });
  return true;
}

function updatePlayerArrows(){
  for(let i=PLAYER_ARROWS.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const a=PLAYER_ARROWS[i];
    a.x+=a.vx;
    a.y+=a.vy;
    a.remaining-=Math.hypot(a.vx,a.vy);

    if(a.remaining<=0){
      const t=a.target;
      if(t&&t.hp>0){
        try{
          applyBasicAttackHit(t);
        }catch(_){
          const raw=Math.max(1,Math.floor(P.atk||12)-(t.def||0));
          t.hp=Math.max(0,t.hp-raw);
          t.flash=6;t.aggro=true;
        }

        // Small pixel impact only.
        for(let j=0;j<3;j++){
          PT.push({
            x:t.x+(Math.random()-.5)*5,
            y:t.y-8+(Math.random()-.5)*5,
            vx:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,
            vy:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,
            life:7,ml:7,sz:1.3+Math.random(),
            col:j===0?'#e9e1c6':'#8a6b42'
          });
        }
      }
      PLAYER_ARROWS.splice(i,1);
    }
  }
}

function drawPlayerArrows(){
  const _vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),_vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  for(const a of PLAYER_ARROWS){
    const x=a.x-cam.x,y=a.y-cam.y;
    if(x<-28||y<-28||x>_vw+28||y>_vh+28)continue;

    cx.save();
    cx.translate(x,y);
    cx.rotate(a.ang);

    // Pixel-like wooden shaft.
    cx.fillStyle='#b98b55';
    cx.fillRect(-10,-1,16,2);

    // Pale metal arrow head.
    cx.fillStyle='#d9dde0';
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(10,0);
    cx.lineTo(5,-3);
    cx.lineTo(5,3);
    cx.closePath();
    cx.fill();

    // Small dark-green fletching matching the ranger palette.
    cx.fillStyle='#556b3b';
    cx.fillRect(-11,-3,4,2);
    cx.fillRect(-11,1,4,2);

    cx.restore();
  }
}

const P={x:SCENES.safe.startPos.x,y:SCENES.safe.startPos.y,vx:0,vy:0,sp:3,hp:100,mhp:100,mp:100,mmp:100,xp:0,xn:200,lvl:1,kil:0,rebirths:0,statPts:10,statAlloc:{hp:0,mp:0,atk:0,def:0,spd:0,atkspd:0,critdmg:0,crit:0,dodge:0},
  face:1,shootCD:0,shootT:0,recoil:0,bob:0,sz:60,autoT:false,tid:null,dead:false,dmgMul:1,slowMul:1,chain:1,atkSpd:3,critDmg:180,
  anim:'idle',animFrame:0,animTimer:0,attacking:false,
  scene:'safe',portalCD:0,lastDir:2,dir8:4,
  clsKey:null,skills:[],skillCd:[0,0,0,0],buffs:{},gunPulse:0};

// === V211 CHARACTER XP CURVE ===============================================
// XP needed to go from the CURRENT level to the next one.
// Early levels are deliberately slower than before; from level 10 onward
// the requirement grows by 20% per level so progression stays meaningful
// without the old runaway x1.4 curve.
const XP_REQ_EARLY={1:200,2:300,3:450,4:650,5:900,6:1200,7:1600,8:2100,9:2700,10:3400};
function xpRequiredForLevel(level){
  const L=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(level)||1));
  if(L<=10)return XP_REQ_EARLY[L];
  return Math.max(3400,Math.round((3400*Math.pow(1.20,L-10))/100)*100);
}
function syncXpRequirement(){P.xn=xpRequiredForLevel(P.lvl);return P.xn;}

let transitioning=false;

const EN=[],PR=[],PT=[],LOOT=[];
const PLAYER_CANNONBALLS=[]; // V126: dwarf shots; separate from hostile boss projectiles

function gnomeFireCannonball(){
  // Select ONCE at the moment of the shot. No target search is done in the
  // projectile update loop.
  let target=null;
  if(P.tid!=null){
    target=EN.find(function(e){return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0&&targetIsValid(e)})||null;
    if(target){
      const edge=(target.sz&&target.sz>30)?Math.max(0,(target.sz-30)*.4):0;
      if(Math.hypot(target.x-P.x,target.y-P.y)>playerBasicRange()+edge)target=null;
    }
  }
  if(!target)target=findNearBasic();
  P.tid=target?target.id:null;
  if(!target)return false;

  const dx=target.x-P.x,dy=target.y-P.y;
  const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const speed=7;

  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.shootT=1;
  P.recoil=0;

  const muzzleX=P.x+dx/dist*24;
  const muzzleY=P.y-7+dy/dist*10;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.push({
    x:muzzleX,
    y:muzzleY,
    vx:dx/dist*speed,
    vy:dy/dist*speed,
    remaining:dist,
    target:target
  });

  // Very small game-layer muzzle kick so firing while running still reads
  // clearly without adding effects into the sprite sheet.
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    PT.push({
      x:muzzleX,y:muzzleY,
      vx:dx/dist*(1.2+i*.45)+(Math.random()-.5)*.6,
      vy:dy/dist*(1.2+i*.45)+(Math.random()-.5)*.6,
      life:6,ml:6,sz:1.5+i*.45,
      col:i===0?'#ffd36a':'#c47a32'
    });
  }
  return true;
}
function updatePlayerCannonballs(){
  // Extremely small loop: move each ball toward the coordinates captured at
  // fire time. At arrival, use the game's already proven damage routine.
  for(let i=PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const b=PLAYER_CANNONBALLS[i];
    b.x+=b.vx;
    b.y+=b.vy;
    b.remaining-=Math.hypot(b.vx,b.vy);

    if(b.remaining<=0){
      const t=b.target;
      if(t&&t.hp>0){
        try{
          applyBasicAttackHit(t);
        }catch(_){
          const raw=Math.max(1,Math.floor(P.atk||12)-(t.def||0));
          t.hp=Math.max(0,t.hp-raw);
          t.flash=6;t.aggro=true;
        }
      }
      PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.splice(i,1);
    }
  }
}
function drawPlayerCannonballs(){
  const _vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),_vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  for(const b of PLAYER_CANNONBALLS){
    const x=b.x-cam.x,y=b.y-cam.y;
    if(x<-24||y<-24||x>_vw+24||y>_vh+24)continue;
    cx.save();

    // Small but clearly readable cannonball on a phone screen.
    cx.fillStyle='rgba(214,125,42,.30)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x,y,10,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.fillStyle='#24272b';
    cx.strokeStyle='#d68a3d';
    cx.lineWidth=2;
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x,y,7,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.stroke();

    cx.fillStyle='rgba(255,221,155,.75)';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(x-2,y-2,2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.restore();
  }
}

// === V141 ARCHER BASIC ARROW ==============================================
const PLAYER_ARROWS=[];

function archerFireArrow(){
  let target=null;

  // Prefer the target already locked by Smart Attack.
  if(P.tid!=null){
    target=EN.find(function(e){
      return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0&&targetIsValid(e);
    })||null;

    if(target){
      const range=playerBasicRange();
      if(P.scene==='clanboss1'&&target.isClanBoss){
        if(clanBossDistanceFromBody(target,P.x,P.y)>range)target=null;
      }else{
        const edge=(target.sz&&target.sz>30)?Math.max(0,(target.sz-30)*.4):0;
        if(Math.hypot(target.x-P.x,target.y-P.y)>range+edge)target=null;
      }
    }
  }

  if(!target)target=findNearBasic();
  P.tid=target?target.id:null;
  if(!target)return false;

  const dx=target.x-P.x,dy=target.y-P.y;
  const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  const speed=12;

  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.shootT=1;
  P.recoil=0;

  // Start near the bow hand / chest rather than from the character's feet.
  const startX=P.x+Math.cos(ang)*20;
  const startY=P.y-18+Math.sin(ang)*8;

  PLAYER_ARROWS.push({
    x:startX,y:startY,
    vx:Math.cos(ang)*speed,
    vy:Math.sin(ang)*speed,
    remaining:dist,
    target:target,
    ang:ang
  });

  // Tiny bow release read; deliberately light, no glow spam.
  PT.push({
    x:startX,y:startY,
    vx:-Math.cos(ang)*.7,vy:-Math.sin(ang)*.7,
    life:5,ml:5,sz:1.5,col:'#d8c18b'
  });
  return true;
}

function updatePlayerArrows(){
  for(let i=PLAYER_ARROWS.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const a=PLAYER_ARROWS[i];
    a.x+=a.vx;
    a.y+=a.vy;
    a.remaining-=Math.hypot(a.vx,a.vy);

    if(a.remaining<=0){
      const t=a.target;
      if(t&&t.hp>0){
        try{
          applyBasicAttackHit(t);
        }catch(_){
          const raw=Math.max(1,Math.floor(P.atk||12)-(t.def||0));
          t.hp=Math.max(0,t.hp-raw);
          t.flash=6;t.aggro=true;
        }

        // Small pixel impact only.
        for(let j=0;j<3;j++){
          PT.push({
            x:t.x+(Math.random()-.5)*5,
            y:t.y-8+(Math.random()-.5)*5,
            vx:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,
            vy:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,
            life:7,ml:7,sz:1.3+Math.random(),
            col:j===0?'#e9e1c6':'#8a6b42'
          });
        }
      }
      PLAYER_ARROWS.splice(i,1);
    }
  }
}

function drawPlayerArrows(){
  const _vw=cv.width/cameraZoom(),_vh=cv.height/cameraZoom();
  for(const a of PLAYER_ARROWS){
    const x=a.x-cam.x,y=a.y-cam.y;
    if(x<-28||y<-28||x>_vw+28||y>_vh+28)continue;

    cx.save();
    cx.translate(x,y);
    cx.rotate(a.ang);

    // Pixel-like wooden shaft.
    cx.fillStyle='#b98b55';
    cx.fillRect(-10,-1,16,2);

    // Pale metal arrow head.
    cx.fillStyle='#d9dde0';
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.moveTo(10,0);
    cx.lineTo(5,-3);
    cx.lineTo(5,3);
    cx.closePath();
    cx.fill();

    // Small dark-green fletching matching the ranger palette.
    cx.fillStyle='#556b3b';
    cx.fillRect(-11,-3,4,2);
    cx.fillRect(-11,1,4,2);

    cx.restore();
  }
}

for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
  let el=document.getElementById('s'+(i+1));
  if(!el)continue;
  el.style.touchAction='none';
  el.style.webkitTouchCallout='none';
  el.style.userSelect='none';
  el.style.webkitUserSelect='none';
  let holdTimer=0,longHold=false,down=false,activePointer=null;

  function cancelTimer(){if(holdTimer){clearTimeout(holdTimer);holdTimer=0}}
  function begin(ev){
    if(ev){try{ev.preventDefault()}catch(_){}try{ev.stopPropagation()}catch(_){} }
    if(down)return;
    down=true;longHold=false;activePointer=ev&&ev.pointerId!=null?ev.pointerId:null;
    // V192: no pointer capture here — keep the joystick pointer fully independent on Android.
    cancelTimer();
    holdTimer=setTimeout(function(){
      holdTimer=0;if(!down)return;longHold=true;v191ShowSkillInfo(i,el);
    },V191_SKILL_HOLD_MS);
  }
  function finish(ev,shouldCast){
    if(ev){try{ev.preventDefault()}catch(_){}try{ev.stopPropagation()}catch(_){} }
    if(!down)return;
    const wasLong=longHold;
    down=false;longHold=false;cancelTimer();v191HideSkillInfo();
    try{el.blur()}catch(_){}
    activePointer=null;
    if(shouldCast&&!wasLong)triggerHudSkillInput(i,ev);
  }

  if(window.PointerEvent){
    el.addEventListener('pointerdown',begin,{passive:false});
    el.addEventListener('pointerup',function(ev){finish(ev,true)},{passive:false});
    el.addEventListener('pointercancel',function(ev){finish(ev,false)},{passive:false});
  }else{
    el.addEventListener('touchstart',begin,{passive:false});
    el.addEventListener('touchend',function(ev){finish(ev,true)},{passive:false});
    el.addEventListener('touchcancel',function(ev){finish(ev,false)},{passive:false});
    el.addEventListener('mousedown',begin,{passive:false});
    el.addEventListener('mouseup',function(ev){finish(ev,true)},{passive:false});
  }
  el.addEventListener('click',function(ev){ev.preventDefault();ev.stopPropagation()},{passive:false});
  el.addEventListener('contextmenu',function(ev){ev.preventDefault();ev.stopPropagation()});
}
window.addEventListener('blur',v191HideSkillInfo);
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',function(){if(document.hidden)v191HideSkillInfo()});
function v192RefreshSkillCooldowns(){
  try{
    const ck=classKeyFromName(P.cls),cat=ck?GRIMOIRE_CATALOG[ck]:null;
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
      const el=document.getElementById('s'+(i+1));if(!el)continue;
      const sk=cat&&cat.active?cat.active[i]:null,rank=sk?skillRank(sk.id):0;
      const left=sk?activeSkillCooldownLeft(sk.id):0;
      el.classList.toggle('skillLocked',rank<=0);
      el.classList.toggle('skillCooling',left>0);
      let cd=el.querySelector('.skillCd');
      if(rank>0&&left>0){
        if(!cd){cd=document.createElement('span');cd.className='skillCd';el.appendChild(cd)}
        const txt=String(Math.ceil(left));if(cd.textContent!==txt)cd.textContent=txt;
      }else if(cd){cd.remove()}
    }
  }catch(_){ }
}
window.v192RefreshSkillCooldowns=v192RefreshSkillCooldowns;
setInterval(function(){if(typeof __gameLoopStarted!=='undefined'&&!__gameLoopStarted)return;v192RefreshSkillCooldowns()},700);



// Dungeon population batches are cancellable when the player leaves the scene.
let dungeonSpawnToken=0;

function placePlayerAtDungeonSpawn(){
  jA=false;jX=0;jY=0;
  if(jS){jS.style.left='37px';jS.style.top='37px';}
  const sc=SCENES.dungeon;
  let sp;
  if(Number.isInteger(DUNGEON_TELEPORT_PENDING)&&DUNGEON_TELEPORT_PENDING>=0&&DUNGEON_TELEPORT_PENDING<DUNGEON_TELEPORT_ROUTES.length){
    sp=dungeonTeleportRouteWorld(DUNGEON_TELEPORT_PENDING);
    DUNGEON_TELEPORT_PENDING=null;
  }else{
    sp=dgNearestWalk(sc.startPos.x,sc.startPos.y);
  }
  P.scene='dungeon';
  P.x=sp.x;P.y=sp.y;
  P.vx=0;P.vy=0;
  P.dead=false;
  if(P.hp<=0)P.hp=P.mhp;
  if(P.mp<0)P.mp=0;
  P.tid=null;
  P.attacking=false;
  P.shootT=0;
  P.recoil=0;
  P.anim='idle';P.animFrame=0;P.animTimer=0;
  P.portalCD=60;
  return sp;
}

// === V240 CLAN SIEGE RUNTIME ==============================================
const PPA_SIEGE={
  active:false,phase:'idle',respawnIndex:3,
  destroyed:{red:false,blue:false,green:false,purple:false},
  bonuses:{attack:0,defense:0,hp:0,atkspd:0},
  captureProgress:0,captureActive:false,captureStartedAt:0,captureLastAt:0,
  enemyInZone:false,winner:'',castleShownAt:0,
  baseStats:null,hud:null
};


function clanSiegeLocalClanKey(){
  try{
    const c=CLAN_LOCAL_STATE&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan;
    if(!c)return '';
    return String(c.id||c.clanId||c.uid||c.name||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  }catch(_){return ''}
}
function clanSiegeTargetClanKey(t){
  if(!t)return '';
  return String(t.clanId||t.clanKey||t.clanName||(t.clan&&((t.clan.id||t.clan.name)))||'').trim().toLowerCase();
}
function clanSiegeIsFriendlyTarget(t){
  if(P.scene!=='clansiege'||!t)return false;
  // Objective crystals are neutral and always attackable.
  if(t.isClanSiegeCrystal)return false;
  const mine=clanSiegeLocalClanKey(),other=clanSiegeTargetClanKey(t);
  if(!mine||!other)return false;
  return mine===other;
}


const CLAN_CASTLE_REWARD_MS=72*60*60*1000;
function clanCastleRewardBase(){
  return {clanKey:'',clanName:'',capturedAt:0,expiresAt:0,xpPct:5,goldPct:5,resourcePct:5,movePct:3};
}
function clanCastleRewardState(){
  try{
    if(typeof CLAN_LOCAL_STATE!=='undefined'&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.connected&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.siegeReward){
      return Object.assign(clanCastleRewardBase(),CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.siegeReward);
    }
  }catch(_){}
  return Object.assign(clanCastleRewardBase(),INV.clanSiegeReward||{});
}
function clanCastleRewardActive(){
  const r=clanCastleRewardState(),mine=(typeof clanSiegeLocalClanKey==='function'?clanSiegeLocalClanKey():'');
  return !!(mine&&r.clanKey&&mine===String(r.clanKey).trim().toLowerCase()&&Number(r.expiresAt)>Date.now());
}
function clanCastleRewardRemainingMs(){
  const r=clanCastleRewardState();
  return clanCastleRewardActive()?Math.max(0,Number(r.expiresAt)-Date.now()):0;
}
function clanCastleXpMul(){const r=clanCastleRewardState();return clanCastleRewardActive()?1+(Number(r.xpPct)||5)/100:1}
function clanCastleGoldMul(){const r=clanCastleRewardState();return clanCastleRewardActive()?1+(Number(r.goldPct)||5)/100:1}
function clanCastleResourceMul(){const r=clanCastleRewardState();return clanCastleRewardActive()?1+(Number(r.resourcePct)||5)/100:1}
function clanCastleMoveMul(){const r=clanCastleRewardState();return clanCastleRewardActive()?1+(Number(r.movePct)||3)/100:1}
function clanCastleRewardText(){
  const r=clanCastleRewardState(),ms=clanCastleRewardRemainingMs();
  if(!ms)return 'НЕТ АКТИВНОГО ВЛАДЕЛЬЦА';
  const h=Math.floor(ms/3600000),m=Math.floor((ms%3600000)/60000);
  return 'ВЛАДЕЛЕЦ: '+(r.clanName||'КЛАН')+' · '+h+'ч '+m+'м';
}
function clanCastleGrantReward(){
  const key=(typeof clanSiegeLocalClanKey==='function'?clanSiegeLocalClanKey():'');
  const name=(typeof clanSiegeClanName==='function'?clanSiegeClanName():'ТВОЙ КЛАН');
  if(!key)return false;
  const now=Date.now();
  const reward={clanKey:key,clanName:name,capturedAt:now,expiresAt:now+CLAN_CASTLE_REWARD_MS,xpPct:5,goldPct:5,resourcePct:5,movePct:3};
  INV.clanSiegeReward=Object.assign({},reward);
  try{
    if(typeof CLAN_LOCAL_STATE!=='undefined')CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.siegeReward=Object.assign({},reward);
  }catch(_){}
  // Server-ready hook: backend should persist this once and broadcast it to all members.
  try{
    if(typeof CLAN_LOCAL_STATE!=='undefined'&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.connected&&typeof window.PPA_CLAN_SIEGE_HANDLER==='function'){
      window.PPA_CLAN_SIEGE_HANDLER({action:'castleCaptured',reward:Object.assign({},reward)});
    }
  }catch(_){}
  try{saveGame();sendClanState();updateUI()}catch(_){}
  showPickup('🏰 НАГРАДА КЛАНУ НА 3 ДНЯ · XP +5% · ЗОЛОТО +5% · РЕСУРСЫ +5% · СКОРОСТЬ +3%','#ffe071');
  return true;
}

function clanSiegeClanName(){
  try{
    return (CLAN_LOCAL_STATE&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan.name)||'ТВОЙ КЛАН';
  }catch(_){return 'ТВОЙ КЛАН'}
}

function clanSiegeFmtCycle(ms){
  var sec=Math.max(0,Math.ceil((Number(ms)||0)/1000));
  var d=Math.floor(sec/86400);sec%=86400;
  var h=Math.floor(sec/3600);sec%=3600;
  var m=Math.floor(sec/60),ss=sec%60;
  return (d?d+'д ':'')+String(h).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(m).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(ss).padStart(2,'0');
}
function clanSiegeCycleStatus(){
  var r=clanCastleRewardState();
  var capturedAt=Math.max(0,Number(r.capturedAt)||0);
  var nextAt=capturedAt?capturedAt+CLAN_CASTLE_REWARD_MS:0;
  var now=Date.now();
  var ready=!nextAt||now>=nextAt;
  return {
    ready:ready,
    nextAt:nextAt,
    remainingMs:ready?0:Math.max(0,nextAt-now),
    text:ready?'ОСАДА ДОСТУПНА':'ДО СЛЕДУЮЩЕЙ ОСАДЫ · '+clanSiegeFmtCycle(nextAt-now)
  };
}

function clanSiegeMenuState(){
  const cyc=clanSiegeCycleStatus();
  return {
    ready:cyc.ready,available:cyc.ready,nextAt:cyc.nextAt,remainingMs:cyc.remainingMs,cycleText:cyc.text,cycleDays:3,
    title:'Цитадель Феникса',
    clans:4,playersPerClan:8,crystals:4,captureSeconds:60,
    rules:'4 кристалла → бонусы кланам → появляется замок → один игрок держит зону, 7 защищают',
    captureReward:{days:3,xpPct:5,goldPct:5,resourcePct:5,movePct:3,status:clanCastleRewardText(),active:clanCastleRewardActive(),expiresAt:Number(clanCastleRewardState().expiresAt)||0},
    bonuses:[
      '🔴 Сила: +10% атаки','🔵 Защита: +10% защиты',
      '🟢 Жизнь: +12% HP','🟣 Бездна: +8% скорости атаки'
    ]
  };
}
function clanSiegePointInPoly(x,y){
  let inside=false;
  for(let i=0,j=CLAN_SIEGE_WALK_POLY.length-1;i<CLAN_SIEGE_WALK_POLY.length;j=i++){
    const xi=CLAN_SIEGE_WALK_POLY[i][0],yi=CLAN_SIEGE_WALK_POLY[i][1];
    const xj=CLAN_SIEGE_WALK_POLY[j][0],yj=CLAN_SIEGE_WALK_POLY[j][1];
    const hit=((yi>y)!==(yj>y))&&(x<(xj-xi)*(y-yi)/((yj-yi)||1e-9)+xi);
    if(hit)inside=!inside;
  }
  return inside;
}
function clanSiegeLivingCrystalAt(x,y,r){
  for(const e of EN){
    if(!e||!e.isClanSiegeCrystal||e.hp<=0)continue;
    const rr=r+40;
    if(Math.hypot(x-e.x,y-e.y)<rr)return e;
  }
  return null;
}
function clanSiegeCircleWalk(x,y,r){
  const pts=[[0,0],[r,0],[-r,0],[0,r],[0,-r],[r*.7,r*.7],[-r*.7,r*.7],[r*.7,-r*.7],[-r*.7,-r*.7]];
  for(const q of pts)if(!clanSiegePointInPoly(x+q[0],y+q[1]))return false;
  if(clanSiegeLivingCrystalAt(x,y,r))return false;
  if(PPA_SIEGE.phase==='castle'||PPA_SIEGE.phase==='won'){
    // Compact round collision sits fully inside the castle footprint.
    const c=CLAN_SIEGE_CASTLE_COLLISION;
    if(Math.hypot(x-c.x,y-c.y)<c.r+r)return false;
  }
  return true;
}
function clanSiegeSlide(x,y,dx,dy,r){
  const tx=x+dx,ty=y+dy;
  if(clanSiegeCircleWalk(tx,ty,r))return {x:tx,y:ty};
  if(clanSiegeCircleWalk(tx,y,r))return {x:tx,y:y};
  if(clanSiegeCircleWalk(x,ty,r))return {x:x,y:ty};
  return {x:x,y:y};
}
function clanSiegeEnsureHud(){
  if(PPA_SIEGE.hud&&PPA_SIEGE.hud.isConnected)return PPA_SIEGE.hud;
  const el=document.createElement('div');
  el.id='clanSiegeHud';
  el.style.cssText='position:fixed;z-index:48;left:50%;top:8px;transform:translateX(-50%);min-width:min(92vw,520px);max-width:94vw;padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid rgba(231,177,82,.62);border-radius:9px;background:rgba(7,8,10,.86);box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.45);color:#ead8b1;font:9px/1.35 monospace;text-align:center;pointer-events:none;display:none';
  document.body.appendChild(el);PPA_SIEGE.hud=el;return el;
}
function clanSiegeHudUpdate(){
  const h=clanSiegeEnsureHud();
  if(P.scene!=='clansiege'||!PPA_SIEGE.active){h.style.display='none';return}
  h.style.display='block';
  const alive=EN.filter(e=>e&&e.isClanSiegeCrystal&&e.hp>0).length;
  const b=PPA_SIEGE.bonuses;
  const badges=[
    b.attack?'🔴+10% ATK':'⚫ СИЛА',
    b.defense?'🔵+10% DEF':'⚫ ЗАЩИТА',
    b.hp?'🟢+12% HP':'⚫ ЖИЗНЬ',
    b.atkspd?'🟣+8% ASPD':'⚫ БЕЗДНА'
  ].join(' · ');
  let phase=alive>0?('КРИСТАЛЛЫ: '+alive+' / 4'):
    (PPA_SIEGE.phase==='won'?('🏰 ЗАМОК ЗАХВАЧЕН · '+PPA_SIEGE.winner):
      ('🏰 ЗАХВАТ ЗАМКА · '+Math.floor(PPA_SIEGE.captureProgress)+' / '+CLAN_SIEGE_CAPTURE.seconds+' сек · '+(PPA_SIEGE.captureActive?'ИДЁТ ЗАХВАТ':'ПОДОЙДИ К ЗАМКУ И ОСТАНОВИСЬ')));
  h.innerHTML='<b>'+phase+'</b><br><span style="color:#aeb7bd">'+badges+'</span>';
}
function clanSiegeSnapshotStats(){
  if(PPA_SIEGE.baseStats)return;
  PPA_SIEGE.baseStats={mhp:P.mhp,hp:P.hp,atkSpd:P.atkSpd};
}
function clanSiegeApplyTempStats(){
  clanSiegeSnapshotStats();
  const bs=PPA_SIEGE.baseStats,b=PPA_SIEGE.bonuses;
  const ratio=bs.mhp>0?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,P.hp/Math.max(1,P.mhp))):1;
  P.mhp=Math.max(1,Math.round(bs.mhp*(1+(b.hp||0))));
  P.hp=Math.min(P.mhp,Math.max(1,Math.round(P.mhp*ratio)));
  P.atkSpd=Math.max(.1,bs.atkSpd*(1+(b.atkspd||0)));
  updateUI();
}

  };
}

    b.defense?'🔵+10% DEF':'⚫ ЗАЩИТА',
    b.hp?'🟢+12% HP':'⚫ ЖИЗНЬ',
    b.atkspd?'🟣+8% ASPD':'⚫ БЕЗДНА'
  ].join(' · ');
  let phase=alive>0?('КРИСТАЛЛЫ: '+alive+' / 4'):
    (PPA_SIEGE.phase==='won'?('🏰 ЗАМОК ЗАХВАЧЕН · '+PPA_SIEGE.winner):
      ('🏰 ЗАХВАТ ЗАМКА · '+Math.floor(PPA_SIEGE.captureProgress)+' / '+CLAN_SIEGE_CAPTURE.seconds+' сек · '+(PPA_SIEGE.captureActive?'ИДЁТ ЗАХВАТ':'ПОДОЙДИ К ЗАМКУ И ОСТАНОВИСЬ')));
  h.innerHTML='<b>'+phase+'</b><br><span style="color:#aeb7bd">'+badges+'</span>';
}
function clanSiegeSnapshotStats(){
  if(PPA_SIEGE.baseStats)return;
  PPA_SIEGE.baseStats={mhp:P.mhp,hp:P.hp,atkSpd:P.atkSpd};
}
function clanSiegeApplyTempStats(){
  clanSiegeSnapshotStats();
  const bs=PPA_SIEGE.baseStats,b=PPA_SIEGE.bonuses;
  const ratio=bs.mhp>0?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,P.hp/Math.max(1,P.mhp))):1;
  P.mhp=Math.max(1,Math.round(bs.mhp*(1+(b.hp||0))));
  P.hp=Math.min(P.mhp,Math.max(1,Math.round(P.mhp*ratio)));
  P.atkSpd=Math.max(.1,bs.atkSpd*(1+(b.atkspd||0)));
  updateUI();
}
function clanSiegeRestoreStats(){
  const bs=PPA_SIEGE.baseStats;if(!bs)return;
  const ratio=P.mhp>0?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,P.hp/P.mhp)):1;
  P.mhp=bs.mhp;P.hp=Math.max(1,Math.min(P.mhp,Math.round(P.mhp*ratio)));
  P.atkSpd=bs.atkSpd;PPA_SIEGE.baseStats=null;
  updateUI();
}
function clanSiegeOutgoingMul(){
  return (P.scene==='clansiege'&&PPA_SIEGE.active)?(1+(PPA_SIEGE.bonuses.attack||0)):1;
}
function clanSiegeIncomingMul(){
  return (P.scene==='clansiege'&&PPA_SIEGE.active)?(1-(PPA_SIEGE.bonuses.defense||0)):1;
}
function clanSiegeSpawnCrystals(){
  const hp=8000;
  for(const d of CLAN_SIEGE_CRYSTALS){
    EN.push({
      id:eid++,type:{n:d.name},x:d.x,y:d.y,hx:d.x,hy:d.y,
      hp:hp,mhp:hp,mp:0,mmp:0,sp:0,sz:48,col:'#ffffff',
      dmg:0,dmgType:'physical',def:55,xp:0,gold:0,lvl:40,
      atkCD:999999,flash:0,bob:0,isBoss:true,isClanSiegeCrystal:true,
      siegeKey:d.key,siegeBonus:d.bonus,siegeBonusKey:d.bonusKey,siegeBonusValue:d.bonusValue,
      aggro:false,face:1
    });
  }
}
function clanSiegeResetState(){
  clanSiegeRestoreStats();
  PPA_SIEGE.active=true;PPA_SIEGE.phase='crystals';
  PPA_SIEGE.respawnIndex=3;
  PPA_SIEGE.destroyed={red:false,blue:false,green:false,purple:false};
  PPA_SIEGE.bonuses={attack:0,defense:0,hp:0,atkspd:0};
  PPA_SIEGE.captureProgress=0;PPA_SIEGE.captureActive=false;PPA_SIEGE.captureStartedAt=0;
  PPA_SIEGE.captureLastAt=Date.now();PPA_SIEGE.enemyInZone=false;PPA_SIEGE.winner='';PPA_SIEGE.castleShownAt=0;
  clanSiegeSnapshotStats();
}
function clanSiegePrepareEntry(){
  clanSiegeResetState();
  const sp=CLAN_SIEGE_RESPAWNS[PPA_SIEGE.respawnIndex];
  P.x=sp.x;P.y=sp.y;P.hp=P.mhp;P.mp=P.mmp;
  P.shootCD=0;P.shootT=0;P.recoil=0;P.attacking=false;attackQueued=false;
  EN.length=0;PR.length=0;PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0;PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0;PT.length=0;LOOT.length=0;
  clanSiegeSpawnCrystals();
  clanSiegeHudUpdate();
}
function clanSiegeLeaveCleanup(){
  clanSiegeRestoreStats();
  PPA_SIEGE.active=false;PPA_SIEGE.captureActive=false;PPA_SIEGE.phase='idle';
  const h=clanSiegeEnsureHud();h.style.display='none';
}
function clanSiegeStart(){
  const clanKey=(typeof clanSiegeLocalClanKey==='function'?clanSiegeLocalClanKey():'');
  if(!clanKey){
    showPickup('🏰 ДЛЯ УЧАСТИЯ В ЗАХВАТЕ ЗАМКА ФЕНИКСА НУЖНО СОСТОЯТЬ В КЛАНЕ','#ff9b72');
    try{sendClanState();sendEventsState()}catch(_){}
    return;
  }
  const cyc=clanSiegeCycleStatus();
  if(!cyc.ready){
    showPickup('🏰 '+cyc.text,'#ffd36d');
    try{sendClanState();sendEventsState()}catch(_){}
    return;
  }
  closeClanMenu();
  changeScene('clansiege');
}
function clanSiegeCrystalDestroyed(e){
  if(!e||!e.isClanSiegeCrystal)return;
  PPA_SIEGE.destroyed[e.siegeKey]=true;
  PPA_SIEGE.bonuses[e.siegeBonusKey]=Number(e.siegeBonusValue)||0;
  clanSiegeApplyTempStats();
  showPickup('💥 '+e.type.n+' УНИЧТОЖЕН · '+e.siegeBonus+' · '+clanSiegeClanName(),'#ffd36a');
  const left=EN.filter(x=>x&&x.isClanSiegeCrystal&&x!==e&&x.hp>0).length;
  if(left<=0){
    PPA_SIEGE.phase='castle';PPA_SIEGE.castleShownAt=Date.now();PPA_SIEGE.captureLastAt=Date.now();
    showPickup('🏰 ВСЕ КРИСТАЛЛЫ УНИЧТОЖЕНЫ · ЗАМОК ПОЯВИЛСЯ','#ffe49a');
    const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
    if(ln){ln.innerHTML='🏰 ЦИТАДЕЛЬ ФЕНИКСА<div class="sub">Подойди к краю замка и стой 60 секунд · атаковать во время захвата нельзя</div>';ln.classList.add('show');setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),3600)}
  }
  clanSiegeHudUpdate();
}
function clanSiegeInsideCapture(){
  // The castle's round collision keeps the player out of the building.
  // Any position close enough to its edge is a valid invisible capture position.
  const d=Math.hypot(P.x-CLAN_SIEGE_CAPTURE.x,P.y-CLAN_SIEGE_CAPTURE.y);
  return d<=CLAN_SIEGE_CAPTURE.outerR;
}
function clanSiegeCapturing(){return P.scene==='clansiege'&&PPA_SIEGE.active&&PPA_SIEGE.captureActive}
function clanSiegeUpdateCapture(){
  if(P.scene!=='clansiege'||!PPA_SIEGE.active){PPA_SIEGE.captureActive=false;return}
  const now=Date.now(),dt=Math.min(.1,Math.max(0,(now-(PPA_SIEGE.captureLastAt||now))/1000));
  PPA_SIEGE.captureLastAt=now;
  if(PPA_SIEGE.phase!=='castle'){PPA_SIEGE.captureActive=false;clanSiegeHudUpdate();return}
  const inside=clanSiegeInsideCapture();
  const standing=Math.abs(jX)<.08&&Math.abs(jY)<.08;
  const blocked=!!PPA_SIEGE.enemyInZone;
  PPA_SIEGE.captureActive=inside&&standing&&!blocked;
  if(PPA_SIEGE.captureActive){
    PPA_SIEGE.captureProgress=Math.min(CLAN_SIEGE_CAPTURE.seconds,PPA_SIEGE.captureProgress+dt);
    if(PPA_SIEGE.captureProgress>=CLAN_SIEGE_CAPTURE.seconds){
      PPA_SIEGE.phase='won';PPA_SIEGE.captureActive=false;PPA_SIEGE.winner=clanSiegeClanName();
      clanCastleGrantReward();
      updateUI();
      showPickup('🏆 ЗАМОК ЗАХВАЧЕН · '+PPA_SIEGE.winner,'#ffe37c');
      const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
      if(ln){ln.innerHTML='🏆 ЗАМОК ЗАХВАЧЕН<div class="sub">'+PPA_SIEGE.winner+' · бонус всему клану на 3 дня · можно выйти или продолжить PvP</div>';ln.classList.add('show');setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),5000)}
    }
  }
  clanSiegeHudUpdate();
}
function drawClanSiegeWorld(){
  if(P.scene!=='clansiege'||!PPA_SIEGE.active)return;
  // Respawn coordinates stay active, but their map markers are intentionally hidden.
  cx.save();
  if(PPA_SIEGE.phase==='castle'||PPA_SIEGE.phase==='won'){
    const age=Math.min(1,(Date.now()-(PPA_SIEGE.castleShownAt||Date.now()))/800);
    cx.globalAlpha=Math.max(.18,age);
    const c=CLAN_SIEGE_CASTLE,x=c.x-cam.x,y=c.y-cam.y;
    if(imgClanSiegeCastle.complete&&imgClanSiegeCastle.naturalWidth>0){
      cx.drawImage(imgClanSiegeCastle,x-c.w/2,y-c.h*.78,c.w,c.h);
    }
  }
  cx.restore();
}
function drawClanSiegeCrystal(e){
  const d=CLAN_SIEGE_CRYSTALS.find(x=>x.key===e.siegeKey)||{};
  const im=CLAN_SIEGE_CRYSTAL_IMG[e.siegeKey],sx=e.x-cam.x,sy=e.y-cam.y;
  const flash=e.flash>0;
  cx.save();
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.38)';cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+23,36,11,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  if(im&&im.complete&&im.naturalWidth>0){
    if(flash){cx.globalAlpha=.55}
    const dh=84,dw=dh*(im.naturalWidth/im.naturalHeight);
    cx.drawImage(im,sx-dw/2,sy-dh+30,dw,dh);
    cx.globalAlpha=1;
  }else{
    cx.fillStyle=e.siegeKey==='red'?'#ff4747':e.siegeKey==='blue'?'#52a9ff':e.siegeKey==='green'?'#51dd83':'#b56cff';
    cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(sx,sy-37);cx.lineTo(sx+18,sy-6);cx.lineTo(sx,sy+19);cx.lineTo(sx-18,sy-6);cx.closePath();cx.fill();
  }
  const bw=92,bh=6,ratio=Math.max(0,e.hp/e.mhp);
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.78)';cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,sy-53,bw,bh);
  cx.fillStyle=e.siegeKey==='red'?'#ff4949':e.siegeKey==='blue'?'#4da8ff':e.siegeKey==='green'?'#4adb78':'#b15fff';
  cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,sy-53,bw*ratio,bh);
  cx.strokeStyle='#ead7a0';cx.lineWidth=1;cx.strokeRect(sx-bw/2,sy-53,bw,bh);
  cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.font='bold 9px monospace';cx.fillStyle='#f3e2bc';cx.fillText(d.name||e.type.n,sx,sy-64);
  const hpTxt=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(e.hp)).toLocaleString('ru-RU')+' / '+Math.ceil(e.mhp).toLocaleString('ru-RU')+' HP';
  cx.font='bold 8px monospace';
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.82)';cx.fillText(hpTxt,sx+1,sy-41+1);
  cx.fillStyle='#ffffff';cx.fillText(hpTxt,sx,sy-41);
  cx.restore();
}

function changeScene(target){
  if(target==='clansiege'){
    const clanKey=(typeof clanSiegeLocalClanKey==='function'?clanSiegeLocalClanKey():'');
    if(!clanKey){
      showPickup('🏰 ЗАМОК ФЕНИКСА ДОСТУПЕН ТОЛЬКО УЧАСТНИКАМ КЛАНА','#ff9b72');
      return;
    }
  }
  if(target==='dungeon'){
    const lv=Math.floor(Number(P&&P.lvl)||1);
    const allowed=(DUNGEON_MODE==='1-20')
      ?(lv>=1&&lv<=20)
      :(DUNGEON_MODE==='21+')
        ?(lv>=21&&lv<=40)
        :(DUNGEON_MODE==='41-60')
          ?(lv>=41&&lv<=60)
          :false;
    if(!allowed){
      showPickup('Подземелье недоступно для текущего уровня','#ff9b72');
      return;
    }
  }
  if(target==='fartzone'&&!fartZoneLevelAllowed()){
    showPickup('Фарт Зона доступна только на уровнях 21–40','#ffb36a');
    return;
  }
  if(P.scene==='clansiege'&&target!=='clansiege')clanSiegeLeaveCleanup();
  if(P.scene==='arena'&&target!=='arena'&&typeof v238ArenaXpEndSession==='function')v238ArenaXpEndSession();
  // V225: leaving a started daily AI challenge is a forfeit.
  if(target!=='pvp1'&&P.scene==='pvp1'&&window.PPA_AI_TRAINING_ACTIVE===true&&typeof v225AiForfeit==='function'){
    v225AiForfeit('Выход из испытания',true);
  }
  // V184: AI opponent exists only in the explicit AI-training mode.
  if(target!=='pvp1')window.PPA_AI_TRAINING_ACTIVE=false;
  if(P.scene==='worldboss'&&target!=='worldboss'&&WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active&&!WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.claimed){
    worldBossFallbackReward();
  }
  const fade=document.getElementById('fade');
  fade.classList.add('on');
  transitioning=true;
  setTimeout(()=>{
    P.scene=target;
    if(target!=='dungeon')dungeonSpawnToken++;
    const sc=SCENES[target];
    EN.length=0;PR.length=0;PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0;PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0;cancelSmartAttack();worldCrystalBossResetFx();P.tid=null;if(typeof closeMobInfo==='function')closeMobInfo();
    if(target==='arena'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      if(typeof v238ArenaXpBeginSession==='function')v238ArenaXpBeginSession();
      P.x=sc.center.x;P.y=sc.center.y;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      startWave();
    }else if(target==='pvp1'){
      const dsp=PVP_DUEL_SPAWNS[PVP_LOCAL_SIDE][0];
      P.x=dsp.x;P.y=dsp.y;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      startPvpDuelWarmup();
      if(!PVP_MATCH.active&&window.PPA_AI_TRAINING_ACTIVE===true)setTimeout(function(){v174SpawnAiTrainingFighter(true);v174AiPanelVisibility()},30);
    }else if(target==='pvpteam'){
      if(PVP_MATCH.active)startPvpDuelWarmup();else stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      const ids=PVP_PREVIEW_MODE==='3x3'?[1,2,3]:[0,1,2,3,4];
      const arr=PVP_TEAM_SPAWNS[PVP_LOCAL_SIDE]||PVP_TEAM_SPAWNS.blue;
      const sp=arr[ids[Math.floor(ids.length/2)]];P.x=sp.x;P.y=sp.y;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
    }else if(target==='dungeon'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      // Enter strictly inside the round respawn room on the far left.
      placePlayerAtDungeonSpawn();
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      spawnDungeon();
    }else if(target==='clanboss1'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      P.x=CLAN_BOSS_RESPAWN.x;P.y=CLAN_BOSS_RESPAWN.y;P.hp=P.mhp;P.mp=P.mmp

      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      clanSiegePrepareEntry();
      P.shootCD=0;P.shootT=0;P.recoil=0;P.attacking=false;attackQueued=false;
      _combatAttackInputStamp=0;
      for(let _si=0;_si<4;_si++)_combatSkillInputStamp[_si]=0;
      updateSkillButtons();
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
    }else if(target==='worldboss'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      ensureWorldBossMaskPixels();
      worldCrystalBossResetFx();
      P.x=WORLD_BOSS_RESPAWN.x;P.y=WORLD_BOSS_RESPAWN.y;P.hp=P.mhp;P.mp=P.mmp;
      P.shootCD=0;P.shootT=0;P.recoil=0;P.attacking=false;attackQueued=false;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      spawnWorldCrystalBoss();
    }else if(target==='fartzone'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      P.x=sc.returnPos.x;P.y=sc.returnPos.y;P.hp=P.mhp;P.mp=P.mmp;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      FART_ZONE_STATE.ready=false;
      fartInitZone();
    }else{
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      P.x=sc.returnPos.x;P.y=sc.returnPos.y;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
    }
    P.portalCD=60;

    // Show location name
    const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
    if(target==='clanboss1'&&CLAN_BOSS_RAID&&CLAN_BOSS_RAID.bossId){
      const _locBoss=clanBossById(CLAN_BOSS_RAID.bossId);
      ln.innerHTML='КЛАНОВАЯ АРЕНА · '+(_locBoss?_locBoss.name:'БОСС')+
        '<div class="sub">1800 × 1800 · коллизия тела по тени · корректная глубина</div>';
    }else if(target==='dungeon'){
      ln.innerHTML=dungeonDisplayName()+'<div class="sub">'+dungeonDisplaySub()+'</div>';
    }else{
      ln.innerHTML=sc.name+'<div class="sub">'+sc.sub+'</div>';
    }
    ln.classList.add('show');
    setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2500);
    updateUI();
    setTimeout(()=>{
      fade.classList.remove('on');
      transitioning=false;
      // V174: update once AFTER transition unlocks. Previously updateUI() ran
      // while transitioning=true, so town-only tabs could remain hidden.
      updateUI();
      setTimeout(updateUI,80);
    },400);
  },400);
}

// Respawn after death.
// If the player died in the dungeon, respawn STRICTLY in the left circular respawn room.
// Arena deaths still return to the peaceful town.
function respawnAfterDeath(){
  const diedInDungeon=P.scene==='dungeon';
  const diedInClanBoss=P.scene==='clanboss1';
  const diedInClanSiege=P.scene==='clansiege';
  P.dead=false;
  P.hp=P.mhp;P.mp=P.mmp;
  EN.length=0;PR.length=0;PT.length=0;LOOT.length=0;P.tid=null;
  waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;

  if(diedInDungeon){
    placePlayerAtDungeonSpawn();
    spawnDungeon();
  }else if(diedInClanSiege&&PPA_SIEGE.active){
    P.scene='clansiege';
    const _sr=CLAN_SIEGE_RESPAWNS[PPA_SIEGE.respawnIndex]||CLAN_SIEGE_RESPAWNS[3];
    P.x=_sr.x;P.y=_sr.y;P.hp=P.mhp;P.mp=P.mmp;
  }else if(diedInClanBoss&&CLAN_BOSS_RAID.active&&(CLAN_BOSS_RAID.status==='fighting'||CLAN_BOSS_RAID.status==='maptest')){
    P.scene='clanboss1';P.x=CLAN_BOSS_RESPAWN.x;P.y=CLAN_BOSS_RESPAWN.y;spawnClanBoss1Entity();
  }else{
    P.scene='safe';
    P.x=SCENES.safe.returnPos.x;P.y=SCENES.safe.returnPos.y;
  }

  P.portalCD=60;
  document.getElementById('over').style.display='none';
  updateUI();
  const sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
  ln.innerHTML=sc.name+'<div class="sub">'+(diedInDungeon?'Респ-зона · круглая комната слева':(diedInClanBoss?'Клановый рейд · респ-зона':(diedInClanSiege?'Клановая осада · твоя респ-зона':sc.sub)))+'</div>';
  ln.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2500);
}

// Arena experience ladder:
// 1–20 dungeon mobs -> Phoenix -> 21–40 dungeon mobs -> Lord of Corruption.
// After the Lord, the next stronger 1–40 cycle starts.
function startWave(){
  waveNum++;
  if(waveNum>42){waveNum=1;arenaCycle++}
  waveActive=true;waveTimer=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;
  if(waveNum===21){
    arenaWaveTotal=1;spawnArenaBoss();
    const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
    ln.innerHTML='БОСС — ФЕНИКС<div class="sub">Круг '+arenaCycle+' · после уровня 20</div>';
    ln.classList.add('show');setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2200);
  }else if(waveNum===42){
    arenaWaveTotal=1;spawnArenaLordBoss();
    const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
    ln.innerHTML='БОСС — ВЛАДЫКА СКВЕРНЫ<div class="sub">Круг '+arenaCycle+' · после уровня 40</div>';
    ln.classList.add('show');setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2400);
  }else{
    const level=arenaLevelForStage(waveNum);
    arenaWaveTotal=10+((level+arenaCycle-2)%6);
    arenaFillReinforcements();
    const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
    ln.innerHTML='АРЕНА ОПЫТА · УРОВЕНЬ '+level+'<div class="sub">'+arenaWaveTotal+' мобов · максимум '+ARENA_ACTIVE_CAP+' одновременно</div>';
    ln.classList.add('show');setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),1500);
  }
}

// Dungeon population: плотные пачки по комнатам + Phoenix boss.
// Уровень каждой точки берётся из её ветки: верх нечётные, низ чётные.
const DG_SPAWN_LVL=DG_ACTIVE_SPAWNS.map(p=>DG_ROOM_LVL[p[2]]||1);

function spawnDungeon(){
  const token=++dungeonSpawnToken;
  EN.length=0;PR.length=0;LOOT.length=0;RESPAWN_Q.length=0;
  let si=0;
  function spawnBatch(){
    if(P.scene!=='dungeon'||token!==dungeonSpawnToken)return;
    const end=Math.min(si+32,DG_ACTIVE_SPAWNS.length);
    for(;si<end;si++)spawnMobAtPoint(si,false);
    if(si<DG_ACTIVE_SPAWNS.length){
      setTimeout(spawnBatch,12);
    }else if(DUNGEON_MODE==='1-20'&&Date.now()>=BOSS_READY_AT){
      spawnBoss();
    }else if(DUNGEON_MODE==='21+'){
      spawnDungeon21Boss();
    }else if(DUNGEON_MODE==='41-60'){
      // V270: boss intentionally not added yet.
    }
  }
  // Let the map/camera/player render first, then populate the rooms progressively.
  setTimeout(spawnBatch,0);
}

// Place a single mob near a room centre (always succeeds — falls back to nearest walkable tile)
function spawnMobInRoom(cx0,cy0,lv){
  const base=lv||1;
  const t=mobByLvl(base+(Math.random()<0.35?(Math.random()<0.5?-1:1):0));
  let px=null,py=null;
  for(let tr=0;tr<40;tr++){
    const r=40+Math.random()*260,a=Math.random()*Math.PI*2;
    const mx=cx0+Math.cos(a)*r,my=cy0+Math.sin(a)*r;
    if(dgCircleWalk(mx,my,20)){px=mx;py=my;break}
  }
  if(px===null){const p=dgNearestWalk(cx0,cy0);px=p.x;py=p.y}
  const e=mkMob(t,px,py);
  EN.push(e);
  return e;
}

// Boss — PHOENIX, guardian of the fire hall
function spawnBoss(){
  if(EN.some(e=>e.isBoss))return;
  const bp=dgNearestWalk(DG_BOSS_IMG[0]*DG_SCALE,DG_BOSS_IMG[1]*DG_SCALE);
  const _now=Date.now();
  EN.push({id:eid++,type:{n:'ФЕНИКС'},x:bp.x,y:bp.y,hx:bp.x,hy:bp.y,
    hp:2613,mhp:2613,mp:160,mmp:160,sp:0,sz:150,col:'#ff5522',dmg:85,dmgType:'physical',def:79,xp:350,gold:250,lvl:20,
    atkCD:0,flash:0,bob:0,isBoss:true,isDungeonPhoenixBoss:true,fireCD:150,face:1,spit:0,
    phoenixAoeNextAt:_now+9000+Math.random()*4000,
    phoenixAoePending:false,phoenixAoeImpactAt:0,phoenixAoeFxUntil:0});
}

const PHOENIX_DUNGEON_AOE_RADIUS=205;
const PHOENIX_DUNGEON_AOE_DMG=36;
function phoenixDungeonAoeUpdate(e,d){
  if(!e||!e.isDungeonPhoenixBoss)return;
  const now=Date.now();
  if(!e.phoenixAoePending&&now>=e.phoenixAoeNextAt){
    e.phoenixAoePending=true;
    e.phoenixAoeImpactAt=now+700;
    e.phoenixAoeFxUntil=now+1050;
    e.phoenixAoeNextAt=now+10000+Math.random()*4000;
  }
  if(e.phoenixAoePending&&now>=e.phoenixAoeImpactAt){
    e.phoenixAoePending=false;
    if(d<=PHOENIX_DUNGEON_AOE_RADIUS){
      if(Math.random()*100<effectivePlayerDodge()){
        showPickup('Уворот от огненного AOE!','#88ffcc');
      }else{
        const _aoe=playerDmg(PHOENIX_DUNGEON_AOE_DMG,'magic');
        P.hp-=_aoe;
        showPickup('Огненный AOE · −'+_aoe,'#ff9b45');
      }
    }
    // Tiny particle burst only; no filters / no heavy sprite effect.
    for(let j=0;j<10;j++)PT.push({
      x:e.x+(Math.random()-.5)*PHOENIX_DUNGEON_AOE_RADIUS*1.5,
      y:e.y+(Math.random()-.5)*PHOENIX_DUNGEON_AOE_RADIUS*.55,
      vx:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,vy:-Math.random()*1.6,
      life:16,ml:16,sz:2+Math.random()*2,
      col:Math.random()<.5?'#ff6a24':'#ffc044'
    });
  }
}
function drawPhoenixDungeonAoe(e,sx,sy){
  if(!e||!e.isDungeonPhoenixBoss||!e.phoenixAoeFxUntil)return;
  const now=Date.now();
  if(now>=e.phoenixAoeFxUntil)return;
  const pending=!!e.phoenixAoePending;
  cx.save();
  cx.lineWidth=3;
  cx.strokeStyle

===== shootT #11 @95315972 =====
lWidth>0){
    if(flash){cx.globalAlpha=.55}
    const dh=84,dw=dh*(im.naturalWidth/im.naturalHeight);
    cx.drawImage(im,sx-dw/2,sy-dh+30,dw,dh);
    cx.globalAlpha=1;
  }else{
    cx.fillStyle=e.siegeKey==='red'?'#ff4747':e.siegeKey==='blue'?'#52a9ff':e.siegeKey==='green'?'#51dd83':'#b56cff';
    cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(sx,sy-37);cx.lineTo(sx+18,sy-6);cx.lineTo(sx,sy+19);cx.lineTo(sx-18,sy-6);cx.closePath();cx.fill();
  }
  const bw=92,bh=6,ratio=Math.max(0,e.hp/e.mhp);
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.78)';cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,sy-53,bw,bh);
  cx.fillStyle=e.siegeKey==='red'?'#ff4949':e.siegeKey==='blue'?'#4da8ff':e.siegeKey==='green'?'#4adb78':'#b15fff';
  cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,sy-53,bw*ratio,bh);
  cx.strokeStyle='#ead7a0';cx.lineWidth=1;cx.strokeRect(sx-bw/2,sy-53,bw,bh);
  cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.font='bold 9px monospace';cx.fillStyle='#f3e2bc';cx.fillText(d.name||e.type.n,sx,sy-64);
  const hpTxt=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(e.hp)).toLocaleString('ru-RU')+' / '+Math.ceil(e.mhp).toLocaleString('ru-RU')+' HP';
  cx.font='bold 8px monospace';
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.82)';cx.fillText(hpTxt,sx+1,sy-41+1);
  cx.fillStyle='#ffffff';cx.fillText(hpTxt,sx,sy-41);
  cx.restore();
}

function changeScene(target){
  if(target==='clansiege'){
    const clanKey=(typeof clanSiegeLocalClanKey==='function'?clanSiegeLocalClanKey():'');
    if(!clanKey){
      showPickup('🏰 ЗАМОК ФЕНИКСА ДОСТУПЕН ТОЛЬКО УЧАСТНИКАМ КЛАНА','#ff9b72');
      return;
    }
  }
  if(target==='dungeon'){
    const lv=Math.floor(Number(P&&P.lvl)||1);
    const allowed=(DUNGEON_MODE==='1-20')
      ?(lv>=1&&lv<=20)
      :(DUNGEON_MODE==='21+')
        ?(lv>=21&&lv<=40)
        :(DUNGEON_MODE==='41-60')
          ?(lv>=41&&lv<=60)
          :false;
    if(!allowed){
      showPickup('Подземелье недоступно для текущего уровня','#ff9b72');
      return;
    }
  }
  if(target==='fartzone'&&!fartZoneLevelAllowed()){
    showPickup('Фарт Зона доступна только на уровнях 21–40','#ffb36a');
    return;
  }
  if(P.scene==='clansiege'&&target!=='clansiege')clanSiegeLeaveCleanup();
  if(P.scene==='arena'&&target!=='arena'&&typeof v238ArenaXpEndSession==='function')v238ArenaXpEndSession();
  // V225: leaving a started daily AI challenge is a forfeit.
  if(target!=='pvp1'&&P.scene==='pvp1'&&window.PPA_AI_TRAINING_ACTIVE===true&&typeof v225AiForfeit==='function'){
    v225AiForfeit('Выход из испытания',true);
  }
  // V184: AI opponent exists only in the explicit AI-training mode.
  if(target!=='pvp1')window.PPA_AI_TRAINING_ACTIVE=false;
  if(P.scene==='worldboss'&&target!=='worldboss'&&WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active&&!WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.claimed){
    worldBossFallbackReward();
  }
  const fade=document.getElementById('fade');
  fade.classList.add('on');
  transitioning=true;
  setTimeout(()=>{
    P.scene=target;
    if(target!=='dungeon')dungeonSpawnToken++;
    const sc=SCENES[target];
    EN.length=0;PR.length=0;PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0;PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0;cancelSmartAttack();worldCrystalBossResetFx();P.tid=null;if(typeof closeMobInfo==='function')closeMobInfo();
    if(target==='arena'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      if(typeof v238ArenaXpBeginSession==='function')v238ArenaXpBeginSession();
      P.x=sc.center.x;P.y=sc.center.y;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      startWave();
    }else if(target==='pvp1'){
      const dsp=PVP_DUEL_SPAWNS[PVP_LOCAL_SIDE][0];
      P.x=dsp.x;P.y=dsp.y;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      startPvpDuelWarmup();
      if(!PVP_MATCH.active&&window.PPA_AI_TRAINING_ACTIVE===true)setTimeout(function(){v174SpawnAiTrainingFighter(true);v174AiPanelVisibility()},30);
    }else if(target==='pvpteam'){
      if(PVP_MATCH.active)startPvpDuelWarmup();else stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      const ids=PVP_PREVIEW_MODE==='3x3'?[1,2,3]:[0,1,2,3,4];
      const arr=PVP_TEAM_SPAWNS[PVP_LOCAL_SIDE]||PVP_TEAM_SPAWNS.blue;
      const sp=arr[ids[Math.floor(ids.length/2)]];P.x=sp.x;P.y=sp.y;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
    }else if(target==='dungeon'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      // Enter strictly inside the round respawn room on the far left.
      placePlayerAtDungeonSpawn();
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      spawnDungeon();
    }else if(target==='clanboss1'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      P.x=CLAN_BOSS_RESPAWN.x;P.y=CLAN_BOSS_RESPAWN.y;P.hp=P.mhp;P.mp=P.mmp;
      P.shootCD=0;P.shootT=0;P.recoil=0;P.attacking=false;attackQueued=false;
      _combatAttackInputStamp=0;
      for(let _si=0;_si<4;_si++)_combatSkillInputStamp[_si]=0;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;spawnClanBoss1Entity();
    }else if(target==='clansiege'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      clanSiegePrepareEntry();
      P.shootCD=0;P.shootT=0;P.recoil=0;P.attacking=false;attackQueued=false;
      _combatAttackInputStamp=0;
      for(let _si=0;_si<4;_si++)_combatSkillInputStamp[_si]=0;
      updateSkillButtons();
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
    }else if(target==='worldboss'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      ensureWorldBossMaskPixels();
      worldCrystalBossResetFx();
      P.x=WORLD_BOSS_RESPAWN.x;P.y=WORLD_BOSS_RESPAWN.y;P.hp=P.mhp;P.mp=P.mmp;
      P.shootCD=0;P.shootT=0;P.recoil=0;P.attacking=false;attackQueued=false;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      spawnWorldCrystalBoss();
    }else if(target==='fartzone'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      P.x=sc.returnPos.x;P.y=sc.returnPos.y;P.hp=P.mhp;P.mp=P.mmp;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      FART_ZONE_STATE.ready=false;
      fartInitZone();
    }else{
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      P.x=sc.returnPos.x;P.y=sc.returnPos.y;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
    }
    P.portalCD=60;

    // Show location name
    const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
    if(target==='clanboss1'&&CLAN_BOSS_RAID&&CLAN_BOSS_RAID.bossId){
      const _locBoss=clanBossById(CLAN_BOSS_RAID.bossId);
      ln.innerHTML='КЛАНОВАЯ АРЕНА · '+(_locBoss?_locBoss.name:'БОСС')+
        '<div class="sub">1800 × 1800 · коллизия тела по тени · корректная глубина</div>';
    }else if(target==='dungeon'){
      ln.innerHTML=dungeonDisplayName()+'<div class="sub">'+dungeonDisplaySub()+'</div>';
    }else{
      ln.innerHTML=sc.name+'<div class="sub">'+sc.sub+'</div>';
    }
    ln.classList.add('show');
    setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2500);
    updateUI();
    setTimeout(()=>{
      fade.classList.remove('on');
      transitioning=false;
      // V174: update once AFTER transition unlocks. Previously updateUI() ran
      // while transitioning=true, so town-only tabs could remain hidden.
      updateUI();
      setTimeout(updateUI,80);
    },400);
  },400);
}

// 1–20 dungeon mobs -> Phoenix -> 21–40 dungeon mobs -> Lord of Corruption.
// After the Lord, the next stronger 1–40 cycle starts.
function startWave(){
  waveNum++;
  if(waveNum>42){waveNum=1;arenaCycle++}
  waveActive=true;waveTimer=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;
  if(waveNum===21){
    arenaWaveTotal=1;spawnArenaBoss();
    const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
    ln.innerHTML='БОСС — ФЕНИКС<div class="sub">Круг '+arenaCycle+' · после уровня 20</div>';
    ln.classList.add('show');setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2200);
  }else if(waveNum===42){
    arenaWaveTotal=1;spawnArenaLordBoss();
    const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
    ln.innerHTML='БОСС — ВЛАДЫКА СКВЕРНЫ<div class="sub">Круг '+arenaCycle+' · после уровня 40</div>';
    ln.classList.add('show');setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2400);
  }else{
    const level=arenaLevelForStage(waveNum);
    arenaWaveTotal=10+((level+arenaCycle-2)%6);
    arenaFillReinforcements();
    const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
    ln.innerHTML='АРЕНА ОПЫТА · УРОВЕНЬ '+level+'<div class="sub">'+arenaWaveTotal+' мобов · максимум '+ARENA_ACTIVE_CAP+' одновременно</div>';
    ln.classList.add('show');setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),1500);
  }
}

// Dungeon population: плотные пачки по комнатам + Phoenix boss.
// Уровень каждой точки берётся из её ветки: верх нечётные, низ чётные.
const DG_SPAWN_LVL=DG_ACTIVE_SPAWNS.map(p=>DG_ROOM_LVL[p[2]]||1);

function spawnDungeon(){
  const token=++dungeonSpawnToken;
  EN.length=0;PR.length=0;LOOT.length=0;RESPAWN_Q.length=0;
  let si=0;
  function spawnBatch(){
    if(P.scene!=='dungeon'||token!==dungeonSpawnToken)return;
    const end=Math.min(si+32,DG_ACTIVE_SPAWNS.length);
    for(;si<end;si++)spawnMobAtPoint(si,false);
    if(si<DG_ACTIVE_SPAWNS.length){
      setTimeout(spawnBatch,12);
    }else if(DUNGEON_MODE==='1-20'&&Date.now()>=BOSS_READY_AT){
      spawnBoss();
    }else if(DUNGEON_MODE==='21+'){
      spawnDungeon21Boss();
    }else if(DUNGEON_MODE==='41-60'){
      // V270: boss intentionally not added yet.
    }
  }
  // Let the map/camera/player render first, then populate the rooms progressively.
  setTimeout(spawnBatch,0);
}

// Place a single mob near a room centre (always succeeds — falls back to nearest walkable tile)
function spawnMobInRoom(cx0,cy0,lv){
  const base=lv||1;
  const t=mobByLvl(base+(Math.random()<0.35?(Math.random()<0.5?-1:1):0));
  let px=null,py=null;
  for(let tr=0;tr<40;tr++){
    const r=40+Math.random()*260,a=Math.random()*Math.PI*2;
    const mx=cx0+Math.cos(a)*r,my=cy0+Math.sin(a)*r;
    if(dgCircleWalk(mx,my,20)){px=mx;py=my;break}
  }
  if(px===null){const p=dgNearestWalk(cx0,cy0);px=p.x;py=p.y}
  const e=mkMob(t,px,py);
  EN.push(e);
  return e;
}

// Boss — PHOENIX, guardian of the fire hall
function spawnBoss(){
  if(EN.some(e=>e.isBoss))return;
  const bp=dgNearestWalk(DG_BOSS_IMG[0]*DG_SCALE,DG_BOSS_IMG[1]*DG_SCALE);
  const _now=Date.now();
  EN.push({id:eid++,type:{n:'ФЕНИКС'},x:bp.x,y:bp.y,hx:bp.x,hy:bp.y,
    hp:2613,mhp:2613,mp:160,mmp:160,sp:0,sz:150,col:'#ff5522',dmg:85,dmgType:'physical',def:79,xp:350,gold:250,lvl:20,
    atkCD:0,flash:0,bob:0,isBoss:true,isDungeonPhoenixBoss:true,fireCD:150,face:1,spit:0,
    phoenixAoeNextAt:_now+9000+Math.random()*4000,
    phoenixAoePending:false,phoenixAoeImpactAt:0,phoenixAoeFxUntil:0});
}

const PHOENIX_DUNGEON_AOE_RADIUS=205;
const PHOENIX_DUNGEON_AOE_DMG=36;
function phoenixDungeonAoeUpdate(e,d){
  if(!e||!e.isDungeonPhoenixBoss)return;
  const now=Date.now();
  if(!e.phoenixAoePending&&now>=e.phoenixAoeNextAt){
    e.phoenixAoePending=true;
    e.phoenixAoeImpactAt=now+700;
    e.phoenixAoeFxUntil=now+1050;
    e.phoenixAoeNextAt=now+10000+Math.random()*4000;
  }
  if(e.phoenixAoePending&&now>=e.phoenixAoeImpactAt){
    e.phoenixAoePending=false;
    if(d<=PHOENIX_DUNGEON_AOE_RADIUS){
      if(Math.random()*100<effectivePlayerDodge()){
        showPickup('Уворот от огненного AOE!','#88ffcc');
      }else{
        const _aoe=playerDmg(PHOENIX_DUNGEON_AOE_DMG,'magic');
        P.hp-=_aoe;
        showPickup('Огненный AOE · −'+_aoe,'#ff9b45');
      }
    }
    // Tiny particle burst only; no filters / no heavy sprite effect.
    for(let j=0;j<10;j++)PT.push({
      x:e.x+(Math.random()-.5)*PHOENIX_DUNGEON_AOE_RADIUS*1.5,
      y:e.y+(Math.random()-.5)*PHOENIX_DUNGEON_AOE_RADIUS*.55,
      vx:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,vy:-Math.random()*1.6,
      life:16,ml:16,sz:2+Math.random()*2,
      col:Math.random()<.5?'#ff6a24':'#ffc044'
    });
  }
}
function drawPhoenixDungeonAoe(e,sx,sy){
  if(!e||!e.isDungeonPhoenixBoss||!e.phoenixAoeFxUntil)return;
  const now=Date.now();
  if(now>=e.phoenixAoeFxUntil)return;
  const pending=!!e.phoenixAoePending;
  cx.save();
  cx.lineWidth=3;
  cx.strokeStyle=pending?'rgba(255,120,45,.90)':'rgba(255,175,60,.68)';
  cx.fillStyle=pending?'rgba(255,80,20,.08)':'rgba(255,110,25,.14)';
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(sx,sy+20,PHOENIX_DUNGEON_AOE_RADIUS,0,Math.PI*2);
  cx.fill();cx.stroke();
  cx.restore();
}

// Возрождение моба ровно на его собственной точке спавна
function spawnMobAtPoint(si,fx){


===== shootT #12 @95316497 =====
x-bw/2,sy-53,bw,bh);
  cx.fillStyle=e.siegeKey==='red'?'#ff4949':e.siegeKey==='blue'?'#4da8ff':e.siegeKey==='green'?'#4adb78':'#b15fff';
  cx.fillRect(sx-bw/2,sy-53,bw*ratio,bh);
  cx.strokeStyle='#ead7a0';cx.lineWidth=1;cx.strokeRect(sx-bw/2,sy-53,bw,bh);
  cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.font='bold 9px monospace';cx.fillStyle='#f3e2bc';cx.fillText(d.name||e.type.n,sx,sy-64);
  const hpTxt=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(e.hp)).toLocaleString('ru-RU')+' / '+Math.ceil(e.mhp).toLocaleString('ru-RU')+' HP';
  cx.font='bold 8px monospace';
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.82)';cx.fillText(hpTxt,sx+1,sy-41+1);
  cx.fillStyle='#ffffff';cx.fillText(hpTxt,sx,sy-41);
  cx.restore();
}

function changeScene(target){
  if(target==='clansiege'){
    const clanKey=(typeof clanSiegeLocalClanKey==='function'?clanSiegeLocalClanKey():'');
    if(!clanKey){
      showPickup('🏰 ЗАМОК ФЕНИКСА ДОСТУПЕН ТОЛЬКО УЧАСТНИКАМ КЛАНА','#ff9b72');
      return;
    }
  }
  if(target==='dungeon'){
    const lv=Math.floor(Number(P&&P.lvl)||1);
    const allowed=(DUNGEON_MODE==='1-20')
      ?(lv>=1&&lv<=20)
      :(DUNGEON_MODE==='21+')
        ?(lv>=21&&lv<=40)
        :(DUNGEON_MODE==='41-60')
          ?(lv>=41&&lv<=60)
          :false;
    if(!allowed){
      showPickup('Подземелье недоступно для текущего уровня','#ff9b72');
      return;
    }
  }
  if(target==='fartzone'&&!fartZoneLevelAllowed()){
    showPickup('Фарт Зона доступна только на уровнях 21–40','#ffb36a');
    return;
  }
  if(P.scene==='clansiege'&&target!=='clansiege')clanSiegeLeaveCleanup();
  if(P.scene==='arena'&&target!=='arena'&&typeof v238ArenaXpEndSession==='function')v238ArenaXpEndSession();
  // V225: leaving a started daily AI challenge is a forfeit.
  if(target!=='pvp1'&&P.scene==='pvp1'&&window.PPA_AI_TRAINING_ACTIVE===true&&typeof v225AiForfeit==='function'){
    v225AiForfeit('Выход из испытания',true);
  }
  // V184: AI opponent exists only in the explicit AI-training mode.
  if(target!=='pvp1')window.PPA_AI_TRAINING_ACTIVE=false;
  if(P.scene==='worldboss'&&target!=='worldboss'&&WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active&&!WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.claimed){
    worldBossFallbackReward();
  }
  const fade=document.getElementById('fade');
  fade.classList.add('on');
  transitioning=true;
  setTimeout(()=>{
    P.scene=target;
    if(target!=='dungeon')dungeonSpawnToken++;
    const sc=SCENES[target];
    EN.length=0;PR.length=0;PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0;PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0;cancelSmartAttack();worldCrystalBossResetFx();P.tid=null;if(typeof closeMobInfo==='function')closeMobInfo();
    if(target==='arena'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      if(typeof v238ArenaXpBeginSession==='function')v238ArenaXpBeginSession();
      P.x=sc.center.x;P.y=sc.center.y;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      startWave();
    }else if(target==='pvp1'){
      const dsp=PVP_DUEL_SPAWNS[PVP_LOCAL_SIDE][0];
      P.x=dsp.x;P.y=dsp.y;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      startPvpDuelWarmup();
      if(!PVP_MATCH.active&&window.PPA_AI_TRAINING_ACTIVE===true)setTimeout(function(){v174SpawnAiTrainingFighter(true);v174AiPanelVisibility()},30);
    }else if(target==='pvpteam'){
      if(PVP_MATCH.active)startPvpDuelWarmup();else stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      const ids=PVP_PREVIEW_MODE==='3x3'?[1,2,3]:[0,1,2,3,4];
      const arr=PVP_TEAM_SPAWNS[PVP_LOCAL_SIDE]||PVP_TEAM_SPAWNS.blue;
      const sp=arr[ids[Math.floor(ids.length/2)]];P.x=sp.x;P.y=sp.y;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
    }else if(target==='dungeon'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      // Enter strictly inside the round respawn room on the far left.
      placePlayerAtDungeonSpawn();
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      spawnDungeon();
    }else if(target==='clanboss1'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      P.x=CLAN_BOSS_RESPAWN.x;P.y=CLAN_BOSS_RESPAWN.y;P.hp=P.mhp;P.mp=P.mmp;
      P.shootCD=0;P.shootT=0;P.recoil=0;P.attacking=false;attackQueued=false;
      _combatAttackInputStamp=0;
      for(let _si=0;_si<4;_si++)_combatSkillInputStamp[_si]=0;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;spawnClanBoss1Entity();
    }else if(target==='clansiege'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      clanSiegePrepareEntry();
      P.shootCD=0;P.shootT=0;P.recoil=0;P.attacking=false;attackQueued=false;
      _combatAttackInputStamp=0;
      for(let _si=0;_si<4;_si++)_combatSkillInputStamp[_si]=0;
      updateSkillButtons();
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
    }else if(target==='worldboss'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      ensureWorldBossMaskPixels();
      worldCrystalBossResetFx();
      P.x=WORLD_BOSS_RESPAWN.x;P.y=WORLD_BOSS_RESPAWN.y;P.hp=P.mhp;P.mp=P.mmp;
      P.shootCD=0;P.shootT=0;P.recoil=0;P.attacking=false;attackQueued=false;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      spawnWorldCrystalBoss();
    }else if(target==='fartzone'){
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      P.x=sc.returnPos.x;P.y=sc.returnPos.y;P.hp=P.mhp;P.mp=P.mmp;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
      FART_ZONE_STATE.ready=false;
      fartInitZone();
    }else{
      stopPvpDuelWarmup();
      P.x=sc.returnPos.x;P.y=sc.returnPos.y;
      waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;
    }
    P.portalCD=60;

    // Show location name
    const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
    if(target==='clanboss1'&&CLAN_BOSS_RAID&&CLAN_BOSS_RAID.bossId){
      const _locBoss=clanBossById(CLAN_BOSS_RAID.bossId);
      ln.innerHTML='КЛАНОВАЯ АРЕНА · '+(_locBoss?_locBoss.name:'БОСС')+
        '<div class="sub">1800 × 1800 · коллизия тела по тени · корректная глубина</div>';
    }else if(target==='dungeon'){
      ln.innerHTML=dungeonDisplayName()+'<div class="sub">'+dungeonDisplaySub()+'</div>';
    }else{
      ln.innerHTML=sc.name+'<div class="sub">'+sc.sub+'</div>';
    }
    ln.classList.add('show');
    setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2500);
    updateUI();
    setTimeout(()=>{
      fade.classList.remove('on');
      transitioning=false;
      // V174: update once AFTER transition unlocks. Previously updateUI() ran
      // while transitioning=true, so town-only tabs could remain hidden.
      updateUI();
      setTimeout(updateUI,80);
    },400);
  },400);
}

// Respawn after death.
// If the player died in the dungeon, respawn STRICTLY in the left circular respawn room.
// Arena deaths still return to the peaceful town.
function respawnAfterDeath(){
  const diedInDungeon=P.scene==='dungeon';
  const diedInClanBoss=P.scene==='clanboss1';
  const diedInClanSiege=P.scene==='clansiege';
  P.dead=false;
  P.hp=P.mhp;P.mp=P.mmp;
  EN.length=0;PR.length=0;PT.length=0;LOOT.length=0;P.tid=null;
  waveNum=0;waveActive=false;waveTimer=0;arenaCycle=1;arenaWaveTotal=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;

  if(diedInDungeon){
    placePlayerAtDungeonSpawn();
    spawnDungeon();
  }else if(diedInClanSiege&&PPA_SIEGE.active){
    P.scene='clansiege';
    const _sr=CLAN_SIEGE_RESPAWNS[PPA_SIEGE.respawnIndex]||CLAN_SIEGE_RESPAWNS[3];
    P.x=_sr.x;P.y=_sr.y;P.hp=P.mhp;P.mp=P.mmp;
  }else if(diedInClanBoss&&CLAN_BOSS_RAID.active&&(CLAN_BOSS_RAID.status==='fighting'||CLAN_BOSS_RAID.status==='maptest')){
    P.scene='clanboss1';P.x=CLAN_BOSS_RESPAWN.x;P.y=CLAN_BOSS_RESPAWN.y;spawnClanBoss1Entity();
  }else{
    P.scene='safe';
    P.x=SCENES.safe.returnPos.x;P.y=SCENES.safe.returnPos.y;
  }

  P.portalCD=60;
  document.getElementById('over').style.display='none';
  updateUI();
  const sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
  ln.innerHTML=sc.name+'<div class="sub">'+(diedInDungeon?'Респ-зона · круглая комната слева':(diedInClanBoss?'Клановый рейд · респ-зона':(diedInClanSiege?'Клановая осада · твоя респ-зона':sc.sub)))+'</div>';
  ln.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2500);
}

// Arena experience ladder:
// 1–20 dungeon mobs -> Phoenix -> 21–40 dungeon mobs -> Lord of Corruption.
// After the Lord, the next stronger 1–40 cycle starts.
function startWave(){
  waveNum++;
  if(waveNum>42){waveNum=1;arenaCycle++}
  waveActive=true;waveTimer=0;arenaWaveKilled=0;arenaWaveSpawned=0;
  if(waveNum===21){
    arenaWaveTotal=1;spawnArenaBoss();
    const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
    ln.innerHTML='БОСС — ФЕНИКС<div class="sub">Круг '+arenaCycle+' · после уровня 20</div>';
    ln.classList.add('show');setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2200);
  }else if(waveNum===42){
    arenaWaveTotal=1;spawnArenaLordBoss();
    const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
    ln.innerHTML='БОСС — ВЛАДЫКА СКВЕРНЫ<div class="sub">Круг '+arenaCycle+' · после уровня 40</div>';
    ln.classList.add('show');setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2400);
  }else{
    const level=arenaLevelForStage(waveNum);
    arenaWaveTotal=10+((level+arenaCycle-2)%6);
    arenaFillReinforcements();
    const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
    ln.innerHTML='АРЕНА ОПЫТА · УРОВЕНЬ '+level+'<div class="sub">'+arenaWaveTotal+' мобов · максимум '+ARENA_ACTIVE_CAP+' одновременно</div>';
    ln.classList.add('show');setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),1500);
  }
}

      x:e.x+(Math.random()-.5)*PHOENIX_DUNGEON_AOE_RADIUS*1.5,
      y:e.y+(Math.random()-.5)*PHOENIX_DUNGEON_AOE_RADIUS*.55,
      vx:(Math.random()-.5)*1.8,vy:-Math.random()*1.6,
      life:16,ml:16,sz:2+Math.random()*2,
      col:Math.random()<.5?'#ff6a24':'#ffc044'
    });
  }
}
function drawPhoenixDungeonAoe(e,sx,sy){
  if(!e||!e.isDungeonPhoenixBoss||!e.phoenixAoeFxUntil)return;
  const now=Date.now();
  if(now>=e.phoenixAoeFxUntil)return;
  const pending=!!e.phoenixAoePending;
  cx.save();
  cx.lineWidth=3;
  cx.strokeStyle=pending?'rgba(255,120,45,.90)':'rgba(255,175,60,.68)';
  cx.fillStyle=pending?'rgba(255,80,20,.08)':'rgba(255,110,25,.14)';
  cx.beginPath();
  cx.arc(sx,sy+20,PHOENIX_DUNGEON_AOE_RADIUS,0,Math.PI*2);
  cx.fill();cx.stroke();
  cx.restore();
}

// Возрождение моба ровно на его собственной точке спавна
function spawnMobAtPoint(si,fx){
  const p=DG_ACTIVE_SPAWNS[si];if(!p)return;
  let px=p[0]*DG_SCALE,py=p[1]*DG_SCALE;
  if(!dgCircleWalk(px,py,18)){const q=dgNearestWalk(px,py);px=q.x;py=q.y}
  const t=mobByLvl(DG_SPAWN_LVL[si]);
  let e=mkMob(t,px,py);
  if(DUNGEON_MODE==='21+')e=applyDungeon21MobStats(e,si);
  else if(DUNGEON_MODE==='41-60')e=applyDungeon41MobStats(e,si);
  e.si=si;               // точка спавна (дом моба)
  e.room=p[2];           // номер комнаты
  e.roomLevel=DG_ROOM_LVL[p[2]]||e.lvl;
  e.hx=px;e.hy=py;       // возврат на место п
