const DUNGEON_TELEPORT_ROUTES=[
  {label:'1–2',ix:184,iy:423},
  {label:'3–4',ix:310,iy:423},
  {label:'5–6',ix:435,iy:423},
  {label:'7–8',ix:585,iy:423},
  {label:'9–10',ix:715,iy:423},
  {label:'11–12',ix:836,iy:423},
  {label:'13–14',ix:979,iy:423},
  {label:'15–16',ix:1125,iy:423},
  {label:'17–18',ix:1268,iy:423},
  {label:'19–20',ix:1426,iy:423}
];
let DUNGEON_TELEPORT_PENDING=null;

function dungeonTeleportRouteWorld(i){
  const r=DUNGEON_TELEPORT_ROUTES[i];
  if(!r)return null;
  return dgNearestWalk(r.ix*DG_SCALE,r.iy*DG_SCALE);
}
function dungeonTeleportInCombat(){
  if(P.scene!=='dungeon')return false;
  const lim=900*900;
  for(let i=0;i<EN.length;i++){
    const e=EN[i];
    if(!e||e.hp<=0||!e.aggro)continue;
    const dx=e.x-P.x,dy=e.y-P.y;
    if(dx*dx+dy*dy<lim)return true;
  }
  return false;
}
function closeDungeonTeleportMenu(){
  const m=document.getElementById('dungeonTeleportMenu');
  if(m)m.classList.remove('on');
}
function renderDungeonTeleportMenu(){
  const grid=document.getElementById('dungeonTeleportRoutes');
  const status=document.getElementById('dungeonTeleportStatus');
  if(!grid||!status)return;
  const unlocked=Math.max(0,Math.min(10,Math.floor(Number(INV.dungeonTeleportUnlocked)||0)));
  const count=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(INV.consumables.portalStone)||0));
  status.textContent='Свитков: '+count+' · Открыто маршрутов: '+unlocked+'/10';
  grid.innerHTML='';
  DUNGEON_TELEPORT_ROUTES.forEach(function(r,i){
    const open=i<unlocked;
    const b=document.createElement('button');
    b.className='dungeonTpBtn'+(open?'':' locked');
    b.disabled=!open;
    b.innerHTML=(open?'КОРИДОР '+r.label:'🔒 КОРИДОР '+r.label)+'<small>'+(open?'Телепортировать':'Сначала пройди этот маршрут')+'</small>';
    if(open)b.addEventListener('click',function(){useDungeonTeleportRoute(i)});
    grid.appendChild(b);
  });
}
function openDungeonTeleportMenu(){
  if(transitioning||P.dead)return;
  if(P.scene!=='safe'&&P.scene!=='dungeon'){showPickup('Свиток работает только в городе и подземелье','#ffb36b');return}
  if((INV.consumables.portalStone||0)<=0){showPickup('Нет свитков телепорта','#ff7777');return}
  if(dungeonTeleportInCombat()){showPickup('Нельзя телепортироваться во время боя','#ff7777');return}
  if(INV.open)closeInventory();
  const icon=document.getElementById('dungeonTeleportIcon');if(icon)icon.src=PPA_TELEPORT_SCROLL_IMG;
  renderDungeonTeleportMenu();
  document.getElementById('dungeonTeleportMenu').classList.add('on');
  jA=false;jX=0;jY=0;
}
function useDungeonTeleportRoute(i){
  i=Math.floor(Number(i));
  const unlocked=Math.max(0,Math.min(10,Math.floor(Number(INV.dungeonTeleportUnlocked)||0)));
  if(i<0||i>=DUNGEON_TELEPORT_ROUTES.length||i>=unlocked){showPickup('Этот маршрут ещё не открыт','#ff7777');return}
  if((INV.consumables.portalStone||0)<=0){showPickup('Нет свитков телепорта','#ff7777');closeDungeonTeleportMenu();return}
  if(transitioning||P.dead)return;
  if(P.scene!=='safe'&&P.scene!=='dungeon'){showPickup('Здесь телепорт недоступен','#ff7777');closeDungeonTeleportMenu();return}
  if(dungeonTeleportInCombat()){showPickup('Нельзя телепортироваться во время боя','#ff7777');closeDungeonTeleportMenu();return}
  INV.consumables.portalStone--;
  P.tid=null;P.attacking=false;P.shootT=0;P.recoil=0;PR.length=0;
  closeDungeonTeleportMenu();
  const label=DUNGEON_TELEPORT_ROUTES[i].label;
  if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
    const sp=dungeonTeleportRouteWorld(i);
    if(sp){
      const fade=document.getElementById('fade');
      transitioning=true;fade.classList.add('on');
      setTimeout(function(){
        P.x=sp.x;P.y=sp.y;P.vx=0;P.vy=0;P.portalCD=60;
        fade.classList.remove('on');transitioning=false;updateUI();
        showPickup('📜 Телепорт · коридор '+label,'#8fd3ff');
      },220);
    }
  }else{
    DUNGEON_TELEPORT_PENDING=i;
    changeScene('dungeon');
    setTimeout(function(){showPickup('📜 Телепорт · коридор '+label,'#8fd3ff')},900);
  }
  saveGame();sendInvState();updateConsumableHud(true);
}
function checkDungeonTeleportDiscovery(){
  if(P.scene!=='dungeon'||transitioning||P.dead||typeof INV==='undefined')return;
  let unlocked=Math.max(0,Math.min(10,Math.floor(Number(INV.dungeonTeleportUnlocked)||0)));
  if(unlocked>=DUNGEON_TELEPORT_ROUTES.length)return;
  // Unlock only after physically reaching the corresponding main-corridor waypoint.
  let gained=unlocked;
  for(let i=unlocked;i<DUNGEON_TELEPORT_ROUTES.length;i++){
    const sp=dungeonTeleportRouteWorld(i);if(!sp)break;
    const dx=P.x-sp.x,dy=P.y-sp.y;
    const rr=46*DG_SCALE;
    if(dx*dx+dy*dy<=rr*rr)gained=i+1;
    else break;
  }
  if(gained>unlocked){
    INV.dungeonTeleportUnlocked=gained;
    saveGame();
    showPickup('Открыт маршрут телепорта: '+DUNGEON_TELEPORT_ROUTES[gained-1].label,'#ffd36b');
  }
}
setInterval(checkDungeonTeleportDiscovery,500);
document.getElementById('dungeonTeleportClose').addEventListener('click',closeDungeonTeleportMenu);
document.getElementById('dungeonTeleportMenu').addEventListener('click',function(e){if(e.target===this)closeDungeonTeleportMenu()});

function sendBlacksmithState(){
  var bf=document.getElementById('blacksmithFrame');
  if(!bf||!bf.contentWindow||bf.style.display==='none')return;
  try{
    var ck=currentGearClassKey();
    var necklaces=(JEWELRY_VARIANTS.necklace||[]);
    var necklaceImg=necklaces.length?(necklaces[0].img||''):'';
    // Mechanical medallion is used as the common artifact visual for now.
    // It is the SAME art on every rarity; rarity comes from the cell background.
    var artifactImg=necklaces.length?(necklaces[necklaces.length-1].img||necklaceImg):necklaceImg;

    bf.contentWindow.postMessage({
      type:'blacksmithState',
      state:{
        // V164: keep the blacksmith bridge lightweight. V163 item descriptions
        // made cloning the full inventory unnecessarily expensive on phones.
        bag:(INV.bag||[]).map(function(it){
          if(!it)return it;
          return {
            uid:it.uid||'', name:it.name||'', slot:it.slot||'', rarity:it.rarity||'common',
            enh:Number(it.enh||0), icon:it.icon||'◆', img:'',
            stats:Object.assign({},it.stats||{}), bonusText:it.bonusText||'', bm:Number(it.bm||0),
            classKey:it.classKey||'', className:it.className||'', level:Number(it.level||it.lvl||0),
            sell:Number(it.sell||0), petName:it.petName||'', premiumUniquePet:!!it.premiumUniquePet,
            blackMarket:!!it.blackMarket
          };
        }),
        materials:INV.materials,
        feathers:{phoenix:(INV.feathers&&INV.feathers.phoenix)||0},
        stones:INV.stones,
        currencies:{gold:INV.gold,gram:INV.gram||0,ppa:INV.ppa||0},
        craftVisuals:{
          classKey:ck,
          className:CLASS_DISPLAY[ck]||ck,
          gear:{},
          accessories:{},
          wingsByRarity:{}
        }
      }
    },'*');
  }catch(e){}
}


const ACCESSORY_STAT_VALUES={
  artifact:{
    common:{atk:4,crit:1},
    uncommon:{atk:7,crit:2,critDmg:5},
    rare:{atk:11,crit:3,critDmg:10},
    epic:{atk:16,crit:4,critDmg:15},
    legendary:{atk:48,crit:12,critDmg:45}
  },
  cloak:{
    common:{hp:20,def:2},
    uncommon:{hp:35,def:4,controlResist:2},
    rare:{hp:55,def:7,controlResist:4},
    epic:{hp:85,def:11,controlResist:7},
    legendary:{hp:255,def:33,controlResist:21}
  },
  wings:{
    common:{spdFlat:0.08,dodge:1},
    uncommon:{spdFlat:0.12,dodge:2,slowResist:3},
    rare:{spdFlat:0.18,dodge:3,slowResist:6},
    epic:{spdFlat:0.24,dodge:4,slowResist:10},
    legendary:{spdFlat:0.72,dodge:12,slowResist:30}
  }
};

function accessoryStatValues(kind,rarity){
  return Object.assign({},((ACCESSORY_STAT_VALUES[kind]||{})[rarity])||{});
}

const NECKLACE_STAT_VALUES={
  common:{hpPct:4,mpPct:4},
  uncommon:{hpPct:6,mpPct:6},
  rare:{hpPct:9,mpPct:9,magicResist:8},
  epic:{hpPct:12,mpPct:12,magicResist:12},
  legendary:{hpPct:36,mpPct:36,magicResist:36}
};

function applyGearMagicWard(it){
  if(!it||!it.slot)return it;
  var defensive={helmet:1,armor:1,gloves:1,legs:1,boots:1};
  if(!defensive[it.slot])return it;
  var ward={common:0,uncommon:2,rare:4,epic:6,legendary:18}[it.rarity||'common']||0;
  if(ward>0){
    it.stats=it.stats||{};
    it.stats.magicResist=Math.max(it.stats.magicResist||0,ward);
    if(typeof itemBM==='function')it.bm=itemBM(it.stats);
  }
  return it;
}

function normalizeAccessoryItem(it){
  if(!it||!it.slot)return it;
  it.name=cleanItemName(it.name);
  var r=it.rarity||'common';
  if(it.slot==='artifact'){
    it.stats=accessoryStatValues('artifact',r);
  }else if(it.slot==='cloak'){
    it.stats=accessoryStatValues('cloak',r);
  }else if(it.slot==='wings'){
    it.stats=accessoryStatValues('wings',r);
  }else if(it.slot==='necklace'){
    it.stats=Object.assign({},NECKLACE_STAT_VALUES[r]||{});
  }
  if(typeof syncItemBM==='function')syncItemBM(it);
  return it;
}

function normalizeAllAccessoryItems(){
  (INV.bag||[]).forEach(function(it){
    if(!it)return;
    it.name=cleanItemName(it.name);
    applyGearMagicWard(it);
    if(['artifact','cloak','wings','necklace'].includes(it.slot))normalizeAccessoryItem(it);
    if((it.enh||0)>0)applyEnhancementStats(it); else syncItemBM(it);
  });
  if(INV.equipped){
    for(var s in INV.equipped){
      var it=INV.equipped[s];if(!it)continue;
      it.name=cleanItemName(it.name);
      applyGearMagicWard(it);
      if(['artifact','cloak','wings','necklace'].includes(it.slot))normalizeAccessoryItem(it);
      if((it.enh||0)>0)applyEnhancementStats(it); else syncItemBM(it);
    }
  }
  if(INV.storage){
    ['personal','clan','premium'].forEach(function(k){
      (INV.storage[k]||[]).forEach(function(it){
        if(!it)return;
        applyGearMagicWard(it);
        if(['artifact','cloak','wings','necklace'].includes(it.slot))normalizeAccessoryItem(it);
        if((it.enh||0)>0)applyEnhancementStats(it); else syncItemBM(it);
      });
    });
  }
}

function craftStats(kind,slot,name,rarity){
  if(kind==='gear'){
    var base=({
      weapon:{atk:52,crit:7},
      armor:{def:38,hp:120},
      helmet:{def:24,hp:60},
      gloves:{def:16,crit:4},
      ring:{atk:13,crit:6},
      legs:{def:22,spd:3},
      boots:{def:14,spd:5}
    })[slot]||{def:10};
    if(rarity==='legendary'){
      var tripled={};
      for(var k in base)tripled[k]=Number(base[k])*3;
      return tripled;
    }
    return Object.assign({},base);
  }
  if(kind==='wings'){
    if(rarity==='legendary')return {spd:15};
    return rarity==='epic'?{spd:5}:rarity==='rare'?{spd:4}:rarity==='uncommon'?{spd:3}:{spd:2};
  }
  if(kind==='pet'){
    if(name==='Лесной дракончик')return {hp:rarity==='rare'?35:rarity==='uncommon'?22:14};
    if(name==='Северный волчонок')return {spd:rarity==='rare'?4:rarity==='uncommon'?3:2};
    if(name==='Мудрая сова')return {crit:rarity==='rare'?3:rarity==='uncommon'?2:1};
    if(name==='Механический спутник')return {def:rarity==='rare'?8:rarity==='uncommon'?5:3};
    if(name==='Кристальный големчик')return {def:rarity==='rare'?7:rarity==='uncommon'?4:2};
    if(name==='Лунный лис')return {mp:rarity==='rare'?28:rarity==='uncommon'?18:10};
    if(name==='Водный дух')return {hp:rarity==='rare'?24:rarity==='uncommon'?16:10};
    return {hp:rarity==='rare'?20:rarity==='uncommon'?12:8};
  }
  return {};
}

}

// Repaint only the sprite body over the player. Shadows, names, HP bars and
// attack effects stay in their original render pass, so gameplay visuals do not change.
function ppaDrawWorldBodyOnly(e){
  if(!e||e.hp<=0)return;
  var sx=e.x-cam.x,sy=e.y-cam.y;
  var bob=(e&&e.isFartGuard)?0:Math.sin(e.bob)*2;

  if(e.isBoss&&!e.isClanBoss&&!e.isDungeon21Boss){
    var bossImg=imgPhoenix;
    if(bossImg&&bossImg.complete&&bossImg.naturalWidth>0){
      var spitK=e.spit>0?Math.sin((26-e.spit)/26*Math.PI):0;
      var bw2=e.sz*2.3*(1+spitK*.12);
      var bh2=bw2*(bossImg.naturalHeight/bossImg.naturalWidth);
      var flap=Math.sin(e.bob*.55)*6;
      cx.save();
      cx.translate(sx+(e.face||1)*spitK*14,sy);
      cx.scale(e.face===-1?-1:1,1);
      cx.drawImage(bossImg,-bw2/2,-bh2/2+bob*1.5+flap-14,bw2,bh2);
      cx.restore();
      if(e.flash>0){
        cx.globalAlpha=.4;cx.fillStyle='#fff';cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy+bob,e.sz*.7,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.globalAlpha=1;
      }
    }else{
      cx.fillStyle=e.flash>0?'#ffffff':e.col;
      cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy+bob,e.sz*.45,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
    }
    return;
  }

  var isScavengerSlime=!!(e.type&&e.type.slimeScavenger);
  var isCaveSpider=!!(e.type&&e.type.caveSpider);
  var isApprovedAnimated=!!e.animPack;

  if(isCaveSpider&&CAVE_SPIDER_ATLAS.complete&&CAVE_SPIDER_ATLAS.naturalWidth>0){
    var sf=e.spiderMoving?((Math.floor((Date.now()+(e.spiderPhase||0)*55)/125))%4):0;
    var sr=(e.spiderDir===undefined?1:e.spiderDir),dh=42,dw=72;
    cx.save();cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
    if(e.flash>0){cx.globalAlpha=.72;cx.filter='brightness(1.8)';}
    cx.drawImage(CAVE_SPIDER_ATLAS,sf*CAVE_SPIDER_FW,sr*CAVE_SPIDER_FH,CAVE_SPIDER_FW,CAVE_SPIDER_FH,sx-dw/2,sy-dh*.72+bob,dw,dh);
    cx.filter='none';cx.globalAlpha=1;cx.restore();
  }else if(isApprovedAnimated&&e.animPack.img.complete&&e.animPack.img.naturalWidth>0){
    var af=e.animMoving?((Math.floor((Date.now()+(e.animPhase||0)*43)/e.animPack.ms))%4):0;
    var ar=(e.animDir===undefined?1:e.animDir),adh=e.animPack.h,adw=adh*(MOB_ANIM_FW/MOB_ANIM_FH);
    cx.save();cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
    if(e.flash>0){cx.globalAlpha=.72;cx.filter='brightness(1.8)';}
    cx.drawImage(e.animPack.img,af*MOB_ANIM_FW,ar*MOB_ANIM_FH,MOB_ANIM_FW,MOB_ANIM_FH,sx-adw/2,sy-adh*.78+bob,adw,adh);
    cx.filter='none';cx.globalAlpha=1;cx.restore();
  }else if(e.sprite&&e.sprite.complete&&e.sprite.naturalWidth>0){
    var sdh=isScavengerSlime?25:Math.max(54,Math.min(92,e.sz*2));
    var widthMul=isScavengerSlime?(e.slimeWidthMul||1):1;
    var sdw=sdh*(e.sprite.naturalWidth/e.sprite.naturalHeight)*widthMul;
    cx.save();cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
    if(e.flash>0){cx.globalAlpha=.72;cx.filter='brightness(1.8)';}
    if(isScavengerSlime&&e.slimeFlip){
      cx.translate(sx,0);cx.scale(-1,1);cx.drawImage(e.sprite,-sdw/2,(e.isFartGuard?(sy+(e.sz||38)*.50-sdh*.859375):(sy-sdh*.78+bob)),sdw,sdh);
    }else cx.drawImage(e.sprite,sx-sdw/2,(e.isFartGuard?(sy+(e.sz||38)*.50-sdh*.859375):(sy-sdh*.78+bob)),sdw,sdh);
    cx.filter='none';cx.globalAlpha=1;cx.restore();
  }else{
    cx.fillStyle=e.flash>0?'#ffffff':e.col;
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy+bob,e.sz*.42,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  }
}

function ppaDrawWorldBodyOccluders(){
  if(P.scene!=='dungeon'&&P.scene!=='arena')return;
  var list=[];
  const near=ppaNearWorldBodies();
  for(var i=0;i<near.length;i++){
    var e=near[i];
    if(ppaPlayerIsBehindWorldBody(e))list.push(e);
  }
  // Draw farther bodies first and the closest/lower body last.
  list.sort(function(a,b){
    var ga=ppaWorldBodyGeometry(a),gb=ppaWorldBodyGeometry(b);
    return (ga?ga.cy:0)-(gb?gb.cy:0);
  });
  for(var j=0;j<list.length;j++)ppaDrawWorldBodyOnly(list[j]);
}

function clanBossDirectMelee(){
  var e=clanBossCombatTarget();
  if(!e){
    showPickup('Босс не найден','#ff9c9c');
    return false;
  }

  var edgeD=clanBossDistanceFromBody(e,P.x,P.y);
  if(edgeD>CLAN_BOSS_MELEE_EDGE_RANGE){
    showPickup('ПОДОЙДИ БЛИЖЕ','#d8c09d');
    return false;
  }

  P.tid=e.id;

  var crit=Math.random()*100<(P.crit||0);
  var atkBase=10+(Number(P.atk)||12);
  var dmgMul=Number(P.dmgMul);
  if(!Number.isFinite(dmgMul)||dmgMul<=0)dmgMul=1;

  var raw=atkBase*dmgMul*shopDamageMul()*clanBossDamageMul()*(crit?((P.critDmg||180)/100):1);
  var _pen=Math.max(0,Math.min(.60,((classBaseKey()==='mage'||classBaseKey()==='priest')?(P.magicPen||0):(P.armorPen||0))/100));
  var realDmg=Math.max(5,Math.floor(raw-(e.def||0)*(1-_pen)*0.25));

  e.hp=Math.max(0,e.hp-realDmg);
  applyPlayerVampirism(realDmg,1);
  clanBossTrackDamage(realDmg);
  e.flash=8;e.aggro=true;

  var g=clanBossBodyGeometry(e);
  var playerGround=clanBossPlayerShadowCenterAt(P.x,P.y);
  var ang=Math.atan2(g.cy-playerGround.y,g.cx-playerGround.x);
  P.face=Math.cos(ang)<0?-1:1;
  P.shootT=1;P.recoil=1;P.meleeAng=ang;
  P.attacking=true;P.anim='attack';P.animFrame=0;P.animTimer=0;

  showPickup((crit?'КРИТ · ':'УДАР · ')+'−'+realDmg,'#ffe09b');

  for(var i=0;i<10;i++){
    var a=ang+(Math.random()-.5)*1.1;
    PT.push({
      x:P.x+Math.cos(a)*(28+Math.random()*50),
      y:P.y-8+Math.sin(a)*(28+Math.random()*50),
      vx:Math.cos(a)*(2+Math.random()*4),
      vy:Math.sin(a)*(2+Math.random()*4),
      life:12,ml:12,sz:2+Math.random()*3,
      col:Math.random()<.5?'#fff2b0':'#d9a352'
    });
  }

  return true;
}

function clanBossSkillTargetDirect(maxRange){
  var e=clanBossCombatTarget();
  if(!e)return null;

  // Keep each skill's OWN original range, measured from the boss body edge.
  var edgeD=clanBossDistanceFromBody(e,P.x,P.y);
  if(maxRange&&edgeD>maxRange)return null;

  P.tid=e.id;
  return e;
}

function melee(){
  if(P.scene==='clanboss1'){
    clanBossDirectMelee();
    return;
  }

  let tx,ty,tg=null;
  if(P.tid!=null)tg=EN.find(e=>e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0);
  if(!tg){tg=findNearBasic();P.tid=tg?tg.id:null;}
  if(tg){tx=tg.x;ty=tg.y}
  else{
    // Use joystick direction for attack — enables up/down strikes
    const jlen=Math.hypot(jX,jY);
    if(jlen>0.3){tx=P.x+jX/jlen*200;ty=P.y+jY/jlen*200}
    else{tx=P.x+P.face*200;ty=P.y}
  }

  const dx=tx-P.x,dy=ty-P.y;
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.shootT=1;P.recoil=1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;

  const _basicRoll=basicAttackRoll(tg);
  const dmg=_basicRoll.damage;
  const _crit=_basicRoll.crit;
  const basicRange=playerBasicRange();
  let hitAny=false;

  // Hit only the SINGLE closest enemy within range and frontal arc
  let bestE=null,bestD=Infinity;
  for(const e of EN){
    if(typeof v225AiIsHidden==='function'&&v225AiIsHidden(e))continue;
    const ex=e.x-P.x,ey=e.y-P.y;
    const ed=Math.hypot(ex,ey);
    const edgeReach=(P.scene==='clanboss1'&&e.isClanBoss)
      ? Math.max(150,(e.sz||176)*0.98)
      : Math.max(0,(e.sz-30)*0.4);
    if(ed>basicRange+edgeReach)continue;
    const ea=Math.atan2(ey,ex);
    let diff=ea-ang;
    while(diff>Math.PI)diff-=Math.PI*2;
    while(diff<-Math.PI)diff+=Math.PI*2;
    if(Math.abs(diff)>MELEE_ARC/2)continue;
    if(ed<bestD){bestD=ed;bestE=e}
  }

  if(bestE){
    const e=bestE;
    const ea=Math.atan2(e.y-P.y,e.x-P.x);
    if(e.isAiFighter&&typeof v225AiDodgeRoll==='function'&&v225AiDodgeRoll(e)){showPickup('УВОРОТ ИИ','#9fdcff');return}
    if(e.isAiFighter&&typeof v225AiBeginCombat==='function'&&!v225AiBeginCombat(e))return;
    // dmg already contains class ATK, crit, shop/clan multiplier and target DEF.
    const realDmg=(e===tg)?dmg:basicAttackRoll(e).damage;
    e.hp-=realDmg;
    applyPlayerVampirism(realDmg,1);
    if(e.isClanBoss){
      clanBossTrackDamage(realDmg);
      showPickup('УДАР · −'+realDmg,'#ffe0a0');
    }
    e.flash=6;e.aggro=true;hitAny=true;

    // Boss is anchored and must not twitch from ordinary knockback.
    if(!e.isClanBoss&&!e.isWorldCrystalBoss&&!e.isDungeon21Boss&&!e.isClanSiegeCrystal){
      const kbx=Math.cos(ea)*8,kby=Math.sin(ea)*8;
      e.x+=kbx;e.y+=kby;
    }
    // Blood / hit particles
    for(let j=0;j<6;j++){
      const pa=ea+(Math.random()-0.5)*1.2;
      PT.push({x:e.x,y:e.y,vx:Math.cos(pa)*(3+Math.random()*5),vy:Math.sin(pa)*(3+Math.random()*5),
        life:12,ml:12,sz:2+Math.random()*3,col:Math.random()<0.5?'#cc2222':'#aa3333'});
    }
    // Chain lightning for adjacent enemies (skill bonus)
    if(P.chain>1){
      const ch=[e];
      for(let k=0;k<P.chain-1;k++){
        const last=ch[ch.length-1];
        let cn=null,cd2=Infinity;
        for(const e2 of EN){
          if(ch.includes(e2))continue;
          if(typeof v225AiIsHidden==='function'&&v225AiIsHidden(e2))continue;
          const d2=Math.hypot(e2.x-last.x,e2.y-last.y);
          if(d2<120&&d2<cd2){cd2=d2;cn=e2}
        }
        if(cn){const chainDmg=Math.max(1,Math.floor(dmg*0.4*clanDamageMulFor(cn))-(cn.def||0)*0.4);cn.hp-=chainDmg;if(cn.isClanBoss)clanBossTrackDamage(chainDmg);cn.flash=6;ch.push(cn);
          for(let j=0;j<6;j++)PT.push({x:(last.x+cn.x)/2,y:(last.y+cn.y)/2,
            vx:(Math.random()-.5)*5,vy:(Math.random()-.5)*5,life:10,ml:10,sz:2,col:'#ffff44'});
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // Slash visual — arc of energy in front of player
  for(let i=0;i<9;i++){
    const sa=ang+(Math.random()-0.5)*MELEE_ARC;
    const _vr=Math.min(120,basicRange);
    const sr=20+Math.random()*Math.max(1,_vr-20);
    PT.push({x:P.x+Math.cos(sa)*sr,y:P.y-12+Math.sin(sa)*sr,
      vx:Math.cos(sa)*2,vy:Math.sin(sa)*2,
      life:10,ml:10,sz:2+Math.random()*3,col:Math.random()<0.3?'#ffff44':'#ffffff'});
  }

  // Recoil only while standing. During run-and-attack the joystick remains
  // fully authoritative so the hero never gets pulled backward or slowed.
  if(!playerIsMoving()){
    P.vx-=Math.cos(ang)*2;
    P.vy-=Math.sin(ang)*2;
  }
}

// Update

// === V66 PREMIUM REGEN POTIONS ===
let _premiumRegenStamp=Date.now(),_premiumRegenCarry=0;
function updatePremiumRegen(){
  var now=Date.now(),dt=Math.max(0,Math.min(2500,now-_premiumRegenStamp));
  _premiumRegenStamp=now;
  if(P.dead){_premiumRegenCarry=0;return}
  var inPvp=P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam';
  var hpPotion=!inPvp&&now<Number((INV.activeBoosts||{}).premiumHpUntil||0);
  var mpPotion=!inPvp&&now<Number((INV.activeBoosts||{}).premiumMpUntil||0);
  var hpPet=Math.max(0,Number(P.hpRegen)||0);
  var mpPet=Math.max(0,Number(P.mpRegen)||0);
  if(!hpPotion&&!mpPotion&&!hpPet&&!mpPet){_premiumRegenCarry=0;return}
  _premiumRegenCarry+=dt;
  if(_premiumRegenCarry<1000)return;
  var ticks=Math.min(2,Math.floor(_premiumRegenCarry/1000));
  _premiumRegenCarry-=ticks*1000;
  var hpPer=(hpPotion?5:0)+hpPet,mpPer=(mpPotion?5:0)+mpPet;
  if(hpPer>0&&P.hp<P.mhp)P.hp=Math.min(P.mhp,P.hp+hpPer*ticks);
  if(mpPer>0&&P.mp<P.mmp)P.mp=Math.min(P.mmp,P.mp+mpPer*ticks);
}


// === V107 DEATH REMAINS (MOBILE OPTIMIZED, 7s + 2s fade) ===================
// Bodies and stains are intentionally NOT saved and have NO collision.
// This keeps old character saves fully compatible and avoids adding load to AI.
function ppaDeathStyleFor(e){
  const n=((e&&e.type&&e.type.n)||'').toLowerCase();
  if(e&&e.isWorldCrystalBoss)return 'stone';
  if(e&&e.isDungeon21Boss)return 'blood';
  if(e&&e.isBoss&&!e.isClanBoss)return 'phoenix';
  if(e&&e.isClanBoss)return clanBossIsCerberus(e)?'ember':'blood';
  if(n.indexOf('слайм')>=0)return 'slime';
  if(n.indexOf('паук')>=0)return 'ichor';
  if(n.indexOf('голем')>=0)return 'stone';
  if(n.indexOf('элементаль')>=0||n.indexOf('огнен')>=0||n.indexOf('адск')>=0)return 'ember';
  if(n.indexOf('кост')>=0)return 'bone';
  return 'blood';
}
function ppaDeathGroundishName(n){
  n=(n||'').toLowerCase();
  return n.indexOf('крыса')>=0||n.indexOf('паук')>=0||n.indexOf('жук')>=0||
    n.indexOf('слайм')>=0||n.indexOf('грызун')>=0||n.indexOf('волк')>=0||
    n.indexOf('гонч')>=0||n.indexOf('твар')>=0||n.indexOf('элементаль')>=0||
    n.indexOf('голем')>=0;
}
function ppaAddDeathRemnant(e){
  if(!e)return;
  const now=Date.now();
  const boss=!!e.isBoss;
  const clan=!!e.isClanBoss;
  const m=(!clan&&typeof ppaMobVisualMetrics==='function')?ppaMobVisualMetrics(e):null;
  let cw=0,ch=0;
  if(clan){
    try{cw=clanBossVisualDrawSize(e);ch=cw}catch(_err){cw=e.sz*2.2;ch=cw}
  }
  const _deathScene=(P.scene==='dungeon'&&typeof DUNGEON_MODE!=='undefined'&&DUNGEON_MODE==='41-60')?'dungeon41':((P.scene==='dungeon'&&typeof DUNGEON_MODE!=='undefined'&&DUNGEON_MODE==='21+')?'dungeon21':P.scene);
  const _d21=!!e.isDungeon21Boss;
  const r={
    scene:_deathScene,x:e.x,y:e.y,createdAt:now,
    // V117: Lord of Corruption corpse = 4 sec fully visible + 2 sec fade.
    bodyFadeAt:now+(_d21?4000:7000),bodyEndAt:now+(_d21?6000:9000),
    stainFadeAt:now+(_d21?4000:7000),stainEndAt:now+(_d21?6000:9000),
    style:ppaDeathStyleFor(e),name:(e.type&&e.type.n)||'',lvl:e.lvl||0,
    col:e.col||'#7a1f20',sz:e.sz||30,isBoss:boss,isClanBoss:clan,
    isDungeon21Boss:!!e.isDungeon21Boss,
    isCerberus:clan&&clanBossIsCerberus(e),
    sprite:e.sprite||null,animPack:e.animPack||null,animDir:e.animDir||1,
    spiderDir:e.spiderDir||1,isSpider:!!(e.type&&e.type.caveSpider),
    isSlime:!!(e.type&&e.type.slimeScavenger),slimeFlip:!!e.slimeFlip,
    face:e.face||1,w:m?m.w:cw,h:m?m.h:ch,
    clanSprite:null,clanFrame:null,clanCell:0
  };
  if(clan){
    try{
      r.clanSprite=clanBossSpriteImage(e);
      r.clanFrame=clanBossSpriteFrame(e);
      r.clanCell=clanBossSpriteCell(e);
    }catch(_err){}
  }
  DEAD_REMAINS.push(r);
  // V107 mobile cap: dungeon rooms can contain many mobs, so keep only a small
  // rolling window of remains. Old normal corpses are discarded before bosses.
  while(DEAD_REMAINS.length>24){
    let k=DEAD_REMAINS.findIndex(q=>!q.isBoss);
    if(k<0)k=0;
    DEAD_REMAINS.splice(k,1);
  }
}
function ppaDeathAlpha(now,start,end){
  if(now>=end)return 0;
  if(now<=start)return 1;
  return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(end-now)/Math.max(

  });

}

function melee(){
  if(P.scene==='clanboss1'){
    clanBossDirectMelee();
    return;
  }

  let tx,ty,tg=null;
  if(P.tid!=null)tg=EN.find(e=>e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0);
  if(!tg){tg=findNearBasic();P.tid=tg?tg.id:null;}
  if(tg){tx=tg.x;ty=tg.y}
  else{
    // Use joystick direction for attack — enables up/down strikes
    const jlen=Math.hypot(jX,jY);
    if(jlen>0.3){tx=P.x+jX/jlen*200;ty=P.y+jY/jlen*200}
    else{tx=P.x+P.face*200;ty=P.y}
  }

  const dx=tx-P.x,dy=ty-P.y;
  const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  P.face=dx<0?-1:1;
  P.shootT=1;P.recoil=1;
  P.meleeAng=ang;

  const _basicRoll=basicAttackRoll(tg);
  const dmg=_basicRoll.damage;
  const _crit=_basicRoll.crit;
  const basicRange=playerBasicRange();
  let hitAny=false;

  // Hit only the SINGLE closest enemy within range and frontal arc
  let bestE=null,bestD=Infinity;
  for(const e of EN){
    if(typeof v225AiIsHidden==='function'&&v225AiIsHidden(e))continue;
    const ex=e.x-P.x,ey=e.y-P.y;
    const ed=Math.hypot(ex,ey);
    const edgeReach=(P.scene==='clanboss1'&&e.isClanBoss)
      ? Math.max(150,(e.sz||176)*0.98)
      : Math.max(0,(e.sz-30)*0.4);
    if(ed>basicRange+edgeReach)continue;
    const ea=Math.atan2(ey,ex);
    let diff=ea-ang;
    while(diff>Math.PI)diff-=Math.PI*2;
    while(diff<-Math.PI)diff+=Math.PI*2;
    if(Math.abs(diff)>MELEE_ARC/2)continue;
    if(ed<bestD){bestD=ed;bestE=e}
  }

  if(bestE){
    const e=bestE;
    const ea=Math.atan2(e.y-P.y,e.x-P.x);
    if(e.isAiFighter&&typeof v225AiDodgeRoll==='function'&&v225AiDodgeRoll(e)){showPickup('УВОРОТ ИИ','#9fdcff');return}
    if(e.isAiFighter&&typeof v225AiBeginCombat==='function'&&!v225AiBeginCombat(e))return;
    // dmg already contains class ATK, crit, shop/clan multiplier and target DEF.
    const realDmg=(e===tg)?dmg:basicAttackRoll(e).damage;
    e.hp-=realDmg;
    applyPlayerVampirism(realDmg,1);
    if(e.isClanBoss){
      clanBossTrackDamage(realDmg);
      showPickup('УДАР · −'+realDmg,'#ffe0a0');
    }
    e.flash=6;e.aggro=true;hitAny=true;

    // Boss is anchored and must not twitch from ordinary knockback.
    if(!e.isClanBoss&&!e.isWorldCrystalBoss&&!e.isDungeon21Boss&&!e.isClanSiegeCrystal){
      const kbx=Math.cos(ea)*8,kby=Math.sin(ea)*8;
      e.x+=kbx;e.y+=kby;
    }
    // Blood / hit particles
    for(let j=0;j<6;j++){
      const pa=ea+(Math.random()-0.5)*1.2;
      PT.push({x:e.x,y:e.y,vx:Math.cos(pa)*(3+Math.random()*5),vy:Math.sin(pa)*(3+Math.random()*5),
        life:12,ml:12,sz:2+Math.random()*3,col:Math.random()<0.5?'#cc2222':'#aa3333'});
    }
    // Chain lightning for adjacent enemies (skill bonus)
    if(P.chain>1){
      const ch=[e];
      for(let k=0;k<P.chain-1;k++){
        const last=ch[ch.length-1];
        let cn=null,cd2=Infinity;
        for(const e2 of EN){
          if(ch.includes(e2))continue;
          if(typeof v225AiIsHidden==='function'&&v225AiIsHidden(e2))continue;
          const d2=Math.hypot(e2.x-last.x,e2.y-last.y);
          if(d2<120&&d2<cd2){cd2=d2;cn=e2}
        }
        if(cn){const chainDmg=Math.max(1,Math.floor(dmg*0.4*clanDamageMulFor(cn))-(cn.def||0)*0.4);cn.hp-=chainDmg;if(cn.isClanBoss)clanBossTrackDamage(chainDmg);cn.flash=6;ch.push(cn);
          for(let j=0;j<6;j++)PT.push({x:(last.x+cn.x)/2,y:(last.y+cn.y)/2,
            vx:(Math.random()-.5)*5,vy:(Math.random()-.5)*5,life:10,ml:10,sz:2,col:'#ffff44'});
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // Slash visual — arc of energy in front of player
  for(let i=0;i<9;i++){
    const sa=ang+(Math.random()-0.5)*MELEE_ARC;
    const _vr=Math.min(120,basicRange);
    const sr=20+Math.random()*Math.max(1,_vr-20);
    PT.push({x:P.x+Math.cos(sa)*sr,y:P.y-12+Math.sin(sa)*sr,
      vx:Math.cos(sa)*2,vy:Math.sin(sa)*2,
      life:10,ml:10,sz:2+Math.random()*3,col:Math.random()<0.3?'#ffff44':'#ffffff'});
  }

  // Recoil only while standing. During run-and-attack the joystick remains
  // fully authoritative so the hero never gets pulled backward or slowed.
  if(!playerIsMoving()){
    P.vx-=Math.cos(ang)*2;
    P.vy-=Math.sin(ang)*2;
  }
}

// Update

// === V66 PREMIUM REGEN POTIONS ===
let _premiumRegenStamp=Date.now(),_premiumRegenCarry=0;
function updatePremiumRegen(){
  var now=Date.now(),dt=Math.max(0,Math.min(2500,now-_premiumRegenStamp));
  _premiumRegenStamp=now;
  if(P.dead){_premiumRegenCarry=0;return}
  var inPvp=P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam';
  var hpPotion=!inPvp&&now<Number((INV.activeBoosts||{}).premiumHpUntil||0);
  var mpPotion=!inPvp&&now<Number((INV.activeBoosts||{}).premiumMpUntil||0);
  var hpPet=Math.max(0,Number(P.hpRegen)||0);
  var mpPet=Math.max(0,Number(P.mpRegen)||0);
  if(!hpPotion&&!mpPotion&&!hpPet&&!mpPet){_premiumRegenCarry=0;return}
  _premiumRegenCarry+=dt;
  if(_premiumRegenCarry<1000)return;
  var ticks=Math.min(2,Math.floor(_premiumRegenCarry/1000));
  _premiumRegenCarry-=ticks*1000;
  var hpPer=(hpPotion?5:0)+hpPet,mpPer=(mpPotion?5:0)+mpPet;
  if(hpPer>0&&P.hp<P.mhp)P.hp=Math.min(P.mhp,P.hp+hpPer*ticks);
  if(mpPer>0&&P.mp<P.mmp)P.mp=Math.min(P.mmp,P.mp+mpPer*ticks);
}


// === V107 DEATH REMAINS (MOBILE OPTIMIZED, 7s + 2s fade) ===================
// Bodies and stains are intentionally NOT saved and have NO collision.
// This keeps old character saves fully compatible and avoids adding load to AI.
function ppaDeathStyleFor(e){
  const n=((e&&e.type&&e.type.n)||'').toLowerCase();
  if(e&&e.isWorldCrystalBoss)return 'stone';
  if(e&&e.isDungeon21Boss)return 'blood';
  if(e&&e.isBoss&&!e.isClanBoss)return 'phoenix';
  if(e&&e.isClanBoss)return clanBossIsCerberus(e)?'ember':'blood';
  if(n.indexOf('слайм')>=0)return 'slime';
  if(n.indexOf('паук')>=0)return 'ichor';
  if(n.indexOf('голем')>=0)return 'stone';
  if(n.indexOf('элементаль')>=0||n.indexOf('огнен')>=0||n.indexOf('адск')>=0)return 'ember';
  if(n.indexOf('кост')>=0)return 'bone';
  return 'blood';
}
function ppaDeathGroundishName(n){
  n=(n||'').toLowerCase();
  return n.indexOf('крыса')>=0||n.indexOf('паук')>=0||n.indexOf('жук')>=0||
    n.indexOf('слайм')>=0||n.indexOf('грызун')>=0||n.indexOf('волк')>=0||
    n.indexOf('гонч')>=0||n.indexOf('твар')>=0||n.indexOf('элементаль')>=0||
    n.indexOf('голем')>=0;
}
function ppaAddDeathRemnant(e){
  if(!e)return;
  const now=Date.now();
  const boss=!!e.isBoss;
  const clan=!!e.isClanBoss;
  const m=(!clan&&typeof ppaMobVisualMetrics==='function')?ppaMobVisualMetrics(e):null;
  let cw=0,ch=0;
  if(clan){
    try{cw=clanBossVisualDrawSize(e);ch=cw}catch(_err){cw=e.sz*2.2;ch=cw}
  }
  const _deathScene=(P.scene==='dungeon'&&typeof DUNGEON_MODE!=='undefined'&&DUNGEON_MODE==='41-60')?'dungeon41':((P.scene==='dungeon'&&typeof DUNGEON_MODE!=='undefined'&&DUNGEON_MODE==='21+')?'dungeon21':P.scene);
  const _d21=!!e.isDungeon21Boss;
  const r={
    scene:_deathScene,x:e.x,y:e.y,createdAt:now,
    // V117: Lord of Corruption corpse = 4 sec fully visible + 2 sec fade.
    bodyFadeAt:now+(_d21?4000:7000),bodyEndAt:now+(_d21?6000:9000),
    stainFadeAt:now+(_d21?4000:7000),stainEndAt:now+(_d21?6000:9000),
    style:ppaDeathStyleFor(e),name:(e.type&&e.type.n)||'',lvl:e.lvl||0,
    col:e.col||'#7a1f20',sz:e.sz||30,isBoss:boss,isClanBoss:clan,
    isDungeon21Boss:!!e.isDungeon21Boss,
    isCerberus:clan&&clanBossIsCerberus(e),
    sprite:e.sprite||null,animPack:e.animPack||null,animDir:e.animDir||1,
    spiderDir:e.spiderDir||1,isSpider:!!(e.type&&e.type.caveSpider),
    isSlime:!!(e.type&&e.type.slimeScavenger),slimeFlip:!!e.slimeFlip,
    face:e.face||1,w:m?m.w:cw,h:m?m.h:ch,
    clanSprite:null,clanFrame:null,clanCell:0
  };
  if(clan){
    try{
      r.clanSprite=clanBossSpriteImage(e);
      r.clanFrame=clanBossSpriteFrame(e);
      r.clanCell=clanBossSpriteCell(e);
    }catch(_err){}
  }
  DEAD_REMAINS.push(r);
  // V107 mobile cap: dungeon rooms can contain many mobs, so keep only a small
  // rolling window of remains. Old normal corpses are discarded before bosses.
  while(DEAD_REMAINS.length>24){
    let k=DEAD_REMAINS.findIndex(q=>!q.isBoss);
    if(k<0)k=0;
    DEAD_REMAINS.splice(k,1);
  }
}
function ppaDeathAlpha(now,start,end){
  if(now>=end)return 0;
  if(now<=start)return 1;
  return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(end-now)/Math.max(1,end-start)));
}
function ppaDrawBloodStain(sx,sy,scale,a,dark){
  cx.save();cx.globalAlpha=a;
  const base=dark?'#3c0710':'#620b12',hi=dark?'#6b1118':'#9d171d';
  cx.fillStyle=base;
  cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+5,31*scale,10*scale,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx-23*scale,sy+7,12*scale,5*scale,-.15,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx+25*scale,sy+4,10*scale,4*scale,.2,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  cx.fillStyle=hi;
  cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx-5*scale,sy+3,17*scale,5*scale,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  for(let i=0;i<5;i++)cx.fillRect(Math.round(sx+(-34+i*17)*scale),Math.round(sy+(i%2?9:12)*scale),Math.max(2,Math.round(4*scale)),Math.max(1,Math.round(2*scale)));
  cx.restore();
}
function ppaDrawSlimeStain(sx,sy,scale,a,col){
  cx.save();cx.globalAlpha=a;
  cx.fillStyle=col||'#66508d';
  cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+5,30*scale,10*scale,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  cx.globalAlpha=a*.65;
  cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx-20*scale,sy+9,13*scale,5*scale,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx+22*scale,sy+8,11*scale,4*scale,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(180,255,120,.75)';
  for(let i=0;i<5;i++)cx.fillRect(Math.round(sx+(-18+i*9)*scale),Math.round(sy+(3+(i%2)*4)*scale),Math.max(1,Math.round(2*scale)),Math.max(1,Math.round(2*scale)));
  cx.restore();
}

===== shootT #16 @97831367 =====
cx.strokeStyle=idx>=0?'rgba(255,197,88,.95)':'rgba(215,215,215,.75)';

    }
    cx.restore();
  }

  cx.textAlign='left';cx.restore();
}
// ========================================================================

let __ppaHudNextAt=0;
function update(){
  PPAOnlineTick();
  ppaCleanupDeathRemnants();
  updateConsumableHud(false);
  updatePremiumRegen();
  if(P.dead)return;
  v174AiTickPlayerDebuffs();

  const _smartMove=updateSmartAttackInput();
  // V139: Smart Attack only supplies movement when joystick is neutral.
  // Any live joystick input keeps full manual movement while target lock stays active.
  const _moveX=_smartMove?_smartMove.x:jX;
  const _moveY=_smartMove?_smartMove.y:jY;

  // V174: class-AI crowd control is numeric only (no particle FX).
  const _aiMoveMul=Date.now()<(P.aiRootUntil||0)?0:(Date.now()<(P.aiSlowUntil||0)?Math.max(.30,Number(P.aiSlowMul)||.55):1);
  P.vx=_moveX*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  P.vy=_moveY*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  let nx=P.x+P.vx, ny=P.y+P.vy;

  // Keep in bounds (per scene)
  const sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  nx=Math.max(sc.bounds.minX,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxX,nx));
  ny=Math.max(sc.bounds.minY,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxY,ny));

  if(P.scene==='arena'){
    // Stay on the circular arena floor — no wandering into the black void
    const c=sc.center,rad=460;
    const dx=nx-c.x,dy=ny-c.y,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
    if(dist>rad){nx=c.x+dx/dist*rad;ny=c.y+dy/dist*rad}
  }else if(P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam'){
    const mv=pvpSlide(P.scene,P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
    // Dungeon walls — slide along corridors, never clip through
    const mv=dgSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clansiege'){
    const mv=clanSiegeSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clanboss1'){
    const mv=clanBossMoveWithBodyCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='worldboss'){
    const mv=worldBossMoveWithCrystalCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='fartzone'){
    // Exact user-drawn meadow boundary: outside = solid collision.
    // Do not alter town/building hitboxes or other scenes.
    if(!pointInPolygonFartZone(nx,ny)){
      nx=P.x;ny=P.y;
    }
  }else{
    // Solid buildings — can't walk through walls or across rooftops
    const resolved=resolveCollision(nx,ny);
    nx=resolved.x;ny=resolved.y;
  }
  // V104: keep the player's visible foot shadow outside Phoenix/mob shadows.
  // The map/wall collision above remains authoritative; this only adds body blocking.
  if(P.scene==='dungeon'||P.scene==='arena'){
    const _bodyMv=ppaMoveWithWorldBodyCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*.24);
    nx=_bodyMv.x;ny=_bodyMv.y;
  }
  P.x=nx;P.y=ny;
  if(P.scene==='clansiege')clanSiegeUpdateCapture();

  // Portal collision
  if(P.portalCD>0)P.portalCD--;
  if(P.portalCD<=0&&!transitioning&&sc.portal){
    const p=sc.portal;
    if(Math.hypot(P.x-p.x,P.y-p.y)<p.r+20){
      changeScene(p.target);
    }
  }
  // Town teleport -> dungeon (walk into the rune circle)
  if(P.portalCD<=0&&!transitioning&&P.scene==='safe'&&sc.teleport&&sc.teleport.target){
    const tp=sc.teleport;
    if(Math.hypot(P.x-tp.x,P.y-tp.y)<tp.r){
      changeScene(tp.target);
    }
  }
  // 1x1 duel preparation: both players may move, but PvP damage stays locked for 10 seconds.
  updatePvpDuelWarmup();

  // Arena ladder: only a fully cleared stage advances.
  if(P.scene==='arena'){
    if(waveActive&&arenaWaveKilled>=arenaWaveTotal&&arenaActiveCount()===0){
      arenaRecordWave(arenaStageProgress());
      waveActive=false;
      waveTimer=180; // 3 sec break: enough to read the HUD, short enough to keep pace
    }
    if(!waveActive&&waveTimer>0){
      waveTimer--;
      if(waveTimer<=0)startWave();
    }
  }

  if(Math.abs(P.vx)>0.1||Math.abs(P.vy)>0.1)P.bob+=0.28;
  else P.bob+=0.04;

  if(P.shootT<=0){
    // Update facing based on horizontal movement for side sprite flip
    if(P.vx > 0.1) P.face = 1;
    else if(P.vx < -0.1) P.face = -1;
    // When moving vertically, keep last horizontal facing
  }

  if(P.shootCD>0)P.shootCD--;
  if(P.shootT>0)P.shootT-=0.12;
  if(P.recoil>0)P.recoil-=0.1;

  if(attackQueued&&P.shootCD<=0){
    attackQueued=false;

    // Preserve the joystick state. Combat must never cancel movement.
    const movingNow=playerIsMoving();

    if(playerUsesGnomeSprites()){
      try{gnomeFireCannonball()}catch(_){PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0}
    }else if(playerUsesArcherSprites()){
      try{archerFireArrow()}catch(_){PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0}
    }else{
      melee();
    }

    P.shootCD=Math.max(1,Math.round(60/((P.atkSpd||1)*shopAtkSpeedMul())));

    if(movingNow && P.attackMode!=='melee' && !playerUsesArcherSprites() && !playerUsesPriestSprites() && !playerUsesMageSprites()){
      // Gnome/ranged legacy behavior stays unchanged:
      // projectile can fire while the running animation continues.
      P.attacking=false;
      P.anim='run';
      P.animTimer=Math.min(P.animTimer||0,6);
      P.runAttackT=14;
    }else{
      // Assassin AND Archer now use their real ATTACK sprites while moving.
      // Movement itself is NOT cancelled; joystick/Smart Attack still moves P.x/P.y.
      P.attacking=true;
      P.anim='attack';
      P.animFrame=0;
      P.animTimer=0;
      P.runAttackT=movingNow?14:0;
    }
  }

  if(P.runAttackT>0)P.runAttackT--;

  // Sprite animation state machine:
  // attack > run > idle.
  // Assassin and Archer keep their real ATTACK animation while moving.
  // Gnome keeps its approved run+separate-projectile behavior.
  const movingForAnim=playerIsMoving();
  if(P.attacking&&movingForAnim&&P.attackMode!=='melee'&&!playerUsesArcherSprites()&&!playerUsesPriestSprites()&&!playerUsesMageSprites()){
    P.attacking=false;
    P.anim='run';
    P.animTimer=0;
    P.animFrame=P.animFrame%Math.max(1,playerAnimDef('run').frames);
  }

  if(P.attacking){
    P.animTimer++;
    const a=playerAnimDef('attack');
    const frameDur = Math.max(1, Math.round(60/a.fps));
    if(P.animTimer >= frameDur){
      P.animTimer = 0;
      P.animFrame++;
      if(P.animFrame >= a.frames){
        P.attacking = false;
        P.animFrame = 0;
        P.animTimer = 0;
      }
    }
  } else {
    const moving = movingForAnim;
    const nextAnim = moving ? 'run' : 'idle';
    if(P.anim !== nextAnim){
      // Smooth transition: don't reset to frame 0 if coming from similar pose
      P.anim = nextAnim;
      P.animFrame = 0;
      P.animTimer = 0;
    }
    const a = playerAnimDef(nextAnim);
    P.animTimer++;
    const frameDur = Math.max(1, Math.round(60 / a.fps));
    if(P.animTimer >= frameDur){
      P.animTimer = 0;
      P.animFrame = (P.animFrame + 1) % a.frames;
    }
  }

  // Сброс зависшей цели:
  // если моб умер, ушёл слишком далеко, скрылся за стеной
  // или игрок покинул его комнату — красная метка исчезает.
  if(P.tid!=null){
    const locked=EN.find(x=>x.id===P.tid);
    if(!targetIsValid(locked))P.tid=null;
  }

  // Fart Zone mine production + 30-minute guard respawns.
  updateFartZoneSystem();

  // Enemies
  for(let i=EN.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const e=EN[i];
    const dx=P.x-e.x,dy=P.y-e.y,d2=dx*dx+dy*dy;
    const reach=42+(e.sz-30)*0.35;
    // В подземелье одновременно размещено более 500 мобов.
    // Далёкие нетронутые мобы "спят" уже после 500px. Проверяем квадрат расстояния,
    // чтобы не делать сотни Math.hypot() на мобильном каждый кадр.
    if(P.scene==='dungeon'&&!e.isBoss&&!e.aggro&&e.hp===e.mhp&&d2>250000){
      if(e.atkCD>0)e.atkCD--;
      e.bob+=0.025;
      continue;
    }
    // V276 FPS: Fart Zone can hold 39–65 guards. Untouched guards far away
    // do not need movement/path/attack AI until the player approaches.
    if(P.scene==='fartzone'&&e.isFartGuard&&!e.aggro&&e.hp===e.mhp&&d2>360000){
      if(e.atkCD>0)e.atkCD--;
      e.bob+=0.025;
      continue;
    }
    const d=Math.sqrt(d2);
    if(e.isAiFighter){
      v174UpdateAiTrainingFighter(e,d);
      continue;
    }
    if(e.isBoss){
      // Boss/static siege objects never leave their anchor.
      if(e.hx!==undefined){e.x=e.hx;e.y=e.hy}

      if(e.isClanSiegeCrystal){

        }else{
          clanBossUpdateAoe(e,d);
          if(e.aoeState==='idle')clanBossUpdateWhip(e,dx,dy,d);
        }
      }else if(d<=reach&&e.atkCD<=0){
        if(Math.random()*100<effectivePlayerDodge()){
          showPickup('Уворот!','#88ffcc');
        }else{

      }
    }else{
      // === STANDARD MOB AGGRO / ROOM LEASH ===
      // A mob wakes only when the player enters this mob's personal aggro radius.
      // Dungeon mobs never leave their own room.
      // If the player runs out, the mob reaches the room edge, drops aggro,
      // and returns to its exact spawn point.
      if(e.hx===undefined){e.hx=e.x;e.hy=e.y;e.hunter=true}

      const homeD=Math.hypot(e.x-e.hx,e.y-e.hy);
      let chaseX=P.x,chaseY=P.y;

          if(e.aggro&&_minePlayerD>FART_GUARD_AGGRO_RADIUS*2.25)e.aggro=false;
        }
      }else if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
        playerInOwnRoom=(e.room===undefined)||dgPointInRoom(e.room,P.x,P.y,0);

        // Proximity aggro is individual, not a whole-room group aggro.
        if(!e.aggro && playerInOwnRoom && d<=dungeonAggroRadiusFor(e)){
          e.aggro=true;
          e.edgeChase=false;
          e.edgeWait=0;
        }

        // Direct damage already sets e.aggro=true in the hit code.
        // Once player is outside this room, chase only to the nearest room edge.

          if(edgeD<=Math.max(14,e.sp*3.2)){
            e.aggro=false;
            e.edgeChase=false;
            e.edgeWait=0;
          }
        }else if(e.aggro){
          e.edgeChase=false;
          e.edgeWait=0;
        }
      }else{
        if(d<=AGGRO_R)e.aggro=true;
        if(e.aggro&&(d>LEASH_R||homeD>LEASH_R))e.aggro=false;
      }

      if(playerSmokeActive()){
        e.aggro=false;
        e.edgeChase=false;
        e.edgeWait=0;
      }

      let tx,ty,move=false;

      if(e.aggro){

        const stopDist=(P.scene==='dungeon'&&e.edgeChase)?10:(e.isFartGuard?FART_GUARD_ATTACK_REACH:reach);

          tx=cdx/cd*e.sp*P.slowMul*(typeof v189EnemyMoveMul==='function'?v189EnemyMoveMul(e):1);
          ty=cdy/cd*e.sp*P.slowMul*(typeof v189EnemyMoveMul==='function'?v189EnemyMoveMul(e):1);
          move=true;

        tx=(e.hx-e.x)/homeD*s2;
        ty=(e.hy-e.y)/homeD*s2;
        move=true;
      }

      if(e.isFartGuard){
        e.fartMoving=!!move;
        if(move&&Math.abs(tx)>0.02)e.face=tx<0?-1:1;
      }

      if(e.type&&e.type.caveSpider){
        if(move){
          if(Math.abs(tx)>Math.abs(ty))e.spiderDir=tx<0?2:3;
          else e.spiderDir=ty<0?0:1;
          e.spiderMoving=true;
        }else{
          e.spiderMoving=false;
        }
      }

      if(e.animPack){
        if(move){
          if(Math.abs(tx)>Math.abs(ty))e.animDir=tx<0?2:3;
          else e.animDir=ty<0?0:1;
          e.animMoving=true;
        }else{
          e.animMoving=false;
        }
      }

      if(move){

          const mv=dgSlide(e.x,e.y,tx,ty,e.sz*0.3);

            const roomPad=Math.max(10,e.sz*0.18);

let __ppaHudNextAt=0;
function update(){
  PPAOnlineTick();
  ppaCleanupDeathRemnants();
  updateConsumableHud(false);
  updatePremiumRegen();
  if(P.dead)return;
  v174AiTickPlayerDebuffs();

  const _smartMove=updateSmartAttackInput();
  // V139: Smart Attack only supplies movement when joystick is neutral.
  // Any live joystick input keeps full manual movement while target lock stays active.
  const _moveX=_smartMove?_smartMove.x:jX;
  const _moveY=_smartMove?_smartMove.y:jY;

  // V174: class-AI crowd control is numeric only (no particle FX).
  const _aiMoveMul=Date.now()<(P.aiRootUntil||0)?0:(Date.now()<(P.aiSlowUntil||0)?Math.max(.30,Number(P.aiSlowMul)||.55):1);
  P.vx=_moveX*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  P.vy=_moveY*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  let nx=P.x+P.vx, ny=P.y+P.vy;

  // Keep in bounds (per scene)
  const sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  nx=Math.max(sc.bounds.minX,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxX,nx));
  ny=Math.max(sc.bounds.minY,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxY,ny));

  if(P.scene==='arena'){
    // Stay on the circular arena floor — no wandering into the black void
    const c=sc.center,rad=460;
    const dx=nx-c.x,dy=ny-c.y,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
    if(dist>rad){nx=c.x+dx/dist*rad;ny=c.y+dy/dist*rad}
  }else if(P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam'){
    const mv=pvpSlide(P.scene,P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
    // Dungeon walls — slide along corridors, never clip through
    const mv=dgSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clansiege'){
    const mv=clanSiegeSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clanboss1'){
    const mv=clanBossMoveWithBodyCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='worldboss'){
    const mv=worldBossMoveWithCrystalCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='fartzone'){
    // Exact user-drawn meadow boundary: outside = solid collision.
    // Do not alter town/building hitboxes or other scenes.
    if(!pointInPolygonFartZone(nx,ny)){
      nx=P.x;ny=P.y;
    }
  }else{
    // Solid buildings — can't walk through walls or across rooftops
    const resolved=resolveCollision(nx,ny);
    nx=resolved.x;ny=resolved.y;
  }
  // V104: keep the player's visible foot shadow outside Phoenix/mob shadows.
  // The map/wall collision above remains authoritative; this only adds body blocking.
  if(P.scene==='dungeon'||P.scene==='arena'){
    const _bodyMv=ppaMoveWithWorldBodyCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*.24);
    nx=_bodyMv.x;ny=_bodyMv.y;
  }
  P.x=nx;P.y=ny;
  if(P.scene==='clansiege')clanSiegeUpdateCapture();

  // Portal collision
  if(P.portalCD>0)P.portalCD--;
  if(P.portalCD<=0&&!transitioning&&sc.portal){
    const p=sc.portal;
    if(Math.hypot(P.x-p.x,P.y-p.y)<p.r+20){
      changeScene(p.target);
    }
  }
  // Town teleport -> dungeon (walk into the rune circle)
  if(P.portalCD<=0&&!transitioning&&P.scene==='safe'&&sc.teleport&&sc.teleport.target){
    const tp=sc.teleport;
    if(Math.hypot(P.x-tp.x,P.y-tp.y)<tp.r){
      changeScene(tp.target);
    }
  }
  // 1x1 duel preparation: both players may move, but PvP damage stays locked for 10 seconds.
  updatePvpDuelWarmup();

  // Arena ladder: only a fully cleared stage advances.
  if(P.scene==='arena'){
    if(waveActive&&arenaWaveKilled>=arenaWaveTotal&&arenaActiveCount()===0){
      arenaRecordWave(arenaStageProgress());
      waveActive=false;
      waveTimer=180; // 3 sec break: enough to read the HUD, short enough to keep pace
    }
    if(!waveActive&&waveTimer>0){
      waveTimer--;
      if(waveTimer<=0)startWave();
    }
  }

  if(Math.abs(P.vx)>0.1||Math.abs(P.vy)>0.1)P.bob+=0.28;
  else P.bob+=0.04;

  if(P.shootT<=0){
    // Update facing based on horizontal movement for side sprite flip
    if(P.vx > 0.1) P.face = 1;
    else if(P.vx < -0.1) P.face = -1;
    // When moving vertically, keep last horizontal facing
  }

  if(P.shootCD>0)P.shootCD--;
  if(P.shootT>0)P.shootT-=0.12;
  if(P.recoil>0)P.recoil-=0.1;

  if(attackQueued&&P.shootCD<=0){
    attackQueued=false;

    // Preserve the joystick state. Combat must never cancel movement.
    const movingNow=playerIsMoving();

    if(playerUsesGnomeSprites()){
      try{gnomeFireCannonball()}catch(_){PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0}
    }else if(playerUsesArcherSprites()){
      try{archerFireArrow()}catch(_){PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0}
    }else{
      melee();
    }

    P.shootCD=Math.max(1,Math.round(60/((P.atkSpd||1)*shopAtkSpeedMul())));

    if(movingNow && P.attackMode!=='melee' && !playerUsesArcherSprites() && !playerUsesPriestSprites() && !playerUsesMageSprites()){
      // Gnome/ranged legacy behavior stays unchanged:
      // projectile can fire while the running animation continues.
      P.attacking=false;
      P.anim='run';
      P.animTimer=Math.min(P.animTimer||0,6);
      P.runAttackT=14;
    }else{
      // Assassin AND Archer now use their real ATTACK sprites while moving.
      // Movement itself is NOT cancelled; joystick/Smart Attack still moves P.x/P.y.
      P.attacking=true;
      P.anim='attack';
      P.animFrame=0;
      P.animTimer=0;
      P.runAttackT=movingNow?14:0;
    }
  }

  if(P.runAttackT>0)P.runAttackT--;

  // Sprite animation state machine:
  // attack > run > idle.
  // Assassin and Archer keep their real ATTACK animation while moving.
  // Gnome keeps its approved run+separate-projectile behavior.
  const movingForAnim=playerIsMoving();
  if(P.attacking&&movingForAnim&&P.attackMode!=='melee'&&!playerUsesArcherSprites()&&!playerUsesPriestSprites()&&!playerUsesMageSprites()){
    P.attacking=false;
    P.anim='run';
    P.animTimer=0;
    P.animFrame=P.animFrame%Math.max(1,playerAnimDef('run').frames);
  }

  if(P.attacking){
    P.animTimer++;
    const a=playerAnimDef('attack');
    const frameDur = Math.max(1, Math.round(60/a.fps));
    if(P.animTimer >= frameDur){
      P.animTimer = 0;
      P.animFrame++;
      if(P.animFrame >= a.frames){
        P.attacking = false;
        P.animFrame = 0;
        P.animTimer = 0;
      }
    }
  } else {
    const moving = movingForAnim;
    const nextAnim = moving ? 'run' : 'idle';
    if(P.anim !== nextAnim){
      // Smooth transition: don't reset to frame 0 if coming from similar pose
      P.anim = nextAnim;
      P.animFrame = 0;
      P.animTimer = 0;
    }
    const a = playerAnimDef(nextAnim);
    P.animTimer++;
    const frameDur = Math.max(1, Math.round(60 / a.fps));
    if(P.animTimer >= frameDur){
      P.animTimer = 0;
      P.animFrame = (P.animFrame + 1) % a.frames;
    }
  }

  // Сброс зависшей цели:
  // если моб умер, ушёл слишком далеко, скрылся за стеной
  // или игрок покинул его комнату — красная метка исчезает.
  if(P.tid!=null){
    const locked=EN.find(x=>x.id===P.tid);
    if(!targetIsValid(locked))P.tid=null;

xt(tag,bx+9.5,yy+12);
    cx.fillStyle='#ffd783';cx.font='bold 8px monospace';
    cx.fillText(txt.slice(0,22),bx+19+(tw-22)/2,yy+12);
  }

  // Tiny, cheap hit stroke: ranged attacks visibly connect to the target;
  // melee attacks produce a short slash at the player's feet.
  if(now<(e.aiHitFxUntil||0)){
    const px=P.x-cam.x,py=P.y-cam.y;
    const alpha=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,((e.aiHitFxUntil||0)-now)/170));
    cx.save();cx.globalAlpha=alpha;
    cx.strokeStyle=e.aiHitFxType==='magic'?'#b98cff':'#ffbd62';
    cx.lineWidth=e.aiHitFxCrit?3:2;
    if(c.range>150){
      cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(sx,sy-e.sz*.12);cx.lineTo(px,py-e.sz*.18);cx.stroke();
    }else{
      cx.beginPath();cx.moveTo(px-13,py-18);cx.lineTo(px+14,py+2);
      cx.moveTo(px+11,py-19);cx.lineTo(px-9,py+3);cx.stroke();
    }
    cx.restore();
  }

  cx.textAlign='left';cx.restore();
}
// ========================================================================

let __ppaHudNextAt=0;
function update(){
  PPAOnlineTick();
  ppaCleanupDeathRemnants();
  updateConsumableHud(false);
  updatePremiumRegen();
  if(P.dead)return;
  v174AiTickPlayerDebuffs();

  const _smartMove=updateSmartAttackInput();
  // V139: Smart Attack only supplies movement when joystick is neutral.
  // Any live joystick input keeps full manual movement while target lock stays active.
  const _moveX=_smartMove?_smartMove.x:jX;
  const _moveY=_smartMove?_smartMove.y:jY;

  // V174: class-AI crowd control is numeric only (no particle FX).
  const _aiMoveMul=Date.now()<(P.aiRootUntil||0)?0:(Date.now()<(P.aiSlowUntil||0)?Math.max(.30,Number(P.aiSlowMul)||.55):1);
  P.vx=_moveX*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  P.vy=_moveY*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  let nx=P.x+P.vx, ny=P.y+P.vy;

  // Keep in bounds (per scene)
  const sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  nx=Math.max(sc.bounds.minX,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxX,nx));
  ny=Math.max(sc.bounds.minY,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxY,ny));

  if(P.scene==='arena'){
    // Stay on the circular arena floor — no wandering into the black void
    const c=sc.center,rad=460;
    const dx=nx-c.x,dy=ny-c.y,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
    if(dist>rad){nx=c.x+dx/dist*rad;ny=c.y+dy/dist*rad}
  }else if(P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam'){
    const mv=pvpSlide(P.scene,P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
    // Dungeon walls — slide along corridors, never clip through
    const mv=dgSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clansiege'){
    const mv=clanSiegeSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clanboss1'){
    const mv=clanBossMoveWithBodyCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='worldboss'){
    const mv=worldBossMoveWithCrystalCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='fartzone'){
    // Exact user-drawn meadow boundary: outside = solid collision.
    // Do not alter town/building hitboxes or other scenes.
    if(!pointInPolygonFartZone(nx,ny)){
      nx=P.x;ny=P.y;
    }
  }else{
    // Solid buildings — can't walk through walls or across rooftops
    const resolved=resolveCollision(nx,ny);
    nx=resolved.x;ny=resolved.y;
  }
  // V104: keep the player's visible foot shadow outside Phoenix/mob shadows.
  // The map/wall collision above remains authoritative; this only adds body blocking.
  if(P.scene==='dungeon'||P.scene==='arena'){
    const _bodyMv=ppaMoveWithWorldBodyCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*.24);
    nx=_bodyMv.x;ny=_bodyMv.y;
  }
  P.x=nx;P.y=ny;
  if(P.scene==='clansiege')clanSiegeUpdateCapture();

  // Portal collision
  if(P.portalCD>0)P.portalCD--;
  if(P.portalCD<=0&&!transitioning&&sc.portal){
    const p=sc.portal;
    if(Math.hypot(P.x-p.x,P.y-p.y)<p.r+20){
      changeScene(p.target);
    }
  }
  // Town teleport -> dungeon (walk into the rune circle)
  if(P.portalCD<=0&&!transitioning&&P.scene==='safe'&&sc.teleport&&sc.teleport.target){
    const tp=sc.teleport;
    if(Math.hypot(P.x-tp.x,P.y-tp.y)<tp.r){
      changeScene(tp.target);
    }
  }
  // 1x1 duel preparation: both players may move, but PvP damage stays locked for 10 seconds.
  updatePvpDuelWarmup();

  // Arena ladder: only a fully cleared stage advances.
  if(P.scene==='arena'){
    if(waveActive&&arenaWaveKilled>=arenaWaveTotal&&arenaActiveCount()===0){
      arenaRecordWave(arenaStageProgress());
      waveActive=false;
      waveTimer=180; // 3 sec break: enough to read the HUD, short enough to keep pace
    }
    if(!waveActive&&waveTimer>0){
      waveTimer--;
      if(waveTimer<=0)startWave();
    }
  }

  if(Math.abs(P.vx)>0.1||Math.abs(P.vy)>0.1)P.bob+=0.28;
  else P.bob+=0.04;

  if(P.shootT<=0){
    // Update facing based on horizontal movement for side sprite flip
    if(P.vx > 0.1) P.face = 1;
    else if(P.vx < -0.1) P.face = -1;
    // When moving vertically, keep last horizontal facing
  }

  if(P.shootCD>0)P.shootCD--;
  if(P.shootT>0)P.shootT-=0.12;
  if(P.recoil>0)P.recoil-=0.1;

  if(attackQueued&&P.shootCD<=0){
    attackQueued=false;

    // Preserve the joystick state. Combat must never cancel movement.
    const movingNow=playerIsMoving();

    if(playerUsesGnomeSprites()){
      try{gnomeFireCannonball()}catch(_){PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0}
    }else if(playerUsesArcherSprites()){
      try{archerFireArrow()}catch(_){PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0}
    }else{
      melee();
    }

    P.shootCD=Math.max(1,Math.round(60/((P.atkSpd||1)*shopAtkSpeedMul())));

    if(movingNow && P.attackMode!=='melee' && !playerUsesArcherSprites() && !playerUsesPriestSprites() && !playerUsesMageSprites()){
      // Gnome/ranged legacy behavior stays unchanged:
      // projectile can fire while the running animation continues.
      P.attacking=false;
      P.anim='run';
      P.animTimer=Math.min(P.animTimer||0,6);
      P.runAttackT=14;
    }else{
      // Assassin AND Archer now use their real ATTACK sprites while moving.
      // Movement itself is NOT cancelled; joystick/Smart Attack still moves P.x/P.y.
      P.attacking=true;
      P.anim='attack';
      P.animFrame=0;
      P.animTimer=0;
      P.runAttackT=movingNow?14:0;
    }
  }

  if(P.runAttackT>0)P.runAttackT--;

  // Sprite animation state machine:
  // attack > run > idle.
  // Assassin and Archer keep their real ATTACK animation while moving.
  // Gnome keeps its approved run+separate-projectile behavior.
  const movingForAnim=playerIsMoving();
  if(P.attacking&&movingForAnim&&P.attackMode!=='melee'&&!playerUsesArcherSprites()&&!playerUsesPriestSprites()&&!playerUsesMageSprites()){
    P.attacking=false;
    P.anim='run';
    P.animTimer=0;
    P.animFrame=P.animFrame%Math.max(1,playerAnimDef('run').frames);
  }

  if(P.attacking){
    P.animTimer++;
    const a=playerAnimDef('attack');
    const frameDur = Math.max(1, Math.round(60/a.fps));
    if(P.animTimer >= frameDur){
      P.animTimer = 0;
      P.animFrame++;
      if(P.animFrame >= a.frames){
        P.attacking = false;
        P.animFrame = 0;
        P.animTimer = 0;
      }
    }
  } else {
    const moving = movingForAnim;
    const nextAnim = moving ? 'run' : 'idle';
    if(P.anim !== nextAnim){
      // Smooth transition: don't reset to frame 0 if coming from similar pose
      P.anim = nextAnim;
      P.animFrame = 0;
      P.animTimer = 0;
    }
    const a = playerAnimDef(nextAnim);
    P.animTimer++;
    const frameDur = Math.max(1, Math.round(60 / a.fps));
    if(P.animTimer >= frameDur){
      P.animTimer = 0;
      P.animFrame = (P.animFrame + 1) % a.frames;
    }
  }

  // Сброс зависшей цели:
  // если моб умер, ушёл слишком далеко, скрылся за стеной
  // или игрок покинул его комнату — красная метка исчезает.
  if(P.tid!=null){
    const locked=EN.find(x=>x.id===P.tid);
    if(!targetIsValid(locked))P.tid=null;
  }

  // Fart Zone mine production + 30-minute guard respawns.
  updateFartZoneSystem();

  // Enemies
  for(let i=EN.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const e=EN[i];
    const dx=P.x-e.x,dy=P.y-e.y,d2=dx*dx+dy*dy;
    const reach=42+(e.sz-30)*0.35;
    // В подземелье одновременно размещено более 500 мобов.
    // Далёкие нетронутые мобы "спят" уже после 500px. Проверяем квадрат расстояния,
    // чтобы не делать сотни Math.hypot() на мобильном каждый кадр.
    if(P.scene==='dungeon'&&!e.isBoss&&!e.aggro&&e.hp===e.mhp&&d2>250000){
      if(e.atkCD>0)e.atkCD--;
      e.bob+=0.025;
      continue;
    }
    // V276 FPS: Fart Zone can hold 39–65 guards. Untouched guards far away
    // do not need movement/path/attack AI until the player approaches.
    if(P.scene==='fartzone'&&e.isFartGuard&&!e.aggro&&e.hp===e.mhp&&d2>360000){
      if(e.atkCD>0)e.atkCD--;
      e.bob+=0.025;
      continue;
    }
    const d=Math.sqrt(d2);
    if(e.isAiFighter){
      v174UpdateAiTrainingFighter(e,d);
      continue;
    }
    if(e.isBoss){
      // Boss/static siege objects never leave their anchor.
      if(e.hx!==undefined){e.x=e.hx;e.y=e.hy}

      if(e.isClanSiegeCrystal){
        // Destructible objective: does not move and never attacks.
      }else if(e.isWorldCrystalBoss){
        worldCrystalBossUpdate(e,dx,dy,d);
      }else if(e.isDungeon21Boss){
        dungeon21BossUpdate(e,dx,dy,d);
      }else if(e.isClanBoss){
        if(clanBossIsCerberus(e)){
          cerberusUpdateVisual(e,dx,dy,d);
        }else{
          clanBossUpdateAoe(e,d);
          if(e.aoeState==='idle')clanBossUpdateWhip(e,dx,dy,d);
        }
      }else if(d<=reach&&e.atkCD<=0){
        if(Math.random()*100<effectivePlayerDodge()){
          showPickup('Уворот!','#88ffcc');
        }else{
          P.hp-=playerDmg(e.dmg,e.dmgType||'physical');
        }
        e.atkCD=mobAttackCooldown(e);
        for(let j=0;j<6;j++)PT.push({x:P.x,y:P.y-15,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*5,vy:(Math.random()-.5)*5,life:12,ml:12,sz:2+Math.random()*2,col:'#ff0000'});
      }
    }else{
      // === STANDARD MOB AGGRO / ROOM LEASH ===
      // A mob wakes only when the player enters this mob's personal aggro radius.
      // Dungeon mobs never leave their own room.
      // If the player runs out, the mob reaches the room edge, drops aggro,
      // and returns to its exact spawn point.
      if(e.hx===undefined){e.hx=e.x;e.hy=e.y;e.hunter=true}

      const homeD=Math.hypot(e.x-e.hx,e.y-e.hy);
      let chaseX=P.x,chaseY=P.y;
      let playerInOwnRoom=true;

      if(P.scene==='arena'){
        e.aggro=true;
      }else if(P.scene==='fartzone'&&e.isFartGuard){
        const _fm=fartMineById(e.fartMineId);
        if(_fm){
          const _minePlayerD=Math.hypot(P.x-_fm.x,P.y-_fm.y);
          // Every guard protects its mine, not just its own personal position.
          if(_minePlayerD<=FART_GUARD_AGGRO_RADIUS||d<=FART_GUARD_AGGRO_RADIUS*.82)e.aggro=true;
          if(e.aggro&&_minePlayerD>FART_GUARD_AGGRO_RADIUS*2.25)e.aggro=false;
        }
      }else if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
        playerInOwnRoom=(e.room===undefined)||dgPointInRoom(e.room,P.x,P.y,0);

        // Proximity aggro is individual, not a whole-room group aggro.
        if(!e.aggro && playerInOwnRoom && d<=dungeonAggroRadiusFor(e)){
          e.aggro=true;
          e.edgeChase=false;
          e.edgeWait=0;
        }

        // Direct damage already sets e.aggro=true in the hit code.
        // Once player is outside this room, chase only to the nearest room edge.
        if(e.aggro && e.room!==undefined && !playerInOwnRoom){
          e.edgeChase=true;
          const edgePad=Math.max(12,e.sz*0.22);
          const edge=dgClampToRoom(e.room,P.x,P.y,edgePad);
          chaseX=edge.x;
          chaseY=edge.y;

          const edgeD=Math.hypot(edge.x-e.x,edge.y-e.y);
          if(edgeD<=Math.max(14,e.sp*3.2)){
            e.aggro=false;
            e.edgeChase=false;
            e.edgeWait=0;
          }
        }else if(e.aggro){
          e.edgeChase=false;
          e.edgeWait=0;
        }
      }else{
        if(d<=AGGRO_R)e.aggro=true;
        if(e.aggro&&(d>LEASH_R||homeD>LEASH_R))e.aggro=false;
      }

      if(playerSmokeActive()){
        e.aggro=false;
        e.edgeChase=false;
        e.edgeWait=0;
      }

      let tx,ty,move=false;

      if(e.aggro){
        const cdx=chaseX-e.x,cdy=chaseY-e.y;
        const cd=Math.hypot(cdx,cdy);
        const stopDist=(P.scene==='dungeon'&&e.edgeChase)?10:(e.isFartGuard?FART_GUARD_ATTACK_REACH:reach);
        if(cd>stopDist){
          tx=cdx/cd*e.sp*P.slowMul*(typeof v189EnemyMoveMul==='function'?v189EnemyMoveMul(e):1);
          ty=cdy/cd*e.sp*P.slowMul*(typeof v189EnemyMoveMul==='function'?v189EnemyMoveMul(e):1);
          move=true;
        }
      }else if(homeD>4){
        const s2=Math.min(e.sp*P.slowMul*(typeof v189EnemyMoveMul==='function'?v189EnemyMoveMul(e):1),homeD);
        tx=(e.hx-e.x)/homeD*s2;
        ty=(e.hy-e.y)/homeD*s2;
        move=true;
      }

      if(e.isFartGuard){
        e.fartMoving=!!move;
        if(move&&Math.abs(tx)>0.02)e.face=tx<0?-1:1;
      }

      if(e.type&&e.type.caveSpider){
        if(move){
          if(Math.abs(tx)>Math.abs(ty))e.spiderDir=tx<0?2:3;
          else e.spiderDir=ty<0?0:1;
          e.spiderMoving=true;
        }else{
          e.spiderMoving=false;
        }
      }

      if(e.animPack){
        if(move){
          if(Math.abs(tx)>Math.abs(ty))e.animDir=tx<0?2:3;
          else e.animDir=ty<0?0:1;
          e.animMoving=true;
        }else{
          e.animMoving=false;
        }
      }

      if(move){
        if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
          const bx=e.x,by=e.y;
          const mv=dgSlide(e.x,e.y,tx,ty,e.sz*0.3);

          if(e.room!==undefined){
            const roomPad=Math.max(10,e.sz*0.18);
            if(dgPointInRoom(e.room,mv.x,mv.y,roomPad)){
              e.x=mv.x;e.y=mv.y;
            }else{
              const cl=dgClampToRoom(e.room,mv.x,mv.y,roomPad);
              if(dgCircleWalk(cl.x,cl.y,e.sz*0.3)){
                e.x=cl.x;e.y=cl.y;
              }
            }
          }else{
            e.x=

===== shootT #19 @98225673 =====
sArcherSprites()){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*ARCHER_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(184/224)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&ASSASSIN_ANIM[P.anim]){
    var sc=(P.anim==='idle')?ASSASSIN_IDLE_DRAW_SCALE:(P.anim==='attack'?ASSASSIN_ATTACK_DRAW_SCALE:ASSASSIN_RUN_DRAW_SCALE);
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*sc,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(156/192)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesTankSprites()&&TANK_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*TANK_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(232/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&BERSERKER_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*BERSERKER_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(232/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesPriestSprites()&&PRIEST_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*PRIEST_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(236/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesMageSprites()&&MAGE_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*MAGE_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(236/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesPaladinSprites()&&PALADIN_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*PALADIN_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(234/256)*dh;
  }else{
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*1.4,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-dh;
  }
  return sy+(bob+dy)*_sv;
}
function ppaPlayerClanName(){
  try{
    var c=(typeof CLAN_LOCAL_STATE!=='undefined'&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan)
      ?String(CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan.name||'').trim():'';
    return c;
  }catch(_){return ''}
}
function drawPlayerNickname(){
  var name=ppaPlayerNickname();
  if(!name)return;
  var clan=ppaPlayerClanName();
  var sx=P.x-cam.x;
  var y=ppaPlayerVisualTopScreenY()-9;

  cx.save();
  cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.textBaseline='bottom';
  cx.lineJoin='round';

  if(clan){
    var clanText='['+clan.slice(0,18)+']';
    cx.font='700 8px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
    cx.lineWidth=2.4;
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(8,12,20,.94)';
    cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.78)';
    cx.shadowBlur=3;
    cx.strokeText(clanText,sx,y-12);
    cx.shadowBlur=0;
    cx.fillStyle='#a9cfff';
    cx.fillText(clanText,sx,y-12);
  }

  cx.font='600 10px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
  cx.lineWidth=2.6;
  cx.strokeStyle='rgba(18,8,5,.92)';
  cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.72)';
  cx.shadowBlur=3;
  cx.strokeText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.shadowBlur=0;
  cx.fillStyle='#f2d39a';
  cx.fillText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.restore();
}

function drawPlayer(){
  const sx=P.x-cam.x,sy=P.y-cam.y;
  const siegeVisualScale=P.scene==='clansiege'?CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1;

  // V277: only the player's 8 classes are drawn 10 px smaller.
  // No collision, camera, NPC, mob, AI or combat values are changed.
  const PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX=10;
  const playerClassDrawHeight=(baseHeight)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterDrawHeight(baseHeight,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const playerClassBodySize=(baseSize)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterBodySize(baseSize,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const isGnome=playerUsesGnomeSprites();
  const isArcher=playerUsesArcherSprites();
  const isAssassinCustom=playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&!!ASSASSIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isTank=playerUsesTankSprites()&&!!TANK_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isBerserker=playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&!!BERSERKER_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPriest=playerUsesPriestSprites()&&!!PRIEST_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isMage=playerUsesMageSprites()&&!!MAGE_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPaladin=playerUsesPaladinSprites()&&!!PALADIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isCustomDirectional=isGnome||isArcher||isAssassinCustom||isTank||isBerserker||isPriest||isMage||isPaladin;
  const phoneBodyBase=playerClassBodySize(P.sz);
  const visualBody=(isGnome?phoneBodyBase*0.72:phoneBodyBase)*siegeVisualScale;
  const bob=P.scene==='fartzone'?0:Math.sin(P.bob)*(isGnome?2.0:3);

  // Shadow follows the visible character size.
  // Fart Zone: exact feet-to-ground anchor, no visual hovering.
  const playerShadowY=P.scene==='fartzone'?sy+visualBody*0.40:sy+visualBody*0.45;
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.4)';
  cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,playerShadowY,visualBody*0.38,visualBody*0.14,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Dust when running
  if((Math.abs(P.vx)>0.5||Math.abs(P.vy)>0.5)&&Math.random()<0.25)
    PT.push({x:P.x+(Math.random()-.5)*(isGnome?6:12)*siegeVisualScale,y:P.y+visualBody*0.35,
      vx:-P.vx*0.4+(Math.random()-.5),vy:-P.vy*0.2+(Math.random()-.5)*0.5,
      life:10,ml:10,sz:(isGnome?1.5:2)*siegeVisualScale,col:'rgba(120,100,70,0.4)'});

  cx.save();
  cx.translate(sx,sy+bob);
  if(P.scene==='clansiege')cx.scale(CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE,CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE);

  // Legacy lean/recoil is disabled for the dwarf because his approved
  // 4-frame attack already contains the physical movement.
  if(!isCustomDirectional){
    let lean=0;
    if(P.shootT>0)lean=P.face>0?0.12:-0.12;
    const rc=P.recoil*6*(P.face>0?-1:1);
    cx.translate(rc,0);
    cx.rotate(lean);
  }

  const cur=playerAnimDef(P.anim);
  const canonicalDir=isCustomDirectional?playerDir8():0;
  const dirRow=isGnome
    ? GNOME_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
    : (isArcher
        ? ARCHER_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
        : (isAssassinCustom
            ? (P.anim==='run'
                ? ASSASSIN_RUN_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
                : ASSASSIN_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir])
            : (isTank
                ? TANK_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                : (isBerserker
                    ? BERSERKER_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                    : (isPriest
                        ? PRIEST_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                        : (isMage
                            ? MAGE_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                            : (isPaladin
                                ? PALADIN_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                                : (cur.rows ? getDirRow() : 0))))))));
  const useFlip=isGnome
    ? GNOME_FLIP_BY_DIR[canonicalDir]
    : (isArcher
        ? ARCHER_FLIP_BY_DIR[canonicalDir]
        : ((isAssassinCustom||isTank||isBerserker||isPriest||isMage||isPaladin) ? false : (dirRow===2 && P.face>0)));
  const frameIndex=((P.animFrame%cur.frames)+cur.frames)%cur.frames;

  if(cur.img.complete && cur.img.naturalWidth > 0){
    const fh_row=cur.fh;
    const fw_frame=cur.fw;
    const footY=P.sz*0.40;
    const srcY=dirRow*fh_row;

    let dh,dw,dx,dy;
    if(isGnome){
      // Approved dwarf size stays unchanged.
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*GNOME_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(113/128)*dh);
    }else if(isArcher){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*ARCHER_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(184/224)*dh);
    }else if(isAssassinCustom){
      const assScale=(P.anim==='idle')
        ? ASSASSIN_IDLE_DRAW_SCALE
        : (P.anim==='attack' ? ASSASSIN_ATTACK_DRAW_SCALE : ASSASSIN_RUN_DRAW_SCALE);
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*assScale,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(156/192)*dh);
    }else if(isTank){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*TANK_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(232/256)*dh);
    }else if(isBerserker){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*BERSERKER_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(232/256)*dh);
    }else if(isPriest){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*PRIEST_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(236/256)*dh);
    }else if(isMage){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*MAGE_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(236/256)*dh);
    }else if(isPaladin){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*PALADIN_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(234/256)*dh);
    }else{
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*1.4,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh*(fw_frame/fh_row);
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(footY-dh);
    }

    if(useFlip){
      cx.save();
      cx.scale(-1,1);
      cx.drawImage(cur.img,fw_frame*frameIndex,srcY,fw_frame,fh_row,dx,dy,dw,dh);
      cx.restore();
    }else{
      cx.drawImage(cur.img,fw_frame*frameIndex,srcY,fw_frame,fh_row,dx,dy,dw,dh);
    }
  } else {
    // Fallback while sprites load
    cx.fillStyle='#4488ff';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(0,0,phoneBodyBase*0.4,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
    cx.fillStyle='#fff';cx.font='10px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
    cx.fillText('?',0,-2);
  }
  cx.restore();


  // Melee slash arc visual
  if(P.shootT>0&&P.meleeAng!==undefined&&P.attackMode==='melee'&&!playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&!playerUsesTankSprites()&&!playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&!playerUsesPaladinSprites()){
    const slashAlpha=P.shootT*0.6;
    cx.save();
    cx.translate(sx,sy-8+bob);
    cx.rotate(P.meleeAng);
    // Arc slash
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,200,'+slashAlpha+')';
    cx.lineWidth=4;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.arc(0,0,MELEE_RANGE*0.7,-Math.PI*0.3,Math.PI*0.3);
    cx.stroke();
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,'+slashAlpha*0.5+')';
    cx.lineWidth=2;
    cx.stroke();
    cx.restore();
  }
}

// Toggle combat buttons visibility based on scene

let __v216DungeonHudLast='';
let __v218DungeonHudAt=0;
function v216DungeonTargetHud(){
  const sm=document.getElementById('sceneMode');
  if(!sm||P.scene!=='dungeon')return;
  const now=(typeof performance!=='undefined'&&performance.now)?performance.now():Date.now();
  if(now-__v218DungeonHudAt<80)return;
  __v218DungeonHudAt=now;
  let t=null;
  if(P.tid!=null)t=EN.find(function(e){return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0})||null;
  let out='';
  if(t){
    const hp=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(Number(t.hp)||0));
    const mhp=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Number(t.mhp)||1));
    let nm=(t.type&&t.type.n)||'ЦЕЛЬ';
    if(t.isDungeon21Boss)nm='ВЛАДЫКА СКВЕРНЫ';
    else if(t.isDungeonPhoenixBoss)nm='ФЕНИКС';
    out='<span style="color:#ffb0a5;font-weight:700">HP '+hp+' / '+mhp+'</span> · '+
        '<span style="color:#ead6a7">['+(t.lvl||'?')+'] '+nm+'</span>';
  }else{
    out='<span style="color:#c6b99f">ПОДЗЕМЕЛЬЕ · ЦЕЛЬ НЕ ВЫБРАНА</span>';
  }
  if(out!==__v216DungeonHudLast){
    __v216DungeonHudLast=out;
    sm.innerHTML=out;
  }
  sm.style.display='block';
}
