// 

===== shootT #21 @98229810 =====
ne==='fartzone'?sy+visualBody*0.40:sy+visualBody*0.45;
  cx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.4)';
  cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,playerShadowY,visualBody*0.38,visualBody*0.14,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

  // Dust when running
  if((Math.abs(P.vx)>0.5||Math.abs(P.vy)>0.5)&&Math.random()<0.25)
    PT.push({x:P.x+(Math.random()-.5)*(isGnome?6:12)*siegeVisualScale,y:P.y+visualBody*0.35,
      vx:-P.vx*0.4+(Math.random()-.5),vy:-P.vy*0.2+(Math.random()-.5)*0.5,
      life:10,ml:10,sz:(isGnome?1.5:2)*siegeVisualScale,col:'rgba(120,100,70,0.4)'});

  cx.save();
  cx.translate(sx,sy+bob);
  if(P.scene==='clansiege')cx.scale(CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE,CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE);

  // Legacy lean/recoil is disabled for the dwarf because his approved
  // 4-frame attack already contains the physical movement.
  if(!isCustomDirectional){
    let lean=0;
    if(P.shootT>0)lean=P.face>0?0.12:-0.12;
    const rc=P.recoil*6*(P.face>0?-1:1);
    cx.translate(rc,0);
    cx.rotate(lean);
  }

  const cur=playerAnimDef(P.anim);
  const canonicalDir=isCustomDirectional?playerDir8():0;
  const dirRow=isGnome
    ? GNOME_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
    : (isArcher
        ? ARCHER_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
        : (isAssassinCustom
            ? (P.anim==='run'
                ? ASSASSIN_RUN_SOURCE_ROW[canonicalDir]
                : ASSASSIN_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir])
            : (isTank
                ? TANK_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                : (isBerserker
                    ? BERSERKER_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                    : (isPriest
                        ? PRIEST_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                        : (isMage
                            ? MAGE_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                            : (isPaladin
                                ? PALADIN_4DIR_ROW[canonicalDir]
                                : (cur.rows ? getDirRow() : 0))))))));
  const useFlip=isGnome
    ? GNOME_FLIP_BY_DIR[canonicalDir]
    : (isArcher
        ? ARCHER_FLIP_BY_DIR[canonicalDir]
        : ((isAssassinCustom||isTank||isBerserker||isPriest||isMage||isPaladin) ? false : (dirRow===2 && P.face>0)));
  const frameIndex=((P.animFrame%cur.frames)+cur.frames)%cur.frames;

  if(cur.img.complete && cur.img.naturalWidth > 0){
    const fh_row=cur.fh;
    const fw_frame=cur.fw;
    const footY=P.sz*0.40;
    const srcY=dirRow*fh_row;

    let dh,dw,dx,dy;
    if(isGnome){
      // Approved dwarf size stays unchanged.
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*GNOME_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(113/128)*dh);
    }else if(isArcher){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*ARCHER_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(184/224)*dh);
    }else if(isAssassinCustom){
      const assScale=(P.anim==='idle')
        ? ASSASSIN_IDLE_DRAW_SCALE
        : (P.anim==='attack' ? ASSASSIN_ATTACK_DRAW_SCALE : ASSASSIN_RUN_DRAW_SCALE);
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*assScale,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(156/192)*dh);
    }else if(isTank){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*TANK_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(232/256)*dh);
    }else if(isBerserker){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*BERSERKER_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(232/256)*dh);
    }else if(isPriest){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*PRIEST_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(236/256)*dh);
    }else if(isMage){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*MAGE_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(236/256)*dh);
    }else if(isPaladin){
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*PALADIN_DRAW_SCALE,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh;
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(visualBody*0.40-(234/256)*dh);
    }else{
      dh=playerClassDrawHeight(P.sz*1.4,siegeVisualScale);
      dw=dh*(fw_frame/fh_row);
      dx=Math.round(-dw/2);
      dy=Math.round(footY-dh);
    }

    if(useFlip){
      cx.save();
      cx.scale(-1,1);
      cx.drawImage(cur.img,fw_frame*frameIndex,srcY,fw_frame,fh_row,dx,dy,dw,dh);
      cx.restore();
    }else{
      cx.drawImage(cur.img,fw_frame*frameIndex,srcY,fw_frame,fh_row,dx,dy,dw,dh);
    }
  } else {
    // Fallback while sprites load
    cx.fillStyle='#4488ff';
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(0,0,phoneBodyBase*0.4,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
    cx.fillStyle='#fff';cx.font='10px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
    cx.fillText('?',0,-2);
  }
  cx.restore();


  // Melee slash arc visual
  if(P.shootT>0&&P.meleeAng!==undefined&&P.attackMode==='melee'&&!playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&!playerUsesTankSprites()&&!playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&!playerUsesPaladinSprites()){
    const slashAlpha=P.shootT*0.6;
    cx.save();
    cx.translate(sx,sy-8+bob);
    cx.rotate(P.meleeAng);
    // Arc slash
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,200,'+slashAlpha+')';
    cx.lineWidth=4;
    cx.beginPath();
    cx.arc(0,0,MELEE_RANGE*0.7,-Math.PI*0.3,Math.PI*0.3);
    cx.stroke();
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,'+slashAlpha*0.5+')';
    cx.lineWidth=2;
    cx.stroke();
    cx.restore();
  }
}

// Toggle combat buttons visibility based on scene

let __v216DungeonHudLast='';
let __v218DungeonHudAt=0;
function v216DungeonTargetHud(){
  const sm=document.getElementById('sceneMode');
  if(!sm||P.scene!=='dungeon')return;
  const now=(typeof performance!=='undefined'&&performance.now)?performance.now():Date.now();
  if(now-__v218DungeonHudAt<80)return;
  __v218DungeonHudAt=now;
  let t=null;
  if(P.tid!=null)t=EN.find(function(e){return e&&e.id==P.tid&&e.hp>0})||null;
  let out='';
  if(t){
    const hp=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(Number(t.hp)||0));
    const mhp=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Number(t.mhp)||1));
    let nm=(t.type&&t.type.n)||'ЦЕЛЬ';
    if(t.isDungeon21Boss)nm='ВЛАДЫКА СКВЕРНЫ';
    else if(t.isDungeonPhoenixBoss)nm='ФЕНИКС';
    out='<span style="color:#ffb0a5;font-weight:700">HP '+hp+' / '+mhp+'</span> · '+
        '<span style="color:#ead6a7">['+(t.lvl||'?')+'] '+nm+'</span>';
  }else{
    out='<span style="color:#c6b99f">ПОДЗЕМЕЛЬЕ · ЦЕЛЬ НЕ ВЫБРАНА</span>';
  }
  if(out!==__v216DungeonHudLast){
    __v216DungeonHudLast=out;
    sm.innerHTML=out;
  }
  sm.style.display='block';
}

function updateUI(){
  // V158: Premium + Events are town-only UI.
  // Outside the peaceful city they are hidden completely and any open panel
  // is closed automatically. Returning to town restores the side tabs.
  const cityOnlyUiAllowed=(P.scene==='safe'&&!transitioning);
  const _eventsTab=document.getElementById('eventsSideTab');
  const _premiumTab=document.getElementById('premiumSideTab');
  const _walletTab=document.getElementById('gramWalletSideTab');

  if(!cityOnlyUiAllowed){
    try{if(typeof window.closeEventsMenu==='function')window.closeEventsMenu()}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof window.closePremiumStore==='function')window.closePremiumStore()}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof window.closeGramWallet==='function')window.closeGramWallet()}catch(_){}
  }
  if(_eventsTab)_eventsTab.style.display=cityOnlyUiAllowed?'':'none';
  if(_premiumTab)_premiumTab.style.display=cityOnlyUiAllowed?'':'none';
  if(_walletTab)_walletTab.style.display=cityOnlyUiAllowed?'':'none';
  // Some menu close paths also add .hidden. Clear that stale state whenever
  // we are back in town and the corresponding panel is not open.
  if(cityOnlyUiAllowed){
    const _ep=document.getElementById('eventsPanel'),_pp=document.getElementById('premiumPanel'),_wp=document.getElementById('gramWalletPanel');
    if(_eventsTab&&!(_ep&&_ep.classList.contains('open')))_eventsTab.classList.remove('hidden');
    if(_premiumTab&&!(_pp&&_pp.classList.contains('open')))_premiumTab.classList.remove('hidden');
    if(_walletTab&&!(_wp&&_wp.classList.contains('open')))_walletTab.classList.remove('hidden');
  }
  try{v174AiPanelVisibility()}catch(_){}

  const showCombat=(P.scene==='arena'||P.scene==='dungeon'||P.scene==='clanboss1'||P.scene==='worldboss'||P.scene==='clansiege'||P.scene==='fartzone'||((P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam')&&pvpCombatEnabled));
  ['bAtk','s1','s2','s3','s4'].forEach(function(id){
    const el=document.getElementById(id);
    if(!el)return;
    el.style.opacity=showCombat?'1':'0.28';
    el.style.pointerEvents=showCombat?'auto':'none';
  });
  if(!showCombat)P.tid=null;
  if(typeof INV!=='undefined'){
    document.getElementById('potCount').textContent=String(INV.potions.hp||0);
    const lb=document.getElementById('leaveBtn');
    const canLeave=(P.scene==='clanboss1'||P.scene==='worldboss'||P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam'||(P.scene==='clansiege'&&PPA_SIEGE.phase==='won'));
    const showSceneMode=(P.scene==='arena'||P.scene==='dungeon'||P.scene==='clanboss1'||P.scene==='worldboss'||P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam'||P.scene==='clansiege'||P.scene==='fartzone');
    lb.style.display=canLeave?'block':'none';
    lb.style.top=(P.scene==='clansiege'&&PPA_SIEGE.phase==='won')?'54px':'';
    if(P.scene==='clanboss1')lb.textContent='ПОКИНУТЬ КЛАНОВОГО БОССА';
    else if(P.scene==='worldboss')lb.textContent='ПОКИНУТЬ МИРОВОГО БОССА';
    else if(P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam')lb.textContent='ПОКИНУТЬ PvP ЛОКАЦИЮ';
    else if(P.scene==='clansiege')lb.textContent='В МИРНЫЙ ГОРОД';
    const sm=document.getElementById('sceneMode');
    if(sm){
      sm.style.display=showSceneMode?'block':'none';
      if(P.scene==='arena')sm.textContent='БОЕВАЯ ЗОНА · ВОЛНЫ';
      else if(P.scene==='dungeon'){sm.textContent='ПОДЗЕМЕЛЬЕ · ЦЕЛЬ НЕ ВЫБРАНА';__v216DungeonHudLast='';}
      else if(P.scene==='clanboss1')sm.textContent='БОЕВАЯ ЗОНА · КЛАНОВЫЙ БОСС';
      else if(P.scene==='worldboss')sm.textContent='МИРОВОЙ БОСС · КРИСТАЛЬНЫЙ ТИТАН · 70K HP';
      else if(P.scene==='clansiege')sm.textContent='КЛАНОВАЯ ОСАДА · БОЕВАЯ ЗОНА';
      else if(P.scene==='fartzone')sm.textContent='ФАРТ ЗОНА · БОЕВАЯ ЗОНА · ДОБЫЧА РЕСУРСОВ И PPA';
      else if(P.scene==='pvp1')sm.textContent=(window.PPA_AI_TRAINING_ACTIVE===true)?'ИИ-ИСПЫТАНИЕ · 1 × 1':(PVP_MATCH.active?('1 × 1 · РАУНД '+PVP_MATCH.round+'/'+PVP_MATCH.maxRounds+' · '+pvpMatchScoreText()):(pvpCombatEnabled?'1 × 1 · СВОБОДНЫЙ БОЙ':'1 × 1 · ПОДГОТОВКА 10 СЕК'));
      else if(P.scene==='pvpteam')sm.textContent=PVP_MATCH.active?(PVP_PREVIEW_MODE.replace('x',' × ')+' · РАУНД '+PVP_MATCH.round+'/'+PVP_MATCH.maxRounds+' · '+pvpMatchScoreText()):('КОМАНДНАЯ АРЕНА · '+PVP_PREVIEW_MODE.replace('x',' × '));
    }
  }
}

// Paid class change uses exactly the same class definitions.
const PREMIUM_CLASS_CHANGE_STATS=CLASS_BASE;

function classBaseKey(){
  return classKeyFromName(P.cls)||'assassin';
}
function simpleClassStatsAtLevel(key,lvl){
  const c=CLASS_BASE[key]||CLASS_BASE.assassin;
  const L=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(lvl)||1));
  return {
    key:key,name:c.name,type:c.type,attackMode:c.attackMode,
    hp:c.hp+(L-1)*20,
    mp:c.mp+(L-1)*10,
    atk:c.atk,
    def:c.def_,
    spd:c.spd,
    atkSpd:c.atkSpd,
    range:c.range,
    crit:c.crit,
    dodge:c.dodge,
    critDmg:c.critDmg
  };
}
function applyClassBaseProfile(key,lvl){
  const s=simpleClassStatsAtLevel(key,lvl);
  P.cls=s.name;
  P.baseMhp=s.hp;P.baseMmp=s.mp;
  P.baseAtk=s.atk;P.baseDef=s.def;
  P.baseSp=s.spd;P.baseAtkSpd=s.atkSpd;
  P.baseRange=s.range;P.baseCrit=s.crit;
  P.baseDodge=s.dodge;P.baseCritDmg=s.critDmg;
  P.attackMode=s.attackMode;
  P.attackRange=s.range;
  P.sp=s.spd;
  return s;
}
// ========================================================================


function premiumClassChangeResult(ok,msg,targetKey){
  try{
    if(premiumShopFrame&&premiumShopFrame.contentWindow){
      premiumShopFrame.contentWindow.postMessage({
        type:'premiumClassChangeResult',
        ok:!!ok,message:String(msg||''),targetKey:targetKey||''
      },'*');
    }
  }catch(_){}
}

function premiumClassChangeItem(it,oldKey,newKey){
  if(!it||it.classKey!==oldKey||it.classKey==='all')return false;
  const slots=['weapon','helmet','armor','gloves','ring','legs','boots'];
  if(!slots.includes(it.slot))return false;

  const newName=(CLASS_ITEM_NAMES[newKey]&&CLASS_ITEM_NAMES[newKey][it.slot])||it.name||'Предмет';
  it.name=cleanItemName(newName);
  it.classKey=newKey;
  it.className=CLASS_DISPLAY[newKey]||newKey;
  it.img=classGearArt(it.rarity||'common',newKey,it.slot)||it.img||'';

  // Preserve everything the player already earned on this exact item:
  // rarity, level, enhancement, rolled stats, BM and UID.
  // Converted gear becomes account-bound to prevent class-change arbitrage.
  it.bound=true;
  it.tradeLocked=true;
  it.classChangeConverted=true;
  syncItemBM(it);
  return true;
}

function premiumConvertOwnedClassGear(oldKey,newKey){
  let converted=0;
  Object.keys(INV.equipped||{}).forEach(function(slot){
    if(premiumClassChangeItem(INV.equipped[slot],oldKey,newKey))converted++;
  });
  (INV.bag||[]).forEach(function(it){
    if(premiumClassChangeItem(it,oldKey,newKey))converted++;
  });

  // Only the player's own storages. Clan storage and auction lots are NEVER
  // transformed, otherwise class change could be abused as a market converter.
  if(INV.storage){
    ['personal','premium'].forEach(function(mode){
      (INV.storage[mode]||[]).forEach(function(it){
        if(premiumClassChangeItem(it,oldKey,newKey))converted++;
      });
    });
  }
  return converted;
}

function premiumApplyTargetClass(targetKey){
  const c=PREMIUM_CLASS_CHANGE_STATS[targetKey];
  if(!c)return false;

  applyClassBaseProfile(targetKey,P.lvl);

  // Keep level / XP / rebirths. Return all earned stat points for a fresh
  // redistribution on the new class.
  const a=P.statAlloc||{};
  const refunded=(Number(P.statPts)||0)+Object.keys(a).reduce(function(sum,k){
    return sum+Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(a[k])||0));
  },0);
  P.statAlloc={hp:0,mp:0,atk:0,def:0,spd:0,atkspd:0,critdmg:0,crit:0,dodge:0};
  P.statPts=refunded;

  // Old class combat state never carries across.
  P.anim='idle';P.animFrame=0;P.animTimer=0;P.attacking=false;
  P.dir8=4;P.tid=null;P.shootT=0;P.recoil=0;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0;
  PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0;
  P.skills=[];
  P.skillCd=[0,0,0,0];
  P.dmgMul=1;P.slowMul=1;P.chain=1;

  recomputeStats();
  P.hp=P.mhp;
  P.mp=P.mmp;
  return true;
}

function premiumChangeClass(targetKey){
  targetKey=String(targetKey||'').toLowerCase();
  const cfg=PREMIUM_CLASS_CHANGE_STATS[targetKey];
  if(!cfg){premiumClassChangeResult(false,'Класс не найден',targetKey);return}

  // Paid class change is a town service, not a combat switch.
  if(P.scene!=='safe'){
    premiumClassChangeResult(false,'Сменить класс можно только в Мирном городе',targetKey);return;
  }

  const oldKey=currentGearClassKey();
  if(oldKey===targetKey){
    premiumClassChangeResult(false,'Этот класс уже выбран',targetKey);return;
  }

  const have=Number(INV.gram)||0;
  if(have+1e-9<PREMIUM_CLASS_CHANGE_PRICE){
    premiumClassChangeResult(false,'Не хватает Gram · нужно '+PREMIUM_CLASS_CHANGE_PRICE,targetKey);return;
  }

  const oldName=CLASS_DISPLAY[oldKey]||P.cls||oldKey;
  const converted=premiumConvertOwnedClassGear(oldKey,targetKey);

  // 10 Gram is the service price. This is SPENDING, not a verified real-money
  // top-up, so it must not create auction slots.
  INV.gram=Math.round((have-PREMIUM_CLASS_CHANGE_PRICE)*10)/10;
  if(typeof recordGramSpend==='function')recordGramSpend(PREMIUM_CLASS_CHANGE_PRICE);

  premiumApplyTargetClass(targetKey);

  if(!INV.premiumShop)INV.premiumShop={purchasedBundles:{}};
  INV.premiumShop.lastClassChangeAt=Date.now();
  INV.premiumShop.classChangeCount=Math.max(0,Number(INV.premiumShop.classChangeCount)||0)+1;

  syncAllItemBM();
  saveGame();
  sendInvState();sendPremiumState();sendAuctionState();sendStorageState();sendClanState();
  updateUI();updateConsumableHud(true);

  const msg='Класс изменён: '+oldName+' → '+(CLASS_DISPLAY[targetKey]||cfg.name)+
    ' · экипировки преобразовано: '+converted;
  showPickup('⚔ '+msg+' · −'+PREMIUM_CLASS_CHANGE_PRICE+' Gram','#ffd16b');
  premiumClassChangeResult(true,msg,targetKey);
}

function premiumValidateChoices(id,sel){
  sel=sel||{};
  var pets=Object.keys(PET_DIR_ART||{});
  if(id==='adventurer'||id==='unique'){
    if(!pets.includes(sel.pet))return 'Выбери питомца';
  }
  return '';
}
function premiumBuyBundle(id,sel){
  var cfg=PREMIUM_BUNDLE_CONFIG[id];
  if(!cfg){premiumBundleResult(false,'Набор не найден',id);return}
  if(!INV.premiumShop)INV.premiumShop={purchasedBundles:{}};
  if(!INV.premiumShop.purchasedBundles)INV.premiumShop.purchasedBundles={};
  var err=premiumValidateChoices(id,sel);
  if(err){premiumBundleResult(false,err,id);return}
  var have=Number(INV.gram)||0;
  if(have+1e-9<cfg.price){premiumBundleResult(false,'Не хватает Gram · нужно '+cfg.price,id);return}
  if((INV.bag||[]).length+cfg.bag>100){
    premiumBundleResult(false,'В сумке нужно свободных ячеек: '+cfg.bag,id);return;
  }

  var items=[],slots=PREMIUM_BUNDLE_GEAR_SLOTS.slice();
  if(id==='starter'){
    slots.forEach(function(slot){items.push(premiumMakeGear(slot,'common',3))});
    INV.potions.hp=(INV.potions.hp||0)+100;
    INV.potions.mp=(INV.potions.mp||0)+100;
    INV.consumables.portalStone=(INV.consumables.portalStone||0)+5;
  }else if(id==='growth'){
    slots.forEach(function(slot){items.push(premiumMakeGear(slot,'uncommon',4))});
    INV.potions.hp=(INV.potions.hp||0)+100;
    INV.potions.mp=(INV.potions.mp||0)+100;
    INV.consumables.premiumHpRegen=(INV.consumables.premiumHpRegen||0)+5;
    INV.consumables.premiumMpRegen=(INV.consumables.premiumMpRegen||0)+5;
  }else if(id==='adventurer'){
    slots.forEach(function(slot){items.push(premiumMakeGear(slot,'uncommon',5))});
    items.push(premiumMakePet(sel.pet,'uncommon',5));
  }else if(id==='unique'){
    slots.forEach(function(slot){items.push(premiumMakeGear(slot,'rare',5))});
    items.push(premiumMakePet(sel.pet,'rare',5));
  }else if(id==='epic'){
    slots.forEach(function(slot){items.push(premiumMakeGear(slot,'epic',5))});
    items.push(premiumMakePet(STELLAR_GUARDIAN_NAME,'epic',5));
  }

  if(items.some(function(x){return !x})){
    premiumBundleResult(false,'Не удалось создать один из предметов',id);return;
  }

  INV.gram=Math.round((have-cfg.price)*10)/10;
  if(typeof recordGramSpend==='function')recordGramSpend(cfg.price);
  INV.bag.push.apply(INV.bag,items);
  INV.premiumShop.purchasedBundles[id]=Math.max(0,Number(INV.premiumShop.purchasedBundles[id])||0)+1;
  syncAllItemBM();
  saveGame();
  sendInvState();sendPremiumState();sendAuctionState();sendStorageState();sendClanState();
  updateUI();updateConsumableHud(true);
  showPickup('🎁 Набор получен · −'+String(cfg.price).replace('.',',')+' Gram','#ffd16b');
  premiumBundleResult(true,id==='epic'
    ?'Набор получен! Полный эпический комплект +5 и Звёздный Хранитель +5 добавлены в сумку.'
    :'Набор получен! Полный комплект +5 и питомец +5 добавлены в сумку.',id);
}
function gearBonus(){var b={atk:0,def:0,hp:0,mp:0,crit:0,spd:0,spdFlat:0,critDmg:0,dodge:0,controlResist:0,slowResist:0,hpPct:0,mpPct:0,magicResist:0,armorPen:0,magicPen:0,damageReduction:0,critDamageResist:0,atkSpeedPct:0,moveSpeedPct:0,defPct:0};for(var s in INV.equipped){var it=INV.equipped[s];if(it&&it.stats){for(var k in it.stats)b[k]=(b[k]||0)+it.stats[k];}}return b;}
function talentBonusBM(){return INV.books.filter(function(b){return b.applied}).length*40;}
function allocatedStatBM(){
  var a=P.statAlloc||{};
  return Math.round(
    (Number(a.hp)||0)*5+
    (Number(a.mp)||0)*2+
    (Number(a.atk)||0)*3+
    (Number(a.def)||0)*3+
    (Number(a.spd)||0)*4+
    (Number(a.atkspd)||0)*6+
    (Number(a.critdmg)||0)*2.5+
    (Number(a.crit)||0)*2+
    (Number(a.dodge)||0)*2
  );
}
function computeBM(){
  var gbm=0;
  for(var s in INV.equipped){
    var it=INV.equipped[s];
    if(it){syncItemBM(it);gbm+=(it.bm||0);}
  }
  P.bm=Math.round(gbm+(P.lvl||1)*35+talentBonusBM()+runeBonusBM()+allocatedStatBM());
  return P.bm;
}
function recomputeStats(opts){
  opts=opts||{};
  // Rapid character-stat taps must not rescan the entire bag/storage on every tap.
  // Full recomputes still normalize everything; the stat-only path uses already-normalized gear.
  if(!opts.skipNormalize)normalizeAllAccessoryItems();
  var g=gearBonus(),r=runeBonus(),a=P.statAlloc||{};
  var ck=classBaseKey();
  var c=CLASS_BASE[ck]||CLASS_BASE.assassin;

  // Simple old-style progression: HP/MP live in baseMhp/baseMmp,
  // ATK gets a universal +1.5 per level. The class identity itself stays fixed.
  if(!Number.isFinite(P.baseMhp))P.baseMhp=c.hp+(Math.max(1,P.lvl)-1)*20;
  if(!Number.isFinite(P.baseMmp))P.baseMmp=c.mp+(Math.max(1,P.lvl)-1)*10;
  P.baseAtk=c.atk;P.baseDef=c.def_;
  P.baseSp=c.spd;P.baseAtkSpd=c.atkSpd;
  P.baseRange=c.range;P.baseCrit=c.crit;
  P.baseDodge=c.dodge;P.baseCritDmg=c.critDmg;
  P.attackMode=c.attackMode;

  var _rawHp=P.baseMhp+g.hp+(a.hp||0)*STAT_POINT_GAIN.hp;
  var _rawMp=P.baseMmp+g.mp+(a.mp||0)*STAT_POINT_GAIN.mp;
  P.mhp=Math.round(_rawHp*(1+((g.hpPct||0)+(r.hpPct||0))/100)*clanHpMul());
  P.mmp=Math.round(_rawMp*(1+((g.mpPct||0)+(r.mpPct||0))/100)*clanMpMul());
  if(P.hp>P.mhp)P.hp=P.mhp;if(P.mp>P.mmp)P.mp=P.mmp;

  P.equipAtk=g.atk;P.equipDef=g.def;
  P.atk=c.atk+g.atk+Math.floor((Math.max(1,P.lvl)-1)*1.5)+(a.atk||0)*STAT_POINT_GAIN.atk;
  P.def=(c.def_+g.def+(a.def||0)*STAT_POINT_GAIN.def)*(1+((g.defPct||0)+(r.defPct||0))/100);
  P.crit=c.crit+g.crit+(a.crit||0)*STAT_POINT_GAIN.crit+(r.crit||0);
  P.spd=(c.spd+g.spd*0.12+(g.spdFlat||0)+(a.spd||0)*STAT_POINT_GAIN.spd)*(1+(g.moveSpeedPct||0)/100);
  P.sp=P.spd;
  P.dodge=Math.min(60,c.dodge+g.spd*0.4+(g.dodge||0)+(a.dodge||0)*STAT_POINT_GAIN.dodge+(r.dodge||0));
  P.atkSpd=Math.max(.35,(c.atkSpd+(a.atkspd||0)*STAT_POINT_GAIN.atkspd)*(1+((g.atkSpeedPct||0)+(r.atkSpeedPct||0))/100));
  P.critDmg=c.critDmg+(g.critDmg||0)+(a.critdmg||0)*STAT_POINT_GAIN.critdmg;
  P.attackRange=Math.max(60,c.range);
  P.controlResist=Math.min(80,g.controlResist||0);
  P.slowResist=Math.min(80,g.slowResist||0);
  P.magicResist=Math.min(80,g.magicResist||0);
  P.hpRegen=Math.max(0,Number(g.hpRegen)||0);
  P.mpRegen=Math.max(0,Number(g.mpRegen)||0);
  P.damagePct=Math.max(0,(Number(g.damagePct)||0)+(Number(r.damagePct)||0));
  P.vampirism=Math.max(0,Number(r.vampirism)||0);
  P.physDamagePct=Math.max(0,Number(g.physDamagePct)||0);
  P.magicDamagePct=Math.max(0,Number(g.magicDamagePct)||0);
  P.armorPen=Math.max(0,Math.min(60,Number(g.armorPen)||0));
  P.magicPen=Math.max(0,Math.min(60,Number(g.magicPen)||0));
  P.damageReduction=Math.max(0,Math.min(50,Number(g.damageReduction)||0));
  P.critDamageResist=Math.max(0,Math.min(60,Number(g.critDamageResist)||0));
  P.atkSpeedPct=Math.max(0,Number(g.atkSpeedPct)||0);
  P.moveSpeedPct=Math.max(0,Number(g.moveSpeedPct)||0);
  P.defPct=Math.max(0,Number(g.defPct)||0);
  computeBM();
  if(!opts.deferSave)saveGame();
  if(!opts.deferInv)sendInvState();
  updateBMHud();updateSkillButtons();
}

}
function playerUsesEightDirSprites(){
  return playerUsesGnomeSprites()||playerUsesArcherSprites()||
    (playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&P.anim==='run');
}
function playerAnimDef(name){
  if(playerUsesGnomeSprites() && GNOME_ANIM[name]){
    const a=GNOME_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return GNOME_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesArcherSprites() && ARCHER_ANIM[name]){
    const a=ARCHER_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return ARCHER_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesAssassinSprites() && ASSASSIN_ANIM[name]){
    const a=ASSASSIN_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return ASSASSIN_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesTankSprites() && TANK_ANIM[name]){
    const a=TANK_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return TANK_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesBerserkerSprites() && BERSERKER_ANIM[name]){
    const a=BERSERKER_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return BERSERKER_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesPriestSprites() && PRIEST_ANIM[name]){
    const a=PRIEST_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return PRIEST_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesMageSprites() && MAGE_ANIM[name]){
    const a=MAGE_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return MAGE_ANIM.idle;
  }
  if(playerUsesPaladinSprites() && PALADIN_ANIM[name]){
    const a=PALADIN_ANIM[name];
    if(a.img&&a.img.complete&&a.img.naturalWidth>0)return a;
    return PALADIN_ANIM.idle;
  }
  return ANIM[name]||ANIM.idle;
}
// Canonical direction index: 0=N,1=NE,2=E,3=SE,4=S,5=SW,6=W,7=NW.
function dir8Canonical(dx,dy){
  if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)<0.001)return Number.isFinite(P.dir8)?P.dir8:4;
  let a=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  let n=(a+Math.PI/2)%(Math.PI*2);
  if(n<0)n+=Math.PI*2;
  return Math.round(n/(Math.PI/4))%8;
}
function playerDir8(){
  // Run-and-attack: movement continues in joystick direction, but the
  // character visually turns toward the shot/target for the attack moment.
  const aimingNow=(P.attacking||P.runAttackT>0||P.shootT>0);
  if(aimingNow && Number.isFinite(P.meleeAng)){
    P.dir8=dir8Canonical(Math.cos(P.meleeAng),Math.sin(P.meleeAng));
    return P.dir8;
  }
  if(Math.hypot(P.vx,P.vy)>0.15){
    P.dir8=dir8Canonical(P.vx,P.vy);
  }
  if(!Number.isFinite(P.dir8))P.dir8=4;
  return P.dir8;
}

// canonical direction: N,NE,E,SE,S,SW,W,NW

const GNOME_FLIP_BY_DIR=[false,false,true,false,false,true,false,true];

const GNOME_DRAW_SCALE=1.1570;

function classKeyFromName(name){
  var n=String(name||'').trim().toUpperCase();
  var aliases={
    'СТРАЖ':'tank','ТАНК':'tank',
    'ВАРВАР':'barbarian','БЕРСЕРК':'barbarian',
    'ПАЛАДИН':'paladin',
    'ГНОМ':'gnome','ГНОМ-КАНОНИР':'gnome',
    'ЛУЧНИК':'archer',
    'МАГ':'mage',
    'УБИЙЦА':'assassin','АССАСИН':'assassin',
    'ЖРЕЦ':'priest'
  };
  if(aliases[n])return aliases[n];
  for(var k in CLASS_SKILLS){
    if(String(CLASS_SKILLS[k].name||'').trim().toUpperCase()===n)return k;
  }
  return null;
}

var ALL_GRIMOIRES=(function(){
  var a=[];
  Object.keys(GRIMOIRE_CATALOG).forEach(function(ck){
    ['active','passive'].forEach(function(t){
      (GRIMOIRE_CATALOG[ck][t]||[]).forEach(function(s){
        a.push({id:s.id,n:s.n,ic:s.ic,type:t,classKey:ck,className:GRIMOIRE_CATALOG[ck].name,d:s.d});
      });
    });
  });
  return a;
})();
function findGrimoireSkill(id){
  for(var i=0;i<ALL_GRIMOIRES.length;i++){
    if(ALL_GRIMOIRES[i].id===id){
      var g=ALL_GRIMOIRES[i],a=GRIMOIRE_ART[id]||{};
      if(!g.cardArt)g.cardArt=a.card||'';
      if(!g.iconArt)g.iconArt=a.icon||'';
      return g;
    }
  }
  return null;
}
function skillRank(id){return Math.max(0,Math.min(3,(INV.skillRanks&&INV.skillRanks[id])||0));}
function skillRule(rank){
  if(rank<=0)return {cost:1,chance:1,next:1};
  if(rank===1)return {cost:2,chance:.45,next:2};
  if(rank===2)return {cost:4,chance:.40,next:3};
  return null;
}
function grimChanceForLevel(lv){
  lv=Math.max(1,Math.min(20,(lv|0)||1));
  if(lv<=5)return .0030;
  if(lv<=10)return .0036;
  if(lv<=15)return .0045;
  return .0060;
}
function pushGrimoireDrop(e){
  if(!ALL_GRIMOIRES.length)return;
  var g=ALL_GRIMOIRES[Math.floor(Math.random()*ALL_GRIMOIRES.length)];
  LOOT.push({
    x:e.x+(Math.random()-.5)*40,y:e.y+(Math.random()-.5)*40,
    kind:'grimoire',skillId:g.id,name:'Гримуар · '+g.n,skillName:g.n,
    classKey:g.classKey,className:g.className,skillType:g.type,rarity:'rare',icon:'📖',bob:Math.random()*6
  });
}
function tryGrimoireUpgrade(skillId){
  var sk=findGrimoireSkill(skillId);if(!sk)return;
  var ck=classKeyFromName(P.cls);
  if(sk.classKey!==ck){showPickup('Гримуар другого класса: '+sk.className,'#ff8888');return;}
  var rank=skillRank(skillId),rule=skillRule(rank);
  if(!rule){showPickup(sk.n+' уже III ранга','#ffd168');return;}
  var have=(INV.grimoires&&INV.grimoires[skillId])||0;
  if(have<rule.cost){showPickup('Нужно '+rule.cost+' гримуар(а) · есть '+have,'#ffb36a');return;}
  INV.grimoires[skillId]=have-rule.cost;
  var ok=Math.random()<rule.chance;
  if(ok){
    INV.skillRanks[skillId]=rule.next;
    var roman=['','I','II','III'][rule.next];
    showPickup(sk.n+' · ранг '+roman+' открыт!','#8dff9a');
  }else{
    showPickup('Неудача · '+rule.cost+' гримуар(а) сгорели','#ff6868');
  }
  recomputeStats();
}
// Determine which sprite row to use based on facing direction
// 0=back/up, 1=front/down, 2=side
function getDirRow(){
  // During standing attack OR run-and-attack, face the target/shot while
  // movement itself remains controlled by the joystick.
  if((P.attacking||P.runAttackT>0||P.shootT>0) && P.meleeAng !== undefined){
    const ax = Math.cos(P.meleeAng);
    const ay = Math.sin(P.meleeAng);
    if(Math.abs(ay) > Math.abs(ax) * 0.8){
      return ay > 0 ? 1 : 0;
    }
    return 2;
  }
  // During movement, use velocity with smooth direction switching
  const sp = Math.sqrt(P.vx*P.vx + P.vy*P.vy);
  if(sp > 0.5){
    // Use a threshold that favors vertical for more natural 4-dir movement
    if(Math.abs(P.vy) > Math.abs(P.vx) * 0.8){
      P.lastDir = P.vy > 0 ? 1 : 0;
    } else if(Math.abs(P.vx) > Math.abs(P.vy) * 0.8) {
      P.lastDir = 2;
    } else {
      // Diagonal: pick based on dominant axis
      if(Math.abs(P.vy) >= Math.abs(P.vx)) {
        P.lastDir = P.vy > 0 ? 1 : 0;
      } else {
        P.lastDir = 2;
      }
    }
  }
  return P.lastDir;
}

function worldBossLootUpdate(){
  if(!WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active)return;
  const now=Date.now();

  if(P.scene!=='worldboss'){
    worldBossFallbackReward();
    return;
  }
  if(now>=WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.expiresAt){
    worldBossFallbackReward();
    return;
  }

  for(const c of WORLD_BOSS_CHESTS){
    const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(now-c.born)/720));
    const ease=1-Math.pow(1-t,3);
    c.x=c.sx+(c.tx-c.sx)*ease;
    c.y=c.sy+(c.ty-c.sy)*ease;
    c.bob+=0.08;

    // Сундуки персональные: пет их не подбирает.
    if(t>=0.92&&Math.hypot(P.x-c.x,P.y-c.y)<55){
      worldBossGrantChestReward(c);
      break;
    }
  }
}
function drawWorldBossPersonalChests(){

    const sy=c.y-cam.y+Math.sin(c.bob)*3;
    const ancient=c.rarity==='ancient',rare=c.rarity==='rare';
    const edge=ancient?'#d999ff':(rare?'#6bbaff':'#e0b56c');
    const body=ancient?'#613875':(rare?'#234d72':'#6b4528');
    const lid=ancient?'#9b62bd':(rare?'#3d7aa6':'#95683a');

    cx.save();

    cx.beginPath();cx.ellipse(sx,sy+13,19,6,0,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();

    cx.globalAlpha=.18;
    cx.fillStyle=edge;
    cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy,28,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();
    cx.globalAlpha=1;

    cx.restore();
  }

  const sec=Math.max(0,Math.ceil((WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.expiresAt-now)/1000));
  cx.save();
  cx.font='bold 12px monospace';cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.fillStyle='#d9efff';
  cx.fillText('ВЫБЕРИ 1 СУНДУК · '+sec+' сек',_wbViewW/2,36);
  cx.textAlign='left';cx.restore();
}

// V178: independent post-kill heartbeat. It is intentionally separate from
// the main update() chain so one unrelated combat/UI exception cannot strand
// the player or the personal reward phase after the Titan dies.
let WORLD_BOSS_POSTKILL_HEARTBEAT=null;
function worldBossEnsurePostKillHeartbeat(){
  if(WORLD_BOSS_POSTKILL_HEARTBEAT)return;
  WORLD_BOSS_POSTKILL_HEARTBEAT=setInterval(function(){
    try{
      if(!WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active){
        clearInterval(WORLD_BOSS_POSTKILL_HEARTBEAT);
        WORLD_BOSS_POSTKILL_HEARTBEAT=null;
        return;
      }
      if(P.scene!=='worldboss'){worldBossFallbackReward();return}
      const now=Date.now();
      if(now>=WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.expiresAt){worldBossFallbackReward();return}
      transitioning=false;
      if(P.hp>0)P.dead=false;
      for(const c of WORLD_BOSS_CHESTS){
        c.x=c.tx;c.y=c.ty;c.bob+=0.05;
        if(Math.hypot(P.x-c.x,P.y-c.y)<58){
          worldBossGrantChestReward(c);
          break;
        }
      }
    }catch(err){
      console.error('V178 world boss heartbeat',err);
    }
  },150);
}

function worldCrystalBossResetFx(){
  WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES.length=0;
  WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX=null;
  WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX=null;
}
// V177: hard unlock after Crystal Titan death. The killing hit can happen while
// Smart Attack, an attack animation or a captured joystick pointer is active.
// Reset every transient combat/input state before the post-kill chest phase.
function worldBossPostKillUnlock(bossX,bossY){
  try{cancelSmartAttack()}catch(_){}
  P.tid=null;
  attackQueued=false;
  P.attacking=false;P.shootT=0;P.recoil=0;P.runAttackT=0;
  P.anim='idle';P.animFrame=0;P.animTimer=0;
  P.vx=0;P.vy=0;
  // Stop every input pointer that can survive the killing frame on Android.
  try{if(typeof activePointer!=='undefined')activePointer=null}catch(_){}
  try{if(typeof joyPointer!=='undefined')joyPointer=null}catch(_){}
  try{resetJoystick()}catch(_){}
  worldCrystalBossResetFx();
  WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES.length=0;
  PR.length=0;
  // Never keep the hero buried under the Titan's former body/loot origin.
  const bx=Number(bossX)||WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_POS.x,by=Number(bossY)||WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_POS.y;
  if(Math.hypot(P.x-bx,P.y-by)<125){
    const dirs=[[0,1],[1,0],[-1,0],[0,-1],[.707,.707],[-.707,.707],[.707,-.707],[-.707,-.707]];
    for(let rr=150;rr<=270;rr+=30){
      let placed=false;
      for(const q of dirs){
        const tx=bx+q[0]*rr,ty=by+q[1]*rr;
        if(worldBossCircleWalk(tx,ty,(P.sz||60)*.24)){P.x=tx;P.y=ty;placed=true;break}
      }
      if(placed)break;
    }
  }
  // This scene is not transitioning after a kill; make sure no stale lock survives.
  transitioning=false;
  try{updateUI()}catch(_){}
}

function worldCrystalBossBodyGeometry(e){
  if(!e)return null;
  return{
    cx:e.x,cy:e.y+WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_SHADOW.cy,
    rx:WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_SHADOW.rx,ry:WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_SHADOW.ry
  };
}
function worldCrystalBossBodyMetric(e,x,y){
  const g=worldCrystalBossBodyGeometry(e);
  if(!g)return Infinity;
  const px=x,py=y+(P.sz||60)*.45;
  const rx=g.rx+(P.sz||60)*.38;
  const ry=g.ry+(P.sz||60)*.14;
  const dx=(px-g.cx)/Math.max(1,rx),dy=(py-g.cy)/Math.max(1,ry);
  return dx*dx+dy*dy;
}
function worldCrystalBossBodyFreeAt(x,y,fromX,fromY){
  const e=worldCrystalBossEntity();
  if(!e)return true;
  const q=worldCrystalBossBodyMetric(e,x,y);
  if(q>=1)return true;
  if(fromX!==undefined&&fromY!==undefined){
    const prev=worldCrystalBossBodyMetric(e,fromX,fromY);
    if(prev<1&&q>prev)return true;
  }
  return false;
}
function worldBossMoveWithCrystalCollision(x,y,dx,dy,r){
  let m=worldBossSlide(x,y,dx,dy,r);
  if(worldCrystalBossBodyFreeAt(m.x,m.y,x,y))return m;
  m=worldBossSlide(x,y,dx,0,r);
  if(worldCrystalBossBodyFreeAt(m.x,m.y,x,y))return m;
  m=worldBossSlide(x,y,0,dy,r);
  if(worldCrystalBossBodyFreeAt(m.x,m.y,x,y))return m;
  return{x:x,y:y};
}
function worldCrystalBossPlayerIsBehind(e){
  if(!e)return false;
  const playerFoot=P.y+(P.sz||60)*.40;
  const bossFoot=e.y+WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_SHADOW.cy+8;
  const overlapX=Math.abs(P.x-e.x)<WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_DRAW*.46+(P.sz||60)*.30;
  const overlapY=playerFoot>bossFoot-WORLD_CRYSTAL_BOSS_DRAW*.73&&playerFoot<bossFoot+5;
  return overlapX&&overlapY;
}
function worldCrystalBossLaunch(e){
  const dx=P.x-e.x,dy=(P.y-18)-e.y;
  const d=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
  const speed=8.5;
  WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES.push({
    x:e.x,y:e.y-105,
    vx:dx/d*speed,vy:dy/d*speed,
    life:220,born:Date.now(),angle:Math.atan2(dy,dx),
    dmg:WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILE_DAMAGE
  });
}
function worldCrystalBossDoAoe(e,now){
  WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_FX={x:e.x,y:e.y+8,born:now,duration:950,r:WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_RADIUS};
  e.wbState='aoe';
  e.wbActionUntil=now+900;
  const d=Math.hypot(P.x-e.x,(P.y+(P.sz||60)*.45)-(e.y+8));
  if(d<=WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_RADIUS){
    if(Math.random()*100<effectivePlayerDodge()){
      showPickup('Уворот от AOE!','#88ffcc');
    }else{
      P.hp-=playerDmg(WORLD_CRYSTAL_AOE_DAMAGE,'magic');
      showPickup('Кристальный удар','#66bbff');
    }
  }
}
function worldCrystalBossUpdate(e,dx,dy,d){
  if(!e||e.hp<=0)return;
  e.x=e.hx;e.y=e.hy;
  e.wbDir=worldCrystalBossDir(dx,dy);
  const now=Date.now();

  // Exact 5-second crystal cadence.
  if(now>=e.wbNextCrystal){
    while(now>=e.wbNextCrystal)e.wbNextCrystal+=WORLD_CRYSTAL_CRYSTAL_MS;
    worldCrystalBossLaunch(e);
    if(e.wbState==='idle'){
      e.wbState='throw';
      e.wbActionUntil=now+520;
    }
  }

  // Random AoE cadence: next interval is one of 7 / 12 / 16 seconds.
  if(now>=e.wbNextAoe&&!e.wbAoePending){
    e.wbNextAoe=now+worldCrystalBossNextAoeDelay();
    e.wbAoePending=true;
    e.wbAoeImpactAt=now+620;
    e.wbState='stomp';
    e.wbActionUntil=now+700;
    WORLD_CRYSTAL_STOMP_FX={x:e.x,y:e.y+10,born:now,duration:700};
  }
  if(e.wbAoePending&&now>=e.wbAoeImpactAt){
    e.wbAoePending=false;
    worldCrystalBossDoAoe(e,now);
  }
  if(!e.wbAoePending&&now>=e.wbActionUntil&&e.wbState!=='idle'){
    e.wbState='idle';
  }
}
function worldCrystalBossUpdateProjectiles(){
  if(P.scene!=='worldboss'){WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES.length=0;return}
  for(let i=WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const f=WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES[i];
    f.x+=f.vx;f.y+=f.vy;f.life--;
    let kill=f.life<=0||!worldBossPointWalk(f.x,f.y);
    if(!kill&&Math.hypot(f.x-P.x,f.y-(P.y-8))<30){
      if(Math.random()*100<effectivePlayerDodge())showPickup('Уворот!','#88ffcc');
      else P.hp-=playerDmg(f.dmg,'magic');
      kill=true;
      // Tiny impact only: avoids heavy particle spam on mobile.
      for(let k=0;k<3;k++)PT.push({
        x:f.x,y:f.y,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*2.5,vy:(Math.random()-.5)*2.5,
        life:9,ml:9,sz:2,col:'#66bbff'
      });
    }
    if(kill)WORLD_CRYSTAL_PROJECTILES.splice(i,1);
  }
}

let __ppaHudNextAt=0;
function update(){
  PPAOnlineTick();
  ppaCleanupDeathRemnants();
  updateConsumableHud(false);
  updatePremiumRegen();
  if(P.dead)return;
  v174AiTickPlayerDebuffs();

  const _smartMove=updateSmartAttackInput();
  // V139: Smart Attack only supplies movement when joystick is neutral.
  // Any live joystick input keeps full manual movement while target lock stays active.
  const _moveX=_smartMove?_smartMove.x:jX;
  const _moveY=_smartMove?_smartMove.y:jY;

  // V174: class-AI crowd control is numeric only (no particle FX).
  const _aiMoveMul=Date.now()<(P.aiRootUntil||0)?0:(Date.now()<(P.aiSlowUntil||0)?Math.max(.30,Number(P.aiSlowMul)||.55):1);
  P.vx=_moveX*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  P.vy=_moveY*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;

    }else{
      // === STANDARD MOB AGGRO / ROOM LEASH ===
      // A mob wakes only when the player enters this mob's personal aggro radius.

        }else{

        }
      }

        }else{

        }

              }
            }
          }else{
            e.x=mv.x;e.y=mv.y;
          }

            if(moved<0.05)e.edgeWait=(e.edgeWait||0)+1;
            else e.edgeWait=0;
            if(e.edgeWait>18){
              e.aggro=false;
              e.edgeChase=false;
              e.edgeWait=0;
            }
          }
        }else if(P.scene==='fartzone'&&e.isFartGuard){

      P.animFrame++;
      if(P.animFrame >= a.frames){
        P.attacking = false;
        P.animFrame = 0;
        P.animTimer = 0;
      }
    }
  } else {
    const moving = movingForAnim;
    const nextAnim = moving ? 'run' : 'idle';
    if(P.anim !== nextAnim){
      // Smooth transition: don't reset to frame 0 if coming from similar pose
      P.anim = nextAnim;
      P.animFrame = 0;
      P.animTimer = 0;
    }
    const a = playerAnimDef(nextAnim);
    P.animTimer++;
    const frameDur = Math.max(1, Math.round(60 / a.fps));
    if(P.animTimer >= frameDur){
      P.animTimer = 0;
      P.animFrame = (P.animFrame + 1) % a.frames;
    }
  }

  // Сброс зависшей цели:
  // если моб умер, ушёл слишком далеко, скрылся за стеной
  // или игрок покинул его комнату — красная метка исчезает.
  if(P.tid!=null){
    const locked=EN.find(x=>x.id===P.tid);
    if(!targetIsValid(locked))P.tid=null;
  }

  // Fart Zone mine production + 30-minute guard respawns.
  updateFartZoneSystem();

  // Enemies
  for(let i=EN.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const e=EN[i];
    const dx=P.x-e.x,dy=P.y-e.y,d2=dx*dx+dy*dy;
    const reach=42+(e.sz-30)*0.35;
    // В подземелье одновременно размещено более 500 мобов.
    // Далёкие нетронутые мобы "спят" уже после 500px. Проверяем квадрат расстояния,
    // чтобы не делать сотни Math.hypot() на мобильном каждый кадр.
    if(P.scene==='dungeon'&&!e.isBoss&&!e.aggro&&e.hp===e.mhp&&d2>250000){
      if(e.atkCD>0)e.atkCD--;
      e.bob+=0.025;
      continue;
    }
    // V276 FPS: Fart Zone can hold 39–65 guards. Untouched guards far away
    // do not need movement/path/attack AI until the player approaches.
    if(P.scene==='fartzone'&&e.isFartGuard&&!e.aggro&&e.hp===e.mhp&&d2>360000){
      if(e.atkCD>0)e.atkCD--;
      e.bob+=0.025;
      continue;
    }

      continue;
    }
    if(e.isBoss){

      }else if(e.isDungeon21Boss){
        dungeon21BossUpdate(e,dx,dy,d);
      }else if(e.isClanBoss){
        if(clanBossIsCerberus(e)){
          cerberusUpdateVisual(e,dx,dy,d);
        }else{
          clanBossUpdateAoe(e,d);
          if(e.aoeState==='idle')clanBossUpdateWhip(e,dx,dy,d);
        }
      }else if(d<=reach&&e.atkCD<=0){
        if(Math.random()*100<effectivePlayerDodge()){
          showPickup('Уворот!','#88ffcc');
        }else{
          P.hp-=playerDmg(e.dmg,e.dmgType||'physical');
        }
        e.atkCD=mobAttackCooldown(e);
        for(let j=0;j<6;j++)PT.push({x:P.x,y:P.y-15,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*5,vy:(Math.random()-.5)*5,life:12,ml:12,sz:2+Math.random()*2,col:'#ff0000'});
      }
    }else{
      // === STANDARD MOB AGGRO / ROOM LEASH ===
      // A mob wakes only when the player enters this mob's personal aggro radius.
      // Dungeon mobs never leave their own room.
      // If the player runs out, the mob reaches the room edge, drops aggro,
      // and returns to its exact spawn point.
      if(e.hx===undefined){e.hx=e.x;e.hy=e.y;e.hunter=true}

      const homeD=Math.hypot(e.x-e.hx,e.y-e.hy);
      let chaseX=P.x,chaseY=P.y;
      let playerInOwnRoom=true;

      if(P.scene==='arena'){
        e.aggro=true;
      }else if(P.scene==='fartzone'&&e.isFartGuard){
        const _fm=fartMineById(e.fartMineId);
        if(_fm){
          const _minePlayerD=Math.hypot(P.x-_fm.x,P.y-_fm.y);
          // Every guard protects its mine, not just its own personal position.
          if(_minePlayerD<=FART_GUARD_AGGRO_RADIUS||d<=FART_GUARD_AGGRO_RADIUS*.82)e.aggro=true;
          if(e.aggro&&_minePlayerD>FART_GUARD_AGGRO_RADIUS*2.25)e.aggro=false;
        }
      }else if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
        playerInOwnRoom=(e.room===undefined)||dgPointInRoom(e.room,P.x,P.y,0);

        // Proximity aggro is individual, not a whole-room group aggro.
        if(!e.aggro && playerInOwnRoom && d<=dungeonAggroRadiusFor(e)){
          e.aggro=true;
          e.edgeChase=false;
          e.edgeWait=0;
        }

        // Direct damage already sets e.aggro=true in the hit code.
        // Once player is outside this room, chase only to the nearest room edge.
        if(e.aggro && e.room!==undefined && !playerInOwnRoom){
          e.edgeChase=true;
          const edgePad=Math.max(12,e.sz*0.22);
          const edge=dgClampToRoom(e.room,P.x,P.y,edgePad);
          chaseX=edge.x;
          chaseY=edge.y;

          const edgeD=Math.hypot(edge.x-e.x,edge.y-e.y);
          if(edgeD<=Math.max(14,e.sp*3.2)){
            e.aggro=false;
            e.edgeChase=false;
            e.edgeWait=0;
          }
        }else if(e.aggro){
          e.edgeChase=false;
          e.edgeWait=0;
        }
      }else{
        if(d<=AGGRO_R)e.aggro=true;
        if(e.aggro&&(d>LEASH_R||homeD>LEASH_R))e.aggro=false;
      }

      if(playerSmokeActive()){
        e.aggro=false;
        e.edgeChase=false;
        e.edgeWait=0;
      }

      let tx,ty,move=false;

      if(e.aggro){
        const cdx=chaseX-e.x,cdy=chaseY-e.y;
        const cd=Math.hypot(cdx,cdy);
        const stopDist=(P.scene==='dungeon'&&e.edgeChase)?10:(e.isFartGuard?FART_GUARD_ATTACK_REACH:reach);
        if(cd>stopDist){
          tx=cdx/cd*e.sp*P.slowMul*(typeof v189EnemyMoveMul==='function'?v189EnemyMoveMul(e):1);
          ty=cdy/cd*e.sp*P.slowMul*(typeof v189EnemyMoveMul==='function'?v189EnemyMoveMul(e):1);
          move=true;
        }
      }else if(homeD>4){
        const s2=Math.min(e.sp*P.slowMul*(typeof v189EnemyMoveMul==='function'?v189EnemyMoveMul(e):1),homeD);
        tx=(e.hx-e.x)/homeD*s2;
        ty=(e.hy-e.y)/homeD*s2;
        move=true;
      }

      if(e.isFartGuard){
        e.fartMoving=!!move;
        if(move&&Math.abs(tx)>0.02)e.face=tx<0?-1:1;
      }

      if(e.type&&e.type.caveSpider){
        if(move){
          if(Math.abs(tx)>Math.abs(ty))e.spiderDir=tx<0?2:3;
          else e.spiderDir=ty<0?0:1;
          e.spiderMoving=true;
        }else{
          e.spiderMoving=false;
        }
      }

      if(e.animPack){
        if(move){
          if(Math.abs(tx)>Math.abs(ty))e.animDir=tx<0?2:3;
          else e.animDir=ty<0?0:1;
          e.animMoving=true;
        }else{
          e.animMoving=false;
        }
      }

      if(move){
        if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
          const bx=e.x,by=e.y;
          const mv=dgSlide(e.x,e.y,tx,ty,e.sz*0.3);

          if(e.room!==undefined){
            const roomPad=Math.max(10,e.sz*0.18);
            if(dgPointInRoom(e.room,mv.x,mv.y,roomPad)){
              e.x=mv.x;e.y=mv.y;
            }else{
              const cl=dgClampToRoom(e.room,mv.x,mv.y,roomPad);
              if(dgCircleWalk(cl.x,cl.y,e.sz*0.3)){
                e.x=cl.x;e.y=cl.y;
              }
            }
          }else{
            e.x=mv.x;e.y=mv.y;
          }

          // Collision can make the exact mathematical edge unreachable.
          // A short stall therefore counts as "reached room boundary".
          if(e.edgeChase){
            const moved=Math.hypot(e.x-bx,e.y-by);
            if(moved<0.05)e.edgeWait=(e.edgeWait||0)+1;
            else e.edgeWait=0;
            if(e.edgeWait>18){
              e.aggro=false;
              e.edgeChase=false;
              e.edgeWait=0;
            }
          }
        }else if(P.scene==='fartzone'&&e.isFartGuard){
          const gx=e.x+tx,gy=e.y+ty;
          // Guards actively chase, but never leave the hand-drawn playable contour.
          if(pointInPolygonFartZone(gx,gy)){
            e.x=gx;e.y=gy;
          }else{
            // Slide one axis at a time along the collision edge.
            if(pointInPolygonFartZone(gx,e.y))e.x=gx;
            if(pointInPolygonFartZone(e.x,gy))e.y=gy;
          }
        }else{
          e.x+=tx;e.y+=ty;
        }
      }

      // No attacks through the room boundary after the player has escaped.
      const canHitPlayer=(P.scene!=='dungeon'||e.room===undefined||playerInOwnRoom);
      const _

ies movement when joystick is neutral.
  // Any live joystick input keeps full manual movement while target lock stays active.
  const _moveX=_smartMove?_smartMove.x:jX;
  const _moveY=_smartMove?_smartMove.y:jY;

  // V174: class-AI crowd control is numeric only (no particle FX).
  const _aiMoveMul=Date.now()<(P.aiRootUntil||0)?0:(Date.now()<(P.aiSlowUntil||0)?Math.max(.30,Number(P.aiSlowMul)||.55):1);
  P.vx=_moveX*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  P.vy=_moveY*P.sp*shopSpeedMul()*_aiMoveMul;
  let nx=P.x+P.vx, ny=P.y+P.vy;

  // Keep in bounds (per scene)
  const sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  nx=Math.max(sc.bounds.minX,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxX,nx));
  ny=Math.max(sc.bounds.minY,Math.min(sc.bounds.maxY,ny));

  if(P.scene==='arena'){
    // Stay on the circular arena floor — no wandering into the black void
    const c=sc.center,rad=460;
    const dx=nx-c.x,dy=ny-c.y,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
    if(dist>rad){nx=c.x+dx/dist*rad;ny=c.y+dy/dist*rad}
  }else if(P.scene==='pvp1'||P.scene==='pvpteam'){
    const mv=pvpSlide(P.scene,P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='dungeon'){
    // Dungeon walls — slide along corridors, never clip through
    const mv=dgSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='clansiege'){
    const mv=clanSiegeSlide(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;

    const mv=clanBossMoveWithBodyCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;

    const mv=worldBossMoveWithCrystalCollision(P.x,P.y,nx-P.x,ny-P.y,P.sz*0.24);
    nx=mv.x;ny=mv.y;
  }else if(P.scene==='fartzone'){
    // Exact user-drawn meadow boundary: outside = solid collision.
    // Do not alter town/building hitboxes or other scenes.
    if(!pointInPolygonFartZone(nx,ny)){
      nx=P.x;ny=P.y;
    }
  }else{
    // Solid buildings — can't walk through walls or across rooftops
    const resolved=resolveCollision(nx,ny);
    nx=resolved.x;ny=resolved.y;
  }
  // V104: keep the player's visible foot shadow outside Phoenix/mob shadows.
  // The map/wall collision above remains authoritative; this only adds body blocking.
  if(P.scene==='dungeon'||P.scene==='arena'){

    nx=_bodyMv.x;ny=_bodyMv.y;

  P.x=nx;P.y=ny;

  if(P.portalCD>0)P.portalCD--;

      changeScene(p.target);

      // Gnome/ranged legacy behavior stays unchanged:
      // projectile can fire while the running animation continues.

      P.anim='run';
      P.animTimer=Math.min(P.animTimer||0,6);

      // Assassin AND Archer now use their real ATTACK sprites while moving.
      // Movement itself is NOT cancelled; joystick/Smart Attack still moves P.x/P.y.
      P.attacking=true;
      P.anim='attack';
      P.animFrame=0;
      P.animTimer=0;
      P.runAttackT=movingNow?14:0;

  if(P.runAttackT>0)P.runAttackT--;
